# Suttantapiṭake Khuddakanikāyassa Apadānaṃ (Paṭhamo Bhāgo)

> **Source:** Syāmaraṭṭhassa Tepiṭakaṃ (Siam Rath Edition, Volume 32)
> **Edition:** Royal Thai Edition (King Rama VII)

---


<!-- Siam Rath Page 1 -->
<a id="page-1"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 1]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa    
apadānaṃ    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

Paṭhamo buddhavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ buddhāpadānaṃ    
[1] tathāgataṃ jetavane vasantaṃ    
apucchi vedehamunī nataṅgo    
sabbaññubuddhā kira nāma honti    
bhavanti te hetuhi kehi dhīrā [^1-1] .

Tadāha sabbaññuvaro mahesī    
ānandabhaddaṃ madhurassarena    
ye sabbabuddhesu katādhikārā    
aladdhamokkhā jinasāsanesu .

Teneva sambodhimukhena dhīrā    
ajjhāsayenāpi mahābalena    
paññāya tejena sutikkhapaññā    
sabbaññubhāvaṃ anupāpuṇanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 2 -->
<a id="page-2"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 2]*

Ahampi pubbabuddhesu           buddhattaṃ abhipatthayiṃ    
tiṃsa pārami sampuṇṇā [1]     dhammarājā asaṅkhiyā .

Sambodhiṃ buddhaseṭṭhānaṃ         sasaṅghe lokanāyake    
dasaṅgulī namassitvā           sirasā abhivādaye [^2-2] .

Yāvatā buddhakhettesu         ratanā vijjantisaṅkhiyā    
ākāsaṭṭhā ca bhummaṭṭhā       manasā sabbamāhare [^2-3] .

Tattha rūpiyabhūmiyaṃ              pāsādaṃ māpaye ahaṃ    
nekabhummaṃ ratanamayaṃ [^2-4]         ubbiddhaṃ nabhamuggataṃ .

Vicittathamabhaṃ sukataṃ             		suvibhattaṃ mahārahaṃ    
kanakāmayasaṅghāṭaṃ [^2-5]          	kontacchattehi maṇḍitaṃ .

Paṭhamā veḷuriyā bhūmi          		vimalabbhasamā subhā    
naḷinājalajākiṇṇā [^2-6]         varakañcanabhūmiyā .

Pahaṭṭhasākhāpavāḷa-            vaṇṇā lohitakā subhā [^2-7]    
indagopakavaṇṇābhā           bhūmi obhāsatī disā .

Suvibhattā gharamukhā                niyyuhā [^2-8] sīhapañjarā    
caturo vedikā jālā             gandhāveḷā manoramā .

Nīlā pītā lohitakā            odātā suddhakāḷakā    
kūṭāgāravarūpetā                 sattaratanabhūsitā .

Olokamayā padumā              vāḷavihaṅgasobhitā    
nakkhattatārakākiṇṇā        candasuriyehi [^2-9] maṇḍitā .

#[^2-1] ma.   Manasāyeva hutvāna         dhammarājā asaṅkhiyā .

#   atha buddhāpadānāni               suṇātha suddhamānasā .

#[^2-2] ma. Abhivādayiṃ . 3. Ma. Sabbamāhariṃ . [^2-4] ma. Ratanāmayaṃ . [^2-5] ma. Kanakamaya- .

#[^2-6] ma. Naḷinajalajākiṇṇā . [^2-7] ma. Pavāḷasā pavāḷavaṇṇā kāci lohitakā subhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 3 -->
<a id="page-3"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 3]*

Hemajālena sañchannā         	soṇṇakiṃkiṇikāyutā    
vātavegena kujjanti          	soṇṇajālā [^3-1] manoramā .

Mañjeṭṭhakaṃ lohitakaṃ           	pītakaṃ haripañjaraṃ    
nānāraṅgehi saṃcittaṃ [^3-2]       	ussitaddhajamālinī .

Nānā [^3-3] bahūnekasatā        	phalakā [^3-4] rajatāmayā    
maṇimayā lohitaṅkā           	masāragallamayā tathā .

Nānāsayanacittitā [^3-5]         	saṇhakāsikasanthatā    
kambalā dukulā cīnā          	pattuṇṇā paṇḍupāvurā .

Vividhattharaṇaṃ sabbaṃ             	manasā paññapemahaṃ    
tāsu tāsveva bhūmīsu          	ratanakūṭalaṅkatā .

Maṇiverocanā ukkā          	dhārayantā sutiṭṭhare    
sobhanti esikā thambhā        	subhā kañcanatoraṇā .

Jambonadā sāramayā          	atho rajatamayāpica    
nekāsandhī suvibhattā          	kavāṭaggalacittitā .

Ubhato puṇṇaghaṭānekā         	padumuppalasaṃyutā    
atīte sabbabuddhe ca          	sasaṅghe lokanāyake .

Pakativaṇṇarūpena              		nimminitvā sasāvake    
tena dvārena pavīsitvā       	sabbabuddhā sasāvakā .

Sabbasovaṇṇamaye pīṭhe         	nisinnā ariyamaṇḍalā    
ye ca etarahi atthi          		buddhā loke anuttarā .

#[^3-1] ma. Soṇṇamālā . [^3-2] ma. Sampitaṃ . [^3-3] ma. Na naṃ . [^3-4] ma. Phalikā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 4 -->
<a id="page-4"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 4]*

Atītā [^4-1] vattamānā ca       	bhavanaṃ sabbe samāruhuṃ    
paccekabuddhenekasate         	sayambhū aparājite .

Atīte vattamāne ca          		bhavanaṃ sabbe samāruhuṃ    
kapparukkhā bahū atthi          	ye dibbā ye ca mānusā .

Sabbaṃ dussaṃ samāhantvā        acchādemi ticīvaraṃ    
khajjabhojjaṃ sāyaniyaṃ           	sampannaṃ pānabhojanaṃ .

Maṇimaye subhe patte          		sampūretvā adāsahaṃ    
dibbavatthā samāhutvā         	maṭṭhā cīvarasaṃyutā .

Madhurā sakkharā ceva          		telā ca madhuphāṇitā    
tappitā paramannena           	sabbe ariyamaṇḍalā .

Ratanagabbhaṃ pavīsitvā           	kesarīva guhāsaye    
mahārahamhi sayane            	sīhaseyyamakappayuṃ .

Sampajānā samuṭṭhāya          	seyye pallaṅkamābhajuṃ    
gocaraṃ sabbabuddhānaṃ           	jhānaratisamappitā .

Aññe dhammāni desenti       	aññe kīḷanti iddhiyā    
appanāyapi kīḷanti            	abhiññāvasibhāvitā .

Vikubbanā vikubbanti           	anekasatasahassiyo    
buddhāpi buddhe pucchanti        visayaṃ sabbaññumālayaṃ .

Gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ ṭhānaṃ            		paññāya vinibujjhare    
sāvakā buddhe pucchanti       	 buddhā pucchanti sāvake .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 5 -->
<a id="page-5"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 5]*

Aññamaññañca pucchanti [^5-1]       aññamaññaṃ byākaronti te    
buddhā paccekabuddhā ca        sāvakā paricārakā .

Evaṃ ratīsu ramamānā          		pāsādebhiramanti te    
chattātichattā tiṭṭhanti         	ratanāveḷusannibhā [^5-2] .

Suvaṇṇajālasaṃyuttaṃ             	rajatajālakhacitaṃ [^5-3]    
muttājālaparikkhittaṃ [^5-4]        	sabbe dhārentu matthake .

Bhavanti [^5-5] celavitānā        	soṇṇatārakacittitā    
vicittā malayavitatā           	sabbe dhārentu matthake .

Vitatā malayadāmehi           	gandhadāmehi sobhitā    
dussadāmehi parikiṇṇā         	ratanadāmabhūsitā .

Pupphābhikiṇṇā suvicittā        surabhigandhadhūpitā    
gandhapañcāṅgulaṅkatā          	hemacchadanachāditā .

Catuddisā pokkharañño         padumuppalasanthatā    
sovaṇṇarūpe khāyantu          	padumareṇurajuggatā .

Pupphantu pādapā sabbe        	pāsādassa samantato    
sayañca pupphā muñcitvā        	gantvā bhavanamokiruṃ .

Sikhino tattha naccantu          	dibbā haṃsā pakujjare    
karavikā ca gāyantu           		dijasaṅghā samantato .

Bheriyo sabbā vajjantu        	vīṇā sabbā ravantu tā [^5-6]    
sabbā saṅgīti vattantu         pāsādassa samantato .

#[^5-1] ma. Pucchitvā . [^5-2] ma. Chattā tiṭṭhantu ratanā kāñcanāveṭhapantikā . [^5-3] ma.

#ayaṃ upaḍḍhagāthā natthi . [^5-4] ma. Muttājālaparikkhittā . [^5-5] ma. Bhavantu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 6 -->
<a id="page-6"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 6]*

Yāvatā buddhakhettamhi         	cakkavāḷā [^6-1] camūpari    
mahantā jotisampannā         	acchiddā ratanāmayā .

Tiṭṭhantu soṇṇapallaṅkā        dīparukkhā jalantu te    
bhavantu ekapajjotā          	dasasahassaparamparā .

Gaṇikā lāsikā ceva          		naccantu accharāgaṇā    
nānāraṅgā padissantu         	pāsādassa samantato .

Dumagge pabbatagge vā        	sinerugirimuddhane    
ussāpemi dhajaṃ sabbaṃ          	vicittaṃ pañcavaṇṇikaṃ .

Narā nāgā ca gandhabbā       	sabbe devā upentu te    
namassantā pañjalikā          	pāsādaṃ parivārayuṃ .

Yaṅkiñci kusalaṃ kammaṃ           kattabbaṃ kiriyaṃ mama    
kāyena vācāmanasā          tidase sugataṃ kataṃ .

Ye sattā saññino atthi       ye ca sattā asaññino    
kataṃ puññaphalaṃ mayhaṃ            sabbe bhāgī bhavantu te .

Ye [^6-2] taṃ kataṃ suviditaṃ         dinnaṃ puññaphalaṃ mayā    
ye ca tattha na jānanti        devā gantvā nivedayuṃ .

Sabbe lokamhi ye sattā      jīvantāhārahetukā    
manuññaṃ bhojanaṃ sabbe [^6-3]       labhantu mama cetasā .

Manasā dānaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        manasā pasādamādayiṃ [^6-4]    
pūjitvā [^6-5] sabbasambuddhaṃ       paccekānañca pūjayiṃ .

#[^6-1] ma. Cakkavāḷe tato pare . [^6-2] ma. Yesaṃ . [^6-3] ma. Sabbaṃ . [^6-4] ma. Pasādamāvahiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 7 -->
<a id="page-7"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 7]*

Tena kammena sukatena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahetvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Duve bhave pajānāmi          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
aññaṃ gatiṃ na jānāmi          manasā patthanāphalaṃ .

Devānaṃ adhiko homi          bhavāmi manujādhipo    
rūpalakkhaṇasampanno            paññāya asamo bhave .

Bhojanaṃ vividhaṃ seṭṭhaṃ           ratanañca anappakaṃ    
nānāvidhāni vatthāni          nabhasā khippaṃ upenti maṃ .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha [^7-1] hatthaṃ pasāremi       dibbā bhakkhā upenti maṃ .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          ratanā sabbe upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          sabbe gandhā upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          sabbe yānā upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          sabbe mālā upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          alaṅkārā upenti me .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 8 -->
<a id="page-8"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 8]*

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          sabbā kaññā upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          madhusakkharā upenti me .

Paṭhabyā pabbate ceva         ākāse udake vane    
yattha hatthaṃ pasāremi          sabbe khajjā upenti me .

Adhane addhike jane          yācake ca pathāvino    
dadāmi taṃ dānavaraṃ            sambodhivarapattiyā .

Nādento pabbataṃ selaṃ        gajjento bahalaṃ giriṃ    
sadevakaṃ hāsayanto           buddho loke bhavāmahaṃ .

Disā dasavidhā loke          yāyato natthi antakaṃ    
tasmiñca disābhāgamhi          buddhakhettā asaṅkhiyā .

Pabhā pakittitā mayhaṃ          yamakā raṃsivāhanā    
etthantare raṃsijālaṃ          āloko vipulo bhave .

Ettake lokadhātumhi         sabbe passantu maṃ janā    
sabbeva [^8-1] sumanā hontu      sabbe maṃ anuvattare .

Visiṭṭhamadhuranādena            amataṃ bherimāhare [^8-2]    
etthantare janā sabbe       suṇantu madhuraṃ giraṃ .

Dhammameghena vassante         sabbe hontu anāsavā    
ye tattha pacchimakā sattā      sotāpannā bhavantu te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 9 -->
<a id="page-9"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 9]*

Datvā dātabbakaṃ dānaṃ         sīlaṃ pūre [^9-1] asesato    
nekkhammapāramiṃ gantvā        patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Paṇḍite paripucchitvā          katvā viriyamuttamaṃ    
khantiyā pāramiṃ gantvā        patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Katvā daḷhamadhiṭṭhānaṃ          saccapārami pūraye    
mettāya pāramiṃ gantvā       patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Lābhālābhe sukhadukkhe         sammānane vimānane    
sabbattha samako hutvā         patto sambodhimuttamaṃ .

Kosajjaṃ bhayato disvā         viriyañcāpi khemato    
āraddhaviriyā hotha           esā buddhānusāsanī .

Vivādaṃ bhayato disvā          avivādañca khemato    
samaggā sakhilā hotha          esā buddhānusāsanī .

Pamādaṃ bhayato disvā          appamādañca khemato    
bhāvethaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          esā buddhānusāsanī .

Samāgatā bahū buddhā          arahanto ca sabbaso    
sambuddhe arahante ca         vandamānā namassatha .

Evaṃ acintiyā buddhā         buddhadhammā acintiyā    
acintiyesu pasannānaṃ          vipāko hotyacintiyoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ bhagavā attano buddhacaritaṃ sambodhayamāno [^9-2]    
Buddhāpadāniyaṃ nāma dhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsitthāti .

Buddhāpadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 10 -->
<a id="page-10"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 10]*

Dutiyaṃ paccekabuddhāpadānaṃ    
[2] atha paccekabuddhāpadānaṃ suṇātha .

Tathāgataṃ jetavane vasantaṃ    
apucchi vedehamunī nataṅgo    
paccekabuddhā kira nāma honti    
bhavanti te hetuhi kehi dhīrā [^10-1] .

Tadāha sabbaññuvaro mahesī    
ānandabhaddaṃ madhurassarena    
ye sabbabuddhesu [^10-2] katādhikārā    
aladdhamokkhā jinasāsanesu .

Teneva saṃvegamukhena dhīrā    
vināpi buddhehi sutikkhapaññā    
ārammaṇenāpi parittakena    
paccekabodhiṃ anupāpuṇanti .

Sabbamhi lokamhi mamaṃ ṭhapetvā    
paccekabuddhāna [^10-3] samova natthi    
tesaṃ imaṃ vaṇṇapadesamattaṃ    
vakkhāmahaṃ sādhu mahāmunīnaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 11 -->
<a id="page-11"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 11]*

Sayameva buddhāna mahāisīnaṃ    
sādhūni vākyāni madhadhava khuddaṃ    
anuttaraṃ bhesajjaṃ patthayantā    
suṇātha sabbe supasannacittā .

Paccekabuddhāna samāgatānaṃ    
paramparaṃ byākaraṇāni yāni    
ādīnavo yañca virāgavatthuṃ    
yathā ca bodhiṃ anupāpuṇiṃsu .

Sarāgavatthūsu virāgasaññī    
rattamhi lokamhi virattacittā    
hitvā papañcaṃ [^11-1] jitaphanditāni    
tattheva bodhiṃ anupāpuṇiṃsu .

Sabbesu bhūtesu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ    
aviheṭhayaṃ aññatarampi tesaṃ    
mettena cittena hitānukampī    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Sabbesu bhūtesu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ    
aviheṭhayaṃ aññatarampi tesaṃ    
na puttamiccheyya kuto sahāyaṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 12 -->
<a id="page-12"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 12]*

saṃsaggajātassa bhavanti snehā    
snehanvayaṃ dukkhamidaṃ pahoti    
ādīnavaṃ snehajaṃ pekkhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Mitte suhajje anukampamāno    
hāpeti atthaṃ paṭibandhacitto    
etaṃ bhayaṃ santhave pekkhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Vaṃso visālova yathā visatto    
puttesu dāresu ca yā apekkhā    
vaṃsakkaḷīrova asajjamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Migo araññamhi yathā abandho    
yenicchakaṃ gacchati gocarāya    
viññū naro seritaṃ pekkhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Āmantanā hoti sahāyamajjhe    
vāse ṭhāne gamane cārikāya    
anabhijjhitaṃ seritaṃ pekkhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 13 -->
<a id="page-13"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 13]*

khiḍḍā ratī hoti sahāyamajjhe    
puttesu pemaṃ vipulañca hoti    
piyavippayogaṃ vijigucchamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Cātuddiso appaṭigho ca hoti    
santussamāno itarītarena    
parissayānaṃ sahitā achambhī    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Dussaṅgahā pabbajitāpi eke    
atho gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā    
appossukko paraputtesu hutvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Oropayitvā gihibyañjanāni    
sañchinnapatto yathā koviḷāro    
chetvāna vīro gihibandhanāni    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Sace labhetha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ    
saddhiñcaraṃ sādhuvihāridhīraṃ    
abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni    
careyya tenattamano satimā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 14 -->
<a id="page-14"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 14]*

no ce labhetha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ    
saddhiñcaraṃ sādhuvihāridhīraṃ    
rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya    
eko care mātaṅgaraññeva nāgo .

Addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadaṃ    
seṭṭhā samā sevitabbā sahāyā    
ete aladdhā anavajjabhojī    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni    
kammāraputtena suniṭṭhitāni    
saṅghaṭṭamānāni duve bhujasmiṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Evaṃ dutiyena sahā mamassa    
vācābhilāpo abhisajjanā vā    
etaṃ bhayaṃ āyati pekkhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Kāmā hi citrā madhurā manoramā    
virūparūpena mathenti cittaṃ    
ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇesu disvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 15 -->
<a id="page-15"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 15]*

Ītī ca gaṇḍo ca upaddavo ca    
rogo ca sallañca bhayañca metaṃ    
etaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇesu disvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Sītañca uṇhañca khudaṃ pipāsaṃ    
vātātape ḍaṃsasiriṃsape ca    
sabbānipetāni abhibhavitvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Nāgova yūthāni vivajjayitvā    
sañjātakhandho padumī uḷāro    
yathābhirantaṃ viharaṃ araññe    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Aṭṭhānataṃ saṅgaṇikāratassa    
yaṃ phussaye [^15-1] sāmayikaṃ vimuttiṃ    
ādiccabandhussa vaco nisamma    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Diṭṭhīvisūkāni upātivatto    
patto niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggo    
uppannañāṇomhi anaññaneyyo    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 16 -->
<a id="page-16"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 16]*

nillolupo nikkuho nippipāso    
nimmakkho niddhantakasāvamoho    
nirāsayo sabbaloke bhavitvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ parivajjayetha    
anatthadassiṃ visame niviṭṭhaṃ    
sayaṃ na seve pasutaṃ pamattaṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ bhajetha    
mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhāṇavantaṃ    
aññāya atthāni vineyya kaṅkhaṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ kāmasukhañca loke    
analaṅkaritvā anapekkhamāno    
vibhūsanaṭṭhānā virato saccavādī    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Puttañca dāraṃ pitarañca mātaraṃ    
dhanāni dhaññāni ca bandhavāni    
hitvāna kāmāni yathodhikāni    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 17 -->
<a id="page-17"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 17]*

saṅgo eso parittamettha sokhyaṃ    
appassādo dukkhamevettha bhiyyo    
kaṇḍo eso iti ñatvā matimā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Sandālayitvāna saṃyojanāni    
jālaṃva bhitvā salilambucārī    
aggīva daḍḍhaṃ anivattamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Okkhittacakkhū na ca pādalolo    
guttindriyo rakkhitamānasāno    
anavassuto appariḍayhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Ohārayitvā gihibyañjanāni    
sañchinnapatto yathā pārichatto    
kāsāyavattho abhinikkhamitvā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Rasesu gedhaṃ akaraṃ alolo    
anaññaposī sapadānacārī    
kule kule appaṭibaddhacitto    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 18 -->
<a id="page-18"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 18]*

pahāya pañcāvaraṇāni cetaso    
upakkilese byapanujja sabbe    
anissito chejja sinehadosaṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhañca dukkhaṃ    
pubbeva somanassaṃ domanassaṃ    
laddhānupekkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhaṃ    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Āraddhaviriyo paramatthapattiyā    
alīnacitto akusītavutti    
daḷhanikkamo thāmabalūpapanno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Paṭisallānaṃ jhānamariñcamāno    
dhammesu niccaṃ anudhammacārī    
ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavesu    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Taṇhakkhayaṃ patthayamappamatto    
anelamūgo sutavā satimā    
saṅkhātadhammo niyato padhānavā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 19 -->
<a id="page-19"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 19]*

sīhova saddesu asantasanto    
vātova jālamhi asajjamāno    
padumaṃva toyena alimpamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Sīho yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha    
rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyyacārī    
sevetha pantāni senāsanāni    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Mettaṃ upekkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ    
āsevamāno muditañca kāle    
sabbena lokena avirujjhamāno    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Rāgañca dosañca pahāya mohaṃ    
sandālayitvāna saṃyojanāni    
asantasaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .

Bhajanti sevanti ca kāraṇatthā    
nikkāraṇā dullabhā ajja mittā    
attatthapaññā asucī manussā    
eko care khaggavisāṇakappo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 20 -->
<a id="page-20"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 20]*

Visuddhasīlā suvisuddhapaññā    
samāhitā jāgariyānuyuttā    
vipassakā dhammavisesadassī    
maggaṅgabojjhaṅgagate vijaññā .

Suññappaṇidhiñca tathānimittaṃ    
āsevayitvā jinasāsanamhi    
ye sāvakattaṃ na vajanti dhīrā    
bhavanti paccekajinā sayambhū .

Mahantadhammā bahudhammakāyā    
cittissarā sabbadukkhoghatiṇṇā    
udaggacittā paramatthadassī    
sīhopamā khaggavisāṇakappā .

Santindriyā santamanā samādhī    
paccantasattesu matippacārā    
dīpā parattha idha vijjalantā    
paccekabuddhā sattahitāme [^20-1] .

Pahīnasabbāvaraṇā janindā    
lokappadīpā ghanakañcanābhā    
nissaṃsayaṃ lokasudakkhiṇeyyā    
paccekabuddhā satatappitāme .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 21 -->
<a id="page-21"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 21]*

Paccekabuddhāna subhāsitāni    
caranti lokamhi sadevakamhi    
sutvā tathā ye na karonti bālā    
vajjanti [^21-1] dukkhesu punappunante .

Paccekabuddhāna subhāsitāni    
madhadha yathā khuddamivassavantaṃ    
sutvā tathā ye paṭipattiyuttā    
bhavanti te saccadasā sapaññā .

Paccekabuddhehi jinehi bhāsitā    
gāthā [^21-2] uḷārā abhinikkhamitvā    
tā sakyasīhena naruttamena    
pakāsitā dhammavijānanatthaṃ .

Lokānukampāya imāni tesaṃ    
paccekabuddhāna vikubbitāni    
saṃvegasaṅgamativaḍḍhanatthaṃ    
sayambhusīhena pakāsitānīti .

Paccekabuddhāpadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 22 -->
<a id="page-22"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 22]*

Tatiyaṃ sārīputtattherāpadānaṃ (1)    
atha therāpadānaṃ suṇātha    
[3] himavantassa avidūre       lambako nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ        paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Uttānakūlā nadikā         supatitthā manoramā    
susuddhapuḷinākiṇṇā          avidūre mamassamaṃ .

Asakkharā apabbhārā        sādu appaṭigandhikā    
sandanti nadikā tattha        sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Kumbhīlā makarā cettha       sasasumārā ca kacchapā    
sandanti [^22-1] nadiyā tattha     sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      valajā muñjarohitā    
vagguḷā papatāyanti         sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Ubhokūlesu nadiyā          pupphino phalino dumā    
ubhato atilambanti          sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Ambā sālā ca tilakā      pāṭalī sinduvāritā    
dibbā gandhā sampavanti      pupphitā mama assame .

Campakā salaḷā nīpā        nāgapunnāgaketakā    
dibbā gandhā sampavanti      pupphitā mama assame .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 23 -->
<a id="page-23"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 23]*

Atimuttā asokā ca        bhaginīmālā ca pupphitā    
aṅkolā bimbijālā ca      pupphitā mama assame .

Ketakā kadalī ceva         kebukā tiṇasūlikā    
dibbā [^23-1] gandhā sampavanti   sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Kaṇikā kaṇṇikārā ca        asanā añjanā bahū    
dibbā [^23-1] gandhā sampavanti   sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Puṇṇāvā [^23-2] giripuṇṇāvā [^23-3] koviḷārā ca pupphitā    
dibbā [^23-1] gandhā sampavanti   sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Uddālakā ca kuṭajā        kadambā bakulā bahū    
dibbā [^23-1] gandhā sampavanti   sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Āḷakā isimuggā ca        kadalī mātuluṅgiyo    
gandhodakena saṃvaḍḍhā        phalāni dhārayanti te .

Aññe pupphanti padumā       aññe jāyanti kesarī    
aññe opupphā padumā      pupphitā taḷake tadā .

Gabbhaṃ gaṇhanti padumā        niddhāvanti muḷāliyo    
siṅghāṭapattamākiṇṇā        sobhayanti taḷake tadā .

Nayitā ambagaṇḍī ca         uttarā [^23-4] hi bandhujīvakā    
dibbā gandhā sampavanti      pupphitā taḷake tadā .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      valajā muñjarohitā    
saṅkulā maggurā ceva       vasanti taḷake tadā .

#[^23-1] ma. Dibbagandhaṃ sambhavantā sobhayanti mamassamaṃ . 2-[^23-3] ma. Puṇṇāgā . Girapuṇṇāgā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 24 -->
<a id="page-24"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 24]*

Kumbhīlā sasasumārā ca        tantiggāhā ca rakkhasā    
ogāhā [^24-1] ajagarā ca     vasanti taḷake tadā .

Pārevatā ravihaṃsā         cakkavākā nadīcarā    
kokilā suvasālikā         upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Kukkutthakā kuḷīrakā         vane pokkharasātakā    
dindibhā suvapotā ca        upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Haṃsā koñcā mayurā ca      kokilā lambacūḷakā [^24-2]    
campakā jīvajīvā ca         upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Kosikā poṭṭhasīsā ca       kurarā senakā bahū    
mahākāḷā ca sakuṇā        upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Pasadā [^24-3] migā varāhā ca   vakā bheraṇḍakā bahū    
rohiccā suttapotā [^24-4] ca   upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Sīhā byagghā ca dīpi ca      acchakokataracchabhi [^24-5]    
tidhappabhinnā mātaṅgā       upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Kinnarā vānarā ceva       athopi vanakammikā    
cetā ca luddakā ceva      upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Tindukāni piyālāni         madhukā kāsamāriyo    
dhuvaṃ phalāni dhārenti        avidūre mamassamaṃ .

Kosumbhā [^24-6] salaḷā nīpā [^24-7] sāraphalasamāyutā    
dhuvaṃ phalāni dhārenti        avidūre mamassamaṃ .

#[^24-1] ma. Oguhā . [^24-2] ma. Tammacūḷakā .  [^24-3] ma. Pasadā ca varāhā ca camarā kaṇḍakā    
#bahū . [^24-4] ma. Sukapotā . [^24-5] ma. Acchakokataracchikā . [^24-6] ma. Kosambhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 25 -->
<a id="page-25"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 25]*

Harītakā āmalakā          ambā jambū vibhedakā    
kolā bhallātakā billā     phalāni dhārayanti te .

Āluvā ca kalambā ca       biḷālitakkaḷāni ca    
jīvakā sambakā ceva        bahukā mama assame .

Assamassāvidūramhi          taḷākāsi [^25-1] sunimmitā    
acchodakā sītajalā         supatitthā manoramā .

Padumuppalasañchannā          puṇḍarīkasamāyutā    
mandālakehi sañchannā       dibbo gandho pavāyati .

Evaṃ sabbaṅgasampanne       pupphite phalite vane    
sukate assame ramme       viharāmi ahaṃ tadā .

Sīlavā vattasampanno        jhāyī jhānarato sadā    
pañcābhiññābalappatto       suruci nāma tāpaso .

Catubbīsasahassāni           sissā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahuṃ    
sabbeva brāhmaṇā ete    jātimanto yasassino .

Lakkhaṇe itihāse ca        sanighaṇḍusakeṭubhe    
padakā veyyākaraṇā        saddhamme pāramiṃ gatā .

Uppātesu nimittesu        lakkhaṇesu ca kovidā    
paṭhabyā bhummantalikkhe       mama sissā susikkhitā .

Appicchā nipakā ete      appāhārā alolupā    
lābhālābhena santuṭṭhā      parivārenti maṃ sadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 26 -->
<a id="page-26"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 26]*

Jhāyī jhānaratā dhīrā        santacittā samāhitā    
ākiñcaññaṃ patthayantā       parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Abhiññāpāramippattā        pettike gocare ratā    
antalikkhacarā dhīrā         parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Saṃvutā chasu dvāresu        aneñjā rakkhitindriyā    
asaṃsaṭṭhā ca te dhīrā       mama sissā durāsadā .

Pallaṅkena nisajjāya        ṭhānā caṅkamanena ca    
vītināmenti te rattiṃ       mama sissā durāsadā .

Rajjanīye na rajjanti        dosanīye na dussare    
mohanīye na muyhanti        mama sissā durāsadā .

Iddhiṃ vimaṃsamānā te        vattanti niccakālikaṃ    
paṭhaviṃ te pakampenti        sārambhena durāsadā .

Kīḷamānāva te sissā       kīḷanti jhānakīḷitaṃ    
jambuto phalamānenti        mama sissā durāsadā .

Aññe gacchanti goyānaṃ      aññe pubbavidehanaṃ    
aññe ca uttarakuruṃ         mama sissā durāsadā .

Purato pesenti te khāriṃ    pacchato ca vajanti te    
catubbīsasahassehi           chāditaṃ hoti ambaraṃ .

Aggipākaṃ [^26-1] anaggiṃ ca      dantodukkhalikāpica    
ambanā koṭikā keci       pavattaphalabhojanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 27 -->
<a id="page-27"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 27]*

Udakorohakā keci         sāyaṃ pāto suciratā    
toyābhisekacaraṇā [^27-1]       mama sissā durāsadā .

Parūḷhakacchanakhalomā         paṅkadantā rajassirā    
gandhitā sīlagandhena         mama sissā durāsadā .

Pātova sannipātetvā      jaṭilā uggatāpanā    
lābhālābhaṃ pakittetvā      gacchanti ambare tadā .

Etesaṃ pakkamantānaṃ        mahāsaddo pavattati    
ajinacammasaddena           moditā honti devatā .

Disodisā pakkamanti         antalikkhacarā isī    
sakabalenupatthaddhā          te gacchanti yathicchakaṃ .

Paṭhavīkampakā ete         sabbeva nabhacārino    
uggatejā duppasahā        sāgarova akhobhiyā .

Ṭhānacaṅkamiyā keci         keci nesajjikā isī    
pavattabhojanā keci         mama sissā durāsadā .

Mettāvihārino ete      hitesī sabbapāṇinaṃ    
anattukkaṃsakā sabbe        na te vambhenti kassaci .

Sīharājāva sambhītā         gajarājāva thāmavā    
durāsadā byagghāriva        āgacchanti mamantike .

Vijjādharā devatā ca       nāgagandhabbarakkhasā    
kumbhaṇḍā dānavā garuḷā     upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 28 -->
<a id="page-28"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 28]*

te jaṭākhāribharitā         ajinuttaravāsino    
antalikkhacarā sabbe        upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ .

Tadānucchavikā ete        aññamaññaṃ sagāravā    
catubbīsasahassānaṃ           khittasaddo na vijjati .

Pāde pādaṃ nikkhipantā      appasaddā susaṃvutā    
upasaṅkamma sabbe te       sirasā vandare mamaṃ .

Tehi sissehi parivuto       santehi ca tapassibhi    
vasāmi assame tattha        jhāyī jhānarato ahaṃ .

Isīnaṃ sīlagandhena           pupphagandhena cūbhayaṃ    
phalīnaṃ phalagandhena           gandhito hoti assamo .

Rattindivaṃ na jānāmi        arati me na vijjati    
sake sisse ovadanto      bhiyyo hāsaṃ labhāmahaṃ .

Pupphānaṃ pupphamānānaṃ        phalānañca vipaccataṃ    
dibbā gandhā pavāyanti      sobhayantā mamassamaṃ .

Samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā       ātāpī nipako ahaṃ    
khāribhāraṃ gahetvāna        vanaṃ ajjhogahiṃ ahaṃ .

Uppāte supine cāpi       lakkhaṇesu susikkhito    
pavattamānaṃ mantapadaṃ         dhārayāmi ahaṃ tadā .

Anomadassī bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
vivekakāmo sambuddho       himavantaṃ upāgami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 29 -->
<a id="page-29"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 29]*

Ajjhogahetvā himavantaṃ      aggo kāruṇiko muni    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna        nisīdi purisuttamo .

Tamaddasāhaṃ sambuddhaṃ         sappabhāsaṃ manoramaṃ    
indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ            ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva           vijjajava [^29-1] gagaṇe yathā    
suphullaṃ sālarājaṃva          addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ayaṃ nāgo mahāvīro        dukkhassantakaro muni    
imaṃ dassanamāgamma          sabbadukkhā pamuccare .

Disvānāhaṃ devadevaṃ        lakkhaṇaṃ upadhārayiṃ    
buddho nu kho na vā buddho   handa passāmi cakkhumaṃ .

Sahassārāni cakkāni        dissanti caraṇuttame    
lakkhaṇānissa disvāna        niṭṭhaṃ gañchiṃ tathāgate .

Sammajjaniṃ gahetvā         sammajjitvānahaṃ tadā    
aṭṭha pupphe samānetvā     buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Pūjayitvāna taṃ buddhaṃ         oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         namassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Yena ñāṇena sambuddho      viharati anāsavo    
taṃ ñāṇaṃ kittayissāmi        suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Samuddharayimaṃ lokaṃ           sayambhu amitodaya    
tava dassanamāgamma          kaṅkhāsotaṃ taranti te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 30 -->
<a id="page-30"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 30]*

Tuvaṃ satthā ca ketu ca       dhajo yūpo ca pāṇinaṃ    
parāyano patiṭṭhā ca        dīpo ca dipaduttamo .

Sakkā samudde udakaṃ        pametuṃ āḷhakena vā    
na tveva tava sabbaññu       ñāṇaṃ sakkā pametave .

Dhāretuṃ paṭhaviṃ sakkā        ṭhapetvā tulamaṇḍale    
na tveva tava sabbaññu       ñāṇaṃ sakkā pametave .

Ākāsaṃ minituṃ sakkā        rajjunā [^30-1] aṅgulena vā    
na tveva tava sabbaññu       ñāṇaṃ sakkā pametave .

Mahāsamudde udakaṃ          paṭhaviñcākhilañjahe    
buddhañāṇaṃ upādāya         upamāto na yujjare .

Sadevakassa lokassa         cittaṃ yesaṃ pavattati    
antojāligatā ete       tava ñāṇamhi cakkhuma .

Yena ñāṇena pattosi       kevalaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
tena ñāṇena sabbaññu       maddasi paratitthiye .

Imā gāthā paṭhetvāna      suruci nāma tāpaso    
ajinaṃ pattharitvāna          paṭhaviyaṃ nisīdi so .

Cullāsītisahassāni          ajjhogāḷho mahaṇṇave    
accuggato tāvadeva        girirājā pavuccati .

Tāva accuggato neru       āyato vitthato ca so    
cuṇṇito saṅkhabhedena [^30-2]     koṭisatasahassiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 31 -->
<a id="page-31"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 31]*

Lakkhe ṭhapiyamānamhi         parikkhayamagacchatha    
na tveva tava sabbaññu       ñāṇaṃ sakkā pametave .

Sukhumacchikena jālena        udakaṃ yo parikkhipe    
ye keci udake pāṇā      antojāligatā siyuṃ .

Tatheva hi mahāvīra          ye keci puthutitthiyā    
diṭṭhiggahaṇapakkhantā         parāmāsena mohitā .

Tava suddhena ñāṇena        anāvaraṇadassinā    
antojāligatā ete       ñāṇante nātivattare .

Bhagavā ca tamhi samaye       anomadassī mahāyaso    
vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā       disaṃ olokayī jino .

Anomadassimunino           nisabho nāma sāvako    
parivuto satasahassehi        santacittehi tādibhi .

Khīṇāsavehi suddhehi         chaḷabhiññehi tādihi [^31-1]    
cittamaññāya buddhassa        upesi lokanāyakaṃ .

Antalikkhe ṭhitā tattha       padakkhiṇamakaṃsu te    
namassantā pañjalikā        oruhuṃ [^31-2] buddhasantike .

Amodassī bhagavā           lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        sitaṃ pātuṃ karī jino .

Varuṇo nāmupaṭṭhāko        anomadassissa satthuno    
ekaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā         āpucchi lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 32 -->
<a id="page-32"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 32]*

Ko nu kho bhagavā hetu      sitakammassa satthuno    
na hi buddhā ahetūhi        sitaṃ pātuṃ karonti te .

Anomadassī bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhumajjhe nisīditvā        imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Yo maṃ pupphena pūjesi       ñāṇañcāpi anutthavi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Buddhassa giramaññāya         sabbe devā samānusā [^32-1]    
saddhammaṃ sotukāmā te      sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamama .

Dasasu lokadhātūsu           devakāyā mahiddhikā    
saddhammaṃ sotukāmā te      sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamama .

Hatthī assā rathā pattī      senā ca caturaṅginī    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ       buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         bheriyo samalaṅkatā    
upaṭṭhissantimaṃ niccaṃ         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soḷasitthīsahassāni          nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .

Āḷāramukhā [^32-2] hasulā      susaññā tanumajjhimā    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ       buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kappasatasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ cakkavatti       rājā raṭṭhe bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 33 -->
<a id="page-33"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 33]*

sahassakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte        manussattaṃ gamissati    
brāhmaṇisāriyā nāma       dhārayissati kucchinā .

Mātuyā nāmagottena       paññāyissatiyaṃ naro    
sārīputtoti nāmena        tikkhapañño bhavissati .

Asītikoṭī chaḍḍetvā        pabbajissatikiñcano    
gavesanto santipadaṃ         carissati mahiṃ imaṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sārīputtoti nāmena        hessati aggasāvako .

Ayaṃ bhāgīrasī gaṅgā         himavantā pabhāvitā    
mahāsamuddamappeti          tappayantī mahodadhiṃ .

Tathevāyaṃ sārīputto        sakko tīsu visārado    
paññāya pāramiṃ gantvā      tappayissati pāṇino .

Himavantaṃ upādāya          sāgarañca mahodadhiṃ    
etthantare yaṃ puḷinaṃ        gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Tampi sakkā asesena       saṅkhyātuṃ gaṇanā yathā    
na tveva sārīputtassa       paññāyanto bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 34 -->
<a id="page-34"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 34]*

Lakkhe ṭhapiyamānamhi         khīye gaṅgāya vālukā    
na tveva sārīputtassa       paññāyanto bhavissati .

Mahāsamudde ūmiyo         gaṇanāto asaṅkhayā    
tatheva sārīputtassa         paññāyanto na hessati .

Ārādhayitvā sambuddhaṃ       gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
paññāya pāramiṃ gantvā      hessati aggasāvako .V    
pavattitaṃ dhammacakkaṃ          sakyaputtena tādinā    
anvattissati sammā         vassanto dhammavuṭṭhiyo .

Sabbametaṃ abhiññāya         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapessati .

Aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        anomadassissa satthuno    
yassādhikāraṃ [^34-1] katvāna     sabbattha pāramiṃ gato .

Aparimeyye kataṃ kammaṃ       phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto saravegova         kilese jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ .

Asaṅkhataṃ gavesanto         nibbānaṃ acalaṃ padaṃ    
vicinaṃ titthiye sabbe        esāhaṃ saṃsariṃ bhave .

Yathāpi byādhiko poso      pariyeseyya osathaṃ    
vicineyya dhanaṃ sabbaṃ         byādhito parimuttiyā .

Asaṅkhataṃ gavesanto         nibbānaṃ amataṃ padaṃ    
abbokiṇṇaṃ pañcasataṃ         pabbajiṃ isipabbajaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 35 -->
<a id="page-35"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 35]*

Jaṭāya bhārabharito          ajinuttaranivāsahaṃ [^35-1]    
abhiññāpāramiṃ gantvā       brahmalokaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Natthi bāhirake suddhi        ṭhapetvā jinasāsanaṃ    
ye keci buddhimā sattā     sujjhanti jinasāsane .

Atthakāmaṃ [^35-2] mamametaṃ       na hi nisiṃ ahaṃ iti    
asaṅkhataṃ gavesanto         kutitthaṃ sañcariṃ ahaṃ .

Yathā sāratthiko poso      kadaliṃ chetvāna phālaye    
na tattha sāraṃ vindeyya      sārena rittako hi so .

Tatheva titthiyā loke       nānādiṭṭhī bahū janā    
asaṅkhatena rittā te       sārena kadalī yathā .

Pacchime bhavasampatte        brahmabandhu ahosahaṃ    
mahābhogaṃ chaḍḍayitvāna       pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Paṭhamabhāṇavāraṃ .

Ajjhāyako mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
brāhmaṇo sañjayo nāma     tassa mūle vasāmahaṃ .

Sāvako te mahāvīra        assaji nāma brāhmaṇo    
durāsado uggatejo        piṇḍāya caratī tadā .

Tamaddasāsiṃ sappaññaṃ         muniṃ mone samāhitaṃ    
santacittaṃ mahānāgaṃ         suphullaṃ padumaṃ yathā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 36 -->
<a id="page-36"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 36]*

Disvā me cittamuppajji      sudantaṃ suddhamānasaṃ    
usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ            arahāyaṃ bhavissati .

Pāsādiko iriyati          abhirūpo susaṃvuto    
uttame damathe danto       amatadassī bhavissati .

Yannūnāhaṃ uttamatthaṃ         puccheyyaṃ tuṭṭhamānasaṃ    
so ce [^36-1] puṭṭho kathissati   paṭipucchāmahantadā .

Piṇḍacāraṃ [^36-2] carantassa      pacchato agamāsahaṃ    
okāsaṃ paṭimānento       pucchituṃ amataṃ padaṃ .

Vīthantare anuppattaṃ         upagantvāna pucchahaṃ    
kathaṃ gottosi tvaṃ vīra [^36-3]    kassa sissosi mārisa .

So me puṭṭho viyākāsi     asambhītova kesarī    
buddho loke samuppanno     tassa sissomhi āvuso [^36-4] .

Kidisante mahāvīra          anujāta mahāyasa [^36-5]    
buddhassa sāsanaṃ dhammaṃ        sādhu me kathayassu bho .

So me puṭṭho kathī sabbaṃ     gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ padaṃ    
taṇhāsallassa hantāraṃ       sabbadukkhāpanūdanaṃ .

Ye dhammā hetupabhavā       tesaṃ hetuṃ tathāgato āha    
tesañca yo nirodho        evaṃvādī mahāsamaṇo .

Sohaṃ vissajjite pañhe      paṭhamaṃ phalamajjhagaṃ    
virajo vimalo āsiṃ         sutvāna jinasāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 37 -->
<a id="page-37"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 37]*

Sutvāna munino vākyaṃ       passitvā dhammamuttamaṃ    
pariyogāḷhasaddhammo        imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsahaṃ .

Eseva dhammo yadi tāvadeva  paccabyathā padamasokaṃ    
adiṭṭhaṃ abbhatītaṃ            bahukehi kappanahutehi .

Yohaṃ dhammaṃ gavesanto       kutitthe sañcariṃ ahaṃ    
so me attho anuppatto    kālo me nappamajjituṃ .

Tositohaṃ assajinā         patvāna acalaṃ padaṃ .

Sahāyakaṃ gavesanto         assamaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
dūratova mamaṃ disvā         sahāyo me susikkhito    
iriyāpathasampanno          idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Pasannamukhanettosi          munibhāvova dissati    
amatādhigato kacci          nibbānaṃ accutaṃ padaṃ .

Subhānurūpo āyāsi         āneñjakārito viya    
dantova [^37-1] dantadamathe [^37-2]   upasantosi brāhmaṇa .

Amataṃ mayā adhigataṃ          sokasallavinodanaṃ    
tuvaṃpi taṃ adhigacchehi [^37-3]      gacchāma buddhasantikaṃ .

Sādhūti so paṭissutvā       sahāyo me susikkhito    
hatthena hatthaṃ gaṇhitvā      upāgamma tavantikaṃ [^37-4] .

Ubhopi pabbajissāma         sakyaputta tavantike .

Tava sāsanamāgamma          viharāma anāsavā .

#[^37-1] yu. Ca . [^37-2] ma. Dantadamatho . Yu. Uttadamathe . [^37-3] ma. Adhigacchesi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 38 -->
<a id="page-38"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 38]*

Kolito iddhiyā seṭṭho     ahaṃ paññāya pārago    
ubhova ekato hutvā       sāsanaṃ sobhayāma se .

Apariyositasaṅkappo         kutitthe sañcariṃ ahaṃ    
tava dassanamāgamma          saṅkappo pūrito mama .

Paṭhaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya           pupphanti samaye dumā    
dibbā gandhā sampavanti      tosenti sabbapāṇinaṃ .

Tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          sakyaputta mahāyasa่    
sāsane vo [^38-1] patiṭṭhāya    samayesāmi pupphituṃ .

Vimuttipupphamesanto         bhavasaṃsāramocanaṃ    
vimuttipupphalābhena          tosemi sabbapāṇinaṃ .

Yāvatā buddhakhettamhi       ṭhapetvāna mahāmuniṃ    
paññāya sadiso natthi        tava puttassa cakkhuma .

Suvinītā ca te sissā       parisā ca susikkhitā    
uttame damathe dantā       parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Jhāyī jhānaratā dhīrā        santacittā samāhitā    
munī moneyyasampannā       parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Appicchā nipakā dhīrā       appāhārā alolupā    
lābhālābhena santuṭṭhā      parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Āraññikā dhutaratā         jhāyino lūkhacīvarā    
vivekābhiratā dhīrā         parivārenti taṃ sadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 39 -->
<a id="page-39"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 39]*

Paṭipannā phalaṭṭhā ca        sekhā phalasamaṅgino    
āsiṃsakā [^39-1] uttamatthaṃ      parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Sotāpannā ca vimalā       sakadāgāmino ca ye    
anāgāmī ca arahā         parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Satipaṭṭhānakusalā           bojjhaṅgabhāvanāratā    
sāvakā vo [^39-2] bahū sabbe   parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Iddhipādesu kusalā         samādhibhāvanāratā    
sammappadhānamanuyuttā        parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Tevijjā chaḷabhiññā ca       iddhiyā pāramiṃ gatā    
paññāya pāramimpattā       parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Īdisā [^39-3] vo [^39-2] mahāvīra   tava sissā susikkhitā    
durāsadā uggatejā        parivārenti taṃ sadā .

Tehi sissehi parivuto       saññatehi tapassibhi    
migarājāvasambhīto          uḷurājāva sobhasi .

Paṭhaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya           ruhanti kharaṇīruhā    
vepullattaṃ pāpuṇanti        phalañca dassayanti te .

Paṭhavīsadiso tvaṃsi           sakyaputta mahāyasa .

Sāsane te patiṭṭhāya       labhanti amataṃ phalaṃ .

Sindhu sarassatī ceva         nadiyo candabhāgikā [^39-4]    
gaṅgā ca yamunā ceva       sarabhū ca atho mahī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 40 -->
<a id="page-40"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 40]*

Etāsaṃ sandamānānaṃ        sāgaro sampaṭicchati    
jahanti purimaṃ nāmaṃ          sāgarotveva ñāyati .

Tathevime catuvaṇṇā         pabbajitvā tavantike    
jahanti purimaṃ nāmaṃ          buddhaputtāti ñāyare .

Yathāpi cando vimalo        gacchaṃ ākāsadhātuyā    
sabbe tāragaṇe loke      ābhāya atirocati .

Tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         parivuto devamānuse    
buddhakkhettaṃ atikkamma       jalasi sabbadā tuvaṃ .

Gambhīre uṭṭhitā ūmi        na velaṃ ativattati    
sabbavelaṃ paphussanti         sañcuṇṇā vikiranti tā .

Tatheva titthiyā loke       nānādiṭṭhī bahū janā    
dhammaṃ taritukāmā te        nātivattanti taṃ muniṃ .

Sace ca [^40-1] taṃ pāpuṇanti     paṭivārehi [^40-2] cakkhuma    
tavantikaṃ upāgantvā        sañcuṇṇāva bhavanti te .

Yathāpi udake jātā        kumudā mandālakā [^40-3] bahū    
upalimpanti toyena         kaddamakalalena ca .

Tatheva bahukā sattā        loke jātā virūhare    
aḍḍitā rāgadosena        kaddame kumudaṃ yathā .

Yathā padumaṃ jalajaṃ           jalamajjhe virocati [^40-4]    
na so limpati toyena       parisuddho hi kesarī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 41 -->
<a id="page-41"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 41]*

Tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         loke jāto mahāmuni    
nopalimpasi lokena         toyena padumaṃ yathā .

Yathā hi rammake māse      bahū pupphanti vārijā    
nātikkamanti taṃ māsaṃ        samayo pupphanāya so .

Tatheva tvaṃ sakyaputta [^41-1]     pupphito te vimuttiyā    
sāsanaṃ nātivattanti         padumaṃ vārinā yathā .

Supupphito sālarājāva       dibbagandhaṃ pavāyati    
aññasālehi parivuto        sālarājātisobhati [^41-2] .

Tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         buddhañāṇena pupphito    
bhikkhusaṅghena parivuto        sālarājāva sobhasi .

Yathāpi selo himavā        osatho sabbapāṇinaṃ    
nāgānaṃ asurānañca         devānaṃ ālayopica .

Tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         osatho viya pāṇinaṃ    
tevijjā chaḷabhiññā ca       iddhiyā pāramiṃ gatā .

Anusiṭṭhā mahāvīra          tayā kāruṇikena teh    
ramanti dhammaratiyā          vasanti tava sāsane .

Migarājā yathā sīho        abhinikkhamma āsayā    
catuddisā [^41-3] viloketvā    tikkhattuṃ abhinādati [^41-4] .

Sabbe migā uttasanti       migarājassa gajjato    
tathā hi jātimā eso      pasuṃ [^41-5] tāseti sabbadā .

#[^41-1] ma. Mahāvīra . [^41-2] ma. Yu. Sālarājāva sobhati . [^41-3] ma. Catuddisānuviloketvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 42 -->
<a id="page-42"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 42]*

Gajjato te mahāvīra        basudhāyaṃ pakampati    
bodhaneyyā pabujjhanti       tasanti mārakāyikā .

Tasanti titthiyā sabbe       nadato te mahāmuni    
kākasenāva vibbhantā       migaraññā yathā migā .

Ye keci gaṇino loke      satthāroti pavuccare    
paramparābhataṃ dhammaṃ          desenti parisāya te .

Na heva tvaṃ mahāvīra        dhammaṃ desesi pāṇinaṃ    
sāmaṃ saccāni bujjhitvā      kevalaṃ bodhipakkhikaṃ .

Āsayānusayaṃ ñatvā         indriyānaṃ balābalaṃ    
bhabbābhabbe viditvāna       mahāmeghova gajjasi .

Cakkavāḷapariyantā          nisinnā parisā bhave    
nānādiṭṭhī vicintenti       vimaticchedanāya taṃ .

Sabbesaṃ cittamaññāya        opammakusalo muni    
ekaṃ pañhaṃ kathentova       vimatiṃ chindi pāṇinaṃ .

Upadisāsadiseheva [^42-1]       vasudhā pūritā bhave    
sabbeva te pañjalikā       kittayuṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Kappaṃ vā te kittayantā     nānāvaṇṇehi kittayuṃ    
parimetuṃ na kappeyyuṃ [^42-2]     appameyyo tathāgato .

Tathā [^42-3] sakena thāmena     kittito hi mahājino [^42-4]    
kappakoṭī pakittentā       evamevaṃ pakittayuṃ .

#[^42-1] ma. Upatissasadiseheva . [^42-2] ma. Sakkeyyaṃ . Yu. Pappayuyaṃ . [^42-3] yu. Yathā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 43 -->
<a id="page-43"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 43]*

Sace hi koci devo vā     manusso vā susikkhito    
pametuṃ parikappeyya [^43-1]      vighātaṃva labheyya so .

Sāsane te patiṭṭhāya       sakyaputta mahāyasa    
paññāya pāramiṃ gantvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Titthiye sampamaddāmi        vattemi jinasāsanaṃ    
dhammasenāpati ajja         sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Aparimeyye kataṃ kammaṃ       phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto [^43-2] saravegova      kilese jhāpayiṃ mamaṃ .

Yo koci manujo bhāraṃ       dhāreyya matthake sadā    
bhārena dukkhito assa       bhāro hi bharito tathā .

Ḍayhamāno tihaggīhi         bhavesu saṃsariṃ ahaṃ    
bharito bhavabhārena          neru uddharito yathā [^43-3] .

Oropito ca me bhāro     bhavā ugghāṭitā mayā    
karaṇīyaṃ kataṃ sabbaṃ           sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Yāvatā buddhakhettamhi       ṭhapetvā sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
ahaṃ aggomhi paññāya       sadiso me na vijjati .

Samādhimhi sukusalo          iddhiyā pāramiṃ gato    
icchamāno ahaṃ [^43-4] ajja     sahassaṃ abhinimmine .

Anupubbavihārassa           vasībhūto mahāmuni    
kathesi sāsanaṃ mayhaṃ         nirodho sayanaṃ mama .

#[^43-1] yu. Pūritaṃ parikaddheyya . [^43-2] ma. Sukhitto . Yu sukhito . [^43-3] ma. Yu. Giriṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 44 -->
<a id="page-44"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 44]*

Dibbacakkhuṃ visuddhaṃ me        samādhikusalo ahaṃ    
sammappadhānamanuyutto        bojjhaṅgabhāvanārato .

Sāvakena hi pattabbaṃ        sabbameva kataṃ mama [^44-1]    
lokanāthaṃ ṭhapetvāna        sadiso me na vijjati .

Samāpattimhi [^44-2] kusalo      jhānavimokkhānaṃ khippaṃ paṭilābhī    
bojjhaṅgabhāvanārato        sāvakaguṇapāramiṃ gatosmi .

Sāvakaguṇaphussena [^44-3]        buddhiyā purisuttamagāravā [^44-4]    
saddhāsaṅgahitaṃ cittaṃ [5]    sadā sabrahmacārisu .

Uddhaṭadāḍhova [^44-6] sappo     chinnavisāṇova usabho    
nikkhittamānadappova         upemi garugāravena gaṇaṃ .

Yadi rūpinī bhaveyya          paññā me vasu patīnaṃ [^44-7] sameyya    
anomadassissa bhagavato       phalametaṃ ñāṇathavanāya .

Pavattitaṃ dhammacakkaṃ          sakyaputtena tādinā    
anuvattemahaṃ sammā         ñāṇathavanāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Mā me kadāci pāpiccho     sameto hīnavīriyo    
appassuto anācāro [^44-8]    sameto katthaci ahu .

Bahussuto ca medhāvī        sīlesu susamāhito    
cetosamathānuyutto         api muddhani tiṭṭhatu .

Taṃ vo vadāmi bhaddaṃ vo [^44-9]   yāvantettha samāgatā    
appicchā hotha santuṭṭhā     dānaṃ datvā sadā ahu 10 .

#[^44-1] ma. Mayā . Yu. Mamaṃ . [^44-2] ma. Samāpattinaṃ . Yu. Samāpattivinayakusalo . [^44-3] ma. Yu.

#sāvakaguṇenāpi phussena . [^44-4] ma. Purisuttamabhāravā . [^44-5] (yaṃ) . [^44-6] ma. Yu. Uddhavisova .

#[^44-7] ma. Vasumatīpi na sameyya . [^44-8] ma. Anādaro . [^44-9] ma. Yu. Bhadante .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 45 -->
<a id="page-45"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 45]*

Yamahaṃ paṭhamaṃ disvā          virajo vimalo ahu [^45-1]    
so me ācariyo dhīro [^45-2]   assaji nāma sāvako .

Tassāhaṃ sāvako ajja [^45-3]    dhammasenāpatī ahu [^45-1]    
sabbattha pāramiṃ patvā       viharāmi anāsavo .

Yo me ācariyo āsi      assaji nāma sāvako    
yassaṃ disāyaṃ vasati          ussīsamhi karomahaṃ .

Mama kammaṃ saritvāna         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapesi maṃ .

(4) paṭisambhidā catasso    vimokkhāpica aṭṭhame    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti [^45-5] .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sārīputto thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sārīputtattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ mahāmoggallānattherāpadānaṃ (2)    
[4] anomadassī bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
vihāsi himavantamhi          devasaṅghapurakkhato .

Varuṇo nāma nāmena        nāgarājā ahaṃ tadā    
kāmarūpī vikubbāmi          mahodadhinivāsahaṃ .

#[^45-1] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ .  [^45-2] yu. Vīro .  [^45-3] ma. Yu. Tassāhaṃ āhasā ajja .

#[^45-4] ma.   Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
#       māgova bandhanaṃ hitvā       viharāmi anāsavo    
#       svāgataṃ vata me āsi       buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
#       tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 46 -->
<a id="page-46"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 46]*

Aṅganiyagaṇaṃ hitvā          turiyaṃ paṭṭhapesahaṃ    
sambuddhaṃ parivāretvā       vādesuṃ accharā tadā .

Vijjamānesu [^46-1] turiyesu     devaturiyāni vajjayuṃ    
ubhinnaṃ saddaṃ sutvāna        buddhopi sampabujjhatha .

Nimantayitvā sambuddhaṃ        sakaṃ bhavanamupāgamiṃ    
āsanaṃ paññapetvāna        kālamārocayiṃ ahaṃ .

Khīṇāsavasahassehi           purito [^46-2] lokanāyako    
obhāsento disā sabbā    bhavanamme upāgami .

Upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāvīraṃ           devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
bhikkhusaṅghaṃ santappesiṃ [^46-3]     annapānenahaṃ tadā .

Anumodi mahāvīro          sayambhū aggapuggalo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so saṅghaṃ apūjesi       buddhañca lokanāyakaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        devalokaṃ gamissati .

Sattasattatikkhattuñca         devarajjaṃ karissati    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ aṭṭhasataṃ       vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Pañcapaññāsakkhattuṃ ca        cakkavatti bhavissati    
bhogā asaṅkhayā tassa       uppajjissanti tāvade .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

#[^46-1] ma. Vajjamānesu tūresu devā tūrāni vajjayu . Yu. Ajjamānesu . [^46-2] ma. Parivuto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 47 -->
<a id="page-47"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 47]*

Nirayā so cavitvāna        manussattaṃ gamissati    
kolito nāma nāmena       brahmabandhu bhavissati .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      kusalamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhāvato          dutiyo hessati sāvako .

Āraddhaviriyo pahitatto      iddhiyā pāramiṃ gato    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Pāpamittopanissāya         kāmarāgavasaṃ gato    
mātaraṃ pitarañcāpi          ghātayiṃ duṭṭhamānaso .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         nirayaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
pāpakammasamaṅgitaṃ [^47-1]        bhinnasīso marāmahaṃ [^47-2] .

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
idhāpi īdisaṃ mayhaṃ          maraṇakāle bhavissati .

Pavivekamanuyutto           samādhibhāvanārato    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Dharaṇiṃpi sugambhīraṃ            bahalaṃ duppadhaṃsiyaṃ    
vāmaṅguṭṭhena cāleyyaṃ [^47-3]   iddhiyā pāramiṃ gato .

Asmimānaṃ na passāmi        māno mayhaṃ na vijjati    
sāmaṇere upādāya        garucittaṃ karomahaṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     yaṃ kammaṃ abhinīharaṃ    
tamahaṃ bhūmiṃ anuppatto        pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

#[^47-1] ma. Pāpakammasamaṅgitā . Yu. Pāpakammasamaṅgīnaṃ . [^47-2] yu. Bhavāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 48 -->
<a id="page-48"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 48]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāmoggallāno thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mahāmoggallānattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ mahākassapattherāpadānaṃ (3)    
[5] padumuttarassa bhagavato        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
nibbute lokanāthasmiṃ        pūjaṃ kubbanti satthuno .

Udaggacittā janatā         āmoditappamoditā    
tesu saṃvegajātesu         pīti me upapajjatha [^48-1] .

Ñātimitte samānetvā      idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
parinibbuto mahāvīro        handa pūjaṃ karoma se .

Sādhūti te paṭissutvā       bhiyyo hāsaṃ janiṃsu me    
buddhasmiṃ lokanāthasmiṃ        kāhāma puññasañcayaṃ .

Agghiyaṃ sukataṃ katvā         satahatthaṃ samuggataṃ    
diyaḍḍhaṃ hatthasataṃpi           vimānaṃ nabhamuggataṃ .

Katvāna agghiyaṃ tattha        kusalapantīhi cittitaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       cetiyaṃ pūjayuttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 49 -->
<a id="page-49"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 49]*

Aggikkhandhova jalati         sālarājāva phullito [^49-1]    
indalaṭṭhīva ākāse        obhāsati [^49-2] catuddisā .

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā      katvāna kusalaṃ bahuṃ    
pubbakammaṃ saritvāna         tidasaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Sahassayuttaṃ hayavāhiṃ         dibbayānaṃ adhiṭṭhito    
ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         sattabhummaṃ samuggataṃ .

Kūṭāgārasahassāni          sabbasoṇṇamayā ahu [^49-3]    
jalanti sakatejena          disā sabbā pabhāsayuṃ [^49-4] .

Santi aññepi niyyuhā       lohitaṅkamayā [^49-5] tadā    
tepi jotanti ābhāya       samantā caturo disā .

Puññakammābhinibbattā        kūṭāgārā sunimmitā    
maṇimayāpi jotanti          disodisaṃ [^49-6] samantato .

Tesaṃ ujjotamānānaṃ        obhāso vipulo ahu    
sabbe deve abhibhomi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhikappasahassamhi          ubbiddho nāma khattiyo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         paṭhaviṃ āvasiṃ ahaṃ    
tatheva bhaddake kappe       tiṃsakkhattuṃ ahosahaṃ .

Sakakammābhiraddhomhi         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Sattaratanasampanno          catudīpamhi issaro    
tatthāpi bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         indalaṭṭhīva uggataṃ .

#[^49-1] ma. Aggikkhandhova jalito kiṃsukoiva phullito . Yu... Jalito... . [^49-2] ma. Obhāseti .

#[^49-3] ma. Ahaṃ . Yu. Sabbe sovaṇṇamayā ahaṃ . [^49-4] ma. Yu. Pabhāsayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 50 -->
<a id="page-50"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 50]*

Āyāmato catubbīsā        vitthārena ca dvādasā    
rammakaṃ [^50-1] nāma nagaraṃ       daḷhapākāratoraṇaṃ .

Āyāmato pañcasataṃ         vitthārena tadaḍḍhakaṃ    
ākiṇṇaṃ janakāyehi         tidasānaṃ puraṃ viya .

Yathā sūcighare sūci          pakkhittā paṇṇavīsati    
aññamaññaṃ paghaṭṭenti        ākiṇṇā hoti satatā [^50-2] .

Evampi nagaraṃ mayhaṃ         hatthassarathasaṅkulaṃ [^50-3]    
manussehi tadākiṇṇaṃ         rammakaṃ [^50-1] nagaruttamaṃ .

Tattha bhutvā pivitvā ca      puna devattataṃ gato    
bhave pacchimake mayhaṃ        ahosi kulasampadā .

Brahmaññakulasambhūto         mahāratanasañcayo    
asītikoṭiyo hitvā         hiraññassa paribbajiṃ [^50-4] .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākassapo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mahākassapattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^50-1] ma. Rammaṇaṃ . [^50-2] ma. Ākiṇṇaṃ hoti laṅkataṃ . Yu. .. Hoti taṃ tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 51 -->
<a id="page-51"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 51]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ anuruddhattherāpadānaṃ (4)d    
[6] sumedhaṃ bhagavantāhaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
vūpakaṭṭhaṃ viharantaṃ           addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ ayācahaṃ .

Anukampa mahāvīra           lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
padīpante padassāmi         rukkhamūlamhi jhāyato .

Adhivāsesi so dhīro        sayambhū vadataṃ varo    
dumesu vinivijjhitvā         yantaṃ yojetvahantadā [^51-1] .

Sahassavaṭṭipādāsiṃ          buddhassa lokabandhuno    
sattāhaṃ pajjalitvāna        padīpā vūpasammisuṃ [^51-2] .

Tena cittappasādena        cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        vimānaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Upapannassa devattaṃ         byamho [^51-3] āsi sunimmito    
samantato pajjalati          dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhavīsatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
divārattiñca passāmi        samantā yojanantadā .

Samantā yojanaṃ sabbaṃ        virocemi ahaṃ tadā    
sabbe deve abhibhomi       dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^51-1] ma. Yojiyahaṃ tadā . [^51-2] ma. Dīpā vūpasamiṃsu me . [^51-3] ma. Byamhaṃ āsi sunimmitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 52 -->
<a id="page-52"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 52]*

Tiṃsakappāni devindo        deve rajjamakārayiṃ    
na maṃ kecātimaññanti        dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sahassalokaṃ ñāṇena         passāmi satthusāsane    
dibbacakkhuṃ anuppatto        dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho      tiṃsakappasahassiko [^52-1]    
tassa dīpo mayā dinno      vippasannena cetasā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpi ca aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anuruddho thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Anuruddhattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ puṇṇamantānīputtattherāpadānaṃ (5)    
[7] ajjhānako mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
purakkhatomhi sissehi        upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mama kammaṃ pakittesi         saṅkhittena mahāmuni .

Tāhaṃ dhammaṃ suṇitvāna        abhivādetvāna satthuno    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        pakkāmiṃ [^52-2] dakkhiṇāmukho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 53 -->
<a id="page-53"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 53]*

Saṅkhittena suṇitvāna        vitthārena adesayiṃ [^53-1]    
sabbe sissā attamanā      sutvāna mama bhāsato .

Sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ vinodetvā       buddhe cittaṃ pasādayuṃ    
saṅkhittenapi desemi        vitthārena tathevahaṃ .

Abhidhammanayaññohaṃ           kathāvatthuvisuddhiyā    
sabbesaṃ viññāpetvāna      viharāmi anāsavo .

Ito pañcasate kappe       caturo suppakāsakā    
sattaratanasampannā          catudīpamhi issarā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇo mantānīputto thero imā    
Gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Puṇṇassa mantānīputtattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ upālittherāpadānaṃ (6)    
[8] nagare haṃsavatiyā           sujāto nāma brāhmaṇo    
asītikoṭisannicayo [^53-2]       pahūtadhanadhaññavā .

Ajjhāyako mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
lakkhaṇe itihāse ca        saddhamme pāramiṃ gato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 54 -->
<a id="page-54"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 54]*

Paribbajā ekasikhā         gotamā buddhasāvakā    
carakā tāpasā ceva        caranti mahiyā tadā .

Tepi maṃ parivārenti        brāhmaṇo vissuto iti    
bahū janā maṃ pūjenti [^54-1]     nāhaṃ pūjemi kiñcinaṃ .

Pūjārahaṃ na passāmi         mānathaddho ahaṃ tadā    
buddhoti vacanaṃ natthi         tāva nuppajjate jino .

Accayena ahorattaṃ         padumuttaranāyako [^54-2]    
sabbaṃ tamaṃ vinodetvā       loke uppajji cakkhumā .

Vitthārike bahū jaññe       puthubhūte ca sāsane    
upāgami tadā buddho        nagaraṃ haṃsasavhayaṃ .

Pitu atthāya so buddho      dhammaṃ desesi cakkhumā    
tena kālena parisā        samantā yojanaṃ tadā .

Sammato manujānaṃ yo [^54-3]     sunando nāma tāpaso    
yāvatā buddhaparisā         pupphehicchādayi tadā .

Catusaccaṃ pakāsento        seṭṭhe ca pupphamaṇḍape    
koṭisatasahassānaṃ           dhammābhisamayo ahu .

Sattarattindivaṃ buddho        vassitvā  dhammavuṭṭhiyā    
aṭṭhame divase patte       sunandaṃ kittayī jino .

Devaloke manusse vā      saṃsaranto ayaṃ bhave    
sabbesaṃ pavaro hutvā       bhavesu saṃsarissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 55 -->
<a id="page-55"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 55]*

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
mantānīputto puṇṇoti       hessati satthu sāvako .

Evaṃ kittayi sambuddho       sunandaṃ tāpasaṃ tadā    
hāsayanto janaṃ sabbaṃ        dassayanto sakaṃ balaṃ .

Katañjalī namassanti          sunandaṃ tāpasaṃ tadā [^55-1]    
buddhe kāraṃ karitvāna       sodhesi gatimattano .

Tattha me ahu saṅkappo      sutvāna munino vacaṃ    
ahaṃ [^55-2] kāraṃ karissāmi      yathā passāmi gotamaṃ .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        kiriyaṃ cintayiṃ mamaṃ    
kyāhaṃ kammaṃ ācarāmi       puññakkhette anuttare .

Ayañca pāṭhiko bhikkhu        sabbapāṭhīna [^55-3] sāsane    
vinaye agganikkhitto        taṃ ṭhānaṃ patthayiṃ [^55-4] ahaṃ .

Idaṃ me amitaṃ bhogaṃ         akkhobhaṃ sāgarūpamaṃ    
tena bhogena buddhassa       ārāmaṃ māpaye ahaṃ .

Sobhanaṃ nāma ārāmaṃ        nagarassa puratthato    
katvā [^55-5] satasahassena      saṅghārāmaṃ amāpayiṃ .

Kūṭāgāre ca pāsāde      maṇḍape hammiye guhā    
caṅkame sukate katvā       saṅghārāme [^55-6] amāpayiṃ .

#[^55-1] ma. Janā . [^55-2] ma. Ahampi kāraṃ kassāmi . [^55-3] ma. Sabbapāṭhissa . Yu. Sabbapāṭhikasāsane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 56 -->
<a id="page-56"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 56]*

Jantāgharaṃ aggisālaṃ         atho udakamāḷakaṃ    
nhānagharaṃ māpayitvā        bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ .

Āsandiyo ca pīṭhake        paribhoge ca bhājane    
ārāmikañca bhesajjaṃ        sabbametaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Ārakkhaṃ paṭṭhapetvāna       pākāraṃ kārayiṃ daḷhaṃ    
mā naṃ koci viheṭhesi       santacittāna tādinaṃ .

Āvāse [^56-1] satasahasse     saṅghārāme [^56-2] amāpayiṃ    
vepullataṃ māpayitvā        sambuddhaṃ upanāmayiṃ .

Niṭṭhāpito mayārāmo       sampaṭiccha tuvaṃ muni    
niyyādessāmi taṃ [^56-3] dhīra    adhivāsehi cakkhuma .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         adhivāsehi nāyako .

Adhivāsanamaññāya           sabbaññussa mahesino    
bhojanaṃ paṭiyādetvā        kālamārocayiṃ ahaṃ .

Ārocitamhi kālamhi        padumuttaranāyako    
khīṇāsavasahassehi           ārāmaṃ me upāgami .

Nisinnakālamaññāya          annapānena tappayiṃ    
bhuttāvīkālamaññāya         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Kīto satasahassena          tattakeneva kārito    
sobhano nāma ārāmo      sampaṭiccha tuvaṃ muni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 57 -->
<a id="page-57"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 57]*

Iminā bhūmidānena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
bhave nibbattamānohaṃ        labhāmi mama patthitaṃ .

Paṭiggahetvā sambuddho      saṅghārāmaṃ sumāpitaṃ    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Yo so buddhassa pādāsi     saṅghārāmaṃ sumāpitaṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Hatthī assā rathā pattī      senā ca caturaṅginī    
parivārentimaṃ niccaṃ         saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         bheriyo samalaṅkatā    
parivārentimaṃ niccaṃ         saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chaḷāsītisahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .

Āḷāramukhā [^57-1] hasulā      susaññā tanumajjhimā    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ       saṅghārāmassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati .

Devarājena pattabbaṃ        sabbaṃ paṭilabhissati    
anūnabhogo hutvāna         devarajjaṃ karissati .

Sahassakkhattuṃ cakkavatti       rājā raṭṭhe bhavissati .

Paṭhabyā rajjaṃ vipulaṃ         gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ [^57-2] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 58 -->
<a id="page-58"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 58]*

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
upāli nāma nāmena        hessati satthu sāvako .

Vinaye pāramiṃ patvā        ṭhānāṭhāne ca kovido    
jinasāsanaṃ dhārayanto        viharissatināsavo .

Sabbametaṃ abhiññāya         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapessati .

Aparimeyyaṃ upādāya        patthemi tava sāsanaṃ    
so me attho anuppatto    sabbasaṃyojanakkhayo .

Yathā sūlāvuto poso       rājadaṇḍena tajjito    
sūle sātaṃ avindanto       parimuttiṃva icchati .

Tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          bhavadaṇḍena tajjito    
kammasūlāvuto santo        pipāsāvedanāṭṭhito .

Bhave sātaṃ na vindāmi       ḍayhanto tīhi aggihi    
parimuttiṃ gavesāmi          yathā ca rājadaṇḍato .

Yathā visārado puriso       visena paripīḷito    
agadaṃ so gaveseyya        visaghātāyupāyanaṃ [^58-1]  .

Gavesamāno passeyya       agadaṃ visaghātakaṃ    
taṃ pivitvā sukhī assa        visamhā parimuttiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 59 -->
<a id="page-59"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 59]*

Tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          yathā visagato [^59-1] naro    
sampīḷito avijjāya         saddhammāgadamesahaṃ .

Dhammāgadaṃ gavesanto        addakkhiṃ sakyasāsanaṃ    
aggasaccosathānantaṃ [^59-2]      sabbasallavinodanaṃ .

Dhammosathaṃ pivitvāna         visaṃ sabbaṃ samūhaniṃ    
ajarāmaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ          nibbānaṃ passayiṃ [^59-3] ahaṃ .

Yathā bhūtatajjito poso      bhūtaggāhena pīḷito    
bhūtavejjaṃ gaveseyya        bhūtasmā parimuttiyā .

Gavesamāno passeyya       bhūtavijjāsu kovidaṃ    
tassa so vihaññe bhūtaṃ       samūlañca vināsaye .

Tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          tamaggāhena pīḷito    
ñāṇālokaṃ gavesāmi        tamato parimuttiyā .

Athaddasaṃ sakyamuniṃ           kilesatamasodhanaṃ    
so me tamaṃ vinodesi       bhūtavejjova bhūtikaṃ [^59-4] .

Saṃsārasotaṃ sañchindiṃ         taṇhāsotaṃ nivārayiṃ    
bhavaṃ ugghātayiṃ sabbaṃ         bhūtavejjova mūlato .

Garuḷo yathā opatati        pannagaṃ bhakkhamattano    
samantā yojanasataṃ          vikkhobheti mahāsaraṃ .

Pannagaṃ so gahetvāna       adhosīsaṃ viheṭhayaṃ    
ādāya so pakkamati        yena kāmaṃ vihaṅgamo .

#[^59-1] ma. Yu. Visahato . [^59-2] ma. Aggaṃ sabbosadhānaṃ taṃ .  Yu. Aggasabbo ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 60 -->
<a id="page-60"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 60]*

Tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          yathāpi garuḷo balī    
asaṅkhataṃ gavesanto         dose vikkhālayiṃ ahaṃ .

Diṭṭho ahaṃ dhammavaraṃ         santipadaṃ anuttaraṃ    
ādāya viharāmetaṃ         garuḷo pannagaṃ yathā .

Āsāvatī nāma latā        jātā cittalatāvane    
tassā vassasahassena        ekaṃ nibbattate phalaṃ .

Taṃ devā payirupāsanti       tāva dūraphalaṃ [^60-1] sakiṃ    
devānaṃ sā piyā evaṃ      āsāvatī phaluttamā [^60-2] .

Satasahassaṃ upādāya         tāhaṃ paricare muniṃ [^60-3]    
sāyaṃ pātaṃ namassāmi        devā āsāvatiṃ yathā .

Avañjhā pāricariyā         amoghā ca namassanā    
dūrāgataṃpi maṃ santaṃ          khaṇo maṃ [^60-4] na virādhayi .

Paṭisandhiṃ na passāmi         vicinanto bhave ahaṃ    
nirūpadhi vippamutto          upasanto carāmahaṃ .

Yathāpi padumaṃ nāma          suriyaraṃsena pupphati    
tathevāhaṃ mahāvīra          buddharaṃsena pupphito .

Yathā balākayonimhi         na vijjati pumā [^60-4] sadā    
meghesu gajjamānesu        gabbhaṃ gaṇhanti tā sadā .

Ciraṃpi gabbhaṃ dhārenti        yāva megho na gajjati    
bhārato parimuccanti         yadā megho pavassati .

#[^60-1] ma. Tāva dūraphale sati . [^60-2] ma. Yu. Latuttamā . [^60-3] ma. Muni . [^60-4] ma. Yaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 61 -->
<a id="page-61"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 61]*

Padumuttarabuddhassa           dhammameghena gajjato [^61-1]    
saddena dhammameghassa        dhammagabbhaṃ agaṇhahaṃ .

Satasahassaṃ upādāya         puññagabbhaṃ dharemahaṃ    
nappamuñcāmi bhārato        dhammamegho na gajjati .

Yadā tuvaṃ sakyamuni          ramme kāpilavatthave    
gajjasi dhammameghena         bhārato parimuccahaṃ .

Suññataṃ animittañca          athāpaṇihitaṃpi [^61-2] ca    
caturo ca phale sabbe       dhammaṃ [^61-3] vijaṭi taṃpihaṃ .

Dutiyabhāṇavāraṃ .

Aparimeyyaṃ upādāya        patthemi tava sāsanaṃ    
so me attho anuppatto    santipadaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Vinaye pāramiṃ patto        yathāpi pāṭhiko isī    
na me samasamo atthi        dhāremi sāsanaṃ ahaṃ .

Vinaye khandhake cāpi        tikacchedeva pañcake    
ettha me vimati natthi       akkhare byañjanepi vā .

Niggahe paṭikamme ca        ṭhānāṭhāne ca kovido    
osāraṇe vuṭṭhāpane       sabbattha pāramiṃ gato .

Vinaye khandhake cāpi [^61-4]     nikkhipitvā padaṃ ahaṃ    
ubhato [^61-5] vibhaṅge ceva     rasato osareyyahaṃ .

#[^61-1] po. Gajjito . [^61-2] ma. Yu. Tathāppaṇihitampi ca . [^61-3] ma. Dhammevaṃ vijanayiṃ ahaṃ .Ā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 62 -->
<a id="page-62"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 62]*

Niruttiyā ca kusalo [^62-1]      atthānatthe ca kovido    
anaññātaṃ mayā natthi        ekaggo satthu sāsane .

Rūpadakkho ahaṃ ajja         sakyaputtassa sāsane    
kaṅkhaṃ sabbaṃ vinodemi        chindāmi sabbasaṃsayaṃ .

Padaṃ anupadañcāpi           akkharañcāpi byañjanaṃ    
nidāne pariyosāne        sabbattha kovido ahaṃ .

Yathāpi rājā balavā        nihanitvā [^62-2] parantape    
vijinitvāna saṅgāmaṃ         nagaraṃ tattha māpaye .

Pākāraṃ parikkhañcāpi        esikaṃ dvārakoṭṭhakaṃ    
aṭṭālake ca vividhe        kāraye nagare bahū .

Siṃghāṭakaṃ paccurañca [^62-3]       suvibhattantarāpaṇaṃ    
kārāpeyya [^62-4] sabhaṃ tattha    atthānatthavinicchayaṃ .

Nigghāṭatthaṃ amittānaṃ        chiddāchiddañca jānituṃ    
balakāyassa rakkhāya         senāmaccaṃ [^62-5] ṭhapeti so .

Ārakkhatthāya bhaṇḍassa       nidhānakusalaṃ naraṃ    
mā me bhaṇḍaṃ vinassīti       bhaṇḍarakkhaṃ ṭhapeti so .

Samaggo [^62-6] hoti so [^62-7] rañño   vuḍḍhiṃ yassa ca icchati    
tassādhikaraṇaṃ deti          mittassa paṭipajjituṃ .

Uppādesu [^62-8] nimittesu     lakkhaṇesu ca kovidaṃ    
ajjhāyakaṃ mantadharaṃ          porohicce ṭhapeti so .

#[^62-1] ma. Sukusalo . [^62-2] ma. Yu. Niggaṇhitvā . [^62-3] ma. Yu. Siṃghāṭakaṃ caccarañca .

#[^62-4] ma. Yu. Kārayeyya . [^62-5] ma. Yu. Senāpaccaṃ . [^62-6] po. Samatto . Ma. Mamatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 63 -->
<a id="page-63"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 63]*

Etehaṅgehi sampanno      khattiyoti pavuccati    
sadā rakkhanti rājānaṃ       cakkavākova dukkhinaṃ [^63-1] .

Tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         hatāmittova khattiyo    
sadevakassa lokassa         dhammarājāti vuccati .

Titthiye nīharitvāna [^63-2]      mārañcāpi sasenakaṃ    
tamandhakāraṃ vidhaṃsetvā [^63-3]    dhammanagaraṃ amāpayi .

Sīlaṃ pākārikaṃ tattha         ñāṇante dvārakoṭṭhakaṃ    
saddhā te esikā dhīra      dvārapālo ca saṃvaro .

Satipaṭṭhānamaṭṭālaṃ          paññā te caccaraṃ mune    
iddhipādañca siṅghāṭaṃ        dhammavīthi [^63-4] sumāpitaṃ [^63-5] .

Suttantaṃ abhidhammañca         vinayaṃ cāpi kevalaṃ    
navaṅgaṃ buddhavacanaṃ           esā dhammasabhā tava .

Suññataṃ animittañca          vihārañcāpaṇīhitaṃ    
anejo ca nirodho ca       esā dhammakuṭī tava .

Paññāya aggo nikkhitto     paṭibhāṇe ca kovido    
sārīputtoti nāmena        dhammasenāpatī tava .

Cutūpapātakusalo            iddhiyā pāramiṃ gato    
kolito nāma nāmena       porohicco tava [^63-6] mune .

Porāṇakavaṃsadharo           uggatejo durāsado    
dhutavādiguṇe aggo         akkhadasso tava mune .

#[^63-1] ma. Yu. Dukkhitaṃ . [^63-2] ma. Nīhanitvāna . [^63-3] ma. Yu. Vidhamitvā . [^63-4] yu. Dhammavīthiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 64 -->
<a id="page-64"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 64]*

Bahussuto dhammadharo         sabbapāṭhī ca sāsane    
ānando nāma nāmena      dhammarakkho tava mune .

Ete sabbe atikkamma      mahesī bhagavā mama    
vinicchayaṃ me pādāsi        vinaye viññudesitaṃ .

Yo koci vinaye pañhaṃ       pucchati buddhasāvako    
tattha me cintanā natthi      taññevatthaṃ   kathemahaṃ .

Yāvatā buddhakhettamhi       ṭhapetvā ca [^64-1] mahāmuniṃ    
vinaye mādiso natthi        kuto bhiyyo bhavissati .

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        evaṃ gajjati gotamo    
upālissa samo natthi        vinaye khandhakesu ca .

Yāvatā buddhabhaṇitaṃ          navaṅgaṃ satthusāsanaṃ    
vinaye [^64-2] kathitaṃ sabbaṃ       vinayamūlapassino .

Mama kammaṃ saritvāna         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Satasahassaṃ upādāya         imaṃ ṭhānaṃ apatthayiṃ    
so me attho anuppatto    vinaye pāramiṃ gato .

Sakyānaṃ nandijanako [^64-3]      kappako āsihaṃ pure    
vijahitvāna taṃ jātiṃ         putto jāto mahesino .

Ito dutiyake kappe        añjaso nāma khattiyom    
anantatejo amitayaso       bhūmipālo mahaddhano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 65 -->
<a id="page-65"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 65]*

Tassa rañño ahaṃ putto      candano nāma khattiyo    
jātimadena patthaddho        yasobhogamadena ca .

Nāgasatasahassāni           sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
tidhā pabhinnā mātaṅgā      parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Sabalehi paretohaṃ          uyyānaṃ gantukāmako    
āruyha sirikaṃ nāgaṃ         nagarā nikkhamiṃ tadā .

Caraṇena ca sampanno        guttadvāro susaṃvuto    
devalo nāma sambuddho      āgacchi purato mamaṃ .

Pesetvā sirikaṃ nāgaṃ       buddhaṃ āsādayiṃ tadā    
tato sañjātakopova [^65-1]     nāgo nuddharako [^65-2] padaṃ .

Nāgaṃ ruṇṇamanaṃ [^65-3] disvā     buddhe kopaṃ akāsahaṃ    
viheṭhayitvā sambuddhaṃ        uyyānaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Sātaṃ tattha na vindāmi       siro pajjalito yathā    
pariḷāhena ḍayhāmi         macchova balisādako .

Sasāgarantā paṭhavī          ādittā viya hoti me    
pitu santikupāgamma          idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Āsīvisaṃva kupitaṃ            aggikkhandhaṃva āgataṃ    
mattaṃva kuñjaraṃ dantiṃ         yaṃ sayambhuṃ asādayiṃ .

Āsādito mayā buddho      ghoro uggatapo jino    
purā sabbe vinassāma       khamāpessāma taṃ muniṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 66 -->
<a id="page-66"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 66]*

No ce taṃ nijjhāpessāma    attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ    
orena [^66-1] sattame divase   raṭṭhamme vidhamissati .

Sumekhalo kosiyo ca        siggavo cāpi sattako    
āsādayitvā isayo        duggatā te sasenakā .

Yadā kuppanti isayo        saññatā brahmacārino    
sadevakaṃ vināsenti         sasāgaraṃ sapabbataṃ .

Tiyojanasahassamhi           purise sannipātayiṃ    
accayaṃ desanatthāya         sayambhuṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Allavatthā allasirā        sabbeva pañjalīkatā    
buddhassa pāde nipatitvā     idaṃ vacanamabravava .

Khamassu tvaṃ mahāvīra         abhiyācati taṃ jano    
pariḷāhaṃ vinodehi          mā ca [^66-2] raṭṭhaṃ vināsaye .

Sadevamānusā sabbe        sadānavā sarakkhasā    
ayomayena kūṭena          siraṃ bhindeyyu me sadā .

Udake aggi na saṇṭhāti      vījaṃ sele na rūhati    
agade kimi na saṇṭhāti       kopo buddhe na jāyati .

Yathā ca bhūmi acalā         appameyyo ca sāgaro    
anantako ca ākāso       evaṃ buddhā [^66-3] akhobhiyā .

Attadantā [^66-4] mahāvīrā     khamitā ca tapassino    
khantānaṃ khamitānaṃ ca         gamanaṃ vo [^66-5] na vijjati .

#[^66-1] ma. Orena sattadivasā . [^66-2] ma. No . [^66-3] yu. Buddho akhobhiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 67 -->
<a id="page-67"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 67]*

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       pariḷāhaṃ vinodayi [^67-1]    
mahājanassa purato          nabhaṃ abbhuggamī tadā .

Tena kammenahaṃ vīra [^67-2]      hīnattaṃ ajjhupāgato    
samatikkamma taṃ jātiṃ         pāvisiṃ abhayaṃ puraṃ .

Tadāpi maṃ mahāvīra          ḍayhamānaṃ  susaṇṭhitaṃ [^67-3]    
pariḷāhaṃ vinodesi          sayambhuṃ ca khamāpayiṃ .

Ajjāpi maṃ mahāvīra         ḍayhamānaṃ tihaggibhiḷa    
nibbāpesi tayo aggī       sītibhāvañca pāpayiṃ .

Yesaṃ [^67-4] sotāvadhānatthi     suṇātha mama bhāsato    
atthaṃ tuyhaṃ pavakkhāmi        yathādiṭṭhaṃ padaṃ mamaṃ .

Sayambhuṃ taṃ vimānetvā       santacittaṃ samāhitaṃ    
tena kanmenahaṃ ajja        jātomhi nīcayoniyaṃ .

Mā vo khaṇaṃ virādhetha       khaṇātītā hi socare    
sadatthe vāyameyyātha       khaṇo vo paṭipādito .

Ekaccānañca vamanaṃ         ekaccānaṃ virecanaṃ    
visaṃ halāhalaṃ eke [^67-5]      ekaccānañca osathaṃ .

Vamanaṃ paṭipannānaṃ           phalaṭṭhānaṃ virecanaṃ    
osathaṃ phalalābhīnaṃ           puññakkhettaṃ gavesinaṃ .

Sāsanena viruddhānaṃ         visaṃ halāhalaṃ yathā    
āsīviso duṭṭhaviso [^67-6]      ekaṃ [^67-7] jhāpeti taṃ naraṃ .

#[^67-1] ma. Vinodayaṃ . [^67-2] yu. Dhīra . [^67-3] po. Sukhe ṭhitaṃ . [^67-4] po. Saṃsāre sassatā natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 68 -->
<a id="page-68"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 68]*

Sakiṃ pītaṃ halāhalaṃ           uparuddheti jīvitaṃ    
sāsanena virujjhitvā        kappakoṭimhi ḍayhati .

Khantiyā avihiṃsāya          mettacittavatāya ca    
sadevakaṃ so tarati          tasmā te [^68-1] avirodhiyā [^68-2] .

Lābhālābhe na sajjanti      sammānane vimānane    
paṭhavīsadisā buddhā          tasmā te na virodhiyā [^68-3] .

Devadatte ca vadhake        core caṅgulimālake    
rāhule dhanapāle ca        sabbesaṃ samako muni .

Etesaṃ paṭighaṃ natthi         rāgomesaṃ na vijjati    
sabbesaṃ samako buddho       vadhakassorasassa ca .

Panthe disvāna kāsāvaṃ      chaḍḍitaṃ miḷhamakkhitaṃ    
sirasā [^68-4] añjaliṃ katvā     vanditabbaṃ isiddhajaṃ .

Abbhatītā ca ye buddhā      vattamānā anāgatā    
dhajenānena sujjhanti        tasmā ete namassiyā .

Satthukappaṃ suvinayaṃ           dhāremi hadayenahaṃ    
namassamāno vinayaṃ          viharissāmi sabbadā .

Vinayo āsayo mayhaṃ        vinayo ṭhānacaṅkamaṃ    
kappemi vinaye vāsaṃ        vinayo mayha gocaro .

Vinaye pāramippatto        samathe cāpi kovido    
upāli taṃ mahāvīra          pāde vandati satthuno .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 69 -->
<a id="page-69"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 69]*

so ahaṃ vicarissāmi         gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ    
namassamāno sambuddhaṃ        dhammassa ca sudhammataṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāli thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upālittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ aññākoṇḍaññattherāpadānaṃ (7)    
[9] padumuttarasambuddhaṃ           lokajeṭṭhaṃ vināyakaṃ    
buddhabhūmiṃ anuppattaṃ          paṭhamaṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ .

Yāvatā bodhiyā mūle       yakkhā sabbe samāgatā    
sambuddhaṃ parivāretvā       vandanti pañjalīkatā .

Sabbe devā tuṭṭhamanā      ākāse sañcaranti te    
buddho ayaṃ anuppatto       andhakāratamonudo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 70 -->
<a id="page-70"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 70]*

Tesaṃ hāsaparetānaṃ         mahānādo avattatha    
kilese jhāpayissāma        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Devānaṃ giramaññāya         vācāya [^70-1] samudīritaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      ādibhikkhamadāsahaṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
devasaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Sattāhaṃ abhinikkhamma         bodhiṃ ajjhagamaṃ ahaṃ    
idaṃ me paṭhamaṃ bhattaṃ         brahmacārissa yāpanaṃ .

Tusitāhi idhāgantvā        yo me bhikkhaṃ upānayi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsamatte [^70-2] kappasahasse    devarajjaṃ karissati    
sabbe deve abhibhotvā     tidivaṃ āvasissati .

Devalokā cavitvāna        manussattaṃ gamissati    
sahassadhā [^70-3] cakkavatti      tattha rajjaṃ karissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Tidasā so cavitvāna        manussattaṃ gamissati    
agārā pabbajitvāna        chabbassāni vasissati .

Tato sattamake vasse       buddho saccaṃ kathessati    
koṇḍañño nāma nāmena     paṭhamaṃ sacchikāhiti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 71 -->
<a id="page-71"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 71]*

Nikkhantenānupabbajjiṃ        padhānaṃ sukataṃ mayā    
kilese jhāpanatthāya        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Adhigantvāna sabbaññū        buddho loke sadevake    
iminā [^71-1] me mahāraññaṃ [^71-2]  amatabherimāhani .

Sodāni patto amataṃ        santaṃ [^71-3] padamanuttaraṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aññākoṇḍañño [^71-4] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aññākoṇḍaññattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ piṇḍolabhāradvājattherāpadānaṃ (8)    
[10] padumuttaro nāma jino       sayambhū aggapuggalo    
purato himavantassa          cittakūṭe vasī tadā .

Abhītarūpo tatthāsiṃ          migarājā catukkamo    
yassa [^71-5] saddaṃ suṇitvāna     vikkhambhanti bahū janā .

Suphullaṃ padumaṃ gayha          upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
vuṭṭhitassa samādhimhā        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

#[^71-1] ma. Isināme migāraññe . [^71-2] yu. Migaraññe . [^71-3] ma. Santipadamanuttaraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 72 -->
<a id="page-72"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 72]*

Cātuddisaṃ namassitvā        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ naruttamaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       sīhanādaṃ nadiṃ tadā [^72-1] .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
sakāsane nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Buddhassa giramaññāya         sabbe devā samāgatā    
āgato vadataṃ seṭṭho       dhammaṃ sossāma taṃ mayaṃ .

Tesaṃ hāsaparetānaṃ         purato lokanāyako    
mama saddaṃ pakittesi         dīghadassī mahāmuni .

Yenidaṃ padumaṃ dinnaṃ          sīhanādo ca nādito    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ito aṭṭhamake kappe       cakkavatti bhavissati    
sattaratanasampanno          catudīpamhi issaro .
