Kārayissati issaraṃ [^72-2]       mahiyā catusaṭṭhiyā    
padumo nāma nāmena        cakkavatti mahābalo .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Pakāsite pāvacane         brahmabandhu bhavissati    
brahmaññā abhinikkhamma       pabbajissati tāvade .

Padhānaṃ pahitatto so        upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 73 -->
<a id="page-73"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 73]*

Vijjane [^73-1] pantaseyyamhi    vāḷamigasamākule    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piṇḍolabhāradvājo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Piṇḍolabhāradvājattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Ekādasamaṃ khadiravaniyarevatattherāpadānaṃ (9)    
[11] gaṅgā bhāgīrasī [^73-2] nāma     himavantā pabhāvitā    
kutitthe nāviko āsiṃ       orimaṃ [^73-3] ca tare ahaṃ .

Padumuttaranāyako           sambuddho dipaduttamo    
vīsasatasahassehi            gaṅgāsotaṃ [^73-4] tarissati .

Bahū nāvā samānetvā      vaḍḍhakīhi susaṅkhataṃ    
nāvāya chadanaṃ katvā        paṭimāniṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Āgantvāna ca sambuddho     āruyhi [^73-5] nāvalañjakaṃ    
vārimajjhe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so tāresi sambuddhaṃ     saṅghaṃ cāpi anāsavaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        devaloke ramissati .

Nibbattissati te byamhaṃ      sukataṃ nāvasaṇṭhitaṃ    
ākāse pupphachadanaṃ         dhārayissati sabbadā .

#[^73-1] ma. Vijane . Yu. Vipine . [^73-2] ma. Bhāgīrathī . [^73-3] ma. Yu. Orime ca tariṃ ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 74 -->
<a id="page-74"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 74]*

Aṭṭhapaññāsakappamhi         tāraṇo [^74-1] nāma khattiyo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         cakkavatti bhavissati .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         cambako nāma khattiyo    
uggacchantova suriyo        jotissati mahābalo .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Tidasāva [^74-2] cavitvāna       manussattaṃ gamissati    
revato nāma nāmena       brahmabandhu bhavissati .

Agārā nikkhamitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissati .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      yuttayogo vipassako    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ        yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Paṭisambhidā catasso [3]    vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khadiravaniyo revatatthero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Khadiravaniyarevatattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^74-1] ma. Tārako nāma . [^74-2] ma. Yu. Tidasā so .

#[^74-3] ma.   Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ        phalaṃ dassesi me idha  
#       sumutto saravegova         kilese jhāpayī mama  
#       tatomaṃ vananirataṃ            disvā lokantagū muni  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 75 -->
<a id="page-75"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 75]*

Dvādasamaṃ ānandattherāpadānaṃ (10)    
[12] ārāmadvārā nikkhamma      padumuttaro mahāmuni    
vassanto amatavuṭṭhiṃ         nibbāpesi mahājanaṃ .

Satasahassā [^75-1] te dhīrā     chaḷabhiññā mahiddhikā    
parivārenti sambuddhaṃ        chāyāva anupāyinī .

Hatthikkhandhagato āsiṃ        setacchattaṃ varuttamaṃ    
sutānurūpaṃ [^75-2] disvāna       pīti me upapajjatha .

Oruyha hatthikkhandhamhā      upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
ratanamayaṃ chattaṃ me          buddhaseṭṭhassa dhārayiṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         padumuttaro mahāisi    
taṃ kathaṃ ṭhapayitvāna          imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so chattaṃ adhārayi       soṇṇalaṅkārabhūsitaṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ito gantvā ayaṃ poso     tusitaṃ āvasissati    
anubhossati sampattiṃ         accharāhi purakkhato .

Catuttiṃsatikkhattuṃ ca          devarajjaṃ karissati    
narādhipo [^75-3] aṭṭhasataṃ       vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Aṭṭhapaññāsakkhattuṃ ca        cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           mahiyā kārayissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 76 -->
<a id="page-76"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 76]*

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Sakyānaṃ kulaketussa         ñātibandhu bhavissati    
ānando nāma nāmena      upaṭṭhāko mahesino .

Ātāpī nipako cāpi        bāhusaccesu kovido    
nivātavutti atthaddho        sabbapāṭhī bhavissati .

Padhānaṃ pahitatto so        upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Santi āraññakā nāgā      kuñjarā saṭṭhihāyanā    
tidhappabhinnā mātaṅgā       īsādantā uruḷhavā .

Anekasatasahassā           paṇḍitāpi mahiddhikā    
sabbe te buddhanāgassa      na honti [^76-1] parivimbhitā .

Ādiyāme namassāmi        majjhime atha pacchime    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ .

Ātāpī nipako cāpi        sampajāno paṭissato    
sotāpattiphalaṃ patto        sekhabhūmīsu kovido .

Kappeto satasahasse        yaṃ kammamabhinīhariṃ    
tāhaṃ bhūmiṃ anuppatto        ṭhitā [^76-2] saddhā mahapphalā .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 77 -->
<a id="page-77"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 77]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ānando thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ānandattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
buddho paccekasambuddho      sārīputto ca kolito    
kassapo anuruddho ca        puṇṇatthero upāli ca .

Koṇḍañño cāpi piṇḍolo    revatonandapaṇḍito    
cha satāni ca paññāsā       gāthāyo sabbapiṇḍitā .

Apadāne buddhavaggo paṭhamo .

dutiyo sīhāsaniyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sīhāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (11)    
[13] nibbute lokanāthamhi        siddhatthe dīpaduttame    
vitthārite pāvacane        bāhujaññamhi sāsane .

Pasannacitto sumano         sīhāsanamakāsahaṃ    
sīhāsanaṃ karitvāna          pādapīṭhamakāsahaṃ .

Sīhāsane ca vassante       gharaṃ tattha akāsahaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 78 -->
<a id="page-78"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 78]*

Āyāmena catubbīsā [^78-1]     yojanāsiṃsu [^78-2] tāvade    
vimānaṃ sukataṃ mayhaṃ          vitthārena catuddasaṃ .

Satta kaññāsahassāni        parivārenti maṃ sadā    
soṇṇamayañca pallaṅkaṃ        byamhe āsi sunimmitaṃ .

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ         dibbayānaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ    
pāsādā sivikā ceva       nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ .

Maṇimayā ca pallaṅkā        aññe sāramayā bahū    
nibbattanti mamaṃ sabbe       sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soṇṇamayā rūpimayā         phalikā veḷuriyāmayā    
pādukā abhiruyhāmi         pādapiṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tesattati ito kappe       indanāmā tayo janā    
dvesattati ito kappe      tayo sumananāmakā .

Samasattatito kappe         tayo varuṇanāmakā    
sattaratanasampannā          catudīpamhi issarā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sīhāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 79 -->
<a id="page-79"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 79]*

Dutiyaṃ ekatthambhikattherāpadānaṃ (12)    
[14] siddhatthassa bhagavato         mahāpugagaṇo [^79-1] ahu    
saraṇaṃ gatā ca te buddhaṃ      saddahanti tathāgataṃ .

Sabbe saṅgamma mantetvā    māḷaṃ kubbanti satthuno    
ekatthambhaṃ alabhantā        vicinanti brahāvane .

Tehaṃ araññe disvāna       upagamma gaṇaṃ tadāā    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        paripucchiṃ gaṇaṃ ahaṃ .

Te me puṭṭhā viyākaṃsu      sīlavanto upāsakā    
māḷaṃ mayaṃ kattukāmā        ekatthambho na labbhati .

Ekatthambhaṃ mamaṃ detha        ahaṃ dassāmi satthuno    
āharissāmahaṃ thambhaṃ         appossukkā bhavantu te .

Te me thambhaṃ pavecchiṃsu      pasannā tuṭṭhamānasā    
tato paṭinivattitvā         āgamaṃsu sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Aciraṃ gate pugagaṇe [^79-2]      thambhaṃ adāsahaṃ [^79-3] tadā    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      paṭhamaṃ ussapesahaṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        vimānaṃ upapajjahaṃ    
ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         sattabhūmaṃ samuggataṃ .

Vajjamānāsu bherīsu         paricāremahaṃ sadā    
pañcapaññāsakappamhi         rājā āsiṃ yasodharo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 80 -->
<a id="page-80"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 80]*

Tatthāpi bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         sattabhūmaṃ samuggataṃ    
kūṭāgāravarūpetaṃ           ekatthambhaṃ manoramaṃ .

Ekavīsatikappamhi           udeno nāma khattiyo    
tatrāpi bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         sattabhūmaṃ sulaṅkataṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
anubhomi sabbametaṃ [^80-1]       ekatthambhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ thambhamadadiṃ [^80-2] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ekatthambhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekatthambhiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekatthambhikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ nandattherāpadānaṃ (13)    
[15] padumuttarassa bhagavato        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
vatthaṃ khomaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       sayambhussa mahesino .

Tamme buddho viyākāsi      jalajuttamanāyako    
iminā vatthadānena         hemavaṇṇo bhavissasi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 81 -->
<a id="page-81"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 81]*

Dvesampattiṃ anubhotvā      sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          kaniṭṭho tvaṃ bhavissasi .

Rāgaratto sukhasīlo         kāmesu gedhamāyuto    
buddhena codito santo      tato [^81-1] tvaṃ pabbajissasi .

Pabbajitvāna tvaṃ tattha       kusalamūlena codito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Satakappasahassamhi           caturo celanāmakā    
saṭṭhikappasahassamhi          upacelā catujjanā .

Pañcakappasahassamhi          celāva caturo janā    
sattaratanasampannā          catudīpamhi issarā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nando thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nandattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ cullapanthakattherāpadānaṃ (14)    
[16] padumuttaro nāma jino       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭho so       himavante vasī tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 82 -->
<a id="page-82"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 82]*

Ahaṃpi himavantamhi           vasāmi assame tadā    
acirāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ          upesiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Pupphacchattaṃ gahetvāna       upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
samādhiṃ samāpajjantaṃ         antarāyamakāsahaṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        pupphacchattaṃ adāsahaṃ    
paṭiggahesi bhagavā          padumuttaro mahāmuni .

Sabbe devā attamanā      himavantaṃ upenti te    
sādhukāraṃ pavattiṃsu          anumodissati cakkhumā .

Idaṃ vatvāna te devā      upagañchacha naruttamaṃ    
ākāse dhārayantassa       padumaṃ chattamuttamaṃ .

Sattapattachattaṃ [^82-1] paggayha    adāsi tāpaso mama    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Pañcavīsatikappāni           devarajjaṃ karissati    
catuttiṃsatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti bhavissati .

Yaṃ yaṃ yoniṃ saṃsarati          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
abbhokāse patiṭṭhantaṃ       padumaṃ dhārayissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Pakāsite pāvacane         manussattaṃ labhissati    
manomayamhi kāyamhi         uttamo so bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 83 -->
<a id="page-83"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 83]*

Dve bhātaro bhavissanti      ubhopi panthakavhayā [^83-1]    
anubhotvā uttamatthaṃ        jotayissanti sāsanaṃ .

So aṭṭhārasavassohaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
visesāhaṃ na vindāmi        sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Dandhā mayhaṃ gati āsi       paribhūto pure ahaṃ [^83-2]    
bhātā ca maṃ paṇāmesi       gaccha dāni sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Sohaṃ paṇāmito santo       saṅghārāmassa koṭṭhake    
dummano tattha aṭṭhāsiṃ       sāmaññasmiṃ apekkhavā .

Athettha [^83-3] satthā āgacchi   sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi    
bāhāya maṃ gahetvāna       saṅghārāmaṃ pavesayi .

Anukampāya me satthā       adāsi pādapuñchaniṃ    
evaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhehi        ekamantaṃ adhiṭṭhitaṃ .

Hatthehi tamahaṃ gayha         sariṃ kokanadaṃ ahaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ vimuccī me       arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Manomayesu kāyesu         sabbattha pāramiṃ gato    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cullapanthako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Cullapanthakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 84 -->
<a id="page-84"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 84]*

Pañcamaṃ pilindavacchattherāpadānaṃ (15)    
[17] nibbute lokanāthamhi        sumedhe aggapuggale    
pasannacitto sumano         thūpapūjaṃ akāsahaṃ .

Ye ca khīṇāsavā tattha       chaḷabhiññā mahiddhikāๆ    
tehaṃ tattha samānetvā      saṅghabhattamakāsahaṃ .

Sumedhassa bhagavato          upaṭṭhāko tadā ahu    
sumedho nāma nāmena       anumodittha so tadā .

Tena cittappasādena        vimānaṃ upapajjahaṃ    
chaḷāsītisahassāni           accharāyo ahesu [^84-1] me .

Mameva anuvattanti          sabbakāmehi [^84-2] tā sadā    
aññe deve abhibhomi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīsatikappamhi           varuṇo nāma khattiyo    
visuddhabhojano āsiṃ         cakkavatti ahaṃ tadā .

Na te bījaṃ pavappanti        napi nīyanti naṅgalā    
akaṭṭhapākimaṃ sāliṃ          paribhuñjanti mānusā .

Tattha rajjaṃ karitvāna        devattaṃ puna gañchahaṃ [^84-3]    
tadāpi edisā mayhaṃ        nibbattā bhogasampadā .

Na maṃ mittā amittā vā     hiṃsanti sabbapāṇino    
sabbesaṃ ca piyo homi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 85 -->
<a id="page-85"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 85]*

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         gandhalepassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Imamhi bhaddake kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
mahānubhāvo rājisi [^85-1]      cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Sohaṃ pañcasu sīlesu         ṭhapetvā janataṃ bahuṃ    
pāpetvā sugatiṃyeva        devatānaṃ piyo ahaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilindavaccho thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pilindavacchattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ rāhulattherāpadānaṃ (16)    
[18] padumuttarassa bhagavato        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
sattabhūmimhi pāsāde        ādāsaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ .

Khīṇāsavasahassehi           parikiṇṇo mahāmuni    
upāgami gandhakuṭiṃ           dipadindo narāsabho .

Virocayaṃ gandhakuṭiṃ           devadevo narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 86 -->
<a id="page-86"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 86]*

yenāyaṃ jotitā seyyā     ādāsova susanthato .

Tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Soṇṇamayā rūpimayā         atho veḷuriyāmayā    
nibbattissanti pāsādā      yekeci manaso piyā .

Catusaṭṭhikkhattuṃ devindo      devarajjaṃ karissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ cakkavatti       bhavissati anantarā .

Ekavīsatikappamhi           vimalo nāma khattiyo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         cakkavatti bhavissati .

Nagaraṃ reṇuvati nāma         iṭṭhakāhi sumāpitaṃ    
āyāmato tīṇisataṃ          caturassasamāyutaṃ .

Sudassano nāma pāsādo     visukammena māpito    
kūṭāgāravarūpeto          sattaratanabhūsito .

Dasasaddāvivittantaṃ          vijjādharasamākulaṃ    
sudassanaṃva nagaraṃ            devatānaṃ bhavissati .

Pabhā niggacchate tassa       uggacchanteva sūriye    
virocissati taṃ niccaṃ         samantā aṭṭhayojanaṃ .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tusitāva cavitvāna          sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          atrajo so bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 87 -->
<a id="page-87"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 87]*

Sace vaseyya agāraṃ        cakkavatti bhaveyya so    
aṭṭhānametaṃ yaṃ tādi        agāre ratimajjhagā .

Nikkhamitvā agāramhā       pabbajissati subbato    
rāhulo nāma nāmena       arahā so bhavissati .

Kikīva aṇḍaṃ rakkheyya        cāmarīriva vāladhiṃ    
nipako sīlasampanno         mamaṃ dakkhi [^87-1] mahāmuni .

Tassāhaṃ dhammamaññāya        vihāsiṃ sāsane rato    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rāhulo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Rāhulattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ upasenavaṅgantaputtattherāpadānaṃ (17)    
[19] padumuttaraṃ bhagavantaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pabbhāramhi nisinnaṃ [^87-2] taṃ     upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ .

Kaṇikāraṃ [^87-3] pupphitaṃ disvā    vaṇṭe chetvāna taṃ tadā    
alaṅkaritvā chattamhi        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

#[^87-1] po. Ajja rakkhe mahāmuni . Ma. Yu. .. Rakkhi .. . [^87-2] ma. Nisīdantaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 88 -->
<a id="page-88"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 88]*

Piṇḍapātañca pādāsiṃ        paramannaṃ subhojanaṃ    
buddhena navame tattha        samaṇe aṭṭha bhojayī .

Anumodi mahāvīro          sayambhū aggapuggalo    
iminā chattadānena         paramannapavecchanā .

Tena cittappasādena        sampattiṃ anubhossasi    
tiṃsakkhattuṃ [^88-1] ca devindo    devarajjaṃ karissati .

Ekavīsatikkhattuṃ ca          cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Yaṃ vadanti sumedhoti         bhūripaññaṃ sumedhasaṃ    
kappeto satasahasse        okkākakulasambhavo .

Gotamo nāma gottena      eso [^88-2] buddho bhavissati    
sāsane dippamānamhi        manussattaṃ gamissati .

Upasenoti nāmena [3]    hessati satthusāvako    
carimaṃ vattate mayhaṃ [4]    bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        jetvā māraṃ savāhanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upaseno vaṅgantaputto thero imā    
Gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Upasenavaṅgantaputtattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyabhāṇavāraṃ niṭṭhitaṃ .

#[^88-1] ma. Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ . [^88-2] ma. Satthā loke bhavissati . [^88-3] ma. Tassa dhammesu(  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 89 -->
<a id="page-89"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 89]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ raṭṭhapālattherāpadānaṃ (18)    
[20] padumuttarassa bhagavato        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
varanāgo mayā dinno       īsādanto uruḷhavo [^89-1] .

Setacchattopasedhito [^89-2]     sāthabbaṇo [^89-3] sahatthipo    
agghāpetvāna taṃ sabbaṃ      saṅghārāmaṃ akārayiṃ .

Catupaññāsasahassāni         pāsāde kārayiṃ ahaṃ    
mahoghadānaṃ karitvāna        niyyādesiṃ mahesino .

Anumodi mahāvīro          sayambhū aggapuggalo    
sabbe jane hāsayanto      desesi amataṃ padaṃ .

Taṃ me buddho viyākāsi      jaladuttamanāyako [^89-4]    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Catupaññāsasahassāni         pāsāde kārayi ayaṃ    
kathayissāmi vipākaṃ          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Aṭṭhārasasahassāni          kūṭāgārā bhavissare    
byamhuttamamhi nibbattā      sabbasoṇṇamayā ca te .

Paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo      devarajjaṃ karissati    
aṭṭhapaññāsakkhattuṃ ca        cakkavatti  bhavissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

#[^89-1] ma. Uruḷhavā . [^89-2] ma. Setacchattopasodhito . Yu. .. Pasevato . [^89-3] ma. Sakappaṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 90 -->
<a id="page-90"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 90]*

Devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
phīte [^90-1] kule mahābhoge    nibbattissati tāvade .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      sukkamūlena codito    
raṭṭhapāloti nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Padhānaṃ pahitatto so        upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Uṭṭhāya abhinikkhamma         jahitvā bhogasampadā    
kheḷapiṇḍeva bhogamhi        pemaṃ mayhaṃ na vajjati .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ        yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṭṭhapālo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Raṭṭhapālattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sopākattherāpadānaṃ (19)    
[21] pabbhāraṃ sodhayantassa        pavare [^90-2] pabbatuttame    
siddhattho nāma bhagavā       āgacchi mama santikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 91 -->
<a id="page-91"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 91]*

Buddhaṃ upagataṃ disvā         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
santharaṃ paññāpetvāna [^91-1]    pupphāsanamadāsahaṃ .

Pupphāsane nisīditvā        siddhattho lokanāyako    
mamañca gatimaññāya          aniccataṃ udāhari .

Aniccā vata saṅkhārā       uppādavayadhammino    
uppajjitvā nirujjhanti       tesaṃ vūpasamo sukho .

Idaṃ vatvāna sabbaññū        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
nabhe [^91-2] abbhuggami dhīro     haṃsarājāva ambare .

Sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jahitvāna         bhāvayāniccasaññahaṃ    
ekāhaṃ bhāvayitvāna        tattha kālakato ahaṃ .

Dve sampattī anubhotvā     sukkamūlena codito    
pacchimabbhavasampatto [^91-3]      sāpākayonupāgamiṃ .

Agārā abhinikkhamma         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Āraddhaviriyo pahitatto      sīlesu susamāhito    
tosetvāna mahānāgaṃ       alatthaṃ upasampadaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññaṃ bhāvayiṃ tadā    
taṃ saññaṃ bhāvayantassa        patto me āsavakkhayo .

#[^91-1] ma. Santharitvāna . [^91-2] ma. Yu. Nabhaṃ abbhuggami vīro . [^91-3] ma. Yu. Pacchime่  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 92 -->
<a id="page-92"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 92]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sopāko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sopākattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ sumaṅgalattherāpadānaṃ (20)    
[22] āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmohaṃ         paṭiyādetvāna bhojanaṃ    
brāhmaṇe paṭimānento     visāle māḷake ṭhito .

Athaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
sabbalokaṃ vinetāraṃ         sayambhuṃ aggapuggalaṃ .

Bhagavantaṃ jutimantaṃ           sāvakehi purakkhataṃ    
ādiccamiva rocantaṃ         rathiyaṃ paṭipannakaṃ .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
manasāva nimantesiṃ          āgacchatu mahāmuni .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
khīṇāsavasahassehi           mama dvāraṃ upāgami .

Namo te purisājañña        namo te purisuttama    
pāsādaṃ abhirūhitvā         sīhāsane nisīdatu [^92-1] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 93 -->
<a id="page-93"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 93]*

danto dantaparivāro        tiṇṇo tārayataṃ varo    
pāsādaṃ abhirūhitvā         nisīdi pavarāsane .

Yaṃ me atthi sake gehe     āmisaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ    
tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ       pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Pasannacitto sumano         vedajāto katañjalī[^93-1]    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ namassāmi        aho buddhassuḷāratā .

Aṭṭhannaṃ payirupāsataṃ         bhuñjaṃ khīṇāsavā bahū    
tuyheveso ānubhāvo      saraṇantaṃ upemahaṃ .

Piyadassī ca bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so saṅghaṃ abhojesi      ujubhūtaṃ samāhitaṃ    
tathāgatañca sambuddhaṃ         suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Sattavīsatikkhattuṃ so         devarajjaṃ karissati    
sakakammābhiraddho so        devaloke ramissati .

Dasa ca aṭṭhakkhattuṃ so       cakkavatti bhavissati    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ pañcasataṃ       vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Araññaṃ vanamoggayha         kānanaṃ byagghasevitaṃ    
padhānaṃ padahitvāna          kilesā jhāpitā mayā .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhajānāmi         bhattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 94 -->
<a id="page-94"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 94]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumaṅgalo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumaṅgalattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sīhāsanī ekatthambhī         nando ca cullapanthako    
pilindarāhulo ceva         vaṅganto raṭṭhapālako .

Sopāko maṅgalo ceva      daseva dutiye vagge    
satañca sattatiṃsā ca         gāthā cettha pakāsitā .

Sīhāsaniyavaggo dutiyo .

tatiyo subhūtivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ subhūtittherāpadānaṃ (21)    
[23] himavantassa avidūre         nisabho nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ        paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Kosiyo nāma nāmena       jaṭilo uggatāpano    
ekāyiko [^94-1] adutiyo      vasāmi nisabhe tadā .

Phalamūlañca paṇṇañca          na bhuñjāmi ahaṃ tadā    
pavattañca supātāhaṃ         upajīvāmi tāvade .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 95 -->
<a id="page-95"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 95]*

Nāhaṃ kopemi ājīvaṃ        cajamānopi jīvitaṃ    
ārādhemi sakaṃ cittaṃ        vivajjemi anesanaṃ .

Rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ cittaṃ           yadā uppajjate mama    
sayaṃva paccavekkhāmi         ekako [^95-1] taṃ damemahaṃ .

Rajjasi [^95-2] rajanīye ca       dosanīye ca dussase    
muyhase mohanīye ca        nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ .

Visuddhānaṃ ayaṃ vāso        nimmalānaṃ tapassinaṃ    
mā kho visuddhaṃ dūsesi       nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ .

Āgāriko bhavitvāna        dayā yuttaṃ [^95-3] labhissasi    
ubhopi mā virādhesi        nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ .

Chavālātaṃ yathā kaṭṭhaṃ        na kvaci kiccakārakaṃ    
neva gāme araññe vā     na hi taṃ kaṭṭhasammataṃ .

Chavālātūpamo tvaṃsi         na gihi napi saññato    
ubhato muttako ajja        nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ .

Siyā nukho tava etaṃ        ko pajāni [^95-4] hi te idaṃ    
sīghaṃ [^95-5] dhuraṃ vahisi [^95-6] me    kosajjabahulāya ca .

Jigucchissanti taṃ viññū        asuciṃ nāgariko yathā    
ākaḍḍhitvāna isayo        codayissanti taṃ sadā .

Taṃ viññū pavadissanti         samatikkantasāsanaṃ    
saṃvāsaṃ alabhanto hi         kathaṃ jīvihisī tuvaṃ .

#[^95-1] ma. Yu. Ekaggo . [^95-2] ma. Rajjase . [^95-3] ma. Puttaṃ . [^95-4] ma. Pajānāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 96 -->
<a id="page-96"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 96]*

Tidhappabhinnaṃ mātaṅgaṃ         kuñjaraṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ    
balināgo upagantvā        yūthā nīharate gajaṃ .

Yūthā vinissaṭo santo       sukhaṃ sātaṃ na vindati    
dukkhito vimano hoti        pajjhāyanto [^96-1] pavedhati .

Tatheva jaṭilā taṃpi          nīharissanti dummatiṃ    
tehi tvaṃ nissaṭo santo     sukhaṃ sātaṃ na lacchasi .

Divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ      sokasallasamappito    
dayhasi pariḷāhena          gajo yūthāva nissaṭo .

Jātarūpaṃ yathā kuṭaṃ          neva yāyati [^96-2] katthaci    
tathā sīlavihīno tvaṃ         na yāyissasi [^96-3] katthaci .

Agārāvasamānopi [^96-4]       kathaṃ jīvihisī tuvaṃ    
mattikaṃ pettiñcāpi         natthi te nihitaṃ dhanaṃ .

Sakaṃ kammaṃ karitvāna         gatte sedaṃ pamocayaṃ    
evaṃ jīvihisī gehe         sādhu te taṃ na ruccati .

Evāhaṃ tattha vāremi       saṅkilesagataṃ manaṃ    
nānādhammakathaṃ katvā        pāpā cittaṃ nivārayiṃ .

Evaṃ me viharantassa        appamādavihārino    
tiṃsavassasahassāni           pavane me atikkamama .

Appamādena [^96-5] maṃ disvā    uttamatthaṃ gavesakaṃ    
padumuttarasambuddho          āgañchi mama santikaṃ .

#[^96-1] yu. Ojjhāyanto padhāvati . [^96-2] ma. Jhāyati . [^96-3] ma. Jhāyissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 97 -->
<a id="page-97"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 97]*

Timbarūsakavaṇṇābho          appameyyo anūpamo    
rūpenāsadiso buddho        ākāse caṅkamī tadā .

Suphullo sālarājāva        vijjuvabbhaghanantare    
ñāṇenāsadiso buddho       ākāse caṅkamī tadā .

Sīharājāvasambhīto          gajarājāva dappito [^97-1]    
abhīto [^97-2] byaggharājāva     ākāse caṅkamī tadā .

Siṅginikkhasuvaṇṇābho         khadiraṅgārasannibho    
maṇi yathā jotiraso         ākāse caṅkamī tadā .

Visuddhakelāsasannibho        puṇṇamāyeva candimā    
majjhantikeva [^97-3] suriyo      ākāse caṅkamī tadā .

Disvā nabhe caṅkamantaṃ       evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
devo nukho ayaṃ satto      udāhu manujo ayaṃ .

Na me suto va diṭṭho vā    mahiyā ediso naro    
api mantapadaṃ atthi          ayaṃ satthā bhavissati .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
nānāpupphañca gandhañca       sannipātesahaṃ tadā .

Pupphāsanaṃ paññāpetvā      sādhucittaṃ manoramaṃ    
narasārathinaṃ aggaṃ           idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .Ña    
idaṃ me āsanaṃ vīra         paññattaṃ tavanucchakaṃ [^97-4]    
hāsayanto mama cittaṃ        nisīda kusumāsane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 98 -->
<a id="page-98"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 98]*

Nisīdi tattha bhagavā          asambhītova kesarī    
sattarattindivaṃ buddho        pavare kusumāsane .

Namassamāno aṭṭhāsiṃ        sattarattindivaṃ ahaṃ    
vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā       satthā loke anuttaro .

Mama kammaṃ pakittento       idaṃ vacanamabravi    
bhāvehi buddhānussatiṃ        bhāvanānaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Imaṃ satiṃ bhāvayitvā         pūrayissasi mānasaṃ    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissasi .

Asītikkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjaṃ karissasi    
sahassakkhattuṃ cakkavatti       rājā raṭṭhe bhavissasi .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhossasi taṃ sabbaṃ         buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaranto         mahābhogaṃ labhissasi    
bhogehi [^98-1] onatā natthi    buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Asītikoṭiṃ chaḍḍetvā        dāsakammakare bahū    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissasi .

Ārādhayitvāna sambuddhaṃ      gotamaṃ sākyapuṅgavaṃ    
subhūti nāma nāmena         hessasi [^98-2] satthusāvako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 99 -->
<a id="page-99"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 99]*

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        dakkhiṇeyyagaṇamhi [^99-1] taṃ    
tathāraṇavihāre ca          dvīsu aggaṃ [^99-2] ṭhapessati .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       jalajuttamanāyako    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro         haṃsarājāva ambare .

Sāsito lokanāthena        namassitvā tathāgataṃ    
sadā bhāvesiṃ [^99-3] mudito     buddhānussatimuttamaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Asītikkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjaṃ akārayiṃ    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhomi susampattiṃ          buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaranto         mahābhogaṃ labhāmahaṃ    
bhoge me onatā natthi     buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ .

Satasahassito kappe         yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā subhūti thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Subhūtittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 100 -->
<a id="page-100"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 100]*

Dutiyaṃ upavāṇattherāpadānaṃ (22)    
[24] padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
jalitvā aggikkhandhova       sambuddho parinibbuto .

Mahājanā samāgamma         pūjayitvā tathāgataṃ    
citaṃ katvāna sukataṃ          sarīraṃ abhiropayuṃ .

Sarīrakiccaṃ katvāna          dhātuṃ tattha samānayuṃ    
sadevamanussā sabbe        buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu te .

Paṭhamā kañcanamayā          dutiyāpi [^100-1] maṇīmayā    
tatiyā rūpiyamayā           catutthā phalikāmayā .

Tattha [^100-2] pañcami yā bhūmi     lohitaṅgamayā ahu    
chaṭṭhā masāragallassa        sabbaratanamayūpari .

Jaṅghā maṇimayā āsi        vedikā ratanāmayā    
sabbasoṇṇamayo thūpo        uddhaṃ yojanamuggato .

Devā tattha samāgantvā     ekato mantayuṃ tadā    
mayaṃpi thūpaṃ karissāma [^100-3]      lokanāthassa tādino .

Dhātu āveṇikā natthi       sarīraṃ ekapiṇḍitaṃ    
imamhi buddhathūpasmiṃ          karissāma kañcukaṃ mayaṃ .

Devā sattaratanehi         aññaṃ vaḍḍhesu yojanaṃ    
thūpo dviyojanubbedho       timiraṃ byapahanti yo [^100-4] .

#[^100-1] ma. Dutiyāsi maṇīmayā . [^100-2] ma. Tathā pañcamiyā bhūmi . [^100-3] ma. Kassāmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 101 -->
<a id="page-101"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 101]*

Nāgā tattha samāgantvā     ekato mantayuṃ tadā8    
manussā ceva devā ca      buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu te .

Mā no pamattā assumhā    appamattā sadevakā [^101-1]    
mayampi thūpaṃ karissāma        lokanāthassa tādino .

Indanīlaṃ mahānīlaṃ           atho jotirasaṃ maṇiṃ    
ekato sannipātetvā      buddhathūpaṃ achādayuṃ [^101-2] .

Sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ āsi          tāvatā buddhacetiyaṃ    
tiyojanasamubbiddhaṃ           ālokakaraṇaṃ tadā .

Garuḷā [^101-3] ca samāgantvā    ekato mantayuṃ tadā    
manussā devanāgā ca       buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu te .

Mā no pamattā assumhā    appamattā sadevakā    
mayampi thūpaṃ karissāma        lokanāthassa tādino .

Sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ thūpaṃ           akaruṃ tāva [^101-4] kañcukaṃ    
yojanaṃ tepi vaḍḍhesuṃ        āyataṃ buddhacetiyaṃ .

Catuyojanamubbedho          buddhathūpo virocati    
obhāseti disā sabbā      sataraṃsīva uggato .

Kumbhaṇḍā [^101-5] ca samāgantvā  ekato mantayuṃ tadā    
manussā ceva devā ca      nāgā ca garuḷā tadā .

Paccekaṃ buddhaseṭṭhassa       akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ    
mā no pamattā assumhā    appamattā sadevakā .

#[^101-1] yu. Sadevatā . [^101-2] yu. Acchādayuṃ . [^101-3] po. Yu. Kumbhaṇḍā . [^101-4] ma. Te ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 102 -->
<a id="page-102"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 102]*

Mayampi thūpaṃ karissāma        lokanāthassa tādino    
ratanehi [^102-1] chādayissāma     āyataṃ buddhacetiyaṃ .

Yojanantepi vaḍḍhesuṃ        āyataṃ buddhacetiyaṃ    
pañcayojanamubbedho         thūpo obhāsatī tadā .

Yakkhā [^102-2] tattha samāgantvā  ekato mantayuṃ tadā    
manujā devatā nāgā       kumbhaṇḍā garuḷā tadā .

Paccekaṃ buddhaseṭṭhassa       akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ    
mā no pamattā assumhā    appamattā sadevakā .

Mayaṃpi thūpaṃ karissāma         lokanāthassa tādino    
phalikā [^102-3] chādayissāma      āyataṃ buddhacetiyaṃ .

Yojanantepi vaḍḍhesuṃ        āyataṃ buddhacetiyaṃ    
chayojanāni ubbiddho        thūpo obhāsatī tadā .

Gandhabbā ca samāgantvā     ekato mantayuṃ tadā    
manujā devatā nāgā       garuḷā kumbhayakkhakā .

[^102-4] katā buddhathūpaṃ          mayamettha akārakā    
mayampi thūpaṃ karissāma        lokanāthassa tādino .

Vediyo satta katvāna       yāva jaṅghā akaṃsu te    
sabbasovaṇṇamayaṃ thūpaṃ         gandhabbā kārayuṃ tadā .

Sattayojanamubbedho         thūpo obhāsate tadā    
rattindivā na ñāyanti       ālokā [^102-5] honti sabbadā .

#[^102-1] po. Yu. Phalikā chādayissāma . [^102-2] po. Yu. Gandhabbā ca ... . [^102-3] ma. Phalikāhi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 103 -->
<a id="page-103"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 103]*

Abhibhonti na tassābhā       candasūrā satārakā    
samantā yojanasate         padīpo [^103-1] pana pajjali .

Tena kālena yekeci       thūpaṃ pūjenti mānusā    
na te thūpaṃ āruhanti        ambare ukkhipanti te .

Devehi ṭhapito yakkho       abhisammatanāmako    
dhajaṃ vā pupphadāmaṃ vā       abhiropeti uttari .

Na te passanti taṃ yakkhaṃ      dāmaṃ passanti gacchato    
etaṃ [^103-2] passitvā gacchanti   sabbe gacchanti suggatiṃ .

Viruddhā ye pāvacane       pasannā ye ca sāsane    
pāṭihiraṃ daṭṭhukāmā         thūpaṃ pūjenti mānusā .

Nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ varako [^103-3] tadā    
āmoditaṃ janaṃ disvā        evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Oḷāro bhagavā eso      yassa dhātugharedisaṃ    
imā ca janatā tuṭṭhā       kāraṃ kubbaṃ na tappare .

Ahaṃpi kāraṃ karissāmi        lokanāthassa tādino    
tassa dhammesu dāyādo      bhavissāmi anāgate .

Sudhotaṃ rajakenāhaṃ          uttareyya paṭaṃ mama    
veḷugge [^103-4] ālaggetvāna  dhajaṃ ukkhipi ambare .

Abhisammatako gayha          ambare hāsi me dhajaṃ    
vāteritaṃ dhajaṃ disvā        bhiyyo hāsaṃ janesahaṃ .

#[^103-1] ma. Padīpopi na pajjali . [^103-2] ma. Yu. Evaṃ passitvā gacchantā . [^103-3] ma. Bhatako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 104 -->
<a id="page-104"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 104]*

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā      samaṇaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ    
taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādetvā       vipākaṃ pucchahaṃ dhaje .

So me kathesi ānanda      pītisañjananaṃ mama    
tassa dhajassa vipākaṃ         anubhossasi sabbadā .

Hatthī assā rathā pattī      senā ca caturaṅginī    
parivāressanti taṃ nicca      dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         bheriyo samalaṅkatā    
parivāressanti taṃ nicca      dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chaḷāsītisahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .

Āḷāramukhā hasulā         susaññā tanumajjhimā    
parivāressanti taṃ nicca      dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissasi    
asītikkhattuṃ devindo        devarājjaṃ karissasi .

Sahassakkhattuṃ rājāpi [^104-1]     cakkavatti bhavissasi    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
puññakammena saṃyutto        brahmabandhu bhavissasi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 105 -->
<a id="page-105"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 105]*

Asītikoṭiṃ chaḍḍetvā        dāsakammakare bahū    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissasi .

Ārādhayitvā sambuddhaṃ       gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
upavāṇoti nāmena         hessasi satthusāvako .

Satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ        phalaṃ dassesi me idha    
sumutto saravegova         kilese jhāpayiṃ mamaṃ .

Cakkavattissa santassa        cātudīpissarassa me    
tīṇiyojanasamantā           ussissanti [^105-1] dhajā sadā .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upavāṇo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upavāṇattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ tīṇisaraṇāgamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (23)    
[25] nagare candavatiyā [^105-2]       mātupaṭṭhānako [^105-3] ahaṃ    
andhā mātāpitā mayhaṃ      te posemi ahaṃ tadā .

#[^105-1] ma. Ussīsanti . Yu. Dassissanti . [^105-2] po. Bandhamatiyā . [^105-3] ma. Mātupaṭṭhāko  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 106 -->
<a id="page-106"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 106]*

Rahogato nisīditvā         evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
posento mātāpitaro      pabbajjaṃ na labhāmahaṃ .

Tamandhakārapihitā [^106-1]        tividhaggīhi dayhare    
etādise bhave jāte      natthi koci vināyako .

Buddho loke samuppanno     dippeti dāni [^106-2] sāsanaṃ    
sakkā uddharituṃ attā       puññakāmena jantunā .

Uggayha tīṇi saraṇe         paripuṇṇāni gopayiṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       paṭimukkhāmi duggatiṃ .

Nisabho nāma samaṇo         buddhassa aggasāvako    
taṃ ahaṃ upagantvāna         saraṇāgamanaṃ gahiṃ .

Vassasatasahassāni           āyuṃ vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā saraṇāgamanaṃ         paripuṇṇaṃ agopayiṃ .

Carime vattamānamhi         saraṇantamanussariṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Devalokagato santo        puññakammasamāhito    
yaṃ yaṃ desaṃ upapajjāma       aṭṭhahetū labhāmahaṃ .

Disāsu pūjito homi         tikkhapañño bhavāmahaṃ    
sabbe devānuvattanti       amitabhogaṃ labhāmahaṃ .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇo sabbattha        paṭibhāṇo [^106-3] bhavāmahaṃ    
mittānaṃ acalo homi        yaso accuggato mama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 107 -->
<a id="page-107"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 107]*

Asītikkhattuṃ devindo        devarajjamakārayiṃ    
dibbasukhaṃ anubhaviṃ            accharāhi purakkhato .

Pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Pacchime bhavasampatte        puññakammasamāhito    
pure sāvatthiyaṃ jāto       mahāsāle suaddhake .

Nagarā nikkhamitvāna         dārakehi purakkhato    
sāyaṃ [^107-1] khiḍḍāsamaṅgīhaṃ      saṅghārāmaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Tatthaddasāsiṃ samaṇaṃ          vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ    
so me dhammamadesesi       saraṇañca adāsi me .

Sohaṃ sutvāna saraṇaṃ         saraṇaṃ me anussariṃ    
ekāsane nisīditvā        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassena [^107-2]     arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho         guṇamaññāya cakkhumā    
aparimeyye ito kappe     saraṇāni agañchahaṃ    
tāva [^107-3] me sukataṃ kammaṃ     phalaṃ dassesi me idha .

Sugopitaṃ me saraṇaṃ          mānasaṃ suppaṇihitaṃ    
anubhotvā yasaṃ sabbaṃ        pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Yesaṃ sotāvadhānatthi        suṇātha mama bhāsato    
atthaṃ [^107-4] vo kathayissāmi     sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ padaṃ mama .

#[^107-1] ma. Hasakhiḍḍasamaṅgīhaṃ . Yu. Sahassakhiḍḍāsamaṅgīhaṃ . [^107-2] ma. Sattame vasse .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 108 -->
<a id="page-108"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 108]*

Buddho loke samuppanno     vattate jinasāsanaṃ    
amatā vāditā bheri        sokasallavinodanā .

Yathāsakena thāmena         puññakkhette anuttare    
adhikāraṃ kareyyātha         passayissatha nibbutiṃ .

Paggayha tīṇi saraṇe         pañcasīlāni gopiya    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā     dukkhassantaṃ karissatha .

Mamopamaṃ [^108-1] karitvāna       silāni parigopiya    
aciraṃ arahattaṃ vo          sabbepi pāpuṇissatha .

Tevijjo iddhipattomha      cetopariyakovido    
sāvako te mahāvīra        caraṇe [^108-2] vandāmi satthuno .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     saraṇaṃ buddhamagañchahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saraṇāgamane phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇisaraṇāgamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇisaraṇāgamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ pañcasīlasamādāniyattherāpadānaṃ (24)    
[26] nagare candavatiyā          bhatiko [^108-3] āsihaṃ tadā    
parakammāyane yutto        pabbajjaṃ na labhāmahaṃ .

#[^108-1] ma. Sammādhammaṃ bhāvetvā . [^108-2] ma. Saraṇo vandati satthuno . Yu. Saraṇe ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 109 -->
<a id="page-109"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 109]*

mahandhakārapihitā           tividhaggīhi dayhare    
kena nu kho upāyena       visaṃyutto bhave ahaṃ .

Deyyadhammo ca me natthi     varāko bhatiko ahaṃ    
yannūnāhaṃ pañcasīlaṃ          rakkheyyaṃ paripūrayaṃ .

Anomadassissa munino        nisabho nāma sāvako    
tamahaṃ upasaṅkamma           pañcasikkhāpadaggahiṃ .

Vassasatasahassāni           āyu vijjati tāvade    
tāvatā pañcasīlāni         paripuṇṇāni gopayiṃ .

Maccukāle ca sampatte      devā assāsayanti maṃ    
ratho sahassayutto te       mārisāyaṃ upaṭṭhito .

Vattate carime citte       mamaṃ sīlaṃ anussariṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tiṃsakkhattuñca devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
dibbasukhaṃ anubhaviṃ            accharāhi purakkhato .

Pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
pure vesāliyaṃ jāto       mahāsāle suaddhake .

Vassūpanāyike kāle        dippante jinasāsane    
mātā ca me pitā ceva     pañcasikkhāpadaggahuṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 110 -->
<a id="page-110"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 110]*

Saha sutvānahaṃ sīlaṃ          mama sīlaṃ anussariṃ    
ekāsane nisīditvā        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Jātiyā pañcavassena        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho         guṇamaññāya cakkhumā .

Paripuṇṇāni gopetvā       pañcasikkhāpadānahaṃ    
aparimeyye ito kappe     vinipātaṃ na gacchahaṃ .

Sohaṃ yasaṃ anubhaviṃ           tesaṃ sīlāna vāhasā    
kappakoṭiṃ pakittento       kittaye ekadesakaṃ .

Pañcasīlāni gopetvā       tayo hetū labhāmahaṃ    
dīghāyuko mahābhogo        tikkhapañño bhavāmihaṃ .

Pakittente [^110-1] ca sabbesaṃ   adhimattañca porisaṃ    
bhavābhave saṃsaritvā         ete ṭhāne labhāmahaṃ .

Aparimeyyesu sīlesu        vattantā jinasāvakā    
bhavesu yadi rajjeyyuṃ        vipāko kīdiso bhave .

Suciṇṇaṃ me pañcasīlaṃ         bhatakena tapassinā [^110-2]    
tena sīlenahaṃ ajja         mocayiṃ [^110-3] sabbabandhanā .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     pañcasīlāni gopayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pañcasīlānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 111 -->
<a id="page-111"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 111]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañcasīlasamādāniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pañcasīlasamādāniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ annasaṃsāvakattherāpadānaṃ (25)    
[27] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Siddhatthaṃ lokapajjotaṃ        appameyyaṃ anopamaṃ    
alatthaṃ paramaṃ pītiṃ           disvā dantaṃ jutindharaṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ abhināmetvā       bhojayintaṃ mahāmuniṃ    
muni kāruṇiko loke [^111-1]     anumodi mamaṃ tadā .

Tasmiṃ mahākāruṇike         paramassāsakārake    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā     kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhikkhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā annasaṃsāvako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Annasaṃsāvakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 112 -->
<a id="page-112"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 112]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ dhūpadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (26)    
[28] siddhatthassa bhagavato         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
kuṭidhūpaṃ mayā dinnaṃ          vippasannena cetasā .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbesaṃpi piyo homi        dhūpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dhūpamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhūpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhūpadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
abhāsitthāti .

Dhūpadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ puḷinapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (27)    
[29] vipassissa bhagavato          bodhiyā pādamuttame    
purāṇaṃ puḷinaṃ chaḍḍetvā [^112-1]   suddhaṃ puḷinamākiriṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ puḷinamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puḷinadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 113 -->
<a id="page-113"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 113]*

Tipaññāse [^113-1] ito kappe   rājā āsiṃ janābhibhū [^113-2]    
mahāpuḷino nāmena         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puḷinapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Puḷinapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ uttiyattherāpadānaṃ (28)    
[30] candabhāgānadītīre          sasasumāro ahaṃ tadā    
sabhojanapasutāhaṃ [^113-3]         nadītitthaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Siddhattho tamhi samaye       sayambhū aggapuggalo    
nadiṃ taritukāmo so         nadītitthaṃ upāgami .

Opāgate ca sambuddhe      ahaṃpi tatthupāgamiṃ    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        imaṃ vācaṃ udīrayiṃ .

Abhiruyha mahāvīra           tārissāmi ahaṃ tuvaṃ    
pettikaṃ visayaṃ mayhaṃ         anukampa mahāmuni .

Mama uggajjanaṃ sutvā        abhirūhi mahāmuni    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      tāresiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 114 -->
<a id="page-114"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 114]*

nadiyā pārime tīre        siddhattho lokanāyako    
assāsesi mamaṃ tattha        amataṃ pāpuṇissasi .

Tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      devalokaṃ agañchahaṃ    
dibbaṃ sukhaṃ anubhaviṃ           accharāhi purakkhato .

Sattakkhattuñca devindo      devarajjaṃ akāsahaṃ    
tīṇikhattuṃ cakkavatti          mahiyā issaro ahaṃ .

Vivekamanuyuttohaṃ           nipako ca susaṃvuto    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammā sambuddhasāsane .

Catunavute ito kappe       tāresiṃ yaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Uttiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ ekañjalikattherāpadānaṃ (29)    
[31] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
vipassiṃ satthavāhaggaṃ         naravīraṃ vināyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 115 -->
<a id="page-115"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 115]*

Adantadamanaṃ tādiṃ           mahāvādiṃ mahāmatiṃ    
disvā pasanno sumano       ekañjaliṃ akāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ añjalimakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         añjalissa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekañjaliko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekañjalikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ khomadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (30)    
[32] nagare bandhumatiyā          ahosiṃ vāṇijo tadā    
teneva dāraṃ posemi       ropemi bījasampadaṃ .

Vīthiyaṃ [^115-1] paṭipannassa        vipassissa mahesino    
ekaṃ khomaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       kusalatthāya satthuno .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ khomaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         khomadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse [^115-2] ito kappe    eko sindhavasandano    
sattaratanasampanno          cātuddīpamhi issaro .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 116 -->
<a id="page-116"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 116]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khomadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Khomadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
subhūti upavāṇo ca          saraṇo sīlagāhako    
annasaṃsāvako dhūpo         puḷino uttiyena ca .

Añjalī khomadāyī ca         daseva tatiye gaṇe    
pañcāsītisataṃ vuttā         gāthāyo sabbapiṇḍitā .

Subhūtivaggo tatiyo .

Catutthaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

catuttho kuṇḍadhānavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ kuṇḍadhānattherāpadānaṃ (31)    
[33] sattāhaṃ paṭisallīnaṃ          sayambhuṃ aggapuggalaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ .

Vuṭṭhitaṃ kālamaññāya         padumuttaraṃ mahāmuniṃ    
mahantaṃ kadalikaṇṇiṃ           gahetvā upagañchahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 117 -->
<a id="page-117"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 117]*

Paṭiggahesi [^117-1] bhagavā       sabbaññū [^117-2] lokanāyako    
mama cittaṃ pasādento       paribhuñji mahāmuni .

Paribhuñjitvāna sambuddho      satthavāho anuttaro    
sakāsane nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Ye ca santi samītāro [^117-3]    yakkhā imamhi pabbate    
araññe bhūtagaṇā sabbe [^117-4]   suṇantu vacanaṃ mama .

Yo so buddhaṃ upaṭṭhāsi      migarājaṃva kesariṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ekādasañca khattuṃ so       devarājā bhavissati    
catuttiṃsatikhattuñca           cakkavatti bhavissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma gottena      satthā loke bhavissati .

Akkositvāna samaṇe        sīlavante anāsave    
pāpakammavipākena          nāmadheyyaṃ labhissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
kuṇḍadhānoti nāmena        sāvako so bhavissati .

Pavivekaṃ anuyutto          jhāyī jhānarato ahaṃ    
tosayitvāna satthāraṃ        viharāmi anāsavo .

Sāvakehi [^117-5] parivuto       bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        salākaṃ gāhayī jino .

#[^117-1] ma. Yu. Paṭiggahetvā . [^117-2] yu. Taṃ phalaṃ . [^117-3] yu. Ye vasanti sametāro .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 118 -->
<a id="page-118"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 118]*

ekaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā         vanditvā lokanāyakaṃ    
vadataṃ varassa purato         paṭhamaṃ aggahesahaṃ .

Tena kammena bhagavā        dasasahassīpakampako    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapesi maṃ .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ        yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammā sambuddhasāsane .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṇḍadhāno thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kuṇḍadhānattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ sāgatattherāpadānaṃ (32)    
[34] sobhito nāma nāmena       ahosiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
purakkhato sasissehi         ārāmaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye         bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
ārāmadvārā nikkhamma      aṭṭhāsi purisuttamo .

Tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         dantaṃ dantapurakkhataṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Yekeci pādapā sabbe      mahiyā te virūhare  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 119 -->
<a id="page-119"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 119]*

Buddhimanto tathā sattā      ruhanti jinasāsane .

Satthavāhopi sabbaññū        mahesī bahuke jane    
vipathā uddharitvāna         pathaṃ ācikkhase tuvaṃ .

Danto dantapurakkhato [^119-1]     jhāyī jhānaratehi ca    
ātāpipahitattehi          upasantehi tādibhi .

Alaṅkato parisato [^119-2]       puññañāṇehi [^119-3] sobhasi    
pabhā niddhāvate tuyhaṃ       suriyodayano yathā .

Pasannacittaṃ disvāna         mahesī padumuttaro    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so hāsaṃ janetvāna     mamaṃ kittesi brāhmaṇo    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          devaloke ramissati .

Tusitāhi cavitvāna          sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissati .

Tuṭṭhahaṭṭhaṃ labhissati [^119-4]       pabbajitvāna sāsane    
sāgato nāma nāmena       hessati satthu sāvako .

Pabbajitvāna kāyena        pāpakammaṃ vivajjayiṃ    
vacīduccaritaṃ hitvā          ājīvaṃ parisodhayiṃ .

Evaṃ vihāramānohaṃ         tejodhātūsu kovido    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^119-1] ma. Yu. Dantaparikiṇṇo . [^119-2] ma. Alaṅkato parisāhi . [^119-3] yu. Sahagaṇehi . [^119-4] ma. Tena  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 120 -->
<a id="page-120"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 120]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sāgato thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sāgatattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ mahākaccāyanattherāpadānaṃ (33)    
[35] padumuttaranāthassa           padumaṃ nāma cetiyaṃ    
silāpaṭaṃ [^120-1] kārayitvāna     suvaṇṇenābhilepayiṃ .

Ratanāmayachattañca           paggayha vālavījaniṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         lokabandhussa tādino .

Yāvatā devatā bhummā      sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā    
ratanāsanachattānaṃ           vipākaṃ kathayissati .

Tañca sabbaṃ suṇissāma        kathayantassa satthuno    
bhiyyo hāsaṃ janeyyāma      sammā sambuddhasāsane .

Hemāsane nisīditvā        sayambhū aggapuggalo    
bhikkhusaṅghaparibyuḷho         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yenidaṃ āsanaṃ dinnaṃ         sovaṇṇaṃ ratanāmayaṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 121 -->
<a id="page-121"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 121]*

Tiṃsakappāni devindo        devarajjaṃ karissati    
samantā yojanasataṃ          ābhāyābhibhavissati .

Manussalokamāgantvā        cakkavatti bhavissati    
pabhassaroti nāmena         uggatejo bhavissati .

Divā vā yadivā rattiṃ       sataraṃsīva uggato    
samantā aṭṭharatanaṃ          ujjotissati khattiyo .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tusitāhi cavitvāna          sukkamūlena codito    
kaccāno nāma nāmena      brahmabandhu bhavissati .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      buddho [^121-1] hessatyanāsavo    
gotamo lokapajjoto       aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapessati .

Saṅkhittaṃ pucchitaṃ pañhaṃ        vitthārena kathessati    
kathayanto ca taṃ pañhaṃ        ajjhāsayaṃ puressati .

Addhakule abhijāto         brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
ohāya dhanadhaññāni         pabbaji [^121-2] anagāriyaṃ .

Saṅkhittenāpi pucchante      vitthārena kathemahaṃ    
ajjhāsayantesaṃ pūremi       tosemi dipaduttamaṃ .

Tosito me mahāvīro       sayambhū aggapuggalo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 122 -->
<a id="page-122"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 122]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākaccāyano thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mahākaccāyanattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ kāḷudāyittherāpadānaṃ (34)    
[36] padumuttarassa buddhassa        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
addhānaṃ paṭipannassa         carato cārikaṃ tadā .

Suphullaṃ padumaṃ gayha          uppalaṃ mallikañcahaṃ    
paramannaṃ gahetvāna         adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Paribhuñji mahāvīro          paramannaṃ subhojanaṃ    
tañca pupphaṃ gahetvāna       jinassa [^122-1] sampadassayi .

Iṭṭhaṃ kantayidaṃ [^122-2] loke     jalajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
sudukkaraṃ kataṃ tena          yaṃ [^122-3] me pupphaṃ adāsi [^122-4] so .

Yo pupphaṃ abhiropesi        paramannañcadāsi me    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Dasamaṭṭhakkhattuñca [^122-5] so     devarajjaṃ karissati    
uppalaṃ padumañcāpi          mallikañca taduttari .

#[^122-1] ma. Yu. Janassa . [^122-2] ma. Kantaṃ piyaṃ . Yu. Kantaṃ ciraṃ . [^122-3] ma. Yu. Yo me .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 123 -->
<a id="page-123"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 123]*

Assa puññavipākena         dibbagandhasamāyutaṃ    
ākāse chadanaṃ katvā       dhārayissati tāvade .

Pañcavīsatikhattuñca           cakkavatti bhavissati    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ pañcasataṃ       vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Sakakammābhiraddho so        sukkamūlena codito    
sakyānaṃ nandijanako [^123-1]      ñātibandhu bhavissati .

So ca pacchā pabbajitvā     sukkamūlena codito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Paṭisambhidamanuppattaṃ          katakiccaṃ anāsavaṃ    
gotamo lokabandhu taṃ        etadagge ṭhapessati .

Padhānaṃ pahitatto so        upasanto nirūpadhi    
udāyi nāma nāmena        hessati satthu sāvako .

Rāgo doso ca moho [^123-2] ca māno makkho ca dhaṃsito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Tosayiñcāpi sambuddhaṃ        ātāpī nipako ahaṃ    
pasādito [^123-3] ca sambuddho    etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 124 -->
<a id="page-124"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 124]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāḷudāyi thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kāḷudāyittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ mogharājattherāpadānaṃ (35)    
[37] atthadassī tu bhagavā         sayambhū aparājito    
bhikkhusaṅghaparibyuḷho         rathiyaṃ paṭipajjatha .

Sissehi samparivuto         gharamhā abhinikkhamiṃ    
nikkhamitvānahaṃ tattha         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Abhivādetvāna [^124-1] sambuddhaṃ   sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Yāvatā rūpino sattā       arūpī vā asaññino    
sabbe te tava ñāṇamhi      anto honti samogadhā [^124-2] .

Sukhumacchikena [^124-3] jālena     udakaṃ yo parikkhipe    
ye keci udake pāṇā      antojāle bhavanti te .

Yesañca cetanā atthi       rūpino ca arūpino .

Sabbe te tava ñāṇamhi      anto honti samogadhā    
samuddharasimaṃ lokaṃ           andhakārasamākulaṃ .

Tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         kaṅkhāsotaṃ taranti te    
avijjānivuto loko        andhakārena otthaṭo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 125 -->
<a id="page-125"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 125]*

Tava ñāṇamhi jotante       andhakārā padhaṃsitā    
tuvaṃ cakkhusi sabbesaṃ         mahātamavinūdano .

Tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         nibbāyissati bahujjano    
putaraṃ [^125-1] pūrayitvāna        madhadha khuddaṃ anelakaṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        apanesiṃ mahesino    
paṭiggaṇhi mahāvīro         subhakena mahāisi .

Bhuñjitvā tañca sabbaññū      vehāsaṃ nabhamuggami    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      atthadassī narāsabho .

Mama cittaṃ pasādento       imā gāthā abhāsatha    
yenidaṃ thavitaṃ ñāṇaṃ          buddhaseṭṭho ca thomito .

Tena cittappasādena        duggatiṃ so na gacchati    
catusaṭṭhiñca [^125-2] khattuṃ so     devarajjaṃ karissati .

Padesarajjaṭṭhasataṃ [^125-3]        vasudhaṃ āvasissati    
pañceva satakkhattuñca        cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ asaṅkheyyaṃ       mahiyā kārayissati    
ajjhāyiko mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū .

Gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissati    
gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ atthaṃ          ñāṇena vicinissati .

Mogharājāti nāmena        hessati satthusāvako    
tīhi vijjāhi sampanno [^125-4]    katakicco anāsavo .

#[^125-1] ma. Puṭakaṃ . Yu. Pīṭharaṃ . [^125-2] ma. Yu. Catuddasañca . [^125-3] ma. Paṭhabyā rajjaṃ aṭṭhasataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 126 -->
<a id="page-126"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 126]*

Gotamo satthavāhaggo       etadagge ṭhapessati    
hitvā mānusakaṃ yogaṃ        chetvāna bhavabandhanaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mogharājā thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mogharājattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ adhimuttakattherāpadānaṃ (36)    
[38] nibbute lokanāthamhi        atthadassinaruttame    
nimantetvā [^126-1] bhikkhusaṅghaṃ    vippasannena cetasā .

Nimantetvā saṅgharatanaṃ [^126-2]    ujubhūtaṃ samāhitaṃ    
ucchunā maṇḍapaṃ katvā       bhojesiṃ saṅghamuttamaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbe satte abhibhomi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ucchudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 127 -->
<a id="page-127"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 127]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā adhimuttako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Adhimuttakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ lasuṇadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (37)    
[39] himavantassa avidūre         tāpaso āsahaṃ tadā    
lasuṇaṃ upajīvāmi            lasuṇaṃ mayha bhojanaṃ .

Khāriyo pūrayitvāna         saṅghārāmaṃ agañchahaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      saṅghassa lasuṇaṃ adaṃ .

Vipassissa naraggassa         sāsane niratassahaṃ    
saṅghassa lasuṇaṃ datvā        kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      lasuṇaṃ yaṃ adaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         lasuṇānaṃ idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā lasuṇadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Lasuṇadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 128 -->
<a id="page-128"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 128]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ āyātadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (38)    
[40] nibbute lokanāthamhi        sikhimhi vadataṃ vare    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      avandiṃ thūpamuttamaṃ .

Vaḍḍhakīhi kathāpetvā        mūlaṃ datvānahaṃ tadā    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      āyātaṃ kārayesahaṃ [^128-1] .

Aṭṭhakappāni devesu        avokiṇṇaṃ [^128-2] vasiṃ ahaṃ    
avasesesu kappesu         vokiṇṇaṃ saṃsariṃ ahaṃ .

Kāye visaṃ na kamati         satthāni na ca hanti me    
udakehaṃ na mareyyāmi [^128-3]    āyātassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Yadicchāmi ahaṃ vassaṃ         mahāmegho pavassati    
devāpi me vasaṃ enti      puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattaratanasampanno          tiṃsakkhattuṃ ahosahaṃ    
na maṃ kecāvajānanti        puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      āyātaṃ yaṃ akārayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         āyātassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āyātadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Āyātadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^128-1] ma. Yu. Āyāgaṃ kārapesahaṃ . [^128-2] ma. Abbokiṇṇaṃ . Yu. Abbocchinnaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 129 -->
<a id="page-129"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 129]*

Navamaṃ dhammacakkikattherāpadānaṃ (39)    
[41] siddhatthassa bhagavato         sīhāsanassa sammukhā    
dhammacakkaṃ mayā ṭhapitaṃ        sukataṃ viññuvaṇṇitaṃ .

Catuvaṇṇāya [^129-1] sobhāmi      sayoggabalavāhano    
parivārenti maṃ niccaṃ        anuyantā bahū janā .

Saṭṭhīturiyasahassehi          parivāremahaṃ sadā    
parivārena sobhāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ cakkaṃ ṭhapayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhammacakkassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ekādase kappe      aṭṭhāsiṃsu janādhipā    
sahassarājā nāmena        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammacakkiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .้    
dhammacakkikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ kapparukkhiyattherāpadānaṃ (40)    
[42] siddhatthassa bhagavato         thūpaseṭṭhassa sammukhā    
vicittadusse laggetvā [^129-2]   kapparukkhaṃ ṭhapesahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 130 -->
<a id="page-130"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 130]*

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sobhayanto mama dvāre      kapparukkho patiṭṭhati .

Ahañca parisā ceva         ye keci samavassikā [^130-1]    
tamhā dussaṃ gahetvāna      nivāsema mayaṃ tadā [^130-2] .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ rukkhaṃ ṭhapayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kapparukkhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca sattame kappe      sucelā aṭṭha khattiyā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kapparukkhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kapparukkhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kuṇḍasāgatakaccānā         udāyī rājasavhayo [^130-3]    
adhimutto lasuṇado          āyātī [^130-4] dhammacakkiko    
kapparukkhī ca dasamo         gāthādvayaṃ dasasataṃpica .

Kuṇḍadhānavaggo catuttho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 131 -->
<a id="page-131"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 131]*

Pañcamo upālivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ upālittherāpadānaṃ (41)    
[43] khīṇāsavasahassehi           parivuto lokanāyako    
vivekamanuyutto so         gacchate paṭisallituṃ .

Ajinena nivatthohaṃ          tidaṇḍaparicāraṇo    
bhikkhusaṅghaparibyuḷhaṃ          addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         sīse katvāna añjaliṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Yathāṇḍajā ca saṃsedā       opapātī jalambujā    
kākādipakkhino sabbe       antalikkhe sadā carā [^131-1] .

Ye keci pāṇabhūtatthi        saññino vā asaññino    
sabbe te tava ñāṇamhi      anto honti samogadhā .

Gandhā ca pabbateyyā ye    himavante naguttame    
sabbe te tava sīlamhi       kalāyopi na yujjare .

Mohandhakārapakkhanno [^131-2]     ayaṃ loko sadevako    
tava ñāṇamhi jotante       andhakārā vidhaṃsitā .

Yathā atthaṅgate suriye      honti sattā tamogatā    
evaṃ buddhe anuppanne      hoti loko tamogato .

Yathodayanto ādicco       vinodeti tamaṃ sadā    
tatheva tvaṃ buddhaseṭṭha       viddhaṃsesi tamaṃ sadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 132 -->
<a id="page-132"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 132]*

Padhānaṃ pahitattosi          buddho loke sadevake    
tava kammābhiraddhena         tosesi janataṃ bahuṃ .

Taṃ sutvā [^132-1] anumoditvā    padumuttaro mahāmuni    
nabhe abbhuggami dhīro        haṃsarājāva ambare .

Abbhuggantvāna sambuddho     mahesi padumuttaro    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yenidaṃ thavitaṃ ñāṇaṃ          upamehi samāyutaṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Aṭṭhārasañca khattuṃ so       devarājā bhavissati    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ tisataṃ         vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Pañcavīsatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tusitāhi cavitvāna          sukkamūlena codito    
hīnova jātiyā santo       upāli nāma hessati .

So ca pacchā pabbajitvā     virājitvāna pāpakaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Tuṭṭho ca gotamo buddho     sakyaputto mahāyasotha    
vinayādhigataṃ tassa           etadagge ṭhapessati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 133 -->
<a id="page-133"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 133]*

saddhāyahaṃ pabbajito         katakicco anāsavo    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Bhagavā cānukampī maṃ         vinayehaṃ visārado    
sakakammābhiraddho ca         viharāmi anāsavo .

Saṃvuto pāṭimokkhamhi        indriyesu ca pañcasu    
dhāremi vinayaṃ sabbaṃ         kevalaṃ ratanākaraṃ .

Mamañca guṇamaññāya          satthā loke anuttaro    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāli thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upālittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ soṇakoṭiyavessattherāpadānaṃ (42)    
[44] anomadassissa munino        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
sudhāya lepanaṃ katvā        caṅkamaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ .

Nānāvaṇṇehi pupphehi       caṅkamaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ    
ākāse vitānaṃ katvā      bhojayiṃ buddhamuttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 134 -->
<a id="page-134"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 134]*

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        abhivādetvāna subbataṃ    
dīghasālaṃ bhagavato           niyyādesimahantadā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
paṭiggahesi bhagavā          anukampāya cakkhumā .

Paṭiggahetvāna sambuddho     dakkhiṇeyyo sadevake    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so haṭṭhena cittena     dīghasālaṃ adāsi me    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Imassa maccukālamhi         puññakammasamaṅgino    
sahassayuttassaratho          upaṭṭhissati tāvade .

Tena yānenayaṃ poso       devalokaṃ gamissati    
anumodissare devā        sampatte kusale bhave .

Mahārahaṃ byamhaṃ seṭṭhaṃ       ratanamattikalepanaṃ    
kūṭāgāravarūpetaṃ           byamhaṃ ajjhāvasissati .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
pañcavīsatikappāni           devarājā bhavissati .

Sattasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti bhavissati    
yasodharasanāmā te         sabbepi ekanāmakā .

Dve sampattī anubhotvā     cinitvā [^134-1] puññasañcayaṃ    
aṭṭhavīsatikappamhi           cakkavatti bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 135 -->
<a id="page-135"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 135]*

Tatthāpi byamhaṃ pavaraṃ        visukammena māpitaṃ    
dasasaddāvivittantaṃ          puraṃ ajjhāvasissati .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     bhūmipālo mahiddhiko    
okkāko nāma nāmena     rājā raṭṭhe bhavissati .

Soḷasitthisahassānaṃ          sabbāsaṃ pavarāvayā [^135-1]    
abhijātā khattiyānī         nava putte janissati .

Nava putte janitvāna        khattiyānī marissati    
taruṇī ca piyā kaññā        mahesittaṃ karissati .

Okkākaṃ tosayitvāna       varaṃ kaññā labhissati    
varaṃ laddhā ca sā kaññā     putte pabbājayissati .

Pabbājitā [^135-2] ca te sabbe  gamissanti naguttamaṃ    
jātibhedabhayā sabbe        bhaginīhi vasissare [^135-3] .

Ekā ca kaññā byādhīhi     bhavissati purakkhatā [^135-4]    
mā no jāti pabhijjati       nikhaṇissanti khattiyā .

Khattiyo nīharitvāna         tāya saddhiṃ vasissati    
bhavissati tadā bhedo        okkākakulasambhavo .

Tesaṃ pajā bhavissanti        koliyā nāma jātiyā    
tattha mānusakaṃ bhogaṃ         anubhossantinappakaṃ .

Tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      devalokaṃ gamissati    
tatrāpi pavaraṃ byamhaṃ        labhissati manoramaṃ .

#[^135-1] ma. Pavarā ca sā . Yu. Pavarā mayā . [^135-2] yu. Pabbajitvā . [^135-3] yu. Saṃvasissare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 136 -->
<a id="page-136"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 136]*

Devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
āgantvāna manussattaṃ       soṇo nāma bhavissati .

Āraddhaviriyo pahitatto      padahaṃ satthu sāsane    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Anantadassī bhagavā          gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
visesaññū mahāvīro         aggaṭṭhāne ṭhapessati .

Vuṭṭhamhi deve caturaṅgulamhi    
tiṇe anileritaaṅgulamhi [^136-1]    
ṭhapetvāna yogassa payuttatādino    
tatottari pāramatā na vijjati .

Uttame damathe danto       cittaṃ me suppaṇīhitaṃ    
bhāro me ohito sabbo    nibbutomhi anāsavo .

Aṅgīraso mahānāgo        abhijātova kesarī    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā soṇo koṭiyavesso [^136-2] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Soṇakoṭiyavessattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 137 -->
<a id="page-137"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 137]*

Tatiyaṃ bhaddiyakāḷigodhāyaputtattherāpadānaṃ (43)    
[45] padumuttarasambuddhaṃ           mettacittaṃ mahāmuniṃ    
upeti janatā sabbā        sabbalokagganāyakaṃ .

Sattukañca pavākañca [^137-1]      āmisampānabhojanaṃ    
dadanti satthuno sabbe       puññakkhette anuttare .

Ahampi dānaṃ dassāmi        devadevassa tādino    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ nimantetvā      saṅghampi ca anuttaraṃ .

Uyyojitā mayā cete      nimantesuṃ tathāgataṃ    
kevalaṃ bhikkhusaṅghañca         puññakkhettaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Satasahassapallaṅkaṃ           sovaṇṇaṃ gonakatthataṃ(    
tulikāpaṭalikāya            khomakappāsikehi ca .

Mahārahaṃ paññāpayiṃ          āsanaṃ buddhayuttakaṃ    
padumuttaro lokavidū         devadevo narāsabho .

Bhikkhusaṅghaparibyuḷho         mama dvāraṃ upāgami    
paccuggantvāna sambuddhaṃ      lokanāthaṃ yasassinaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         atināmayi sagharaṃ [^137-2]    
bhikkhūnaṃ satasahassaṃ           buddhañca lokanāyakaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         paramannena tappayiṃ    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 138 -->
<a id="page-138"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 138]*

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha    
yenidaṃ āsanaṃ dinnaṃ         sovaṇṇaṃ gonakatthataṃ .

Tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato    
catusattatikkhattuṃ so         devarajjaṃ karissati .

Anubhossati sampattiṃ         accharāhi purakkhato    
padesarajjaṃ sahassaṃ          vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Ekapaññāsakkhattuñca        cakkavatti bhavissati    
sabbāsu bhavayonīsu          uccākulī bhavissati .

So ca pacchā pabbajitvā     sukkamūlena codito    
bhaddiyo nāma nāmena       hessati satthu sāvako .

Vivekamanuyuttomhi          pantasenanivāsahaṃ    
phalaṃ vādhigataṃ [^138-1] sabbaṃ       cittaklesomhi ajjahaṃ .

Mama sabbaṃ abhiññāya         sabbaññū lokanāyako    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhaddiyo kāḷigodhāyaputto thero imā    
Gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Bhaddiyassa kāḷigodhāyaputtattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 139 -->
<a id="page-139"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 139]*

Catutthaṃ sanniṭṭhāpakattherāpadānaṃ (44)    
[46] aññe kuṭikaṃ katvā         vasāmi pabbatantare    
lābhālābhena santuṭṭho      yasena ayasena ca .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusatasahassehi [^139-1]        āgañchi mama santike .

Upāgataṃ mahānāgaṃ [^139-2]       jalajuttamanāmakaṃ    
tiṇasantharaṃ paññāpetvā      adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         āmaṇḍaṃ pāniyañcahaṃ    
adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa           vippasannena cetasā .

Kappe ito satasahasse      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         āmaṇḍassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekatāḷīsakappamhi          eko āsi arindamo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sanniṭṭhāpako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sanniṭṭhāpakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 140 -->
<a id="page-140"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 140]*

Pañcamaṃ pañcahatthiyattherāpadānaṃ (45)    
[47] sumedho nāma sambuddho      gacchate antarāpaṇe    
okkhittacakkhu mitabhāṇī       satimā saṃvutindriyo .

Pañca uppalahatthāni         āvelatthamakāsu [^140-1] me    
tena buddhaṃ apūjesiṃ         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Āropitā ca te pupphā     chadanamassa satthuno    
samādhīsu [^140-2] mahānāgaṃ       sisso [^140-3] ācariyaṃ yathā .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito vīsakappasate          ahesuṃ pañca khattiyā    
hatthiyā nāma nāmena       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañcahatthiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pañcahatthiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ padumacchadaniyattherāpadānaṃ (46)    
[48] nibbute lokanāthamhi        vipassimhaggapuggale    
suphullaṃ padumaṃ gayha          citamāropayiṃ  ahaṃ .

#[^140-1] ma. Āvelatthaṃ ahaṃsu me . Yu. Āvelatthaṃ akāsi me . [^140-2] ma. Samādhiṃ su .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 141 -->
<a id="page-141"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 141]*

Āropiteva [^141-1] citake      vehāsaṃ nabhamuggami    
ākāsacchadanaṃ katvā        citakamhi adhārayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattatāḷīsito kappe        padumissaranāmako    
cāturanto vijitāvī         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumacchadaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumacchadaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ  sayanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (47)    
[49] siddhatthassa bhagavato         mettacittassa tādino    
sayanaggaṃ mayā dinnaṃ         dussabhaṇḍena [^141-2] atthataṃ .

Paṭiggahesi bhagavā          kappiyasayanāsanaṃ    
uṭṭhāya sayanā tamhā       vehāsaṃ uggamī jino .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ sayanamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 142 -->
<a id="page-142"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 142]*

Ekapaññāsito kappe       varuṇo [^142-1] devasavhayo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sayanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ caṅkamadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (48)    
[50] atthadassissa munino         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
iṭṭhakāhi cinitvāna         caṅkamaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ .

Uccato pañcaratanaṃ          caṅkamaṃ sādhu māpitaṃ    
āyāmato hatthasataṃ         bhāvaneyyaṃ manoramaṃ .

Paṭiggahesi bhagavā          atthadassī naruttamo    
hatthena puḷinaṃ gayha         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Iminā puḷinadānena         caṅkamaṃ sukatena ca    
sattaratanasampannaṃ           puḷinaṃ anubhossati .

Tīṇi kappāni devesu        devarajjaṃ karissati    
anubhossati sampattiṃ         accharāhi purakkhato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 143 -->
<a id="page-143"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 143]*

manussalokaṃ āgantvā       rājā raṭṭhe bhavissati    
tikkhattuṃ cakkavattī ca        mahiyā so bhavissati .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         caṅkamassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā caṅkamadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Caṅkamadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ subhaddattherāpadānaṃ (49)    
[51] padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
janataṃ uddharitvāna          nibbāyati mahāyaso .

Nibbāyante ca sambuddhe     dasasahassi kampatha    
janakāyo mahā āsi        devā sannipatuṃ tadā .

Candanaṃ pūrayitvāna          tagarāmallikāhi ca    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      āropesiṃ naruttamaṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
nipannakova sambuddho        imā gāthā abhāsatha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 144 -->
<a id="page-144"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 144]*

Yo me pacchimake kāle     gandhamallena chādayi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ito cuto ayaṃ poso       tusitakāyaṃ gamissati    
tattha rajjaṃ karitvāna        nimmānaṃ so gamissati .
