Eteneva upāyena        datvā mallaṃ [^144-1] varuttamaṃ    
sakakammābhiraddho so        sampattiṃ anubhossati .

Punāpi [^144-2] tusite kāye     nibbattissatiyaṃ naro    
tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      manussattaṃ gamissati .

Sakyaputto mahānāgo       aggo loke sadevake    
bodhayitvā bahū satte       nibbāyissati cakkhumā .

Tadā sopagato santo [^144-3]    sukkamūlena codito    
upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ         pañhaṃ pucchissati tadā .

Hāsayitvāna sambuddho       sabbaññū lokanāyako    
pubbakammamabhiññāya          saccāni vivarissati .

Āraddho ca ayaṃ pañho      tuṭṭho ekaggamānaso    
satthāraṃ abhivādetvā       pabbajjaṃ yācayissati .

Pasannamānasaṃ disvā         sakakammena tositaṃ    
pabbajissati so buddho       aggadhammassa kovido .

Vāyamitvānayaṃ poso        sammā sambuddhasāsane    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Pañcamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 145 -->
<a id="page-145"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 145]*

Pubbakammena saṃyutto        ekaggo susamāhito    
buddhassa oraso putto      dhammatomhi sunimmito .

Dhammarājaṃ upāgamma         āpucchiṃ pañhamuttamaṃ    
kathayanto ca me pañhaṃ       dhammasotaṃ upānayi .

Tassāhaṃ dhammamaññāya        vihāsiṃ sāsane rato    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Satasahasse ito kappe      jalajuttamanāyako    
nibbāyi anupādāno        dīpova telasaṅkhayā .

Sattayojanikaṃ āsi          thūpañca ratanāmayaṃ    
dhajaṃ tattha apūjesiṃ          sabbabhaddaṃ manoramaṃ .

Kassapassa ca buddhassa        tisso nāmaggasāvako    
putto me oraso āsi     dāyādo jinasāsane .

Tassa hīnena manasā         vācaṃ bhāsiṃ abhaddakaṃ    
tena kammavipākena         pacchā me āsi bhaddakaṃ [^145-1] .

Upavattane sālavane        pacchime sayane muni    
pabbājesi mahāvīro        hito kāruṇiko jino .

Ajjeva dāni pabbajjā      ajjeva upasampadā    
ajjeva parinibbānaṃ         sammukhā dipaduttame .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 146 -->
<a id="page-146"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 146]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā subhaddo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Subhaddattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ cundattherāpadānaṃ (50)    
[52] siddhatthassa bhagavato         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
agghiyaṃ kārayitvāna         jātipupphehi chādayiṃ .

Niṭṭhāpetvāna taṃ pupphaṃ      buddhassa abhināmayiṃ    
pupphāvasesaṃ paggayha        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          buddhaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         pupphagghiyamupānayiṃ .

Vitiṇṇakaṅkho sambuddho       tiṇṇoghehi purakkhato    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Dibbagandhaṃ pavāyantaṃ         yo me pupphagghiyaṃ adā    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ito cuto ayaṃ poso       devasaṅghapurakkhato    
jātipupphehi parikiṇṇo       devalokaṃ gamissati .

Ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ tassa         sovaṇṇañca maṇīmayaṃ    
byamhā pātubhavissanti       puññakammapabhāvitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 147 -->
<a id="page-147"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 147]*

Catusattatikkhattuṃ so         devarajjaṃ karissati    
anubhossati sampattiṃ         accharāhi purakkhato .

Paṭhabyā rajjaṃ tisataṃ         vasudhaṃ āvasissati    
pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti bhavissati .

Dujjayo nāma nāmena       hessati manujādhipo    
anubhotvāna taṃ puññaṃ        sakakammūpasaṃhito .

Vinipātaṃ agantvāna         manussattaṃ gamissati    
hiraññaṃ tassa nicitaṃ          koṭisataṃpi anappakaṃ .

Nibbattissati yonimhi        brāhmaṇo so bhavissati    
vaṅgantassa suto dhīmā       sāriyā oraso piyo .

So ca pacchā pabbajitvā     aṅgīrasassa sāsane    
cūḷacundoti nāmena         hessati satthu sāvako .

Sāmaṇerova so santo      khīṇāsavo bhavissati    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Upaṭṭhahiṃ mahāvīraṃ           aññe ca pesale bahū    
bhātaramevupaṭṭhāsiṃ          uttamatthassa pattiyā .

Bhātaraṃ me upaṭṭhahitvā      dhātuṃ pattamhi ociya [^147-1]    
sambuddhaṃ upanāmesiṃ         lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        buddho loke sadevake    
sandassayanto taṃ dhātuṃ       kittayi aggasāvakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 148 -->
<a id="page-148"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 148]*

Cittañca suvimuttaṃ me        saddhā mayhaṃ patiṭṭhitā    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cundatthero imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Cundattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
upāli soṇo bhaddī ca       sanniṭṭhāpaka hatthiyo    
chadanaṃ seyyacaṅkamaṃ          subhaddo cundasavhayo    
gāthāsataṃ ca tāḷīsaṃ [^148-1]      catasso ca tatthuttari .

Upālivaggo pañcamo .

chaṭṭho vījanīvaggo    
paṭhamaṃ vidhūpanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (51)    
[53] padumuttarabuddhassa           lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
vījanekā [^148-2] mayā dinnā    dipadindassa tādino .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivāditvā        pakkāmi [^148-3] uttarāmukho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 149]*

vījaniṃ paggahetvāna         satthā lokagganāyako    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito santo      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Iminā vījanidānena         cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          vinipātaṃ na gacchati .

Āraddhaviriyo pahitatto      cetoguṇasamāhito    
jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Saṭṭhikappasahassamhi          bijjamānasanāmakā    
soḷasāsiṃsu rājāno        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vidhūpanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vidhūpanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

dutiyaṃ sataraṃsiyattherāpadānaṃ (52)    
[54] ubbiddhaṃ selamāruyha        nisīdi purisuttamo    
pabbatassāvidūramhi          brāhmaṇo mantapāragū .

Upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāvīraṃ           devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 150]*

Esa buddho mahāvīro       varadhammappakāsako    
jalati aggikkhandhova         bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato .

Mahāsamuddovakkhobbho       aṇṇavova duruttaro    
migarājāvasambhīto          dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         padumuttaranāmako [^150-1]    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yenāyaṃ añjali dinno       buddhaseṭṭho ca thomito    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           devarajjaṃ karissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           aṅgīrasasanāmako    
vivaṭṭacchado sambuddho       uppajjissati tāvade .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sataraṃsīti nāmena           arahā so bhavissati .

Jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
sataraṃsimhi nāmena          pabhā niddhāvate mama .

Maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā       jhāyī jhānarato ahaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Saṭṭhikappasahassamhi          caturo romanāmakā [^150-2]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 151]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sataraṃsiyo [^151-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sataraṃsiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ sayanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (53)    
[55] padumuttarabuddhassa           sabbalokānukampino    
sayanaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ         vippasannena cetasā .

Tena sayanadānena          sukhette  vījasampadā    
bhogo nibbattate tassa [^151-2]   sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ākāse seyyaṃ kappemi     dhāremi paṭhaviṃ imaṃ    
pāṇesu me issariyaṃ        sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcakappasahassamhi          aṭṭha āsuṃ mahāvirā [^151-3]    
catuttiṃse kappasate         caturova mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sayanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 152]*

Catutthaṃ gandhodakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (54)    
[56] padumuttarabuddhassa           mahābodhimaho ahu    
vicittaṃ ghaṭamādāya          gandhodakamadāsahaṃ .

Nhānakāle ca bodhiyā      mahāmegho pavassatha    
ninnādo ca mahā āsi      asaniyā phalantiyā .

Tenavāsanivegena          tattha kālakato ahaṃ    
devaloke ṭhito santo      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Aho buddho aho dhammo     aho no satthusampadā    
kaḷevaraṃ me patitaṃ          devaloke ramāmahaṃ .

Ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         sattabhummaṃ samuggataṃ    
kaññāsatasahassāni          parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Ābādhā me na vijjanti     soko mayhaṃ na vijjati    
pariḷāhaṃ na passāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhavīse kappasate         rājā saṃvasito ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhodakadāyako [^152-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhodakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 153]*

Pañcamaṃ opavuyhattharāpadānaṃ (55)    
[57] padumuttarabuddhassa           ājānīyaṃ adāsahaṃ    
niyyādetvāna sambuddhe     agamāsiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Devilo nāma nāmena       satthuno aggasāvako    
varadhammassa dāyādo        āgañchi mama santikaṃ .

Sabbatthahāro [^153-1] bhagavā     ājāneyyo na kampati    
tava saṅkappamaññāya         adhivāsesi cakkhumā .

Agghāpetvā vātajavaṃ       sindhavaṃ sīghavāhanaṃ    
padumuttarabuddhassa           khamanīyaṃ [^153-2] adāsahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ [^153-3]    
ājānīyā vātajavā        vitti nibbattare mama [^153-4] .

Lābhā tesaṃ suladdhaṃ vā      ye labhantupasampadaṃ    
punappayirupāseyyaṃ          buddho loke sace bhave .

Aṭṭhavīsatikkhattāhaṃ          rājā āsiṃ mahabbalo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         jambūdīpassa [^153-5] issaro .

Imaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Catuttiṃse sahassamhi         mahātejosi khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

#[^153-1] ma. Yu. Sapattabhāro . [^153-2] yu. Ajānīyaṃ . [^153-3] po. Deve ca mānuse bhave . Yu. Bhavane sabbadā  
#mama . [^153-4] ma. Khamanīyaṃ vātajavaṃ cittaṃ nibbattate mama . Yu. Khamanīyā vātajavā citte  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 154]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā opavuyho thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Opavuyhattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ saparivārāsanattherāpadānaṃ (56)    
[58] padumuttarabuddhassa           piṇḍapātaṃ adāsahaṃ    
gantvā kiliṭṭhakaṭṭhānaṃ [^154-1]    mallikāhi parikkhitaṃ .

Tamhāsanamhi āsīno        buddho lokagganāyako    
akittayi piṇḍapātaṃ          ujubhūto samāhito .

Yathāpi bhaddake khette      bījaṃ appampi ropitaṃ    
sammā dhāraṃ pavecchanto     phalaṃ toseti kassakaṃ .

Tathevāyaṃ piṇḍapāto        sukhette ropito tayā    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        phalante [^154-2] tosayissati .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       jalajuttamanāmako    
piṇḍapātaṃ gahetvāna        pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Saṃvuto pāṭimokkhasmiṃ        indriyesu ca pañcasu    
pavivekamanuyutto           viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 155]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saparivārāsano thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saparivārāsanattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ pañcadīpakattherāpadānaṃ (57)    
[59] padumuttarabuddhassa           sabbabhūtānukampino    
susaddahitvā [^155-1] saddhamme    ujudiṭṭhi ahosahaṃ .

Padīpadānapādāsiṃ           parivāritvāna bodhiyaṃ    
saddahanto padīpāni         akariṃ tāvadevahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
ākāse ukkaṃ dhārenti     dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tirokuṭaṃ [^155-2] tiroselaṃ       samatiggayha pabbataṃ    
samantā yojanasataṃ          dassanaṃ anubhomahaṃ .

Tena kammāvasesena        pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        dipadindassa sāsane .

Catuttiṃse kappasate         satacakkhusanāmakā    
rājāhesuṃ mahātejā       cakkavattī mahabbalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 156]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañcadīpako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pañcadīpakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ dhajadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (58)    
[60] padumuttarabuddhassa           bodhiyā pādaputtamev    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      dhajamāropayiṃ ahaṃ .

Patapattāni gaṇhitvā        bahiddhā chaḍḍayiṃ ahaṃ    
antosuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ         suvimuttaṃ anāsavaṃ [^156-1] .

Sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ        avandiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha    
iminā dhajadānena          upaṭṭhānena cūbhayaṃ .

Kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ so na gacchati    
devesu devasobhagyaṃ        anubhossatyanappakaṃ .

Anekasatakkhattuñca          rājā raṭṭhe bhavissati    
uggato nāma nāmena       cakkavatti bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 157]*

Sampattiṃ anubhotvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsanebhiramissati .

Padhānaṃ pahitattomhi         upasanto nirūpadhi    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Ekapaññāsasahasse         kappe uggatasavhayā [^157-1]    
paññāsasahasse camuno       khattiyā khemasavhayā [^157-2] .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhajadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhajadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ padumattherāpadānaṃ (59)    
[61] catusaccaṃ pakāsento        varadhammaṃ pavattako [^157-3]    
vassate amataṃ vuṭṭhiṃ         nibbāpento mahājanaṃ .

Sadhajaṃ padumaṃ gayha           aḍḍhakose ṭhito ahaṃ    
padumuttaramunissa            haṭṭho [^157-5] ukkhipimambare .

Āgacchante ca padume       abbhūto āsi tāvade    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         paggaṇhi vadataṃ varo .

#[^157-1] ma. Yu. Uggatasavhayo . [^157-2] ma. Yu. Paññāsasatasahasse khattiyo meghasavhayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 158]*

Karaseṭṭhena  paggayha       jalajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yenidaṃ padumaṃ khittaṃ          sabbaññuttamanāyake [^158-1]    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsakappāni devindo        devarajjaṃ karissati    
paṭhabyā rajjaṃ sattasataṃ       vasudhaṃ āvasissati .

Tattha sattaṃ [^158-2] gahetvāna    cakkavatti bhavissati    
ākāsato pupphavuṭṭhi        abhivassissatī tadā .

Kappe satasahassamhi         okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .

Nikkhamitvāna kucchimhā       sampajāno paṭissato    
jātiyā pañcavassohaṃ        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 159]*

Dasamaṃ asanabodhiyattherāpadānaṃ (60)fa    
[62] jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ .

Tissassāhaṃ bhagavato         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      ropayiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Asano nāmadheyyena        dharaṇīrūhapādapo    
pañca vasse paricariṃ         asanaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Pupphitaṃ pādapaṃ disvā        abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
sakaṃ kammaṃ pakittento       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Tisso tadā so [^159-1] sambuddho sayambhū aggapuggalo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yenāyaṃ ropitā bodhi       buddhapūjā ca sakkatā    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsakappāni devesu         devarajjaṃ karissati    
catussaṭṭhiñca khattuṃ so       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Tusitāhi caritvāna          sukkamūlena codito    
dve sampattī anubhotvā     manussatte ramissati .

Padhānaṃ pahitatto so        upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatyanāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 160]*

Vivekamanuyuttohaṃ           upasanto nirūpadhi    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Dvenavute ito kappe      bodhiṃ ropesahaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhiropassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catussattatito kappe        daṇḍasenoti vissuto    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti tadā ahu .

Tesattati ito kappe       sattāhesuṃ mahīpatī    
samantanemi nāmena         rājāno cakkavattino .

Puṇṇavīsatito kappe         puṇṇako nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā asanabodhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Asanabodhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
vījanī sataraṃsī ca            sayanodaki vāhiso [^160-1]    
parivāro padīpañca          dhajo padumapūjako    
bodhi ca dasamo vutto       gāthā dvenavutī tathā .

Vījanīvaggo chaṭṭho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 161]*

Sattamo sakacittaniyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sakacittaniyattherāpadānaṃ (61)    
[63] pavanaṃ kānanaṃ disvā         appasaddaṃ anāvilaṃ    
isīnaṃ anuciṇṇaṃva            āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .

Thūpaṃ katvāna veḷinaṃ [^161-1]      nānāpupphaṃ samokiriṃ    
sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ        nimmitaṃ abhivandahaṃ .

Sattaratanasampanno          rājā raṭṭhasmi issaro    
sakakammābhiraddhohaṃ          pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ [^161-2] phalaṃ .

Asītikappenantayaso         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
sattaratanasampanno          catudīpamhi issaro .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakacittaniyo [^161-3] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sakacittaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ āpopupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (62)    
[64] vihārā abhinikkhamma         abbhuṭṭhāsi ca caṅkame [^161-4]    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento        desento amataṃ [^161-5] padaṃ .

#[^161-1] ma. Pulinaṃ . Yu. Veḷūnaṃ . [^161-2] ma. Pupphāpūjāyidaṃ . Yu. Thūpapūjāyidaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 162]*

Sikhissa giramaññāya          buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino    
nānāpupphaṃ gahetvāna       ākāsamhi samokiriṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito vīsatikappamhi          sumedho nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āpopupphiyo [^162-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Āpopupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ paccāgamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (63)    
[65] sindhuyā nadiyā tīre        cakkavāko ahaṃ tadā    
suddhasevālabhakkhohaṃ         pāṇesu ca susaññato .

Addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          gacchantaṃ anilañjase    
tuṇḍena sālaṃ paggayha       vipassissābhiropayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 163]*

yassa saddhā tathāgate       acalā supatiṭṭhitā    
tena cittappasādena        duggatiṃ so na gacchati .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       buddhaseṭṭhassa santike    
vihaṅgamena santena         subījaṃ ropitaṃ mayā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sucārudassanā nāma         aṭṭhete ekanāmakā    
kappe sattarase āsuṃ       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paccāgamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paccāgamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ parappasādakattherāpadānaṃ 6[^163-4]    
[66] asubhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ            mahesiṃ vijitāvinaṃ    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         ko disvā nappasīdati .

Himavā vāparimeyyo        sāgarova duruttaro    
tatheva jhānaṃ buddhassa        ko disvā nappasīdati .

Vasudhā yathāppameyyā       cittā vanavaṭaṃsakā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 164]*

Tatheva sīlaṃ buddhassa         ko disvā nappasīdati .

Anilañjasāsaṃkhubbho          yathākāso asaṅkhayo    
tatheva ñāṇaṃ buddhassa        ko disvā nappasīdati .

Imāhi catūhi gāthāhi        brāhmaṇo sonaavhayo [^164-1]    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ thavitvāna        siddhatthamaparājitaṃ .

Catunavutikappāni            duggatiṃ nupapajjatha    
sugatīsu susampattiṃ [^164-2]        anubhosiṃ anappakaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       thavitvā lokanāyakaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thomanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Catuddasamhi kappamhi         caturo āsumuggatā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā parappasādako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Parappasādakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ bhisadāyakattherāpadānaṃ 6[^164-5]    
[67] vessabhū nāma nāmena       isīnaṃ tatiyo ahu    
kānanaṃ vanamoggayha         vihāsi purisuttamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 165]*

Bhisamūḷālaṃ gaṇhitvā         agamiṃ buddhasantike    
tañca buddhassa adāsiṃ        pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Karena ca parāmaṭṭho        vessabhū varabuddhinā    
sukhāhaṃ nābhijānāmi         samantena kututtariṃ .

Carimo vattate mayhaṃ        bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo [^165-1] .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Samodhānā ca rājāno      soḷasa manujādhipā    
kappamhi terase [^165-2] āsuṃ    cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhisadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ sucintitattherāpadānaṃ (66)    
[68] giriduggacaro āsiṃ          abhijātova kesarī    
migasaṅghaṃ vadhitvāna          jīvāmi pabbatantare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 166]*

Atthadassī tu bhagavā         sabbaññū vadataṃ varo    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi pabbatuttamaṃ .

Pasadañca migaṃ hantvā        bhakkhetuṃ samupāgamiṃ    
bhagavā tamhi samaye         bhikkhācāro [^166-1] upāgami .

Varamaṃsāni paggayha          adāsi tassa satthuno    
anumodi mahāvīro          nibbāpento mamaṃ tadā .

Tena cittappasādena        giriduggappavisahaṃ [^166-2]    
pītiṃ uppādayitvāna         tattha kālakato ahaṃ .

Etena maṃsadānena         cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
paṇṇarase kappasate         devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ .

Avasesesu kappesu         kusalaṃ karitaṃ [^166-3] mayā    
teneva maṃsadānena         buddhānussaraṇena ca .

Aṭṭhatiṃsamhi kappamhi         aṭṭha dīghāyunāmakā    
saṭṭhimhito kappasate        duve saraṇanāmakā [^166-4] .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sucintito thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sucintitattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^166-1] ma. Bhikkhamāno . Yu. Bhikkhayāno . [^166-2] ma. Giriduggaṃ pavisaṃ ahaṃ . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 167]*

Sattamaṃ vatthadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (67)    
[69] pakkhijāto tadā āsiṃ       supaṇṇo garuḷādhipo    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          gacchantaṃ gandhamādanaṃ .

Jahitvā garuḷavaṇṇaṃ          māṇavattaṃ arādhayiṃ [^167-1]    
ekavatthaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        dipadindassa tādino .

Tañca dussaṃ paṭiggayha        buddho lokagganāyako    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Iminā vatthadānena         cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
pahāya garuḷayoniṃ           devaloke ramissati .

Atthadassī tu bhagavā         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
vatthadānaṃ pasaṃsitvā         pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Bhave nibbattamānamhi        honti me vatthasampadā    
ākāse chadanaṃ hoti        vatthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aruṇasā [^167-2] satta janā      cakkavattī mahabbalā    
chattiṃsatimhi āsiṃsu          kappamhi manujādhipā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vatthadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vatthadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 168]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ ambadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (68)    
[70] anomadassī bhagavā          nisinno pabbatantare    
mettāya aphari loke       appamāṇe nirūpadhi .

Kapi ahaṃ tadā āsiṃ         himavante naguttame    
disvā anomamadhikaṃ [^168-1]       buddhe cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Avidūre himavantassa         ambāsuṃ phalino tadā    
tato pakkaṃ gahetvāna       ambaṃ samadhukaṃ adaṃ .

Tamme buddho viyākāsi      anomadassī mahāmuni    
iminā madhudānena          ambapānena cūbhayaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappāni         devaloke ramissati    
avasesesu kappesu         vokiṇṇaṃ saṃsarissati .

Khepetvā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ      paripakkāya vuddhiyā [^168-2]    
vinipātādihāgantvā        kilese jhāpayissati .

Damena uttamenāhaṃ         damitomhi mahesinā    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Sattasattatikappasate         ambaṭṭhajayanāmakā [^168-3]    
catuddasā te rājāno      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^168-1] ma. Anomadassiṃ taṃ . Yu. Anomamamitaṃ . [^168-2] ma. Yu. Buddhiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 169]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ambadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sumanattherāpadānaṃ (69)    
[71] sumano nāma nāmena        mālākāro ahaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          lokāhutipaṭiggahaṃ [^169-1] .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        sumanaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Imāya pupphapūjāya          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chabbīsatimhi kappamhi         cattārassu mahāyasā    
sattaratanasampannā          rājāno cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumano thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumanattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 170]*

Dasamaṃ pupphacaṅkoṭiyattherāpadānaṃ (70)    
[72] abhītarūpaṃ sīhaṃva             garuḷaggaṃva pakkhinaṃ    
byagghasubhaṃva pavaraṃ           abhijātaṃva kesariṃ .

Sikhiṃ tilokasaraṇaṃ            aneñjaṃ aparājitaṃ    
nisinnaṃ maraṇānaggaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Caṅkoṭake ṭhapetvāna       anojaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
mahācaṅkoṭakeneva [^170-1]      buddhaseṭṭhaṃ samokiriṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        dipadinda narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sampuṇṇe tiṃsakappamhi        devabhūtisanāmakā    
sattaratanasampannā          pañcāsuṃ cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphacaṅkoṭiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphacaṅkoṭiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 171]*

Uddānaṃ    
sakacittaṃ atho pupphi         paccābhigamanena [^171-1] ca    
parappasādabhisado [^171-2]        sucinti vatthadāyako .

Ambadāyi ca sumano         pupphacaṅkoṭiyopica [^171-3]    
gāthekasattati vuttā        gaṇitā atthadassiti [^171-4] .

Sakacittaniyavaggo sattamo .

aṭṭhamo nāgasamālavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ nāgasamālattherāpadānaṃ (71)    
[73] āpāṭaliṃ ahaṃ pupphaṃ         ujjhitaṃ sumahāpathe    
thūpamhi abhiropesiṃ          sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       pupphiyo [^171-5] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgasamālo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nāgasamālattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^171-1] ma. Sakacintī avo pupphī sapaccāgamanena ca . [^171-2] ma. Parappasādībhisado .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 172]*

Dutiyaṃ padasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (72)    
[74] akkantañca padaṃ disvā       tissassādiccabandhuno    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      pade cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         padasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito sattamake kappe       sumedho nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ susaññakattherāpadānaṃ (73)    
[75] dumagge paṃsukūlikaṃ           laggaṃ disvāna satthuno    
tatohaṃ añjaliṃ katvā        paṃsukūlaṃ avandihaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paṃsusaññāyidaṃ [^172-1] phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 173]*

Ito catutthake kappe       dumaharo [^173-1] su khattiyo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā susaññako [^173-2] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Susaññakattherassa [^173-3] apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ bhisāluvadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (74)    
[76] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha         vasāmi pavane [^173-4] ahaṃ    
vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .

Bhisāluvañca pādāsiṃ         udakaṃ hatthadhovanaṃ    
vanditvā sirasā pāde      pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Ekanavute ito kappe      bhisāluvaṃ adaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca tatiye kappe       bhisasammatakhattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^173-1] ma. Dumasāro si . Yu. Dumasāro su . [^173-2] ma. Buddhasaññako . Yu. Saññako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 174]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisāluvadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhisāluvadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Pañcamaṃ ekasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (75)    
[77] khaṇḍo nāmāsi nāmena      vipassissaggasāvako    
ekā bhikkhā mayā dinnā    lokāhutipaṭiggaho [^174-1] .

Tena cittappasādena        dipadinda narāsabha    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ekabhikkhāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Cattāḷīse ito kappe      varuṇo nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ tiṇasanthāradāyakattherāpadānaṃ (76)    
[78] himavantassa avidūre         mahājātassaro ahu    
sattapattehi sañchanno       nānāsakuṇamālayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 175]*

Tamhi nhātvā ca pitvā ca   avidūre vasāmahaṃ    
addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ          gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
abbhato orohitvāna       bhūmiyaṃ ṭhāti [^175-1] tāvade .

Lāyanena [^175-2] tiṇaṃ gayha      nisīdanamadāsahaṃ    
nisīdi bhagavā tattha          tilokapati [^175-3] nāyako .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       avandiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
ukkuṭiko [^175-4] avasakkhi       nijjhāyanto mahāmuniṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        nimmānaṃ upapajjahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         santhārassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito dutiyake kappe        mittasammatakhattiyo [^175-5]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasanthāradāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇasanthāradāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ sucidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (77)    
[79] tiṃsakappasahassamhi           sambuddho lokanāyako    
sumedho nāma nāmena       dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇo .

#[^175-1] po. Ṭhito . Ma. Ṭhāsi. . Yu. Bhūmiyaṭaṭhāsi . [^175-2] ma. Yu. Visāṇena . [^175-3] ma. Tisso    
#lokagganāyako . Yu. Tisso lokavināyako . [^175-4] paṭikuṭiko apasakkiṃ . Yu. Avasakkiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 176]*

Tassa kañcanavaṇṇassa         dipadindassa tādino    
pañca sūcī mayā dinnā       sibbanatthāya cīvaraṃ .

Teneva sūcidānena         nipuṇatthavipassakaṃ    
tikkhalahuñca phāsuñca         ñāṇaṃ me upapajjatha .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Dipadādhipati nāma           rājāno caturo ahuṃ    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sūcidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sūcidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ pāṭalipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (78)    
[80] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Seṭṭhiputto tadā āsiṃ      sukhumālo sukhe ṭhito [^176-1]    
ucchaṅge pāṭalīpupphaṃ        katvā taṃ abhiropayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 177]*

Haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      pupphehi abhipūjayiṃ    
tissaṃ lokaviduṃ nāthaṃ         naradevaṃ namassihaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tesaṭṭhikappamhi        abhisammatanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭalipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pāṭalipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ ṭhitañjaliyattherāpadānaṃ (79)    
[81] migaluddho [^177-1] pure āsiṃ     araññe kānane ahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ        battiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Tatthāhaṃ añjaliṃ katvā       pakkāmiṃ pācināmukho    
avidūre nisinnassa          niyate paṇṇasanthare .

Tato me asanīpāto        matthake nipatī tadā    
sohaṃ maraṇakālamhi          akāsiṃ punarañjaliṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 178]*

Dvenavute ito kappe      añjaliṃ akariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         añjalissa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Catupaṇṇāsakappamhi          migaketusanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ṭhitañjaliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ṭhitañjaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ tīṇipadumiyattherāpadānaṃ (80)    
[82] padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
danto dantaparivuto         nagarā nikkhamī tadā .

Nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ māliko tadā    
yaṃ tattha uttamaṃ tīṇi         padumapupphāni aggahiṃ .

Addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          paṭimaggantarāpaṇe    
saha [^178-1] disvāna sambuddhaṃ     evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Kiṃ me imehi pupphehi       rañño upaṭṭhitehi me    
gāmaṃ vā gāmakhettaṃ vā     sahassaṃ vā labheyyahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 179]*

Adantadamakaṃ [^179-1] vīraṃ [^179-2]      sabbasattasukhāvahaṃ    
lokanāthaṃva pūjetvā        lacchāmi amatandhanaṃ .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
tīṇi lohitake gayha         ākāse ukkhipiṃ tadā .

Mayā ukkhittamattamhi        ākāse patthariṃsu te    
dhāriṃsu matthake tattha        uddhaṃvaṇṭā [^179-3] adhomukhā .

Ye keci manujā disvā      ukkuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayuṃ    
devatā antalikkhamhi        sādhukāraṃ pavattayuṃ .

Accheraṃ loke uppannaṃ      buddhaseṭṭhassa vāhasā    
sabbe dhammaṃ suṇissāma       pupphānaṃ vāhasā mayaṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
vīthiyañhi ṭhito santo        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so buddhaṃ apūjesi       rattapadumehi māṇavo    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
tiṃsakkhattuñca [^179-4] devindo    devarajjaṃ karissati .

Mahāvitthārikaṃ nāma         byamhaṃ hessati tāvade    
tiyojanasatubbiddhaṃ           diyaḍḍhasatavitthataṃ .

Cattāri satasahassāni        niyyuhā ca sumāpitā    
kūṭāgāravarūpetā          mahāsayanamaṇḍitā [^179-5] .

#[^179-1] ma. Adantadamanaṃ . [^179-2] yu. Dhīraṃ . [^179-3] po. Uddhavaṇḍā . [^179-4] ma. Yu. Tasakappāni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 180]*

Koṭisatasahassāyo          parivāressanti accharā    
kusalā naccagītassa          vādite [^180-1] ca padakkhiṇā .

Etādise byamhavare       nārīgaṇasamākule    
vassissati pupphavasso        dibbo lohitako tadā .

Bhittikhīle nāgadante        dvārabāhe ca toraṇe    
cakkamattā lohitakā        olambissanti tāvade .

Pattena pattasañchanne       anto byamhavare imaṃ    
attharitvā pārupitvā       tuvaṭṭissanti tāvade .

Bhavanaṃ parivāretvā         samantā satayojanaṃ [^180-2]    
te [^180-3] visuddhā lohitakā    dibbagandhaṃ pavāyare .

Pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sampattiyo duve bhutvā      anīti anupaddavo    
sampatte pariyosāne       nibbānaṃ pāpuṇissati .

Sudiṭṭho vata me buddho      vāṇijjamupayojitaṃ [^180-4]    
padumāni tīṇi pūjetvā       anubhosintisampadā .

Ajja me dhammapattassa       vippamuttassa sabbaso    
supupphitaṃ lohitakaṃ           dhārayissati matthake .

Mama kammaṃ kathentassa        padumuttarasatthuno    
satapāṇasahassānaṃ           dhammābhisamayo ahu .

#[^180-1] ma. Vāditepi . Yu. Vāditehi . [^180-2] ma. Satayojane . [^180-3] ma. Tepi padamā lohitakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 181]*

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ [^181-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tiṇṇaṃ padumānidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇipadumiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇipadumiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
nāgasamālo padasaññī        susaññāluvadāyako    
ekasaññī tiṇadado          sūcī pāṭalipupphiyo    
ṭhitañjalī tipadumī            gāthāyo pañcasattati .

Nāgasamālavaggo aṭṭhamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 182]*

Navamo timirapupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ timirapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (81)    
[83] candabhāgānadītīre          anusotaṃ vajāmahaṃ    
nisinnaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā         vippasannamanāvilaṃ .

Tassa [^182-1] cittaṃ pasādesī [^182-2]  evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
tārayissati tiṇṇoyaṃ         dantoyaṃ damayissati .

Assāsissati assattho       santo ca samayissati    
mocayissati mutto ca        nibbāpessati nibbuto .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        siddhatthassa mahesino    
gahetvā timirapupphaṃ         matthake okiriṃ tadā [^182-3] .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     pakkāmiṃ aparaṃ disaṃ .

Aciraṃ gatamattaṃ maṃ           migarājā apīḷayi [^182-4]    
papātamanugacchanto          tattheva papatiṃ ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chappaññāsamhi kappamhi       sattevāsuṃ mahārahā [^182-5]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
#[^182-1] ma. Tattha . [^182-2] ma. Yu. Pasādetvā . [^182-3] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ . [^182-4] ma. Aviheṭhayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 183]*

chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā timirapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Timirapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ gatasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (82)    
[84] jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
avandiṃ satthuno pāde       vippasannena cetasā .

Sattanaṅgalakipupphe          ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ    
tissabuddhaṃ samuddissa         anantaguṇasāgaraṃ .

Sugatānugataṃ maggaṃ           pūjetvā haṭṭhamānaso    
añjaliñca tadākāsiṃ         pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito aṭṭhamake kappe       tayo aggisikhā ahu    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime8    
chaḷabhiññā sacchakatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gatasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gatasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 184]*

Tatiyaṃ nipannañjalikattherāpadānaṃ (83)    
[85] rukkhamūle nisinnohaṃ         byādhito paramenahaṃ [^184-1]    
paramakāruññapattomhi        araññe kānane brahā [^184-2] .

Anukampaṃ upādāya          tisso satthā upeti [^184-3] maṃ    
sohaṃ nipannako santo       sire katvāna añjaliṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         sabbasattānamuttamaṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ vandiṃ purisuttamaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito pañcamake kappe       pañcevāsuṃ mahāsikhā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nipannañjaliko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nipannañjalikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ adhopupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (84)    
[86] abhibhū nāma so bhikkhu        sikhino aggasāvako    
mahānubhāvo tevijjo       himavantaṃ upāgami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 185]*

Ahaṃ pi himavantamhi          ramaṇīyassame isi    
vasāmi appamaññāsu         iddhīsu ca tadā vasī .

Pakkhijāto viyākāse       pabbataṃ abhipatthayiṃ [^185-1]    
adhopupphaṃ gahetvāna        āgacchiṃ pabbataṃ ahaṃ .

Satta pupphāni gaṇhitvā      matthake okiriṃ ahaṃ    
ālokito [^185-2] ca vīrena     pakkāmiṃ pācināmukho .

Āvāsaṃ abhisajjhosiṃ [^185-3]      patvāna assamaṃ ahaṃ    
khārihāraṃ [^185-4] gahetvāna     pāyāsiṃ [^185-5] pabbatantaraṃ .

Ajagaropi [^185-6] pīḷesi        ghorarūpo mahabbalo    
pubbakammaṃ saritvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā adhopupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Adhopupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ raṃsisaññakattherāpadānaṃ (85)    
[87] pabbate himavantamhi         vāsaṃ kappesahaṃ pure    
ajinuttaravatthohaṃ           vasāmi pabbatantare .

#[^185-1] ma. Adhivattayiṃ . [^185-2] ma. Ālokite . [^185-3] ma. Yu. Abhisambhosiṃ . [^185-4] ma. Khāribhāraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 186]*

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         sataraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ    
vanantaragataṃ disvā          sālarājaṃva pupphitaṃ .

Raṃse [^186-1] cittaṃ pasādetvā   vipassissa mahesino    
paggayha añjaliṃ vandiṃ        sirasā ukkuṭiko [^186-2] ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         raṃsisaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Raṃsisaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ raṃsisaññikattherāpadānaṃ (86)    
[88] pabbate himavantamhi         vākacīradharo ahaṃ    
caṅkamaṃ ca samāruḷho        nisīdiṃ pācināmukho .

Pabbate sugataṃ disvā        phussaṃ jhānarataṃ sadā    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        raṃse cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ [^186-3] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         raṃsisaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 187]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisaññiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Raṃsisaññikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ phaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (87)    
[89] pabbate himavantamhi         kharājinadharo ahaṃ    
phussaṃ jinavaraṃ disvā         phalahattho phalaṃ adaṃ .

Yamahaṃ phalamadāsiṃ            vippasannena cetasā    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        phalaṃ nibbattate mama .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .K    
paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 188]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ saddasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (88)    
[90] pabbate himavantamhi         vasāmi paṇṇasanthare    
phussassa dhammaṃ bhaṇato        sadde cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saddasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ bodhisiñcakattherāpadānaṃ (89)    
[91] vipassissa bhagavato          mahābodhimaho ahu    
pabbajjūpagato santo        upagañchimahaṃ tadā .

Kusumbhodakamādāya [^188-1]       bodhiyā okiriṃ ahaṃ    
mocayissati no mutto       nibbāpessati nibbuto .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ bodhiṃ abhisiñcahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhisiñcassidaṃ [^188-2] phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 189]*

Tettiṃse vattamānamhi       kappe āsuṃ janādhipā    
udakāsecanā nāma         aṭṭhete cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bodhisiñcako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bodhisiñcakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ padumapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (90)    
[92] pokkharavanaṃ paviṭṭho         bhañjanto padumānahaṃ    
addasaṃ [^189-1] phussasambuddhaṃ      dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Padumapupphaṃ gahetvāna        ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ(    
pāpakammaṃ saritvāna         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Pabbajitvāna kāyena        manasā saṃvutena ca    
vacīduccaritaṃ hitvā          ājīvaṃ parisodhayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Padumabhāsanāmāva [^189-2]        aṭṭhārasa mahīpatī    
aṭṭhārasesu kappesu        aṭṭhatāḷīsamāsiṃsu .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 190]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
timiraṃ naṅgalipupphī           nipannañjaliko adho    
dve raṃsisaññi phalado        saddasaññī ca secako    
padumapupphī ca gāthāyo       chapaññāsā pakittitā .

Timirapupphiyavaggo navamo .

dasamo sudhāvaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sudhāpiṇḍiyattherāpadānaṃ (91)    
[93] pūjārahe pūjayato          buddhe yadica [^190-1] sāvake    
papañcasamatikkante          tiṇṇasokapariddave .

Te tādise pūjayato        nibbute akutobhaye    
na sakkā puññaṃ saṅkhātuṃ      imettamapi kenaci .

Catunnampi ca dīpānaṃ         issaraṃ yodha kāraye    
etissā [^190-2] pūjanāyetaṃ     kalaṃ nāgghati soḷasiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 191]*

Siddhatthassa naraggassa        cetiye iṭṭhakantare [^191-1]    
sudhāpiṇḍo mayā dinno      vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paṭisaṅkhārassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tiṃsatikappamhi          paṭisaṅkhārasavhayā    
sattaratanasampannā          terasa cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sudhāpiṇḍiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sudhāpiṇḍiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ supīṭhiyattherāpadānaṃ [^191-2] (92)    
[94] tissassa lokanāthassa        suddhapīṭhaṃ adāsahaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      buddhassādiccabandhuno .

Aṭṭhatiṃse [^191-3] ito kappe    rājā āsiṃ mahāruci    
bhogo ca vipulo āsi       sayanañca anappakaṃ .

Pīṭhaṃ buddhassa datvāna        vippasannena cetasā    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ          pubbe sukatamattano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 192]*

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pīṭhaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pīṭhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhatiṃse ito kappe       tayo te cakkavattino    
ruci uparuci ceva           mahāruci tatiyako .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supīṭhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Supīṭhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ aḍḍhacelakattherāpadānaṃ (93)    
[95] tissassāhaṃ bhagavato         upaḍḍhadussamadāsahaṃ    
paramakāruññapattomhi [^192-1]     duggatena [^192-2] samappito .

Upaḍḍhadussaṃ datvāna         kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ    
avasesesu kappesu         kusalañcakarimahaṃ [^192-3] .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekapaññāsakappamhi [^192-4]      rājāno cakkavattino    
samantāodanā [^192-5] nāma     khattiyāsuṃ [^192-6] janādhipā .

#[^192-1] ma. Yu. Paramakāpaññapattomhi . [^192-2] po. Yu. Duggandhena . [^192-3] ma. Kusalaṃ kāritaṃ mayā .

#yu. Kusalaṃ tīritaṃ mayā . [^192-4] ma. Ekūnapaññāsakappamhi . [^192-5] ma. Samantacchadanā nāma .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 193]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aḍḍhacelako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aḍḍhacelakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ sūcidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (94)    
[96] kammārohaṃ pure āsiṃ       bandhumāyaṃ puruttame    
sūcidānaṃ mayā dinnaṃ         vipassissa mahesino .

Vajiraggasamaññāṇaṃ           hoti kammena tādisaṃ    
virāgomhi vimuttomhi       pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Atītā [^193-1] ca bhavā sabbe    vattamānā ca anāgatā    
ñāṇena viciniṃ sabbaṃ         sūcidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      sattāsuṃ vajirāsamā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhimeva    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sūcidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sūcidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 194]*

pañcamaṃ gandhamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (95)    
[97] siddhatthassa bhagavato         gandhathūpaṃ akāsahaṃ    
sumanehi paṭicchannaṃ          buddhānucchavikaṃ ahaṃ .

Kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          buddhaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
indīvaraṃva  jalitaṃ           ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ    
byagghusabhaṃva pavaraṃ           abhijātaṃva kesariṃ    
nisinnaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Vanditvā satthuno pāde     pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho    
catunavute ito kappe       gandhamālaṃ yato adaṃ .

Buddhe katassa kārassa       phalenāhaṃ visesato    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Cattāḷīsamhi ekūne        kappe āsiṃsu soḷasa    
devagandhasanāmā te        rājāno cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 195]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ tipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (96)    
[98] migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane brahā [^195-1]    
pāṭaliṃ haritaṃ disvā         tīṇi pupphāni okiriṃ .

Sukkhapaṇṇāni [^195-2] gaṇhitvā    bahi chaḍḍesahaṃ tadā    
antosuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ         vimuttaṃ [^195-3] ca anāsavaṃ .

Sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ        vipassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
pāṭaliṃ abhivādetvā        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ bodhiṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Samantapāsādikā nāma       terasāsiṃsu rājino    
ito tiṃsatikappamhi [^195-4]       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^195-1] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ . [^195-2] ma. Patitapattāni . Yu. Sattapattāni . [^195-3] ma. Yu. Suvimuttaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 196]*

Sattamaṃ madhupiṇḍikattherāpadānaṃ (97)    
[99] vivane [^196-1] kānane disvā    appasadde nirākule    
siddhatthaṃ isinaṃ seṭṭhaṃ        āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .

Nibbutitaṃ [^196-2] mahānāgaṃ       nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā    
osadhīva [^196-3] virocantaṃ       devasaṅghanamassitaṃ .

Vitti me pāhunā tāva      ñāṇaṃ uppajji tāvade    
vuṭṭhitassa samādhimhā        madhadha datvāna satthuno .

Siddhatthassa bhagavato         vipapasannena cetasā    
vanditvā sirasā [^196-4] pāde   pakkāmiṃ pācināmukho .

Catuttiṃsamhi kappamhi         rājā āsiṃ sudassano    
madhubhiṃsehi savati            bhojanamhi ca tāvade    
madhuvassaṃ pavassittha          pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ madhadha adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catuttiṃse ito kappe       cattāro te sudassanā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpi ca aṭṭhime .

Chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti  .

#[^196-1] po. Vivite . Ma. Vipine . [^196-2] ma. Yu. Nibbutattaṃ . [^196-3] ma. Yu. Osadhiṃva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 197]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā madhupiṇḍiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Madhupiṇḍikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ senāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (98)    
[100] siddhatthassa bhagavato         adāsiṃ paṇṇasantharaṃ    
samantā upakārañca         kusumaṃ okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Pāsāde [^197-1] ca guhaṃ rammaṃ    anubhomi mahārahaṃ    
mahagghāni ca pupphāni        sayanebhivassanti [^197-2] me .

Sayanehaṃ tuvaṭṭāmi          vicitte pupphasanthate    
pupphavuṭṭhi ca sayane         abhivassati tāvade .

Catunavute ito kappe       adāsiṃ paṇṇasantharaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ṭhitāsanthārakā [^197-3] nāma     sattete cakkavattino    
ito te pañcame kappe     uppajjiṃsu janādhipā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā senāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Senāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 198]*

Navamaṃ veyyāvaccakattherāpadānaṃ (99)    
[101] vipassissa bhagavato          mahāpuggagaṇo [^198-1] ahu    
veyyāvaccakaro āsiṃ       sabbakiccesu byāvaṭo [^198-2] .

Deyyadhammo ca me natthi     sugatassa mahesino    
avandiṃ satthuno pāde       vippasannena cetasā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      veyyāvaccaṃ akāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         veyyāvaccassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito caṭṭhamake kappe       rājā āsiṃ sucintiyo [^198-3]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā veyyāvaccako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Veyyāvaccakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ buddhupaṭṭhākattherāpadānaṃ (100)    
[102] vipassino bhagavato          ahosiṃ saṅkhadhammako    
niccupaṭṭhānayuttomhi        sugatassa mahesino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 199]*

Upaṭṭhānaphalampassaṃ [^199-1]       lokanāthassa tādino    
saṭṭhituriyasahassāni          parivārenti maṃ sadā    
ekanavute ito kappe      upaṭṭhahiṃ mahāisiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute [^199-2] ito kappe    mahānigghosanāmakā    
soḷasāsiṃsu rājāno        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhupaṭṭhāko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Buddhupaṭṭhākattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sudhā pīṭhañca celañca        kammāro gandhamāliyo    
tipupphiyo madhu senā        veyyāvacco va dhammako    
samasaṭṭhiñca gāthāyo        asmiṃ vagge pakittitā .

Sudhāvaggo dasamo .

Atha vagguddānaṃ  
buddhavaggo hi paṭhamo        sīhāsani subhūti ca  
kuṇḍadhāno upāli ca        vījanī sakacittanī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 200]*

Nāgasamālo timiri          sudhāvaggena te dasa    
catuddasasatā gāthā         pañcapaññāsameva ca .

Buddhavaggadasakaṃ .

Paṭhamasatakaṃ samattaṃ .

ekādasamo bhikkhadāyivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ bhikkhadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (101)    
[103] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         āhutīnampaṭiggahaṃ    
pavanā [^200-1] abhinikkhantaṃ       vānā [^200-2] nibbānamāgataṃ .

Kaṭacchubhikkhapādāsiṃ          siddhatthassa mahesino    
paññassa upasantassa         mahāvīrassa tādino .

Padenānupadāyanto [^200-3]      nibbāpentaṃ [^200-4] mahājanaṃ    
vitti [^200-5] me pāhunā tāva   buddhassādiccabandhuno .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhikkhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattāsītimhito kappe       mahāreṇussanāmakā    
sattaratanasampannā          sattete cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^200-1] ma. Yu. Pavarā . [^200-2] ma. Vanā . [^200-3] ma. Padenānupadāyantaṃ . [^200-4] ma. Nibbāpente .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 201]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhikkhadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhikkhadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ñāṇasaññikattherāpadānaṃ (102)    
[104] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā    
tidhāppabhinnaṃ mātaṅgaṃ        kuñjaraṃva mahesinaṃ .

Obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     sālarājaṃva [^201-1] pupphitaṃ    
rathiyā paṭipajjantaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ naruttamaṃ [^201-2] .

Ñāṇe cittaṃ pasādetvā     paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         siddhatthaṃ abhivādayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ñāṇasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tesattatimhito kappe       soḷasāsuṃ naruttamā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇasaññiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ñāṇasaññikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 202]*

Tatiyaṃ uppalahatthiyattherāpadānaṃ (103)    
[105] tivarāyaṃ nivāsīhaṃ           ahosiṃ māliko tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          siddhatthaṃ lokapūjitaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         pupphahatthaṃ adāsahaṃ    
yattha yatthupapajjāmi         tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Anubhomi phalaṃ iṭṭhaṃ          pubbe sukatamattano    
parikkhitto sumallehi        sasaññāya [^202-1] idaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute upādāya         ṭhapetvā vattamānakaṃ    
pañca rājasatā tattha        najjupamasanāmakā [^202-2] .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uppalahatthiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Uppalahatthiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ padapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (104)    
[106] siddhatthassa bhagavato         jātipupphamadāsihaṃ    
pādesu satta pupphāni       hāsenokiritāni [^202-3] me .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 203]*

tena kammenahaṃ ajja        abhibhomi narāmare    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Samantagandhanāmāsuṃ          terasa cakkavattino    
ito pañcamake kappe       cāturantā gaṇādhipā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ muṭṭhipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (105)    
[107] sudassanoti nāmena         mālākāro ahaṃ tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Jātipupphaṃ gahetvāna        pūjayiṃ padumuttaraṃ    
visuddhacakkhu sumano          dibbacakkhuṃ samajjhagaṃ .

Etissā buddhapūjāya        cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 204]*

Soḷasāsiṃsu rājāno        devuttarasanāmakā    
chattiṃsamhi ito kappe       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā muṭṭhipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Muṭṭhipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ udakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (106)    
[108] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ anilañjase    
ghaṭāsanaṃva jalitaṃ            ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Pāṇinā udakaṃ gayha         ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ    
sampaṭicchi mahāvīro         buddho kāruṇiko mayi [^204-1] .

Antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      padumuttaranāmako    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Iminādakadānena [^204-2]        pītiuppādanena ca    
kappasatasahassamhi           duggatiṃ nūpapajjati .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 205]*

sahassarājanāmena          tayo te cakkavattino    
pañcasaṭṭhikappasate          cāturantā janādhipā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ naḷamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (107)    
[109] padumuttarabuddhassa           lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
tiṇatthare nisinnassa         upasantassa tādino .

Naḷamālaṃ gahetvāna         bandhitvā vījaniṃ ahaṃ    
buddhassa upanāmesiṃ         dipadindassa tādino .

Paṭaggahetvāna sabbaññū      vījaniṃ lokanāyako    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Yathā me kāyo nibbāyi     pariḷāho na vijjati    
tatheva tividhaggīhi           cittaṃ tava vimuccatu .

Sabbe devā samāgañchacha      yekeci dumanissitā    
sussāma buddhavacanaṃ          hāsayantañca dāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 206]*

nisinno bhagavā tattha        devasaṅghapurakkhato    
dāyakaṃ sampahaṃsento        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Iminā vījanidānena         cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
subbato nāma nāmena       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Tena kammāvasesena        sukkamūlena codito    
māluto nāma nāmena       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Iminā vījanidānena         sammānavipulena ca    
kappasatasahassamhi           duggatiṃ nūpapajjati .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           subbatā aṭṭhatiṃsa te    
ekūnatiṃsasahasse           aṭṭha mālutanāmakā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Naḷamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ āsanupaṭṭhāyakattherāpadānaṃ (108)    
[110] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha       appasaddaṃ nirākulaṃ    
sīhāsanaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       atthadassissa tādino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 207]*

Mālahatthaṃ gahetvāna        katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
satthāraṃ payirupāsitvā       pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
sannibbāpemi attānaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito sattakappasate         sannibbāpakakhattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āsanupaṭṭhāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Āsanupaṭṭhāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ biḷālidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (109)    
[111] himavantassa avidūre         vasāmi paṇṇasanthare    
ghāsesu gedhamāpanno       seyyasīlovahaṃ [^207-1] tadā .

Khaṇamālukalambāni [^207-2]        biḷālitakkaḷāni ca    
kolaṃ bhallātakaṃ bellaṃ [^207-3]    āhatvā paṭiyāditaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 208 -->
<a id="page-208"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 208]*

padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         āgacchi mama santikaṃ .

Upāgataṃ mahānāgaṃ          devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
biḷāliṃ paggahetvāna        pattamhi okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Paribhuñji mahāvīro          tosayanto mamaṃ tadā    
paribhuñjitvāna sabbaññū       imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       biḷāliṃ me adā tuvaṃ    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjasi .

Carimaṃ vattate mayhaṃ         bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Catupaññāsito kappe        sumekhalimasavhayo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā biḷālidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Biḷālidāyakatthessa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 209]*

Dasamaṃ reṇupūjakattherāpadānaṃ (110)    
[112] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         sataraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ    
obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     uḷurājaṃva pūritaṃ .

Purakkhataṃ sāvakehi          sāgareneva [^209-1] medaniṃ    
nāgaṃ paggayha reṇūhi        vipassissābhiropayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ reṇumabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṇṇatāḷīsito kappe        reṇu nāmāsi khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā reṇupūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Reṇupūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
bhikkhadāyī ñāṇasaññī         hatthiyo padapūjako    
muṭṭhipupphī udakado          naḷamālī nidhāvako [^209-2]    
biḷālidāyi reṇu ca         gāthāyo cha ca saṭṭhi ca .

Bhikkhadāyivaggo ekādasamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 210 -->
<a id="page-210"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 210]*

dvādasamo mahāparivāravaggo    
paṭhamaṃ mahāparivārattherapadānaṃ (111)    
[113] vipassī nāma bhagavā         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
aṭṭhasaṭṭhisahassehi          pāvisi bandhumaṃ tadā .

Nagarā abhinikkhamma          agamiṃ dīpacetiyaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .

Cullāsītisahassāni          yakkhā mayhaṃ upantike    
upaṭṭhahanti sakkaccaṃ         indaṃva tidasā gaṇā .

Bhavanā abhinikkhamma          dussaṃ paggayhahaṃ tadā    
sirasā abhivādesiṃ          tañcādāsiṃ mahesino .

Aho buddhā aho dhammā     aho no satthusampadā    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        vasudhāyaṃ pakampatha .

Tañca acchariyaṃ disvā        abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādemi       dipadindamhi tādine .

Sohaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā      dussaṃ datvāna satthuno    
saraṇañca upāgañchiṃ          sāmacco saparijjano .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       soḷasāsiṃsu vāhanā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 211]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāparivāro thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mahāparivārattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ sumaṅgalattherāpadānaṃ (112)    
[114] atthadassī jinavaro          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
vihārā abhinikkhamma         taḷākaṃ upasaṅkami .

Nhātvā pitvā ca sambuddho  uttaritvekacīvaro    
aṭṭhāsi bhagavā tattha        vilokento disodisaṃ .

Bhavane upaviṭṭhohaṃ          addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      appoṭhesiṃ ahaṃ tadā .

Sataraṃsiṃva jotantaṃ           pabhāsantaṃva kañcanaṃ    
nacce gīte ca yuttohaṃ      pañcaṅgaturiyepica .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbe satte abhibhomi       vipulo hoti me yaso .

Namo te purisājañña        namo te purisuttama    
attānaṃ tosayitvāna        pare tosesi tvaṃ muni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 212]*

Pariggahitvā [^212-1] nisīditvā    hāsaṃ katvāna subbato    
upaṭṭhahitvāna sambuddhaṃ       tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Soḷasito kappasate         dvinavā ekacintitā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumaṅgalo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumaṅgalattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ saraṇagamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (113)    
[115] ubhinnaṃ devarājūnaṃ          saṅgāmo paccupaṭṭhito [^212-2]    
ahosi samupabyuḷho         mahāghoso avattatha .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      saṃvejesi mahājanaṃ .

Sabbe devā attamanā      nikkhittakavacāvudhā    
sambuddhaṃ abhivāditvā        ekaggāsiṃsu tāvade .

Mayhaṃ saṅkappamaññāya        vācāsabhimudīrayi    
anukampako lokavidū         nibbāpesi mahājanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 213]*

Paduṭṭhacitto manujo         ekaṃ pāṇaṃ viheṭhayaṃ    
tena cittappadosena        apāyaṃ upapajjati .

Saṅgāmasī sa nāgova        bahupāṇe viheṭhayaṃ    
nibbāpetha sakaṃ cittaṃ        mā haññittha punappunaṃ .

Dvinnaṃpi yakkharājūnaṃ         senāpi samitā ahu [^213-1]    
saraṇañca upāgañchacha          lokajeṭṭhaṃ sutādinaṃ .

Saññāpetvāna janataṃ        uddhari pana cakkhumā [^213-2]    
pekkhamānova devehi       pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Paṭhamaṃ saraṇaṃ gañchiṃ           dipadindassa tādino    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Mahācundabhināmā ca         soḷasāsuṃ rathesabhā    
tiṃsakappasahassamhi           rājāno cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saraṇagamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ekāsaniyattherāpadānaṃ (114)    
[116] varuṇo nāma nāmena        devarājā ahaṃ tadā    
upaṭṭhapesiṃ sambuddhaṃ         sayoggabalavāhano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 214]*

Nibbute lokanāthamhi        atthadassidipaduttame [^214-1]    
turiyaṃ sabbamādāya          agamaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Vāditena ca naccena        sabbatāḷasamāhito [^214-2]    
sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ        upaṭṭhiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Upaṭṭhahitvā taṃ bodhiṃ        dharaṇīrūhapādapaṃ    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Sakakammābhiraddhohaṃ          pasanno bodhimuttame    
tena cittappasādena        nimmānaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         parivārenti maṃ sadā    
manussesu ca devesu        vattamānaṃ bhavābhave .

Tidhaggī [^214-3] nibbutā mayhaṃ     bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Subāhu nāma nāmena        catuttiṃsāsu khattiyā    
sattaratanasampannā          pañcakappasate ito .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekāsaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti  .

Ekāsaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 215]*

Pañcamaṃ suvaṇṇapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (115)    
[117] vipassī nāma bhagavā         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
nisinno janakāyassa         desesi amataṃ padaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       dipadindassa tādino    
sovaṇṇapupphāni cattāri      buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Suvaṇṇachadanaṃ āsi           yāvatā parisā tadā    
buddhābhā ca suvaṇṇābhā      āloko vipulo ahu .

Udaggacitto sumano         vedajāto katañjalī    
pītisañjanano [^215-1] tesaṃ       diṭṭhadhammasukhāvaho .

Āyācitvāna sambuddhaṃ       vanditvāna ca subbataṃ    
pāmojjaṃ janayitvāna        sakaṃ bhavanupāvisiṃ .

Bhavanaṃ [^215-2] upaviṭṭhohaṃ        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ    
tena cittappasādena        tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soḷasāsiṃsu rājāno        nemisammatanāmakā    
tetāḷīse ito kappe      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 216]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā suvaṇṇapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Suvaṇṇapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (116)    
[118] vasāmi rājāyatane         sāmacco saparijjano    
parinibbute bhagavati          sikhino lokabandhuno [^216-1] .

Pasannacitto sumano         citakaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
turiyaṃ tattha vādetvā       gandhamālaṃ samokiriṃ .

Citakamhi pūjaṃ katvāna        vanditvā citakaṃ ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         sakaṃ bhavanupāgamiṃ .

Bhavane upaviṭṭhohaṃ          citakapūjaṃ anussariṃ    
tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha .

Anubhutvāna sampattiṃ         devesu mānusesu ca    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         citakapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekūnatiṃse kappamhi         ito soḷasa rājino    
uggatā nāma nāmena       cakkavattī mahabbalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 217]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ buddhasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (117)    
[119] yadā vipassi lokaggo       āyusaṅkhāramossaji    
paṭhavī sampakampittha          medanījalamekhalā .

Ogataṃ [^217-1] vitataṃ mayhaṃ       suci cittaṃ papañcakaṃ    
bhavanaṃpi pakampittha           buddhassa āyusaṅkhaye .

Tāso mayhaṃ samuppanno      bhavane sampakampite    
uppādo nu kimatthāya       āloko vipulo ahu .

Vessavaṇṇo idhāgamma       nibbāpesi mahājanaṃ    
pāṇabhūtaṃ [^217-2] bhayaṃ natthi       ekaggā hotha sagāravā [^217-3] .

Aho buddho aho dhammo     aho no satthusampadā    
yasmiṃ uppajjamānasmiṃ        paṭhavī sampakampati .

Buddhānubhāvaṃ kittetvā      kappaṃ saggamha modahaṃ    
avasesesu kappesu         kusalaṃ karitaṃ mayā .

#[^217-1] ma. Yu. Otataṃ vitataṃ mayhaṃ suvicittavaṭaṃsakaṃ . Ma. Vitataṃ vithataṃ . [^217-2] ma. Pāṇabhūte .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 218]*

ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito cuddasakappamhi         rājā āsiṃ patāpavā    
samito nāma nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Buddhasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ maggasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (118)    
[120] padumuttarabuddhassa           sāvakā vanacārino    
vippanaṭṭhā brahāraññe      andhāva anusuyyare .

Anussaritvā sambuddhaṃ        padumuttaranāyakaṃ    
tassa te munino puttā      vippanaṭṭhā mahāvane .

Bhavanā oruhitvāna         āgamiṃ bhikkhusantike    
tesaṃ maggañca ācikkhiṃ       bhojanañca adāsahaṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
jātiyā sattavassena        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 219]*

Sacakkhu nāma nāmena        dvādasa cakkavattino    
sattaratanasampannā          pañcakappasate ito .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Maggasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ paccupaṭṭhānasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (119)    
[121] atthadassimhi sugate         nibbute samanantarā    
yakkhayoniṃ upapajjiṃ          yasaṃ patto cahaṃ tadā .

Dulladdhaṃ vata me āsi       duppabhātaṃ duruṭṭhitaṃ    
yaṃ me bhoge vijjamāne     parinibbāyi cakkhumā .
