Mama saṅkappamaññāya         sāgaro nāma sāvako    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi mama santike [^219-1] .

Kiṃ nu socasi mā bhāyi       cara dhammaṃ sumedhasa    
anuppadinnā buddhena        sabbesaṃ vijjasampadā .

Yo ca pūjeyya sambuddhaṃ      tiṭṭhantaṃ [^219-2] lokanāyakaṃ    
dhātuṃ sāsapamattampi         nibbutassāpi pūjaye .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 220]*

same cittappasādamhi        samaṃ puññaṃ mahaggataṃ    
tasmā thūpaṃ karitvāna        pūjehi jinadhātuyo .

Sāgarassa vaco sutvā       buddhathūpaṃ akāsahaṃ    
pañca vasse paricariṃ         munino thūpamuttamaṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
sampattiṃ anubhotvāna        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Bhūripaññāva cattāro        sattakappasate ito    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paccupaṭṭhānasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paccupaṭṭhānasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ jātipūjakattherāpadānaṃ (120)    
[122] jāyantassa vipassissa        āloko vipulo ahu    
paṭhavī ca pakampittha          sasāgarā sapabbatā .

Nemittā ca viyākaṃsu        buddho loke bhavissati    
aggo ca sabbasattānaṃ       janataṃ uddharissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 221]*

Nemittānaṃ suṇitvāna        jātipūjaṃ akāsahaṃ    
edisā pūjanā natthi        yādisā jātipūjanā .

Saṃharitvāna [^221-1] kusalaṃ        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
jātipūjaṃ  karitvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbe satte atītomhi [^221-2]   jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dhātiyo maṃ upaṭṭhanti        mama cittavasānugā    
na te [^221-3] sakkonti kopetuṃ  jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pūjamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Supāricariyā nāma          catuttiṃsa janādhipā    
ito tatiyakappamhi          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jātipūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Jātipūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
parivārasumaṅgalyā [^221-4]       saraṇāsanapupphikā    
citapūjī buddhasaññī           maggupaṭṭhānajātinā    
#[^221-1] ma. Saṅkharitvāna . Yu. Saṃsaritvāna . [^221-2] ma. Yu. Abhibhomi . [^221-3] ma. Tā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 222]*

gāthāyo navuti vuttā       gaṇitāyo vibhāvihīti .

Mahāparivāravaggo dvādasamo .

terasamo sereyyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sereyyakattherāpadānaṃ (121)    
[123] ajjhāyiko mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
abbhokāse ṭhito santo     addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Sīhaṃ yathā vanacaraṃ           byaggharājaṃva nittasaṃ    
tidhappabhinna mātaṅgaṃ         kuñjaraṃva mahesinaṃ .

Sereyyakaṃ gahetvāna       ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        parivārenti sabbaso .

Adhiṭṭhahi mahāvīro          sabbaññū lokanāyako    
samantā pupphachadanaṃ          okiriṃsu narāsabhaṃ .

Tato sā pupphakañcukā       antovaṇṭā bahimukhā    
sattāhaṃ chadanaṃ katvā        tato antaradhāyatha .

Tañca acchariyaṃ disvā        abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādesiṃ       sugate lokanāyake .

Tena cittappasādena        sukkamūlena codito    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 223]*

Paṇṇarasasahassamhi           kappānaṃ pañcavīsate    
vilāmālā sanāmā ca [^223-1]    cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sereyyako thero imā gāthāyo[^223-4]    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sereyyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ pupphathūpiyattherāpadānaṃ (122)    
[124] himavantassa avidūre         kukkuro nāma pabbato    
vemajjhe tassa vasati        brāhmaṇo mantapāragū .

Pañca sissasahassāni         parivārenti maṃ sadā    
pubbuṭṭhāyī ca te āsuṃ      mantesu ca visāradā .

Buddho loke samuppanno     taṃ vijānātha no bhavaṃ    
asīti byañjanānassa         battiṃsa varalakkhaṇā .

Byāmappabho jinavaro        ādiccova virocati    
sissānaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        brāhmaṇo mantapāragū .

Assamā abhinikkhamma         disaṃ pucchati brāhmaṇo [^223-2]    
yamhi dese mahāvīro       vasati lokanāyako .

Tāhaṃ disvā [^223-3] namassissaṃ    jinaṃ appaṭipuggalaṃ    
udaggacitto sumano         pūjesiṃ taṃ tathāgataṃ .

#[^223-1] ma. Vītamalā sanāmā ca . Yu. Cīnamālā sanāmā ca . [^223-2] ma. Sissako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 224]*

Etha sissā gamissāma       dakkhissāma tathāgataṃ    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     sossāma jinasāsanaṃ .

Ekāhaṃ abhinikkhamma         byādhiṃ paṭilabhiṃ ahaṃ    
byādhinā pīḷito santo      sālanto sayituṃ gamiṃ [^224-1] .

Sabbe sisse samānetvā    apucchiṃ te tathāgataṃ    
kīdisaṃ lokanāthassa          guṇaṃ paramabuddhino .

Te me puṭṭhā byākariṃsu     yathā dassāvino tathā    
sakkaccaṃ buddhaseṭṭhaṃ taṃ       dassesuṃ mama sammukhā .

Tesāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
pupphehi thūpaṃ karitvāna       tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Te me sarīraṃ jhāpetvā     agamama buddhasantike [^224-2]    
añjaliṃ paggahitvāna         satthāraṃ abhivādayuṃ .

Pupphehi thūpaṃ karitvāna       sugatassa mahesino    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Cattāḷīsasahassamhi          kappe soḷasa khattiyā    
nāmenaggisamā nāma        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Vīsakappasahassamhi           rājāno cakkavattino    
ghaṭāsanasamā nāma          aṭṭhattiṃsa mahīpatī    
paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 225]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphathūpiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphathūpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ pāyāsadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (123)    
[125] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         battiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ [^225-1]    
pavanā abhinikkhantaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Sahatthā [^225-2] kaṃsapāṭiyā      vaḍḍhetvā pāyāsaṃ ahaṃ    
āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmo so       agamiṃ sambaliṃ ahaṃ [^225-3] .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
caṅkamaṃ susamāruḷho         ambare anilāyane .

Tañca acchariyaṃ disvā        abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
ṭhapayitvā kaṃsapāṭiṃ          vipassī abhivādayiṃ .

Tuvaṃ buddhosi [^225-4] sabbaññū     sadevake samānuse    
anukampaṃ upādāya          paṭiggaṇha mahāmuni .

Paṭiggaṇhāsi bhagavā         sabbaññū lokanāyako    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro [^225-5] .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pāyāsassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekatāḷīsito kappe        buddho nāmāsi khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

#[^225-1] ma. Suvaṇṇavaṇṇo sambuddho .Pe. Bhikkhu saṅghapurakkhato . [^225-2] ma. Mahaccā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 226]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāyāsadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pāyāsadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ gandhodakiyattherāpadānaṃ (124)    
[126] nisajja pāsādavare         vipassiṃ addasaṃ jinaṃ    
kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva            sabbaññuttamanāsakaṃ [^226-1] .

Pāsādassāvidūre ca        gacchati lokanāyako    
pabhā niddhāvate tassa       sataraṃsimhi nibbute [^226-2] .

Gandhodakaṃ ca paggayha        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ samokiriṃ    
tena cittappasādena        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ gandhodakamokiriṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      sugandho nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 227]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhodakiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhodakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ sammukhāthavikattherāpadānaṃ (125)    
[127] jāyamāne vipassamhi        nimittaṃ byākariṃ ahaṃ    
nibbāpayaṃ ca janataṃ          buddho loke bhavissati .

Yasmiṃ ca jāyamānasmiṃ        dasasahassī pakampati=    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiṃ ca jāyamānasmiṃ        āloko vipulo ahu    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiṃ ca jāyamānasmiṃ        saritāyo na sandisuṃ [^227-1]    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiṃ ca jāyamānasmiṃ        avīciggi na pajjali    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiṃ ca jāyamānasmiṃ        pakkhisaṅgho na sañcari    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiñca jāyamānasmiṃ        vātakkhandho na vāyati    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 228]*

yasmiñca jāyamānasmiṃ        sabbaratanāni jotisuṃ    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yasmiñca jāyamānasmiṃ        sattāsuṃ padavikkamā    
sodāni bhagavā satthā       dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Jātamattova sambuddho       disā sabbā vilokayi    
vācāsabhimudīresi           esā buddhāna dhammatā .

Saṃvejayitvā janataṃ          thavitvā lokanāyakaṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivāditvā        pakkāmiṃ pācināmukho .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ buddhamabhithomayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thomanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito navutikappamhi          sammukhāthavikavhayo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭhavīdundubhi nāma           ekūnanavutimhito    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Aṭṭhāsītimhito kappe       obhāso nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Sattāsītimhito kappe       saritacchedanavhayo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Agginibbāpano nāma        kappānaṃ chaḷasītiyā    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 229]*

Rājā vātasamo nāma       kappānaṃ pañcasītiyā    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Gatipacchedano nāma         kappānaṃ cullasītiyā [^229-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Ratanappajjalo nāma         kappānaṃ te asītiyā    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Padavikkamano nāma          kappānaṃ dve asītiyā    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Rājā vilokano nāma       kappānaṃ ekasītiyā    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Girisāroti [^229-2] nāmena      kappesītimhi khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sammukhāthaviko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sammukhāthavikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 230]*

chaṭṭhaṃ kusumāsaniyattherāpadānaṃ (126)    
[128] nagare dhaññavatiyā          ahosiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
lakkhaṇe itihāse ca        sanighaṇḍusakeṭubhe .

Padako veyyākaraṇo        nimitte kovido ahaṃ    
mante ca sisse vācesiṃ     tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
pañca uppalahatthāni         piṭṭhiyaṃ ṭhapitāni me    
āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmohaṃ         pitumātusamāgame .

Tadā vipassi bhagavā         bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
obhāsento disā sabbā    āgacchati narāsabho .

Āsanaṃ paññapetvāna        nimantitvā mahāmuniṃ    
santharitvāna taṃ pupphaṃ        atinesiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Yaṃ me atthi sake gehe     āmisaṃ paccupaṭṭhitaṃ    
tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ       pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Bhuttāvīkālamaññāya         ekaṃ atthaṃ adāsahaṃ    
anumoditvāna sabbaññū       pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Anantarā ito kappe       rājāhu varadassano    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 231]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kusumāsaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kusumāsaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ phaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (127)    
[129] ajjhāyiko mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
himavantassavidūre           vasāmi assame ahaṃ .

Aggihuttañca me atthi       puṇḍarīkaphalāni ca    
puṭake nikkhipitvāna         dumagge laggitaṃ mayā .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamuddharitukāmo so         bhikkhanto mamupāgami .

Pasannacitto sumano         phalaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ    
pītisañjanano mayhaṃ          diṭṭhadhamme sukhāvaho .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇo sambuddho       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Iminā phaladānena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 232]*

Teneva sukkamūlena         anubhutvāna sampadā    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Ito sattakappasate         rājā āsiṃ sumaṅgalo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ ñāṇasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (128)    
[130] pabbate himavantamhi         vasāmi pabbatantare    
puḷinaṃ sobhanaṃ disvā         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ .

Ñāṇe upanidhaṃ [^232-1] natthi      saṅgāmaṃ [^232-2] natthi satthuno    
sabbadhammaṃ abhiññāya         ñāṇena adhimuccati .

Namo te purisājañña        namo te purisuttama    
ñāṇena te samo natthi      yāvatā ñāṇamuttamaṃ .

Ñāṇe cittaṃ pasādetvā     kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ    
avasesesu kappesu         kusalaṃ karitaṃ [^232-3] mayā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 233]*

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ñāṇasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tesattatikappe [^233-1]     eko puḷinapupphiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ñāṇasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ gandhapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ [^233-2] (129)    
[131] suvaṇṇavaṇṇo bhagavā         vipassī dakkhiṇāraho    
purakkhato sāvakehi         ārāmā abhinikkhami .

Disvānahaṃ buddhaseṭṭhaṃ        sabbaññutaṃ anāsavaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         gatamaggaṃ [^233-3] apūjayiṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        dipadindassa tādino    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      puna vandiṃ tathāgataṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^233-1] ma. Sattatikappamhi . [^233-2] ma. Yu. Ganthipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 234]*

Ekatāḷīsito kappe        varuṇo [^234-1] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ padumapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (130)    
[132] himavantassavidūre           gotamo nāma pabbato    
nānārukkhehi sañchanno      mahābhūtagaṇālayo .

Vemajjhamhi ca tassāsi       assamo abhinimmito    
purakkhato sasissehi         vasāmi assame ahaṃ .

Āyantu me sissagaṇā       padumaṃ āharantu me    
buddhapūjaṃ karissāmi          dipadindassa tādino .

Evanti te paṭissutvā      padumaṃ āhariṃsu me    
tathā nimittaṃ katvāhaṃ        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Sisse tadā samānetvā     sādhukaṃ anusāsahaṃ    
mā kho tumhe pamajjittha     appamādo sukhāvaho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 235]*

Evaṃ samanusāsitvā         sa [^235-1] sisse vacanakkhame    
appamādaguṇe yutto        tadā kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekapaññāsakappamhi         rājā āsiṃ jaluttamo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sereyyako pupphathūpi        pāyāsodaki [^235-2] thomako    
āsani phalasaññī ca          gandhapadumapupphiyo    
pañcuttarasatā gāthā        gaṇitā atthadassibhi .

Sereyyavaggo terasamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 236]*

Cuddasamo sobhitavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ sobhitattherāpadānaṃ (131)    
[133] padumuttaro nāma jino       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
mahato janakāyassa          deseti amataṃ padaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        vācāsabhimudīritaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        ekaggo āsahaṃ tadā .

Yathā samuddo udadhīnamaggo    
meru nagānaṃ pavaro siluccayo    
tatheva ye cittavasena vattare    
na buddhañāṇassa kalaṃ upenti te .

Dhammavidhiṃ ṭhapetvāna         buddho kāruṇiko isi    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo so ñāṇaṃ pakittesi      buddhamhi lokanāyake    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ [^236-1]       duggatiṃ so na gacchati .

Kilese jhāpayitvāna        ekaggo susamāhito    
sobhito nāma nāmena       hessati satthusāvako .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 237]*

Paññāse kappasahassamhi      sattevāsuṃ samuggatā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sobhito thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sobhittatherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ sudassanattherāpadānaṃ (132)    
[134] vitthatāya [^237-1] nadiyā tīre    milakkhuphalino [^237-2] ahu    
tāhaṃ rukkhaṃ gavesanto       addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ketakaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        vaṇṭe chetvānahaṃ tadā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Yena ñāṇena pattosi       accutaṃ amataṃ padaṃ    
taṃ ñāṇaṃ abhipūjesiṃ [^237-3]       buddhaseṭṭha mahāmuni .

Ñāṇamhi pūjaṃ [^237-4] katvāna     milakkhuṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ    
paṭiladdhomhi taṃ saññaṃ [^237-5]     ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^237-1] ma. Vinatā . [^237-2] ma. Pilakkhuphalito ahu . Yu. Pilakkhaphalino . [^237-3] ma. Yu. Abhipūjemi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 238]*

Ito terasakappamhi         dvādasāsuṃ baluggatā [^238-1]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sudassano thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sudassanattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ candanapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (133)    
[135] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ kinnaro tadā    
pupphabhakkho cahaṃ āsiṃ        pupphānaṃ vasano ahaṃ [^238-2] .

Atthadassī tu bhagavā         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
pavanaggena [^238-3] niyyāsi [^238-4]   haṃsarājāva ambare .

Namo te purisājañña        cittaṃ te suvisodhitaṃ    
pasannamukhavaṇṇosi           vippasannamukhindriyo .

Orohitvāna ākāsā      bhūripañño sumedhaso    
saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvāna        pallaṅkena upāvisi .

Vilinaṃ candanādāya          agamāsiṃ jinantike    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

#[^238-1] ma. Phaluggatā . Yu. Khaluggatā . [^238-2] ma. Pupphāni vasano tathā . Yu. ... Cahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 239]*

abhivādetvāna sambuddhaṃ      lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pāmojjaṃ janayitvāna        pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        candanaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catuddase kappasate         ito āsiṃsu te tayo    
rohiṇī nāma nāmena        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā candanapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Candanapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Catutthaṃ pupphachadaniyattherāpadānaṃ (134)    
[136] sunando nāma nāmena       brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
ajjhāyako yācayogo       vājapeyyaṃ ayājayi .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         aggo kāruṇiko isi    
janataṃ anukampanto          ambare caṅkami tadā .

Caṅkamitvāna sambuddho       sabbaññū lokanāyako    
mettāya aphari satte       appamāṇaṃ nirūpadhiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 240]*

Vaṇṭe chitvāna pupphāni      brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
sabbe sisse samānetvā    ākāse ukkhipāpayi .

Yāvatā nagaraṃ āsi         pupphānaṃ chadanaṃ tadā    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        sattāhaṃ na vigacchatha .

Teneva sukkamūlena         anubhotvāna sampadā    
sabbāsave pariññāya        tiṇṇo loke visattikaṃ .

Ekārase kappasate        pañcattiṃsāsu khattiyā    
ambaraṃsasanāmā te         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphachadaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphachadaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ rahosaññikattherāpadānaṃ (135)    
[137] himavantassavidūre           vasabho nāma pabbato    
tasmiṃ pabbatapādamhi         assamo āsi māpito .

Tīṇi sissasahassāni          vācesiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
saṃsāvitvāna [^240-1] te sisse   ekamantaṃ upāvisiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 241]*

ekamantaṃ nisīditvā         brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
buddhavesaṃ gavesanto        ñāṇe cittaṃ pasādayi .

Tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā      nisīdi paṇṇasanthare    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ñāṇasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīsamhi kappamhi         rājā sirīdharo ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rahosaññiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Rahosaññikattherassa  apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ campakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (136)    
[138] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     osadhiṃ viya tārakaṃ .

Tayo māṇavakā āsuṃ        sake sippe susikkhitā    
khāribhāraṃ gahetvāna        anventi mama pacchato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 242]*

Puṭake satta pupphāni        nikkhittāni tapassinā    
gahetvā tāni ñāṇamhi      vessabhussābhiropayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekūnatiṃsakappamhi           vihatābhāsanāmako [^242-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā campakapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Campakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ atthasandassakattherāpadānaṃ (137)    
[139] visālamāḷe āsinno       addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
khīṇāsavaṃ balappattaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Satasahassā tevijjā        chaḷabhiññā mahiddhikā    
parivārenti sambuddhaṃ        ko disvā nappasīdati .

Ñāṇe upanidhā yassa        na vijjati sadevake    
anantañāṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         ko disvā nappasīdati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 243]*

Dhammakāyañca dīpenti [^243-1]     kevalaṃ ratanākaraṃ    
vikopetuṃ na sakkonti       ko disvā nappasīdati .

Imāhi tīhi gāthāhi         nārado puragacchi [^243-2] so    
padumuttaraṃ thavitvāna         sambuddhaṃ aparājitaṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        buddhasanthavanena ca    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ [^243-3] .

Ito tiṃse kappasate        sumitto [^243-4] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā atthasandassako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Atthasandassakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ ekaraṃsaniyattherāpadānaṃ [^243-5] (138)    
[140] nārado iti nāmena [^243-6]     kesavo iti maṃ vidū    
kusalākusalaṃ esaṃ           agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ .

Mettacitto kāruṇiko       atthadassī mahāmuni    
assāsayanto satte so     dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

#[^243-1] ma. Dīpentaṃ . [^243-2] yu. Saragacchi yo . Ma. Vhayavacchalo . [^243-3] yu. Nūpapajjatha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 244]*

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
satthāraṃ abhivādetvā       pakkāmi [^244-1] pācināmukho .

Sattarase kappasate         rājā āsi mahīpati    
amittatapano [^244-2] nāma       cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekaraṃsaniyo [^244-3] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekaraṃsaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sāladāyakattherāpadānaṃ [^244-4] (139)    
[141] migarājā tadā āsiṃ        abhijātosu [^244-5] kesarī    
giriduggaṃ gavesanto         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ayaṃ nu kho mahāvīro        nibbāpeti mahājanaṃ    
yannūnimaṃ [^244-6] upāseyyaṃ      devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Sākhaṃ sālassa bhañjitvā      sakoṭaṃ pupphamāhariṃ    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        adāsiṃ pupphamuttamaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^244-1] ma. Yu. Pakkāmiṃ . [^244-2] ma. Amittatāpano nāma . Yu. Amittavāsano nāma .

#[^244-3] ma. Ekapasādaniyo . [^244-4] ma. Yu. Sālapupphadāyakattherāpadānaṃ . [^244-5] yu. Abhijātova .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 245]*

Ito ca navame kappe       virocanasanāmakā    
tayo āsiṃsu rājāno       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sāladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sāladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ phaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (104)    
[142] parodhako [^245-1] tadā āsiṃ     paramānuparodhako    
pabbhāre seyyaṃ kappemi     avidūre sikhisatthuno .

Sāyaṃ pātañca passāmi       buddhaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
deyyadhammo ca me natthi     dipadindassa tādino .

Piyālaphalamādāya           agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ    
paṭiggahesi bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho .

Tato paraṃ upādāya         pavāri [^245-2] taṃ vināyakaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 246]*

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       tayo [^246-1] āsiṃsu mālabhi    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
sobhi sudassano ceva        candano pupphachadano    
raho campakapupphī ca         atthasandassakena ca .

Ekaraṃsī [^246-2] sāladado       dasamo phaladāyako    
gāthāyo sattati dve ca     gaṇitāyo vibhāvihīti .

Sobhitavaggo cuddasamo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 247]*

Paṇṇarasamo chattavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ adhichattiyattherāpadānaṃ (141)    
[143] parinibbute bhagavati          atthadassīnaruttame    
chattātichattaṃ [^247-1] kāretvā   thūpamhi abhiropayiṃ .

Kālena kālaṃ āgantvā     namassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
pupphacchadanaṃ katvāna         chattamhi abhiropayiṃ .

Sattarase kappasate         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
manussattaṃ na gacchāmi        thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā adhichattiyo [^247-2] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Adhichattiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ thambhāropakattherāpadānaṃ (142)    
[144] nibbute lokanāthamhi        dhammadassīnarāsabhe    
āropesiṃ dhajatthambhaṃ        buddhaseṭṭhassa cetiye .

Nisseṇiṃ māpayitvāna        thūpaseṭṭhaṃ samāruhaṃ    
jātipupphaṃ gahetvāna        thūpamhi abhiropayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 248]*

Aho buddhā aho dhammā     aho no satthusampadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       thūpasikhasanāmakā    
soḷasāsiṃsu rājāno        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā thambhāropako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Thambhāropakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ vedikārakattherāpadānaṃ (143)    
[145] nibbute lokanāthamhi        piyadassīnaruttame    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhavedimakāsahaṃ .

Maṇīhi parivāretvā         akāsimahamuttamaṃ    
vedikāya mahaṃ katvā        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
maṇiṃ [^248-1] dhārenti ākāse   puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soḷasito kappasate         maṇippabhāsanāmakā    
battiṃsāsiṃsu [^248-2] rājāno     cakkavattī mahabbalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 249]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vedikārako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vedikārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ saparivāriyattherāpadānaṃ (144)    
[146] padumuttaro nāma jino       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
jalitvā aggikkhandhova       sambuddho parinibbuto .

Nibbute ca mahāvīre        thūpo vitthāriko ahu    
thūpadattaṃ [^249-1] upaṭṭhenti      dhātugehe varuttame .

Pasannacitto sumano         akaṃ [^249-2] candanavedikaṃ    
dīyati dhūpagandho ca          thūpānucchavikaṃ [^249-3] tadā .

Bhave nibbattamānamhi        devatte atha mānuse    
omattaṃ me na passāmi      pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcadase kappasate         ito aṭṭha janā ahuṃ    
sabbe samattanāmā te      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 250]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saparivāriyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saparivāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ ummāpupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (145)    
[147] nibbute lokamahite         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahe    
siddhatthamhi bhagavati          mahāthūpamaho ahu .

Mahe pavattamānamhi         siddhatthassa mahesino    
ummāpupphaṃ gahetvāna       thūpamhi abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ [^250-1] phalaṃ .

Ito ca navame kappe       somadevasanāmakā    
pañcāsītisu rājāno        cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ummāpupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ummāpupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 251]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ anulomadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (146)    
[148] anomadassissa munino        bodhivediṃ akāsahaṃ    
sudhāya piṇḍaṃ datvāna        pāṇikammaṃ akāsahaṃ .

Disvā taṃ sukataṃ kammaṃ        anomadassī naruttamo    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Iminā sudhakammena          cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
sampattiṃ anubhotvāna        dukkhassantaṃ karissati .

Pasannamukhavaṇṇomhi          ekaggo susamāhito    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Ito kappasate āsiṃ        paripuṇṇo [^251-1] anūnako    
rājā sampassano [^251-2] nāma   cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anulomadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Anulomadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 252]*

Sattamaṃ maggadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (147)    
[149] uttaritvāna nadikaṃ          vanaṃ gacchati cakkhumā    
tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         siddhatthaṃ varalakkhaṇaṃ .

Kuddālapiṭakamādāya         samaṃ katvāna taṃ pathaṃ    
satthāraṃ abhivādetvā       sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         maggadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         eko āsiṃ janādhipo"    
nāmena suppabuddhoti        nāyako so narissaro .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Maggadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ phalakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (148)    
[150] yānakāro pure āsiṃ       dārukamme susikkhito    
candanena [^252-1] phalakaṃ katvā    adāsiṃ lokabandhuno .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 253]*

Pabhāsati idaṃ byamhaṃ         suvaṇṇassa sunimmitaṃ    
hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ         dibbayānaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ .

Pāsādā sivikā ceva       nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ    
akkhumbhaṃ [^253-1] ratanaṃ mayhaṃ      phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      phalakaṃ yaṃ ahaṃ dadiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         caturo bhavanimmitā [^253-2]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phalakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phalakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ vaṭaṃsakiyattherāpadānaṃ (149)    
[151] sumedho nāma nāmena       sayambhū aparājito    
vivekamanubrūhanto          ajjhogāhi mahāvanaṃ .

Salaḷaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā         ganthitvāna vaṭaṃsakaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sammukhā lokanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 254]*

tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ūnavīse kappasate          soḷasāsiṃsu nimmitā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṭaṃsakiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vaṭaṃsakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pallaṅkadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (150)    
[152] sumedhassa bhagavato          lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
pallaṅko hi mayā dinno     saüttarasapacchado .

Sattaratanasampanno          pallaṅko āsi so tadā    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         nibbattati sadā mama .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ pallaṅkamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pallaṅkassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Vīsakappasahassamhi           suvaṇṇābhā tayo janā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 255]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pallaṅkadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pallaṅkadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
chattathambho ca vedī ca       parivārummapupphiyo    
anulomo ca maggo ca       phalako ca vaṭaṃsako    
pallaṅkadāyi gāthāyo       chappaññāsa pakittitā .

Chattavaggo paṇṇarasamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 256]*

Soḷasamo bandhujīvakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ bandhujīvakattherāpadānaṃ (151)    
[153] candaṃva vimalaṃ suddhaṃ          vippasannamanāvilaṃ    
nandibhavaparikkhīṇaṃ            tiṇṇaṃ loke visattikaṃ .

Nibbāpayantaṃ janataṃ          tiṇṇaṃ tārayataṃ muniṃ [^256-1]    
vanasmiṃ [^256-2] jhāyamānantaṃ      ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ .

Bandhujīvakapupphāni           laggitvā [^256-3] suttake ahaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito sattamake kappe       manujindo mahāyaso    
samantacakkhu nāmāsiṃ         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bandhujīvako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .L    
bandhujīvakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 257]*

Dutiyaṃ tambapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (152)    
[154] parakammāyane yutto        aparādhaṃ akāsahaṃ    
vanantaṃ atidhāvissaṃ [^257-1]       bhayaverasamappito .

Pupphitaṃ pādapaṃ disvā        piṇḍibandhaṃ [^257-2] sunimmitaṃ    
tambapupphaṃ gahetvāna        bodhiyaṃ okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Sammajjitvāna taṃ bodhiṃ       pāṭaliṃ pādaputtamaṃ    
pallaṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna        bodhimūlaṃ [^257-3] upāvisiṃ .

Gatamaggaṃ gavesantā         āgañchacha mama santikaṃ    
te ca disvānahaṃ tattha       āvajjiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Vanditvā ca ahaṃ bodhiṃ       vippasannena cetasā    
anekatāle papatiṃ          giridugge bhayānake .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca tatiye kappe       rājā sammasito [^257-4] ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tambapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tambapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^257-1] ma. Abhidhāvissaṃ . [^257-2] yu. Piṇḍibaddhaṃ . [^257-3] ma. Yu. Bodhimūle .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 258]*

Tatiyaṃ vīthisammajjakattherāpadānaṃ (153)    
[155] udentaṃ sataraṃsīva           pītaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ    
paṇṇarase [^258-1] yathā candaṃ     niyyantaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Aṭṭhasaṭṭhisahassāni          sabbe khīṇāsavā ahuṃ    
parivāriṃsu sambuddhaṃ          dipadindaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Sammijjitvāna taṃ vīthiṃ        niyyante lokanāyake    
ussāpesiṃ dhajaṃ tattha        vippasannena cetasā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dhajaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito catutthake kappe       rājāhosiṃ mahabbalo    
sabbākārena sampanno      sudhajo iti vissuto .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vīthisammajjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vīthisammajjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ kakkārupūjakattherāpadānaṃ (154)    
[156] devaputto ahaṃ santo       pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ    
kakkārupupphaṃ paggayha        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 259]*

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca navame kappe       rājā sattuttamo ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kakkārupūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kakkārupūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ mandāravapupphapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (155)    
[157] devaputto ahaṃ santo       pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ    
mandāravena pupphena        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Sattāhaṃ chadanaṃ āsi         dibbamālyaṃ [^259-1] tathāgate    
sabbe janā samāgantvā     namassiṃsu tathāgataṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ [^259-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca dasame kappe       rājāhosiṃ jutindharo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 260]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mandāravapupphapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mandāravapupphapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ kadambapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (156)    
[158] himavantassavidūre           kukkuṭo nāma pabbato    
tamhi pabbatapādamhi         satta buddhā vasanti te .

Kadambaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        dīparājaṃva uggataṃ    
ubhohatthehi paggayha        satta buddhe samokiriṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      sattāsuṃ [^260-1] pupphanāmakā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadambapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kadambapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 261]*

Sattamaṃ tiṇasūlakattherāpadānaṃ (157)    
[159] himavantassavidūre           bhūtagaṇo nāma pabbato    
vasateko jino tattha        sayambhū lokanissaṭo .

Tiṇasūle gahetvāna         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ    
ekūnasatasahassakappaṃ         na vinipātako .

Ito ekādase kappe      ekosiṃ [^261-1] dharaṇīruho    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasūlako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇasūlakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ nāgapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (158)    
[160] suvaccho nāma nāmena       brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
purakkhato sasissehi         vasati [^261-2] pabbatantare .

Padumuttaro nāma jino       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi mama santikaṃ [^261-3] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 262]*

Vehāyase caṅkamati         dīpo [^262-1] pajjalite tathā    
hāsaṃ [^262-2] mamaṃ viditvāna      pakkāmi pācināmukho .

Tañca acchariyaṃ disvā        abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
nāgapupphaṃ gahetvāna        gatamaggamhi okiriṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ okiriṃ [^262-3] ahaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse [^262-4] ito kappe   rājā āsiṃ mahāratho    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nāgapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ punnāgapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (159)    
[161] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha         vasāmi luddako ahaṃ    
punnāgaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ .

Taṃ pupphaṃ ocinitvāna        sugandhaṃ gandhagandhikaṃ [^262-5]    
thūpaṃ karitvāna puḷine        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

#[^262-1] ma. Dhūpāyati ... . Yu. Dhūpoti jalate tathā . [^262-2] yu. Vehāsaṃ mama ditvāna .

#[^262-3] yu. Abhiropayiṃ . [^262-4] ma. Ekatiṃse kappasate rājā āsi mahāraho . Yu ... Āsiṃ maharatho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 263]*

Dvenavute ito kappe     yaṃ pupphaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekamhi navute kappe      eko āsiṃ tamonudo    
sattaratanasampanno         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso        vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā        kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā punnāgapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Punnāgapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ kumudadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (160)    
[162] himavantassavidūre           mahājātassaro ahu    
padumuppalasañchanno          puṇḍarīkasamohito [^263-1] .

Kukkuṭo nāma nāmena       tatthāsiṃ sakuṇo tadā    
sīlavā vattasampanno        puññāpuññesu kovido .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
jātassarassavidūre          sañcarittha mahāmuni .

Jalajaṃ kumudaṃ bhitvā [^263-2]       upanesiṃ mahesinoǣ    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         paṭiggahi mahāmuni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 264]*

Tañca dānaṃ daditvāhaṃ [^264-1]     sukkamūlena codito    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Soḷaseto kappasate        āsuṃ varuṇanāmakā    
aṭṭha ete janā tattha      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumudadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kumudadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
bandhujīvatambapupphi           vīthi kakkārupupphiyo    
mandāravo kadambi ca        sūlako [^264-2] nāgapupphiyo    
punnāgo komudī gāthā      chappaññāsa pakittitā .

Bandhujīvakavaggo soḷasamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 265]*

Sattarasamo supāricariyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ supāricariyattherāpadānaṃ (161)    
[163] padumo nāma nāmena        dipadindo narāsabho    
pavanā abhinikkhamma          dhammaṃ deseti cakkhumā .

Yakkhānaṃ samayo āsi        avidūre mahesino    
yena kiccena sampattā      ajjhāpekkhiṃsu tāvade .

Buddhassa giramaññāya         amatassava [^265-1] desanaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         appoṭhetvā [^265-2] upaṭṭhahiṃ .

Suciṇṇassa phalaṃ passaṃ [^265-3]      upaṭṭhānassa satthuno    
tiṃsakappasahassesu           duggatiṃ nopapajjahaṃ .

Ūnatiṃse kappasate          samalaṅkatanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti    
itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supāricariyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Supāricariyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 266]*

Dutiyaṃ kaṇaverapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (162)    
[164] siddhattho nāma bhagavā       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
purakkhato sāvakehi         nagaraṃ paṭipajjatha .

Rañño antepure āsiṃ      gopako abhisammato    
pāsāde upaviṭṭhohaṃ        addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Kaṇaveraṃ gahetvāna         bhikkhusaṅghe samokiriṃ    
buddhassa visuṃ katvāna        tato bhiyyo samokiriṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ [^266-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattāsītimhito kappe       caturāsuṃ mahiddhikā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇaverapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kaṇaverapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ khajjadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (163)    
[165] tissassa [^266-2] kho bhagavato     pubbe phalamadāsahaṃ    
nāḷikerañca pādāsiṃ        khajjakaṃ abhisammataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 267]*

Buddhassa tamahaṃ datvā        tissassa tu mahesino    
modāmahaṃ kāmakāmī         upapajjiṃ yamicchakaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito terasakappamhi         rājā indasamo ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā  khajjadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Khajjadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ desapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (164)    
[166] atthadassī tu bhagavā         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
abbhuggantvāna vehāsaṃ      gacchanto [^267-1] anilañjase .

Yamhi dese ṭhito satthā     abbhuggacchi mahāmuni    
tāhaṃ desaṃ apūjesiṃ         pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        addasaṃ yaṃ mahāmuniṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         desapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 268]*

Ekādase kappasate        gosujātasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā desapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Desapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ kaṇikārachadaniyattherāpadānaṃ (165)    
[167] vessabhū nāma sambuddho      lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
divāvihārāya muni          ogāhayi [^268-1] mahāvanaṃ .

Kaṇikāraṃ ocinitvā         chattaṃ katvānahantadā    
pupphacchadanaṃ katvāna         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito vīsatikappamhi          soṇṇābhā aṭṭha khattiyā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 269]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇikārachadaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kaṇikārachadaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ sappidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (166)    
[168] phusso nāmāsi bhagavā       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
gacchate vīthiyā vīro        nibbāpento mahājanaṃ .

Anupubbena bhagavā          āgañchi mama santikaṃ    
tatohaṃ patta paggayha        sappitelaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ sappimadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sappidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chappaññāse ito kappe     eko āsiṃ samodako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sappidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sappidāyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 270]*

Sattamaṃ yudhikapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (167)    
[169] candabhāgānadītīre          anusotaṃ vajāmahaṃ    
sayambhuṃ addasaṃ tattha         sālarājaṃva phullitaṃ [^270-1] .

Pupphaṃ yudhikamādāya          upagacchiṃ mahāmuniṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito  kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasaṭṭhimhito kappe       eko sāmuddharo ahu [^270-2]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yudhikapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Yudhikapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ dussadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (168)    
[170] tivarāyaṃ pure ramme        rājaputto ahaṃ [^270-3] tadā    
paṇṇākāraṃ labhitvāna        upasantassadāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 271]*

Adhivāsesi bhagavā          vatthaṃ hatthena āmasi    
siddhattho adhivāsetvā      vehāsaṃ nabhamuggami .

Buddhassa gacchamānassa        dussā dhāvanti pacchato    
tattha cittaṃ pasādesiṃ        buddho no aggapuggalo .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasaṭṭhimhito kappe       cakkavatti tadā ahu    
parisuddhoti nāmena         manujindo mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dussadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dussadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ samādapakattherāpadānaṃ (169)    
[171] bandhumatiyā nagare          mahāpūgagaṇo ahu    
tesāhaṃ pavaro āsiṃ        mama paṭṭhacarā [^271-1] va te .

Te sabbe sannipātetvā    puññakamme samādayiṃ    
māḷaṃ karissāma saṅghassa      puññakkhettamanuttaraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 272 -->
<a id="page-272"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 272]*

Sādhūti te paṭissutvā       mama chandavasānugā    
niṭṭhāpetvāva [^272-1] taṃ māḷaṃ   vipassissa adamhase .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ māḷamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         māḷadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekūnasaṭṭhikappamhi          eko āsi janādhipo    
āvelo [^272-2] nāma nāmena   cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā samādapako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Samādapakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pañcaṅguliyattherāpadānaṃ (170)    
[172] tisso nāmāsi bhagavā       lokanātho [^272-3] narāsabho    
pavisantaṃ [^272-4] gandhakuṭiṃ        vihārakusalaṃ [^272-5] muniṃ .

Sugandhamālyamādāya         agamāsiṃ jinantikaṃ    
appasaddho [^272-6] ca sambuddhe   pañcaṅgulimadāsahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ gandhamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pañcaṅgulissidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^272-1] ma. Yu. ... Ca . [^272-2] ma. Ādeyyo . Yu. Āveyyo . [^272-3] ma. Yu. Lokajeṭṭho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 273 -->
<a id="page-273"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 273]*

Dvesattatimhito kappe      rājā āsiṃ sayampako [^273-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañcaṅguliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pañcaṅguliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
supācarikaṇaverī            khajjako desapūjako    
kaṇikāro sappidado         yudhiko dussadāyako    
māḷo ca pañcaṅguliko       catupaññāsagāthakāti .

Supāricariyavaggo sattarasamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 274]*

Aṭṭhārasamo kumudavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ kumudamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (171)    
[173] pabbate himavantamhi         mahājātassaro ahu    
tatthajo rakkhaso āsiṃ       ghorarūpo mahabbalo .

Kumudaṃ pupphitaṃ [^274-1] tattha       cakkamattāni jāyare    
ocināmi cahaṃ [^274-2] pupphaṃ      phalino samitaṃ [^274-3] tadā .

Atthadassī tu bhagavā         dipadindo narāsabho    
pupphaṃ saṅkocitaṃ disvā       āgacchi mama santike [^274-4] .

Upāgatañca sambuddhaṃ         devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
sabbañca pupphaṃ paggayha       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Yāvatā himavantasmiṃ [^274-5]      yāva [^274-6] mālā tadā ahu    
tāvacchadanasampanno [^274-7]      agamāsi tathāgato .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       sattāhesuṃ janādhipā    
sahassarathanāmā te         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^274-1] po. Ma. Yu. Pupphate . [^274-2] ma. Cataṃ . [^274-3] po. Ma. Yu. Samitiṃ .

#[^274-4] ma. Yu. Sabbattha santikaṃ . [^274-5] ma. Himavantantā . Yu. Himavantato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 275]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumudamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kumudamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ nisseṇīdāyakattherāpadānaṃ (172)    
[174] koṇḍaññassa bhagavato        lokanāthassa [^275-1] tādino    
ārohatthāya pāsādaṃ       nisseṇī kāritā mayā .

Tena cittappasādena        anubhotvāna sampadā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Ekatiṃsamhi kappānaṃ         sahassamhi tayo mahā [^275-2]    
sambahulā nāma rājāno     cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nisseṇīdāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nisseṇīdāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ rattiyapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (173)    
[175] migaluddo pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane ahaṃ    
vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ         devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 276 -->
<a id="page-276"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 276]*

Rattikaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        kuṭajaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ    
samūlaṃ paggahetvāna         upanesiṃ mahesino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca aṭṭhame kappe      suppasannasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti [^276-1] mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rattiyapupphiyo [^276-2] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Rattiyapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ udapānadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (174)    
[176] vipassino bhagavato          udapāno kato mayā    
piṇḍapātaṃ [^276-3] gahetvāna     niyyādesiṃ ahaṃ tadā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         udapānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^276-1] ma. Rājāhosiṃ mahabbalo . Yu. Rājāhosi ... . [^276-2] ma. Yu. Rattipupphiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 277 -->
<a id="page-277"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 277]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udadānadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udapānadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ sīhāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (175)    
[177] nibbute lokanāthamhi        padumuttaranāyake    
pasannacitto sumano         sīhāsanamadāsahaṃ .

Bahūhi gandhamālehi [^277-1]       paralokasukhāvaho [^277-2]    
tattha pūjaṃ karitvāna         nibbāyati bahujjano .

Pasannacitto sumano         vanditvā bodhimuttamaṃ    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Paṇṇarasasahassamhi           kappānaṃ aṭṭha āsu te    
siluccayasanāmā ca [^277-3]       rājāno cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .U    
sīhāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 278 -->
<a id="page-278"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 278]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ maggadattikattherāpadānaṃ (176)    
[178] anomadassi bhagavā          dipadindo narāsabho    
diṭṭhadhammasukhatthāya          abbhokāsamhi caṅkami .

Uddhate pāde pupphāni      sobhaṃ [^278-1] muddhani tiṭṭhare    
pasannacitto sumano         vanditvā pupphamokiriṃ .

Vīsakappasahassamhi           ito pañca janā ahuṃ    
pupphacchadaniyā nāma         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggadattiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Maggadattikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ ekadīpiyattherāpadānaṃ (177)    
[179] padumuttarassa munino         salaḷe bodhimuttame    
pasannacitto sumano         ekadīpaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Bhave nibbattamānamhi        nibbatte puññasañcaye    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 279 -->
<a id="page-279"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 279]*

Soḷase kappasahasse        ito [^279-1] te caturo janā    
candābhā nāma nāmena      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekadīpiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekadīpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ maṇipūjakattherāpadānaṃ (178)    
[180] orena himavantassa         nadikā sampavattatha    
tassā cānūpakhettamhi       sayambhū vasate tadā .

Maṇiṃ paggayha pallaṅkaṃ        sādhucittaṃ manoramaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ maṇiṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca dvādase kappe     sataraṃsīsanāmakā    
aṭṭha [^279-2] te rājāno āsuṃ  cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 280 -->
<a id="page-280"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 280]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maṇipūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Maṇipūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ tikicchakattherāpadānaṃ (179)    
[181] nagare bandhumatiyā          vejjo āsiṃ susikkhito    
āturānaṃ sudukkhīnaṃ [^280-1]       mahājanasukhāvaho .

Byādhitaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā        sīlavantaṃ mahājutiṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         bhesajjamadadiṃ tadā .

Arogo āsi teneva       samaṇo saṃvutindriyo    
asoko nāma nāmena       upaṭṭhāko vipassino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ osathamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhesajjassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca aṭṭhame kappe      sabbosathasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikicchako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tikicchakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 281 -->
<a id="page-281"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 281]*

Dasamaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākattherāpadānaṃ (180)    
[182] vessabhumhi bhagavati          āsiṃ [^281-1] ārāmiko ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         upaṭṭhiṃ saṅghamuttamaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito te sattame kappe     sattevāsuṃ samotthakā [^281-2]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saṅghupaṭṭhāko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saṅghupaṭṭhākattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kumudo atha nisseṇī         rattiko udapānado    
sīhāsanī maggappado [^281-3]      ekadīpī maṇippado    
tikicchako upaṭṭhāko        ekūnapaññāsagāthakāti .

Kumudavaggo aṭṭhārasamo .

#[^281-1] ma. Yu. Ahosārāmiko ahaṃ . [^281-2] ma. Samodakā . Yu. Samotthatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 282 -->
<a id="page-282"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 282]*

Ekūnavīsatimo kuṭajapupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ kuṭajapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (181)    
[183] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         sataraṃsīva uttamaṃ [^282-1]    
disaṃ anuvilokentaṃ          gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Kuṭajaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā         saṃvitthakasamotthakaṃ [^282-2]    
rukkhato ocinitvāna        phussassa abhiropayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito sattarase kappe       tayo āsiṃsu [^282-3] pupphitā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭajapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kuṭajapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ bandhujīvakattherāpadānaṃ (182)    
[184] sobhito [^282-4] nāma sambuddho   sayambhū sabbhivaṇṇito    
samādhiṃ so samāpanno       nisīdi pabbatantare .

#[^282-1] ma. Yu. Uggataṃ . [^282-2] ma. Saṃvitathatasamotthataṃ . Yu. Haṃ vitthatasamotthaṭaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 283 -->
<a id="page-283"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 283]*

Jātassare gavesanto       dakajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
bandhujīvakapupphāni           addasaṃ samanantaraṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        upagacchiṃ mahāmuniṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         sobhitassābhiropayiṃ [^283-1] .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito catuddase kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
samuddakappo nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bandhujīvako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bandhujīvakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kotumbariyattherāpadānaṃ (183)    
[185] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
appameyyaṃva udadhiṃ          uddhataṃ [^283-2] dharaṇiṃ yathā .

Puretaṃ [^283-3] devasaṅghena      nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā .

Haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 284 -->
<a id="page-284"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 284]*

Satta pupphāni paggayha       kotumbaraṃ samākulaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito vīsatikappamhi          mahānelasanāmako    
eko āsiṃ mahātejo      cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kotumbariyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kotumbariyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ pañcahatthiyattherāpadānaṃ (184)    
[186] tisso nāmāsi bhagavā       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
purakkhato sāvakehi         rathiyaṃ paṭipajjatha .

Pañca uppalahatthā ca        catuoṭṭhapitā [^284-1] mayā    
āhutiṃ dātukāmohaṃ         puttomhi [^284-2] hitasiddhiyā .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
buddharaṃsīhi [^284-3] phuṭṭhomhi [^284-4]   pūjesiṃ dipaduttamaṃ .

#[^284-1] ma. Cāturā ṭhapitā mayā . Yu. Caturoṭṭhapitā mayā . [^284-2] ma. Paggaṇhiṃ vatasiddhiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 285 -->
<a id="page-285"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 285]*

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito terasakappamhi         pañca sulabhasammatā [^285-1]    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañcahatthiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pañcahatthiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ isimuggadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (185)    
[187] udentaṃ sataraṃsīva           pītaraṃsīva [^285-2] bhāṇumaṃ    
kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva            padumuttaranāyakaṃ .

Isimuggaṃ [^285-3] nimantetvā     madhukhudde anīlake    
pāsādepi [^285-4] ṭhito santo   adāsiṃ lokabandhuno .

Aṭṭhasatasahassāni           ahesuṃ buddhasāvakā    
sabbesaṃ patta pūrentaṃ       tato vāpi [^285-5] bahūtaraṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        sukkamūlena codito    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

#[^285-1] ma. Pañca susabhasammatā . Yu. Pañcāsu sabhāsammatā . [^285-2] yu. Uggataṃ iva ... .

#[^285-3] ma. Isimuggāni pisitvā . Yu. Isimuggā nisandetvā . [^285-4] ma. Yu. ... Va .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 286 -->
<a id="page-286"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 286]*

Cattāḷīsamhi sahasse        kappānaṃ aṭṭhatiṃsate    
mahisamantanāmā [^286-1] te      cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā isimuggadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Isimuggadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ bodhiupaṭṭhāyakattherāpadānaṃ (186)    
[188] nagare rammavatiyā          āsiṃ murajavādako [^286-2]    
niccupaṭṭhānayuttomhi        gatohaṃ bodhimuttamaṃ .

Sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhitvā       sukkamūlena codito    
aṭṭhārase kappasate        duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Pañcadase kappasate         itohosiṃ [^286-3] janādhipo    
damatho [^286-4] nāma nāmena     cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bodhiupaṭṭhāyako [^286-5] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bodhiupaṭṭhāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^286-1] ma. Isimuggasanāmā te . [^286-2] yu. Murajanāmako . [^286-3] yu. Rājāhosiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 287]*

Sattamaṃ ekacintikattherāpadānaṃ (187)    
[189] yadā devo devakāyā      cavate āyusaṅkhayā    
tayo saddā niccharanti       devānaṃ anumodataṃ .

Ito bho sugatiṃ gaccha        manussānaṃ sahabyataṃ    
manussabhūto saddhamme        labha saddhaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Sā te saddhā niviṭṭhassa [^287-1]  mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā    
yāvajīvaṃ asaṃhirā           saddhamme supavedite .

Kāyena kusalaṃ katvā        vācāya kusalaṃ bahuṃ    
manasā kusalaṃ katvā         abyāpajjhaṃ nirūpadhiṃ .

Tato upacitaṃ puññaṃ          katvā dānena taṃ bahuṃ    
aññepi macce saddhamme     brahmacariye nivesaya .

Imāya anukampāya          devadevaṃ [^287-2] yathāvidū    
bhavantaṃ anumodanti          ehi deva punappunaṃ .

Saṃviggohaṃ [^287-3] tadā āsiṃ     devasaṅghe samāgate    
kaṃsu nāma ahaṃ yoniṃ         gamissāmi ito cuto .

Mama saṃvegamaññāya          samaṇo bhāvitindriyo    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgacchi mama santike .

Sumano nāma nāmena        padumuttarasāvako    
atthadhammānusāsitvā        saṃvejesi mamaṃ tadā .

#[^287-1] ma. Niviṭṭhāssa . Yu. Naviṭṭhāya . [^287-2] ma. Devādevaṃ . [^287-3] ma. Saṃvego me tadā āsi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 288]*

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        buddhe cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
abhivādetvāpi [^288-1] sambuddhaṃ   tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Upapajjiṃ sa tattheva         sukkamūlena codito    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekacintiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekacintikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ tikaṇṇipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (188)    
[190] devabhūto ahaṃ santo        accharāhi purakkhato    
pubbakammaṃ saritvāna         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ .

Tikaṇṇipupphaṃ paggayha         sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
buddhamhi [^288-2] abhiropesiṃ      vipassimhi narāsabhe .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tesattatimhito kappe       caturosuṃ [^288-3] ramuttamā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

#[^288-1] ma. Yu. Taṃ dhīraṃ abhivādetvā . [^288-2] yu. Buddhassa . [^288-3] yu. Caturāsuṃ naruttamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 289]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikaṇṇipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tikaṇṇipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ ekacāriyattherāpadānaṃ (189)    
[191] tāvatiṃsesu devesu         mahāghoso tadā ahu    
buddho ca loke nibbāti     mayañcamha sarāgino .

Tesaṃ saṃvegajātānaṃ         sokasallasamaṅginaṃ    
sabalena upatthaddho         agamaṃ buddhasantike .

Mandāravaṃ gahetvāna        saṅgīti [^289-1] abhinimmitaṃ    
parinibbānakālamhi [^289-2]       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Sabbe devānumodiṃsu        accharāyo ca me tadā    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Saṭṭhikappasahassamhi          ito soḷasa te janā    
mahāmallajanā nāma         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 290]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekacāriyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekacāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ tivaṇṭipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (190)    
[192] abhibhuṃ [^290-1] theraṃ panijjhāma     sabbe saṅgamma te mayaṃ [^290-2]    
tesaṃ nijjhāyamānānaṃ        pariḷāho ajāyatha .

Sunando nāma nāmena       buddhassa sāvako tadā    
dhammadassissa munino         āgañchi mama santike .

Ye me paṭṭhacarā āsuṃ      te me pupphaṃ aduṃ tadā    
tāhaṃ pupphaṃ gahetvāna       sāvake abhiropayiṃ  .

Sohaṃ kālaṃ kato tattha       punāpi upapajjahaṃ    
aṭṭhārase kappasate        vinipātaṃ na gañchahaṃ .

Teraseto kappasate        aṭṭhāsuṃ dhūmaketuno    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tivaṇṭipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tivaṇṭipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 291 -->
<a id="page-291"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 291]*

Uddānaṃ    
kuṭajā [^291-1] bandhujīvī ca       kotumbarikahatthiyo    
isimuggo ca bodhi ca        ekacintī tikaṇṇiko    
ekacārī tivaṇṭī ca         gāthā saṭṭhī [^291-2] pakittitāti .

Kuṭajapupphiyavaggo ekūnavīsatimo .

vīsatimo tamālapupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tamālapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (191)    
[193] cullāsītisahassāni          thambho sovaṇṇayo ahu    
devalaṭṭhipaṭibhāgaṃ           vimānaṃ me sunimmitaṃ .

Tamālapupphaṃ paggayha         vippasannena cetasā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito vīsatime kappe        candatittoti ekako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 292]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tamālapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tamālapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ tiṇasanthāradāyakattherāpadānaṃ (192)    
[194] yadā [^292-1] vanavāsiko isi     tiṇaṃ lāyāmi satthuno    
sabbe padakkhiṇāvattā       paṭhabyā nipatiṃsu te .

Tamahaṃ tiṇamādāya           santhariṃ dharaṇimuttame    
tīṇeva [^292-2] tālapattāni      āharitvānahaṃ tadā .

Tidaṇḍe [^292-3] chadanaṃ katvā     siddhatthassa adāsahaṃ    
sattāhaṃ dhārayuṃ tattha [^292-4]     devamānusa satthuno .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ tiṇaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tiṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcasaṭṭhimhito kappe       cattārosuṃ mahaddhanā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasanthāradāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇasanthāradāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^292-1] ma. Yadā vanavāsiṃ isi tiṇaṃ lāyati satthuno . Yu. Yaṃ ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 293]*

Tatiyaṃ khaṇḍaphulliyattherāpadānaṃ [^293-1] (193)    
[195] phussassa kho bhagavato        thūpo āsi mahāvane    
kuñjarehi tadā bhinno       saṃrūḷho [^293-2] pādapo tahiṃ .

Visamañca samaṃ katvā         sudhāpiṇḍaṃ adāsahaṃ    
tilokagaruno tassa          guṇehi paritosito .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sudhāpiṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasattatikappamhi          jitasenāsuṃ soḷasa    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khaṇḍaphulliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Khaṇḍaphulliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ asokapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (194)    
[196] tipurāyaṃ [^293-3] pure ramme     rājuyyānaṃ ahu tadā    
uyyānapālo tatthāsiṃ       rañño paṭṭhacaro ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 294]*

Padumo nāma nāmena        sayambhū sappato [^294-1] ahu    
nisinnaṃ puṇḍarīkamhi          chāyā na jahitaṃ muniṃ .

Asokaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        piṇḍibhāraṃ sudassanaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         jalajuttamanāmino .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattatimhi ito kappe       soḷasa araṇañjahā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā asokapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Asokapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ aṅkolakattherāpadānaṃ (195)    
[197] aṅkolaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       mālāvarasamogataṃ [^294-2]    
ocinitvāna taṃ pupphaṃ        agamaṃ buddhasantike .

Siddhattho tamhi samaye       paṭilīno mahāmuni    
muhuttaṃ paṭimānetvā        guhāyaṃ pupphamokiriṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 295]*

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ [^295-1] phalaṃ .

Chattiṃsamhi ito kappe       āseko devagajjito    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aṅkolako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aṅkolakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ kisalayapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (196)    
[198] nagare dvāravatiyā         mālāvaccho mamaṃ ahu    
udapāno ca tattheva        pādapānaṃ virohano .

Sabalena upatthaddho         siddhattho aparājito    
mamānukampamāno so        gacchate anilañjase .

Aññaṃ kiñci na passāmi       pūjāyoggaṃ mahesino    
asokapallavaṃ disvā         ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ .

Buddhassa te kisalayā        gacchato yanti pacchato    
tāhaṃ [^295-2] disvāna saṃvijiṃ      aho buddhassuḷāratā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 296]*

Catunavute ito kappe       pallavaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse [^296-1] ito kappe    eko ekissaro ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kisalayapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kisalayapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ tindukadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (197)    
[199] giriduggacaro āsiṃ          makkaṭo thāmavegiko    
phalinaṃ tindukaṃ disvā         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ .

Nikkhamitvā katipāhaṃ         viciniṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         siddhatthaṃ tibhavantaguṃ .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā loke anuttaro    
khīṇāsavasahassehi           āgañchi mama santike .

Pāmujjaṃ janayitvāna         phalahattho upāgamiṃ    
paṭiggahesi bhagavā          sabbaññū vadataṃ varo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 297]*

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         upanandasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tindukadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tindukadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ girinelamuṭṭhipūjakattherāpadānaṃ [^297-1] (198)    
[200] sumedho nāma bhagavā        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
pacchime anukampāya         padhānaṃ padahi jino .

Tassa caṅkamamānassa         dipadindassa tādino
