girinelassa pupphānaṃ         muṭṭhiṃ buddhassa ropayiṃ .

Tena cittappasādena        sukkamūlena codito    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Tevīsatikappasate           sunelo nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          eko āsiṃ mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 298]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā girinelamuṭṭhipūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Girinelamuṭṭhipūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ tikaṇḍipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ [^298-1] (199)    
[201] sumaṅgaloti nāmena         sayambhū aparājito    
pavanā nikkhamitvāna         nagaraṃ pāvisi jino .

Piṇḍacāraṃ caritvāna         nikkhamma nagarā muni    
katakiccova sambuddho        so vasi vanamantare .

Tikaṇḍipupphaṃ [^298-2] paggayha      buddhassa abhiropayiṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         sayambhussa mahesino .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chaḷāsītimhito kappe        apaselāsanāmako [^298-3]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^298-1] ma. Kiṃkaṇikapupphiyat ... . [^298-2] ma. Kiṃkaṇipupphaṃ . [^298-3] ma. Apilāsisanāmako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 299]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikaṇḍipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tikaṇḍipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ yūthikapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (200)    
[202] padumuttaro nāma jino       āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
pavanā nikkhamitvāna         vihāraṃ yāti cakkhumā .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        yūthikaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         mettacittassa tādino .

Tena cittappasādena        anubhotvāna sampadā    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Ito paññāsakappesu        eko āsi [^299-1] janādhipo    
samitanandano nāma          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yūthikapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Yūthikapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 300]*

uddānaṃ    
tamāli tiṇasanthāro         khaṇḍaphulli asokiyo    
aṅkolako kisalayo         tinduko nelapupphiyo    
tikaṇḍiko ca yūthiko         gāthā paññāsa aṭṭha ca .

Tamālapupphiyavaggo vīsatimo .

Atha vagguddānaṃ    
bhikkhadāyi parivāro         sereyyo sobhito tathā    
chatto ca bandhujīvi ca        supāricariyopica    
kumudo kuṭajo ceva         tamāli dasamo kato    
cha satāni ca gāthānaṃ        chasaṭṭhi ca tatuttari .

Bhikkhavaggadasakaṃ dutiyasatakaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 301]*

Ekavīsatimo kaṇikārapupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ kaṇikārapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (201)    
[203] kaṇikāraṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       ocinitvānahaṃ tadā    
tissassa abhiropesiṃ         oghatiṇṇassa tādino .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcatiṃse ito kappe       aruṇapāloti [^301-1] vissuto    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇikārapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kaṇikārapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ minelapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ [^301-2] (202)    
[204] suvaṇṇavaṇṇo bhagavā         sataraṃsī pabhāvaro [^301-3]    
caṅkamanaṃ samārūḷho         mettacitto sikhi [^301-4] sato .

Pasannacitto sumano         thometvā [^301-5] ñāṇamuttamaṃ    
minelapupphaṃ paggayha         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

#[^301-1] ma. Aruṇapālīti . [^301-2] yu. Vinela ... . [^301-3] ma. Yu. Patāpavā . [^301-4] yu. Sikhī sito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 302]*

ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekūnatiṃse kappamhi         sumeghaghananāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā minelapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Minelapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kiṃkaṇikapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (203)    
[205] kañcanagghiyasaṅkāso         sabbaññū lokanāyako    
odakaṃ dahamoggayha         sināyi lokanāyako .

Paggayha kiṃkaṇipupphaṃ          vipassissābhiropayiṃ    
udaggacitto sumano         dipadindassa tādino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīsatikappamhi           rājā bhimaratho ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 303]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃkaṇikapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kiṃkaṇikapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ taraṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (204)    
[206] atthadassī tu bhagavā         dipadindo narāsabho    
purakkhato sāvakehi         gaṅgātīraṃ upāgami .

Samatitti kākapeyyā        gaṅgā āsi duruttarā    
uttārayiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ         buddhañca dipaduttamaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Terasito kappasate         pañca sabbobhavā ahuṃ    
sattaratanasampannā [1]     cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Taraṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^303-1] ma.   Pacchime ca bhave asmiṃ       jhātohaṃ brāhmaṇe kule  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 304]*

Pañcamaṃ nigguṇḍipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (205)    
[207] vipassissa bhagavato          āsiṃ ārāmiko ahaṃ    
nigguṇḍipupphaṃ paggayha        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ .

Duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcattiṃse [^304-1] ito kappe   eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
mahāpatāpo nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nigguṇḍipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nigguṇḍipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ udakadāyakattherāpadānaṃ [206]    
[208] bhuñjantaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā        vippasannamanāvilaṃ    
ghaṭenodakamādāya          siddhatthassa adāsahaṃ .

Nimmalo homahaṃ ajja        vimalo khīṇasaṃsayo    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        phalaṃ nibbattate mama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 305 -->
<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 305]*

Catunavute ito kappe       udakaṃ [^305-1] adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         udakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekasaṭṭhimhito kappe       ekova vimalo ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo    
paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udakadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .}    
sattamaṃ salaḷamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (207)    
[209] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     siddhatthaṃ narasārathiṃ .

Dhanana abejjhaṃ [^305-2] katvāna     usuṃ sandhāyahaṃ [^305-3] tadā    
pupphaṃ savaṇṭaṃ chetvāna       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekapaññāsito kappe       eko āsi jutindharo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

#[^305-1] ma. Udakaṃ yamadāsahaṃ . Yu. Udakaṃ yaṃ tadā ahaṃ . [^305-2] ma. Advejjhaṃ . Yu. Adejjhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 306]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salaḷamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Salaḷamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ koraṇḍapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (208)    
[210] akkantañca padaṃ disvā       cakkālaṅkārabhūsitaṃ    
padenānupadaṃ yanto         vipassissa mahesino .

Koraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       samūlaṃ pūjitaṃ mayā    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      avandiṃ padamuttamaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ [^306-1] phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         eko vītamalo ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā koraṇḍapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Koraṇḍapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 307]*

Navamaṃ ādhāradāyakattherāpadānaṃ (209)    
[211] ādhārakaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        sikhino lokabandhuno    
dhāremi paṭhaviṃ sabbaṃ         kevalaṃ vasudhaṃ imaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Sattavīse ito kappe       ahesuṃ caturo janā    
samantavaruṇā [^307-1] nāma       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ādhāradāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ādhāradāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pāpanivāriyattherāpadānaṃ [^307-2] (210)    
[212] tissassa [^307-3] hi [^307-4] bhagavato   devadevassa tādino .

Ekacchattaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       vippasannena cetasā .

Nivutaṃ hoti me pāpaṃ        kusalassūpasampadā    
ākāse chattaṃ dhārenti     pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^307-1] ma. Samantavaraṇā nāma . [^307-2] yu. Vātātapanivāriya ... . [^307-3] yu. Tissassāhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 308 -->
<a id="page-308"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 308]*

carimaṃ vattate mayhaṃ         bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ chattamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Dvesattati ito kappe      aṭṭhāsiṃsu janādhipā    
mahānidānanāmena          rājāno cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāpanivāriyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pāpanivāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kaṇikāro minelañca         kiṃkaṇi tāraṇena ca    
nigguṇḍipupphaṃ dakado         salaḷo ca kuraṇḍako    
ādhāradānaṃ pāpañca        aṭṭhatāḷīsa gāthakā .

Kaṇikārapupphiyavaggo ekavīsatimo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 309]*

Bāvīsatimo hatthivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ hatthidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (211)    
[213] siddhatthassa bhagavato         dipadindassa tādino    
nāgaseṭṭho mayā dinno     īsādanto urūḷhavo .

Uttamatthaṃ anubhomi          santipadamanuttaraṃ    
nāgadānaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        sabbalokahitesino .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ nāgamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         nāgadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhasattatikappamhi          soḷasāsiṃsu khattiyā    
samantapāsādikā nāma       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā hatthidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Hatthidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ pānadhidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (212)    
[214] araññikassa jhāyino         mettacittatapassino [^309-1]    
dhammassa [^309-2] bhāvitattassa     adāsiṃ pānadhiṃ ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 310 -->
<a id="page-310"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 310]*

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
dibbayānaṃ anubhomi          pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute [^310-1] ito kappe    yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pānadhissa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasattatimhito kappe      aṭṭha āsiṃsu khattiyā    
suyānā nāma nāmena       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pānadhidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pānadhidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ saccasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (213)    
[215] vessabhū nāma bhagavā [^310-2]     bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
deseti ariyasaccāni        nibbāpento mahājanaṃ .

Paramakāruññapattomhi        samitiṃ agamāsahaṃ    
sohaṃ nisinnako santo       dhammaṃ assosi satthuno    
tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       devalokaṃ agacchahaṃ    
tiṃsa kappāni devesu        avasiṃ tatthahaṃ pure .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 311 -->
<a id="page-311"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 311]*

ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saccasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Chabbīsamhi ito kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
ekaphussitanāmova          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saccasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saccasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ekasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (214)    
[216] dumagge paṃsukūlikaṃ           laggaṃ disvāna satthuno    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        paṃsukūlaṃ avandihaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīse ito kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
amitābhoti nāmena         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 312 -->
<a id="page-312"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 312]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ raṃsisaññakattherāpadānaṃ (215)    
[217] udentaṃ sataraṃsīva           pītaraṃsīva [^312-1] bhāṇumaṃ    
byagghusabhaṃva pavaraṃ           sujātaṃ pabbatantare .

Buddhassa ānubhāvo so      jalate pabbatantare    
raṃse cittaṃ pasādetvā      kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Avasesesu kappesu         kusalaṃ karitaṃ [^312-2] mayā    
tena cittappasādena        buddhānussatiyāpica .

Tiṃsakappasahasseto          yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsakappamhi         eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
sujāto nāma nāmena       cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Raṃsisaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 313 -->
<a id="page-313"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 313]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ sandhitattherāpadānaṃ (216)    
[218] assatthe haritobhāse       saṃvirūḷhamhi pādape    
ekaṃ buddhagataṃ saññaṃ         alabhitthaṃ [^313-1] paṭissato .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā [2]    
tassā saññāya vāhasā      patto me āsavakkhayo .

Ito terasakappamhi         vaniddho [^313-3] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sandhito thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sandhitattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ tālavaṇṭadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (217)    
[219] tālavaṇṭaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        tissassa dibbacakkhuno [^313-4]    
gimhaṃ nibbāpanatthāya        pariḷāhopasantiyā .

Sannibbāpemi rāgaggiṃ       dosaggiñca taduttariṃ    
nibbāpemi ca mohaggiṃ       tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^313-1] ma. Alabhiṃhaṃ . [^313-2] yu. Duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 314 -->
<a id="page-314"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 314]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tesaṭṭhimhi ito kappe      mahārāmasanāmako [^314-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tālavaṇṭadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tālavaṇṭadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ akkantasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (218)    
[220] kusāṭakaṃ gahetvāna         upajjhāyassa pārupe [^314-2]    
mantañca anusikkhāmi         gaṇḍabhedassa [^314-3] pattiyā .

Addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
usabhaṃ pavaraṃ aggaṃ           tissaṃ buddhaṃ gajuttamaṃ [^314-4] .

Kusāṭakaṃ pattharitaṃ           akkamantaṃ naruttamaṃ    
samuggataṃ mahāvīraṃ           lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

#[^314-1] yu. Mahārāmāsunāmakā . [^314-2] ma. Yu. Upajjhāyassahaṃ pure . ... Mahabbalā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 315 -->
<a id="page-315"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 315]*

disvā taṃ lokapajjotaṃ       vimalaṃ candasannibhaṃ    
avandiṃ satthuno pāde       vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ adāsiṃ kusāṭakaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kusāṭakassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattatiṃse ito kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
sunando nāma nāmena       cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā akkantasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Akkantasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sappidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (219)    
[221] nisinno pāsādavare        nārīgaṇapurakkhato    
byādhitaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā        atināmesahaṃ gharaṃ .

Upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāvīraṃ           devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
sappitelaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        siddhatthassa mahesino .

Passaddhadarathaṃ disvā         vippasannamukhindriyaṃ    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     anusaṃsāvayiṃ pure .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 316 -->
<a id="page-316"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 316]*

Disvā maṃ supasannantaṃ        iddhiyā pāramiṃ gato    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami vīro         haṃsarājāva ambare .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sappitelassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito sattarase kappe       dutidevasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sappidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sappidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pāpanivāriyattherāpadānaṃ (220)    
[222] piyadassissa bhagavato         caṅkamaṃ sodhitaṃ mayā    
naḷakehi paṭicchannaṃ          vātātapanivāraṇaṃ .

Pāpaṃ vivajjanatthāya         kusalassūpasampadā    
kilesānaṃ pahānāya         padahiṃ satthusāsane .

Ito ekādase kappe      aggitejoti [^316-1] vissuto    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 317]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāpanivāriyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pāpanivāriyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
hatthi pānadhi saccañca        ekasaññi ca raṃsiyo    
sandhito tālavaṇṭi ca        tathā akkantasaññako    
sappi pāpanivārī ca         catupaññāsa gāthakāti .

Hatthivaggo bāvīsatimo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 318 -->
<a id="page-318"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 318]*

Tevīsatimo ālambanadāyakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ālambanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (221)    
[223] atthadassissa bhagavato        lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
ālambanaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        dipadindassa tādino .

Dharaṇiṃ paṭipajjāmi           vipulaṃ sāgarapparaṃ [^318-1]    
pāṇesu ca issariyaṃ         vattemi vasudhāya ca .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Ito dvesaṭṭhikappamhi       tayo āsiṃsu khattiyāf    
ekādassitanāmā [^318-2] te    cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ālambanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ālambanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ajinadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (222)    
[224] ekattiṃse ito kappe      gaṇasanthārako ahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 319]*

Cammakhaṇḍaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        sikhino lokabandhuno    
tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha .

Sampattiṃ anubhotvāna        kilese jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ    
dhāremi antimaṃ dehaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāsane .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      ajinaṃ yaṃ adāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ajinassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paññāsake [^319-1] kappe   rājā āsiṃ sudāyako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ajinadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ajinadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ dverataniyattherāpadānaṃ (223)    
[225] migaluddho [^319-2] pure āsiṃ     araññe kānane ahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ .

Maṃsapesi mayā dinnā        vipassissa mahesino    
sadevakasmi lokasmiṃ         issaraṃ kārayāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 320]*

Iminā maṃsadānena          ratanaṃ nibbattate mama    
duve me ratanā loke      diṭṭhadhammassa pattiyā .

Tehaṃ sabbe atibhomi        maṃsadānassa pattiyā    
gattañca mudukaṃ mayhaṃ         paññā nipuṇavedinī .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ maṃsamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         maṃsadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito catutthake kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
mahārohitanāmo so        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dverataniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dverataniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Catutthaṃ ārakkhadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (224)    
[226] siddhatthassa bhagavato         vedikā kāritā mayā [^320-1]    
ārakkho ca mayā dinno     sugatassa mahesino .

Tena kammavisesena         na passe bhayabheravaṃ    
kuhiñci upapannassa          tāso mayhaṃ na vijjati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 321 -->
<a id="page-321"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 321]*

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ vediṃ kārayiṃ pure    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito chaṭṭhamhi kappamhi       apassenasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārakkhadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ārakkhadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ abyādhikattherāpadānaṃ (225)    
[227] vipassissa bhagavato          aggisālaṃ adāsahaṃ    
byādhikānañca āvāsaṃ       uṇhodakapaṭiggahaṃ .

Tena kammenayaṃ mayhaṃ        attabhāvo sunimmito    
byādhāhaṃ nābhijānāmi       puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ sālamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         aggisālāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ca sattame kappe      eko āsiṃ narādhipo [^321-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 322 -->
<a id="page-322"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 322]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abyādhiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Abyādhikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ vakulapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (226)    
[228] nārado iti me nāmaṃ       kassapo iti maṃ vidū    
addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ          vipassiṃ devasakkataṃ .

Anubyañjanadharaṃ buddhaṃ         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
vakulapupphaṃ [^322-1] paggayha       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ [^322-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catusattatito kappe         romaso nāma khattiyo    
āmuttamālābharaṇo         sayoggabalavāhano .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vakulapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vakulapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^322-1] ma. Aṅkolapupphaṃ . Yu. Caṅkolapupphaṃ . [^322-2] ma. Yu. Pupphamabhipūjayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 323 -->
<a id="page-323"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 323]*

Sattamaṃ sovaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyattherāpadānaṃ (227)    
[229] uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyanto        addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
vaṭaṃsakaṃ gahetvāna          sovaṇṇaṃ sādhunimmitaṃ .

Sīghaṃ gato [^323-1] samāruyha      hatthikkhandhagato ahaṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sikhino lokabandhuno .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse ito kappe       eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
mahāpatāpo nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sovaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sovaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ miñjavaṭaṃsakiyattherāpadānaṃ (228)    
[230] nibbute lokanāthamhi        sikhimhi vadataṃ vare    
vaṭaṃsakehi ākiṇṇaṃ          bodhipūjaṃ akāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 324 -->
<a id="page-324"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 324]*

ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pūjamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito chabbīsatikappe         ahu meghabbhanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā miñjavaṭaṃsakiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Miñjavaṭaṃsakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sukatāveḷiyattherāpadānaṃ (229)    
[231] asito nāma nāmena        mālākāro ahaṃ tadā    
āveḷaṃ paggahetvāna       rañño dātuṃ vajāmahaṃ .

Asampattomhi rājānaṃ       addasaṃ sikhināyakaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīse ito kappe       rājā homi mahabbalo    
vebhāro nāma nāmena      cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 325 -->
<a id="page-325"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 325]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sukatāveḷiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sukatāveḷiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ ekavandaniyattherāpadānaṃ (230)    
[232] usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ            vessabhuṃ vijitāvinaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ avandahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā๊    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Catuvīsamhi kappamhi          vigatānandanāmako [^325-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekavandaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekavandaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 326 -->
<a id="page-326"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 326]*

uddānaṃ    
ālambanañca ajinaṃ          maṃsadārakkhadāyako    
abyādhivakulasoṇṇaṃ          miñjaṃ āveḷa vandanaṃ    
pañcapaññāsa gāthāyo       gaṇitā atthadassihīti .

Ālambanadāyakavaggo tevīsatimo .

catubbīso udakāsanadāyivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ udakāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (231)    
[233] ārāmadvārā nikkhamma      phalakaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ    
udakañca upaṭṭhāsiṃ          uttamatthassa pattiyā .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         āsane codake phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       abhisāmasamavhayo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakāsanadāyabho thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udakāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 327 -->
<a id="page-327"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 327]*

Dutiyaṃ bhājanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (232)    
[234] nagare bandhumatiyā          kumbhakāro ahaṃ tadā    
bhājanaṃ anupālesiṃ          bhikkhusaṅghassa tāvade .

Ekanavutito kappe         bhājanaṃ anupālayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhājanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Tepaññāse ito kappe     anantajalināmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhājanadāyako [^327-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhājanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ sālapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (233)    
[235] aruṇavatiyā nagare          ahosiṃ puviko [^327-2] tadā    
mama dvārena gacchantaṃ       sikhinaṃ addasaṃ jinaṃ .

Buddhassa pattaṃ paggayha       sālapupphaṃ adāsahaṃ    
sammaggatassa buddhassa        vippasannena cetasā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 328 -->
<a id="page-328"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 328]*

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhidāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sālapupphassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito cuddasakappamhi         ahosiṃ amitañjalo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sālapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ kilañjadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (234)    
[236] tivarāyaṃ pure ramme        naḷakāro ahaṃ tadā    
siddhatthe lokapajjote      pasannā janatā tahiṃ .

Pūjatthaṃ lokanāthassa         kilañjaṃ pariyesati    
buddhapūjaṃ karontānaṃ         kilañjaṃ adadaṃ [^328-1] ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kilañjassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasattatikappamhi          rājā āsi jalandharo [^328-2]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 329 -->
<a id="page-329"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 329]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kilañjadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kilañjadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ vedidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (235)    
[237] vipassino bhagavato          bodhiyā pādaputtame    
pasannacitto sumano         kāresiṃ vedikaṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      kāresiṃ vedikaṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vedikāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ekādase kappe      ahosiṃ sūriyassamo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vedidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vedidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 330 -->
<a id="page-330"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 330]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ vaṇṇakārakattherāpadānaṃ (236)    
[238] nagare aruṇavatiyā          vaṇṇakāro ahaṃ tadā    
cetiye dussabhaṇḍāni        nānāvaṇṇaṃ rajemahaṃ [^330-1] .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ vaṇṇaṃ rajayiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vaṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tevīsatikappe         candasamasanāmako [^330-2]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṇṇakārako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vaṇṇakārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ piyālapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (237)    
[239] migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane ahaṃṭa    
piyālaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā        gatamagge khipiṃ ahaṃ .

Buddhassa pattaṃ paggayha       piyālapupphamadāsahaṃ    
magge gatassa buddhassa       vippasannena cetasā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 331 -->
<a id="page-331"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 331]*

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piyālapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Piyālapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ ambayāgadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (238)    
[240] sake sippe apatthaddho [^331-1]   agamaṃ kānanaṃ ahaṃ    
sambuddhaṃ yantaṃ [^331-2] disvāna    ambayāgaṃ [^331-3] adāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ambayāgassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambayāgadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ambayāgadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 332 -->
<a id="page-332"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 332]*

navamaṃ jagatikārakattherāpadānaṃ (239)    
[241] nibbute lokanāthamhi        atthadassīnaruttame    
jagatī kāritā mayhaṃ         buddhassa thūpamuttame .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jagatikārako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Jagatikārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ vāsidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (240)    
[242] kammārohaṃ pure āsiṃ       tivarāyaṃ puruttame    
ekā vāsi mayā dinnā     sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ vāsimadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vāsidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 333 -->
<a id="page-333"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 333]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vāsidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vāsidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
udakāsani bhājaniyo         sālapupphī kilañjado    
vedikā vaṇṇakāro ca       piyālaambayāgado    
jagatī vāsidāyī ca          gāthā tiṃsa ca aṭṭha cāti .

Udakāsanadāyivaggo catubbīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 334 -->
<a id="page-334"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 334]*

Pañcavīso tuvaradāyivaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tuvaraṭṭhidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (241)    
[243] migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane mahā [^334-1]    
taṃ disvā rudhiramādāya [^334-2]    saṅghassa adadiṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tuvaraṭṭhissidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tuvaraṭṭhidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tuvaraṭṭhidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ nāgakesariyattherāpadānaṃ (242)    
[244] dhanana avejjhaṃ [^334-3] katvāna     vanamajjhogahiṃ ahaṃ    
kesaraṃ osaraṃ [^334-4] disvā     pabbamaggaṃ [^334-5] samuṭṭhitaṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         tissassa lokabandhuno .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^334-1] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ . [^334-2] ma. Pharitvā tūvaramādāya . Yu. Haritvā ... . [^334-3] ma. Advejjhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 335 -->
<a id="page-335"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 335]*

Sattasattatime kappe        samokkharaṇanāmako [^335-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgakesariyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nāgakesariyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ naḷinakesariyattherāpadānaṃ (243)    
[245] jātassarassa vemajjhe       vasāmi jalakukkuṭo    
athaddasaṃ devadevaṃ          gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Tuṇḍena kesaraṃ [^335-2] gayha     vippasannena cetasā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         tissassa lokabandhuno .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tesattatimhi kappamhi        sattapattasanāmako [^335-3]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^335-1] ma. Tesattatimhi kappamhi sattakesaranāmakā . Yu. Pamokkharaṇanāmako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 336 -->
<a id="page-336"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 336]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷinakesariyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Naḷinakesariyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ viravipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (244)    
[246] khīṇāsavasahassehi           niyyāti lokanāyako    
viravipupphaṃ paggayha [^336-1]       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā viravipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Viravipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ kuṭidhūpakattherāpadānaṃ (245)    
[247] siddhatthassa bhagavato         ahosiṃ kuṭigopako    
kālena kālaṃ dhūpesiṃ        pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhūpadānassidaṃ [^336-2] phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 337 -->
<a id="page-337"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 337]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭidhūpako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kuṭidhūpakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ pattadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (246)    
[248] paramena damathena           siddhatthassa mahesino    
pattadānaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        ujubhūtassa tādino .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pattadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pattadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ dhātupūjakattherāpadānaṃ (247)    
[249] nibbute lokanāthamhi        siddhatthamhi naruttame    
ekadhātu mayā laddhā       dipadindassa tādino .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 338 -->
<a id="page-338"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 338]*

Tāhaṃ dhātuṃ gahetvāna       buddhassādiccabandhuno    
pañcavasse paricariṃ          tiṭṭhantaṃva naruttamaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dhātumabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhātupaṭṭhahane phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhātupūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhātupūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sattasattalipupphapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (248)    
[250] satta sattalipupphāni [^338-1]      sīse katvānahantadā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         vessabhumhi naruttame .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattasattalipupphapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sattasattalipupphapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 339 -->
<a id="page-339"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 339]*

Navamaṃ bimbijālapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (249)    
[251] padumuttaro nāma jino       sayambhū aggapuggalo    
catusaccaṃ pakāseti          deseti amataṃ padaṃ .

Bimbijālakapupphāni [^339-1]       puthu katvānahaṃ tadā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         dipadindassa tādino .

Aṭṭhasaṭṭhimhito kappe       caturo kiñjakesarā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bimbijālapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bimbijālapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ uddāladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (250)    
[252] kukkudho [^339-2] nāma nāmena    sayambhū aparājito    
pavanā nikkhamitvāna         anuppatto mahānadiṃ .

Uddālakaṃ gahetvāna        sayambhussa adāsahaṃ    
saṃyatassujubhūtassa            pasannamanaso tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 340 -->
<a id="page-340"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 340]*

ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uddāladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Uddāladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
tuvaraṭṭhināgapuḷinā          viravikuṭidhūpako    
patto dhātusattaliyo        bimbi uddālakena ca    
sattatiṃsati gāthāyo         gaṇitāyo vibhāvihīti .

Tuvaradāyivaggo pañcavīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 341 -->
<a id="page-341"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 341]*

chabbīsatimo thomakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ thomakattherāpadānaṃ (251)    
[253] devaloke ṭhito santo      vipassissa mahesino    
dhammaṃ sutvāna mudito        imaṃ vācaṃ abhāsahaṃ .

Namo te purisājañña        namo te purisuttama    
bahujjanaṃ tārayasi           desento amataṃ padaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ vācaṃ abhaṇiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thomanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā thomako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Thomakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ ekāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (252)    
[254] vijahitvā devavaṇṇaṃ         sabhariyo idhāgamiṃ    
adhikāraṃ kattukāmo         buddhaseṭṭhassa sāsane .

Devalo nāma nāmena       padumuttarasāvako    
tassa bhikkhā mayā dinnā     vippasannena cetasā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 342 -->
<a id="page-342"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 342]*

satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (253)    
[255] ānando nāma sambuddho     sayambhū aparājito    
araññe parinibbāyi         amanussamhi kānane .

Devalokā idhāgantvā      citaṃ katvānahaṃ tadā    
sarīraṃ tattha jhāpesiṃ         sakkārañca akāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 343]*

Catutthaṃ campakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (254)    
[256] himavantaavidūre            vikano [^343-1] nāma pabbato    
tassa vemajjhe vasati        samaṇo bhāvitindriyo .

Disvāna tassopasamaṃ         vippasannena cetasā    
tīṇi campakapupphāni          gahetvāna samokiriṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā campakapupphiyo [^343-2] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Campakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ sattapāṭaliyattherāpadānaṃ (255)    
[257] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
satta pāṭalipupphāni         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 344]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapāṭaliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sattapāṭaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ upāhanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (256)    
[258] ahosiṃ candano nāma        sambuddhassatrajo tadā    
ekopāhano mayā dinno    bodhi [^344-1] sampajja me tuvaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pāduṃ [^344-2] adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         upāhanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāhanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upāhanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ mañjaripūjakattherāpadānaṃ (257)    
[259] mañjarikaṃ karitvāna          rathiyaṃ paṭipajjahaṃ    
addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 345]*

pasannacitto sumano         paramāya ca pītiyā    
ubhohatthehi paggayha        buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tesattatikappe        eko āsiṃ mahīpati    
jotiyo nāma nāmena       cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mañjaripūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mañjaripūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ paṇṇadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (258)    
[260] pabbate himavantamhi         vākacīradharo ahaṃ    
aloṇapaṇṇabhakkhomhi         niyamesu ca saṃvuto .

Pātarāse anuppatte       siddhattho upagañchi maṃ    
tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ       pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ paṇṇamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 346 -->
<a id="page-346"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 346]*

Sattavīsamhi kappamhi         rājā āsiṃ yadatthiyo [^346-1]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṇṇadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paṇṇadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ kuṭidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (259)    
[261] vipinaṃ cāri sambuddho        rukkhamūle vasi tadā    
paṇṇasālaṃ karitvāna         adāsiṃ aparājite .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ paṇṇakuṭikaṃ dadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kuṭidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhatiṃse [^346-2] ito kappe    soḷasāsiṃsu rājino    
sabbattha abhivassīti          vuccare cakkavattino .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kuṭidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 347 -->
<a id="page-347"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 347]*

Dasamaṃ aggapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (260)    
[262] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
obhāsayantaṃ raṃsiyā [^347-1]      sikhinaṃ sikhinaṃ yathā .

Aggajaṃ pupphamādāya         upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīsatikappamhi           ahosiṃ mittaghātako [^347-2]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aggapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aggapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .่  
uddānaṃ  
thomako bhikkhacitakaṃ          campako sattapāṭali  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 348 -->
<a id="page-348"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 348]*

Pādumañjari paṇṇañca         kuṭido aggapupphiyo    
gāthāyo gaṇitā cettha      ekatāḷīsameva ca .

Thomakavaggo chabbīsatimo .

sattavīso padumukkhepavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ākāsukkhipiyattherāpadānaṃ (261)    
[263] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ siddhatthaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
jalajagge duve gayha        upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Ekañca pupphaṃ pādesu       buddhaseṭṭhassa nikkhipiṃ    
ekañca pupphaṃ paggayha       ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ8    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito battiṃsakappamhi [^348-1]      eko āsiṃ mahippati    
antalikkhakaro nāma         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ākāsukkhipiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ākāsukkhipiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 349]*

Dutiyaṃ telamakkhiyattherāpadānaṃ (262)    
[264] siddhatthamhi bhagavati          nibbutamhi narāsabhe    
bodhiyā vedikāyāhaṃ        telaṃ makkhesi tāvade .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ telaṃ makkhayiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         makkhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Catuvīse [^349-1] ito kappe     succhavi nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā telamakkhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Telamakkhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ aḍḍhacandiyattherāpadānaṃ (263)    
[265] tissassa kho bhagavato        bodhiyā pādaputtame [^349-2]    
aḍḍhacandaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        dharaṇīruhapādape .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ candamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bodhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 350]*

Pañcavīse ito kappe      devalo nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso        vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā        kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aḍḍhacandiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aḍḍhacandiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ dīpadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (264)    
[266] devabhūto ahaṃ santo       oruyha paṭhaviṃ tadā    
padīpe pañca pādāsiṃ       pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Catunavute ito kappe      yaṃ dīpamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi        dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcapaññāsake kappe      eko āsiṃ mahīpati|    
samantacakkhu nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso        vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā        kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .(    
itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dīpadāyako [^350-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dīpadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 351]*

Pañcamaṃ viḷālidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (265)    
[267] himavantassa avidūre         romaso nāma pabbato    
tamhi pabbatapādamhi         samaṇo bhāvitindriyo .

Viḷāliyo gahetvāna        samaṇassa adāsahaṃ    
anumodi mahāvīro          sayambhū aparājito .

Viḷāli te mama [^351-1] dinnā    vippasannena cetasā    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        phalaṃ nibbattataṃ tava .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ viḷālimadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         viḷāliyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā viḷālidāyako thero imā gāthāyocha    
Abhāsitthāti .

Viḷālidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ macchadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (266)    
[268] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ [^351-2] ukkuso tadā    
mahantaṃ macchaṃ paggayha        siddhatthassa munino adaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 352]*

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ macchamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         macchadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā macchadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Macchadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ javahaṃsakattherāpadānaṃ (267)    
[269] candabhāgānadītīre          āsiṃ vanacaro tadā    
siddhatthaṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ        gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        olokento [^352-1] mahāmuniṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       abhivandiṃ [^352-2] lokanāyakaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ avandiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā javahaṃsako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Javahaṃsakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 353 -->
<a id="page-353"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 353]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ salaḷapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (268)    
[270] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ kinnaro tadā    
vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ         raṃsijālasamākulaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         paramāya ca pītiyā    
paggayha salaḷaṃ pupphaṃ         vipassissa [^353-1] okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salaḷapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Salaḷapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ upāgatahāsaniyattherāpadānaṃ (269)    
[271] himavantassa vemajjhe        saro āsi sunimmito    
tatthāhaṃ rakkhaso āsiṃ       heṭṭhasīso [^353-2] bhayānako .

Anukampako kāruṇiko        vipassī lokanāyako    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi mama santike .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 354 -->
<a id="page-354"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 354]*

Upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ           devadevaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
āsayā abhinikkhamma         avandiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ vandiṃ purisuttamaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāgatahāsaniyo [^354-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upāgatahāsaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ taraṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (270)    
[272] suvaṇṇavaṇṇo sambuddho       vipassī [^354-2] nāma nāyako    
nadītīre ṭhito satthā        bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato .

Nāvā na vijjate tattha      santāraṇī mahaṇṇave    
nadiyā abhinikkhamma          tāresiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ tāresiṃ  naruttamaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tāraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 355 -->
<a id="page-355"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 355]*

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Taraṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
ukkhepī telacandī ca        dīpado ca viḷālido    
maccho javo salaḷado        rakkhaso taraṇo dasa    
gāthāyo cettha saṅkhātā    tāḷīsamekameva ca .

Padumukkhepavaggo sattavīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 356 -->
<a id="page-356"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 356]*

aṭṭhavīsatimo suvaṇṇabibbohanavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ suvaṇṇabibbohaniyattherāpadānaṃ (271)    
[273] ekāsanaṃ ahamadaṃ           pasanno sehi pāṇihih    
bibbohanañca pādāsiṃ        uttamatthassa pattiyā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      bibbohanamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bibbohanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito tesaṭṭhime kappe      asamo nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā suvaṇṇabibbohaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Suvaṇṇabibbohaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ tilamuṭṭhiyattherāpadānaṃ (272)    
[274] mama saṅkappamaññāya         satthā lokagganāyako    
manomayena kāyena         iddhiyā upasaṅkami .

Satthāraṃ upasaṅkantaṃ         vanditvā purisuttamaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         tilamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 357 -->
<a id="page-357"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 357]*

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tilamuṭṭhiyidaṃ [^357-1] phalaṃ .

Ito soḷasakappamhi         gandhiyo [^357-2] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tilamuṭṭhiyo [^357-3] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tilamuṭṭhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ caṅkoṭakiyattherāpadānaṃ (273)    
[275] mahāsamuddaṃ nissāya         vasati pabbatantare    
paccuggantvāna kāmahaṃ [^357-4]    caṅkoṭakamadāsahaṃ .

Siddhatthassa mahesino        sayambhussānukampino [^357-5]    
pupphacaṅkoṭakaṃ datvā        kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       caṅkoṭakamadaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         caṅkoṭakassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^357-1] yu. Tilamuṭṭhe idaṃ phalaṃ . [^357-2] ma. Tantiso . Yu. Nandiyo . [^357-3] ma. Yu.

#tilamuṭṭhidāyako . [^357-4] ma. Katvāna . [^357-5] ma. Sabbasattānukampino . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 358 -->
<a id="page-358"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 358]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā caṅkoṭakiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Caṅkoṭakiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ abbhañjanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (274)    
[276] koṇḍaññassa bhagavato        vītarāgassa tādino    
akakkasassa [^358-1] cittassa      nipapañcassa jhāyino .

Sabbamohātivattassa         sabbalokahitesino    
abbhañjanaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        dipadindassa tādino .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     abbhañjanamadāsahaṃ [^358-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         abbhañjanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       virappo [^358-3] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abbhañjanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Abbhañjanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 359 -->
<a id="page-359"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 359]*

Pañcamaṃ ekañjaliyattherāpadānaṃ (275)    
[277] udumbare vasantassa         niyate paṇṇasanthare    
vutthokāso mayā dinno     samaṇassa mahesino .

Tissassa dipadindassa         lokanāthassa tādino    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        santhariṃ pupphasantharaṃ .8    
dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kariṃ pupphasantharaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito cuddasakappamhi         ahosiṃ manujādhipo    
ekaañjaliko nāma         cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekañjaliyo [^359-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
abhāsitthāti .

Ekañjaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ potthadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (276)    
[278] satthāraṃ dhammamārabbha        saṅghañcāpi mahesino [^359-2]    
potthadānaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       dakkhiṇeyye anuttare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 360 -->
<a id="page-360"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 360]*

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         potthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā potthadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Potthadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (277)    
[279] candabhāgānadītīre          anusotaṃ vajāmahaṃ    
satta māluvapupphāni         citakāropayiṃ [^360-1] ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       citakaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasaṭṭhimhito kappe       paṭijaggasanāmako [^360-2]    
sattaratanasampanno [^360-3]       cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^360-1] ma. Citamāropayiṃ ahaṃ . [^360-2] ma. Yu. Paṭijaggasanāmakā . [^360-3] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 361 -->
<a id="page-361"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 361]*

Aṭṭhamaṃ āluvadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (278)    
[280] pabbate himavantamhi         mahāsindhu sudassano [^361-1]    
tatthaddasaṃ vītarāgaṃ          sampabhāsaṃ sudassanaṃ .

Paramopasame yuttaṃ          disvā vimhitamānaso [^361-2]    
āluvaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ        pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ āluvamadāsahaṃ [^361-3]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         āluvassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āluvadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Āluvadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ puṇḍarīkattherāpadānaṃ (279)    
[281] romaso nāma nāmena       sayambhū sappabho [^361-4] tadā    
puṇḍarīkaṃ mayā dinnaṃ         vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       puṇḍarīkamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puṇḍarīkassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^361-1] ma. Sudassanā . [^361-2] yu. ... Taṃ vimhitāsayo . [^361-3] ma. Yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 362 -->
<a id="page-362"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 362]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇḍarīko [^362-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Puṇḍarīkattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ taraṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (280)    
[282] mahāpathamhi visame          setu kārāpitaṃ [^362-2] mayā    
taraṇatthāya lokassa         pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ setu [^362-3] kāritaṃ mayā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcapaññāsito kappe       eko āsiṃ samogato [^362-4]    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Taraṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^362-1] ma. Yu. Ekapuṇḍarīko . [^362-2] ma. Yu. Kārāpito . [^362-3] ma. Yo setu kārito mayā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 363 -->
<a id="page-363"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 363]*

Uddānaṃ    
sovaṇṇaṃ tilamuṭṭhi ca         caṅkoṭakabbhañjamañjali    
potthako citamāluvo        ekapuṇḍari setunā    
dvecattāḷīsa gāthāyo      gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi  .

Suvaṇṇabibbohanavaggo aṭṭhavīsatimo .

Ekādasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ    
ekūnatiṃso paṇṇadāyakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ paṇṇadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (281)    
[283] paṇṇasāle nisinnomhi       paṇṇabhojanabhojano    
upaviṭṭhañca maṃ santaṃ         upagañchi mahāisi .

Siddhattho lokapajjoto      sabbalokatikicchako    
tassa paṇṇaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       nisinnassa [^363-1] paṇṇasanthare .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ paṇṇamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṇṇadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paṇṇadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 364 -->
<a id="page-364"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 364]*

Dutiyaṃ phaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (282)    
[284] sinerusamasantoso          dharaṇīdharasādiso [^364-1]    
vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā       bhikkhāya mamupaṭṭhito .

Harītakiṃ [^364-2] āmalakaṃ         ambaṃ [^364-3] jambaba vibhedakaṃ    
kolaṃ bhallātakaṃ bellaṃ [^364-4]    phārusakaphalāni ca .

Siddhatthassa mahesissa        sabbalokānukampino    
tañca sabbaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsito kappe       ekajjho nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ paccuggamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (283)    
[285] sīhaṃ yathā vanacaraṃ           nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā    
kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva            āgacchantaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

#[^364-1] ma. Dharaṇīsamasādiso . Yu. Dharaṇīdhārisādiso . [^364-2] po. Ma. Yu. Harītakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 365 -->
<a id="page-365"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 365]*

Siddhatthaṃ lokapajjotaṃ        sabbalokatikicchakaṃ    
akāsiṃ paccuggamanaṃ          vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       paccuggañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paccuggamanidaṃ [^365-1] phalaṃ .

Sattavīse [^365-2] ito kappe    eko āsiṃ janādhipo    
parivāroti [^365-3] nāmena      cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paccuggamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paccuggamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ekapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (284)    
[286] dakkhiṇamhi duvāramhi         pisāco āsahantadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          pītaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ .

Vipassissa naraggassa         sabbalokahitesino    
ekapupphaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        dipadindassa tādino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 366 -->
<a id="page-366"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 366]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ maghavapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (285)    
[287] nammadānadiyā tīre         sayambhū aparājito    
samādhiṃ so samāpanno       vippasanno anāvilo .

Disvā pasanno sumano       sambuddhaṃ aparājitaṃ    
tāhaṃ maghavapupphena          sayambhuṃ pūjayiṃ tadā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maghavapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Maghavapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 367 -->
<a id="page-367"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 367]*

Chaṭṭhaṃ upaṭṭhāyikattherāpadānaṃ (286)    
[288] rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ           āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
dipadindaṃ mahānāgaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Pakkosāpiyatassāhaṃ         sabbalokahitesino    
upaṭṭhāko mayā dinno      siddhatthassa mahesino .

Paṭiggahetvāna sambuddho     niyyātesi [^367-1] mahāmuni    
uṭṭhāya āsanā tamhā      pakkāmi pācināmukho .

Catunavute ito kappe       upaṭṭhākamadaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattapaññāsito kappe       balasenasanāmako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upaṭṭhāyiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upaṭṭhāyikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ apadāniyattherāpadānaṃ (287)    
[289] apadānaṃ sugatānaṃ           kittayiṃhaṃ [^367-2] mahesinaṃ    
pāde ca sirasā vandiṃ       pasanno sehi pāṇihi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 368 -->
<a id="page-368"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 368]*

dvenavute ito kappe      apadānaṃ pakittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā apadāniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Apadāniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sattāhapabbajitattherāpadānaṃ (288)    
[290] vipassissa bhagavato          saṅgho sakkatamānito    
byasanaṃ me anuppattaṃ        ñātibhedo pure ahu .

Pabbajjaṃ upagantvāna        byasanupasamāyahaṃ    
sattāhābhirato tattha        satthusāsanakamyatā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yamahaṃ pabbajiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pabbajjāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattasaṭṭhimhito kappe       satta āsuṃ mahīpatī    
sunekkhammāti ñāyanti       cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 369 -->
<a id="page-369"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 369]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattāhapabbajito thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sattāhapabbajitattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ buddhupaṭṭhāyikattherāpadānaṃ (289)    
[291] vedhambhinīti [^369-1] me nāmaṃ     pitu santaṃ mamaṃ tadā    
mama hatthaṃ gahetvāna        upānayi mahāmuniṃ .

Ime maṃ uddisiyanti         buddhā lokagganāyakā    
tehaṃ upaṭṭhi [^369-2] sakkaccaṃ     pasanno sehi pāṇihi .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      buddhe paricariṃ [^369-3] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tevīsamhi ito kappe       caturo āsu khattiyā    
samaṇupaṭṭhakā nāma          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhupaṭṭhāyiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Buddhupaṭṭhāyikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^369-1] ma. Veṭambhinīti --- . Yu. Veṭambarīti me nāma . [^369-2] po. Ma. Upaṭṭhiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 370 -->
<a id="page-370"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 370]*

Dasamaṃ pubbaṅgamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (290)    
[292] cullāsītisahassāni          pabbajimha akiñcanā    
tesaṃ pubbaṅgamo āsiṃ       uttamatthassa pattiyā .

Sarāgā samohā [^370-1] cete   vippasannamanāvilaṃ [^370-2]    
upaṭṭhahiṃsu sakkaccaṃ          pasannā sehi pāṇihi .

Khīṇāsavā vantadosā        katakiccā anāsavā    
phariṃsu mettacittena         sayambhū aparājitā .

Tesaṃ upaṭṭhahitvāna         sambuddhānaṃ paṭissato    
maraṇañca anuppattā [^370-3]      devattañca agamhase .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ sīlamanupālayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saññamassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime .

Chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pubbaṅgamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pubbaṅgamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
paṇṇaṃ phalaṃ uggamiyo         ekapupphī ca māghavo    
#[^370-1] ma. -- sabhavā ___ . Yu. ___ sabhavā loke . [^370-2] ma. Vippasannamanāvilā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 371 -->
<a id="page-371"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 371]*

Upaṭṭhākopadānañca         pabbajjā ca upaṭṭhitaṃ .

Pubbaṅgamo ca gāthāyo      aṭṭhatāḷīsa kittitā .

Paṇṇadāyakavaggo ekūnatiṃso .

tiṃso citakapūjakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (291)    
[293] ajito nāma nāmena        ahosiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmohaṃ         nānāpupphaṃ samānayiṃ .

Jalantaṃ citakaṃ disvā [^371-1]      sikhino lokabandhuno    
tañca pupphaṃ samānetvā      citake okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse [^371-2] ito kappe   yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse ito kappe       sattāsuṃ manujādhipā    
supajjalitanāmā te         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 372 -->
<a id="page-372"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 372]*

Dutiyaṃ pupphadhārakattherāpadānaṃ (292)    
[294] vākacīradharo āsiṃ          ajinuttaravāsano    
abhiññā pañca nibbatto [^372-1]   candassa parimajjako .

Vipassiṃ lokapajjotaṃ         disvā abhigataṃ mamaṃ    
pārichattakapupphāni          dhāresiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhidhārayiṃ [^372-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhāraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattāsītimhito kappe       eko āsiṃ mahīpati    
samantadharaṇo nāma          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphadhārako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphadhārakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ chattadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (293)    
[295] putto mama pabbajito        kāsāyavasano tadā    
so ca buddhattasampatto      nibbuto lokapūjito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 373 -->
<a id="page-373"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 373]*

Vicinanto sakaṃ puttaṃ         ārāmaṃ [^373-1] pacchato mamaṃ    
nibbutassa mahantassa         citakaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Paggayha añjaliṃ tattha        vanditvā citakaṃ ahaṃ    
setacchattañca paggayha       āropesiṃ ahaṃ tadā .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ chattamabhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīse ito kappe       satta āsuṃ janādhipā    
mahārahasanāmā te         cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā chattadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Chattadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ saddasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (294)    
[296] anuggatamhi ādicce        pasādo [^373-2] vipulo ahu    
buddhaseṭṭhassa lokamhi       pātubhāvo mahesino .

Saddamassosahaṃ [^373-3] tattha      na ca passāmi taṃ jinaṃ    
maraṇañca anuppatto         buddhasaññamanussariṃ .

#[^373-1] ma. Yu. Āgamaṃ . [^373-2] ma. Panādo . [^373-3] ma. Ghosamasosahaṃ ___ . Po. Na ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 374 -->
<a id="page-374"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 374]*

catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saddasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ gosīsanikkhepakattherāpadānaṃ (295)    
[297] ārāmadvārā nikkhamma      gosīsaṃ santhataṃ mayā    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ          pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ājāniyā vātajavā        sindhavā sīghabāhanā    
anubhomi sabbametaṃ          gosīsassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Aho kāraṃ paramakāraṃ        sukhette sukataṃ mayā    
saṅghe katassa kārassa       na aññaṃ kalamagghati .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ sīsaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcasattatikappamhi          supatiṭṭhitanāmako    
eko āsiṃ mahātejo      cakkavatti mahabbalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 375 -->
<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 375]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti.

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gosīsanikkhepako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gosīsanikkhepakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ padapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (296)    
[298] pabbate himavantamhi         ahosiṃ kinnaro tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          pitaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ .

Upetopi [^375-1] tadā buddhaṃ     vipassiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
candanaṃ tagaraṃ vāpi          pāde osiñcahaṃ tadā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ padaṃ [^375-2] abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padapūjako [^375-3] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^375-1] ma. Upetaṃ tamahaṃ buddhaṃ . Yu. Upetaṃ maṃ tadā buddhaṃ . [^375-2] ma. Yu. ___ pādaṃ ___ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 376 -->
<a id="page-376"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 376]*

Sattamaṃ desakittikattherāpadānaṃ (297)    
[299] upasālhakanāmohaṃ [^376-1]       ahosiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
kānanaṃ vanamogayha[^376-2]        lokanāthaṃ [^376-3] narāsabhaṃ .

Disvāna vandi [^376-4] pādesu    lokāhutipaṭiggahaṃ    
pasannacittaṃ maṃ ñatvā        buddho antaradhāyatha .

Kānanā abhinikkhamma         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ    
tandesaṃ kittayitvāna        kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ desamabhikittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā desakittiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Desakittikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ saraṇagamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (298)    
[300] pabbate himavantamhi         ahosiṃ luddako tadā    
vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ         lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

#[^376-1] ma. Yu. Upasālakanāmohaṃ . [^376-2] ma. Yu. Vanamogāḷhaṃ . [^376-3] ma. Yu. Lokajeṭṭhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 377 -->
<a id="page-377"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 377]*

Upāsitvāna sambuddhaṃ        veyyāvaccamakāsahaṃ    
saraṇañca upāgañchiṃ          dipadindassa tādino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ saraṇaṃ agañchahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saraṇāya [^377-1] idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saraṇagamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ ambapiṇḍiyattherāpadānaṃ (299)    
[301] romaso nāma nāmena       dānavo iti vissuto    
ambapiṇḍo [^377-2] mayā dinno   vipassissa mahesino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ ambamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ambadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambapiṇḍiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ambapiṇḍiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 378 -->
<a id="page-378"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 378]*

Dasamaṃ anusaṃsāvakattherāpadānaṃ (300)    
[302] piṇḍāya caramānāhaṃ         vipassiṃ addasaṃ jinaṃ    
uluṅkabhikkhaṃ adāsiṃ [^378-1]       dipadindassa tādino .

Pasannacitto sumano         abhivādesahaṃ tadā    
anusaṃsāvayiṃ buddhaṃ           uttamatthassa pattiyā .

Ekanavute ito kappe      anusaṃsāvayiṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         anusaṃsāvanāphalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anusaṃsāvako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Anusaṃsāvakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
citakaṃ pārichattañca          saddaṃ gosīsasantharaṃ    
padaṃ padesaṃ saraṇaṃ           ambo saṃsāvakopica    
sattatāḷīsagāthāyo [^378-2]      gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi .

Citakapūjakavaggo tiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 379 -->
<a id="page-379"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 379]*

Atha vagguddānaṃ    
kaṇikāro hatthidado         ālambanudakāsanaṃ    
tuvaraṃ thomako ceva         ukkhepaṃ sīsupadhānaṃ .

Paṇṇado citapūjī ca          gāthāyo ceva sabbaso    
cattāri ca satānīha         ekapaññāsameva ca .

Pañcavīsa satā sabbā        dvāsattati taduttari    
tiṃsa [^379-1] satānaṃ padānaṃ       gaṇitā atthadassibhi .

Tatiyaṃ satakaṃ samattaṃ .

ekattiṃsatimo padumakesariyavaggo
