paṭhamaṃ padumakesariyattherāpadānaṃ (301)    
[303] isisaṅghe ahaṃ pubbe        āsiṃ mātaṅgavāraṇo    
mahesīnaṃ pasādena          padumakesaramokiriṃ [^379-2] .

Paccekajinaseṭṭhesu         dhutarāgesu tādisu    
tesu cittaṃ pasādetvā      kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      kesaraṃ okiriṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 380 -->
<a id="page-380"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 380]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumakesariyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumakesariyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ sabbagandhiyattherāpadānaṃ (302)    
[304] gandhamālaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        vipassissa mahesino    
adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa           koseyyavatthamuttamaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ gandhamadadiṃ [^380-1] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         gandhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito paṇṇarase kappe       suvelo [^380-2] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbagandhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sabbagandhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ paramannadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (303)    
[305] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          udayantaṃva bhāṇumaṃ    
vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ         lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 381]*

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        abhinesiṃ [^381-1] sakaṃ gharaṃ    
abhinetvāna [^381-2] sambuddhaṃ     paramannamadāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      paramannamadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         paramannassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paramannadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Paramannadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ dhammasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (304)    
[306] vipassino bhagavato          mahābodhimaho ahu    
rukkhaṭṭhasseva sambuddho      lokajeṭṭho narāsabho .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye         bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
catusaccaṃ pakāseti          vācāsabhimudīrayaṃ .

Saṅkhittena ca desento     vitthārena ca desayi [^381-3]    
vivaṭacchado sambuddho        nibbāpesi mahājanaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     pakkāmi [^381-4] uttarāmukho .

#[^381-1] po. Yu. Atinesiṃ . [^381-2] po. Yu. Atinetvā . [^381-3] ma. Yu. Desayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 382 -->
<a id="page-382"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 382]*

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dhammamasuṇiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhammassavanassidaṃ [^382-1] phalaṃ .

Tettiṃsamhi ito kappe      eko āsi [^382-2] mahīpati    
sutavā nāma nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhammasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ phaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (305)    
[307] bhāgīrasīnadītīre            ahosi assamo tadā    
tamahaṃ assamāgañchiṃ [^382-3]       phalahattho asekkhavā [^382-4] .

Vipassiṃ tattha addakkhiṃ        pītaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ    
yaṃ me atthi phalaṃ sabbaṃ       adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^382-1] po. Yu. Dhammadānassidaṃ ___ . [^382-2] ma. Āsiṃ . [^382-3] ma. Yu. ___ assamaṃ gacchiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 383 -->
<a id="page-383"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 383]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ sampasādikattherāpadānaṃ (306)    
[308] namo te buddhavīratthu        vippamuttosi sabbadhi    
byasanamhi anuppatto        tassa me saraṇaṃ bhava .

Siddhattho tassa byākāsi     loke appaṭipuggalo    
mahodadhisamo saṅgho         appameyyo anuttaro .

Tattha tvaṃ viraje khette     anantaphaladāyake    
saṅghe cittaṃ pasādehi [^383-1]    suvījaṃ vāpi ropaya .

Idaṃ vatvāna sabbaññū        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
mamevaṃ [^383-2] anusāsitvā      vehāsatalamuggami [^383-3] .

Aciraṃ gatamattamhi [^383-4]        sabbaññumhi narāsabhe    
maraṇaṃ samanuppatto          tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Tadāhaṃ viraje khette       anantaphaladāyake    
saṅghe cittaṃ pasādetvā     kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       pasādamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pasādassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^383-1] ma. ___ pasādetvā      subījaṃ vāpa ropaya .

#  yu. ___ pasādetvā        sukhī bījañca ___ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 384 -->
<a id="page-384"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 384]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sampasādiko [^384-1] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sampasādikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ ārāmadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (307)    
[309] siddhatthassa bhagavato         ārāmo ropito mama [^384-2]    
sītacchāyesu [^384-3] rukkhesu     upāsantesu [^384-4] pakkhisu .

Addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
ārāmaṃ atināmesiṃ         lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      phalaṃ pupphañcadāsahaṃ [^384-5]    
tato jātappasādo ca [^384-6]    taṃ dānaṃ [^384-7] pariṇāmayiṃ .

Buddhassa yamidaṃ dāsiṃ         vippasannena cetasā    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        nibbattati phalaṃ mama .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ ārāmamadaṃ [^384-8] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ārāmassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattatiṃse ito kappe       sattāsuṃ mudusītalā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

#[^384-1] ma. Sampasādako . [^384-2] ma. Mayā . [^384-3] ma. Sandacchāyesu ___ .

#[^384-4] yu. Upavattesu . [^384-5] ma. Pupphamadāsahaṃ . [^384-6] ma. Va . [^384-7] ma. ___ vanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 385 -->
<a id="page-385"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 385]*

paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārāmadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ārāmadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ anulepadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (308)    
[310] atthadassissa munino         addasaṃ sāvakaṃ ahaṃ    
navakammaṃ karontassa         sīmāya upagañchahaṃ .

Niṭṭhite navakamme ca        anulepaṃ adāsahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         puññakkhette anuttare .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         anulepassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anulepadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Anulepadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 386 -->
<a id="page-386"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 386]*

Navamaṃ buddhasaññikattherāpadānaṃ (309)    
[311] udentaṃ sataraṃsīva           pītaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ    
vanantaragataṃ santaṃ           lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Addasaṃ supinantena          siddhatthaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
tattha cittaṃ pasādetvā      sugatiṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasaññiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Buddhasaññikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pabbhāradāyakattherāpadānaṃ (310)    
[312] piyadassino bhagavato         pabbhāro sodhito mayā    
ghaṭakañca upaṭṭhāsiṃ          paribhogāya tādino .

Taṃ me buddho viyākāsi      piyadassī mahāmuni    
sahassakaṇḍo satageṇḍu [^386-1]    dhajālu haritāmayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 387 -->
<a id="page-387"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 387]*

Nibbattissati so yūpo       ratanañca anappakaṃ    
pabbhāradānaṃ datvāna        kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Ito battiṃsakappamhi         subuddho [^387-1] nāma khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pabbhāradāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pabbhāradāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
kesaraṃ gandhamannañca         dhammasaññāphalena ca    
pasādārāmadāyī ca         lepako buddhasaññiko    
pabbhārado ca gāthāyo      ekapaññāsa kittitā .

Padumakesariyavaggo ekattiṃsatimo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 388 -->
<a id="page-388"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 388]*

Battiṃso ārakkhadāyakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ārakkhadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (311)    
[313] dhammadassissa munino         vati kārāpitā mayā    
ārakkho ca mayā dinno     dipadindassa tādino .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
tena kammāvasesena [^388-1]     patto me āsavakkhayo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārakkhadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ārakkhadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ bhojanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (312)    
[314] sujāto sālalaṭṭhīva         sobhañjanaṃ ivuggato    
indalaṭṭhīrivākāse         virocati sadā jino .

Tassa devātidevassa        vessabhussa mahesino    
adāsiṃ bhojanamahaṃ           vippasannena cetasā .

Taṃ me buddho anumodi       sayambhū aparājito    
bhave nibbattamānamhi        phalaṃ nibbattatu tava .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 389 -->
<a id="page-389"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 389]*

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhojanassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcavīse ito kappe       eko āsiṃ amittako    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhojanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhojanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ gatasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (313)    
[315] ākāseva padaṃ natthi        ambare anilañjase    
siddhatthaṃ jinamaddakkhiṃ         gacchantaṃ tidivaṅgaṇaṃ [^389-1] .

Anileneritaṃ disvā         sammāsambuddhacīvaraṃ    
vitti me [^389-2] pāhunā tāva   disvāna gamanaṃ mune [^389-3] .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^389-1] ma. Tidivaṅgaṇe . [^389-2] ma. Vitti mamāhu tāvade . Yu. Vitti me tāvade jātā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 390 -->
<a id="page-390"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 390]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gatasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gatasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ sattapadumiyattherāpadānaṃ (314)    
[316] nadīkūle vasāmahaṃ           nesādo nāma brāhmaṇo    
sattapattehi pupphehi        sammajjitvāna assamaṃ .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         siddhatthaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
disvā vanena [^390-1] gacchantaṃ    hāso me upapajjatha .

Paccuggantvāna sambuddhaṃ      lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
assamaṃ atināmetvā        jalajaggehi okiriṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito te sattame kappe     caturo pādapāvarā    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapadumiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sattapadumiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 391 -->
<a id="page-391"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 391]*

Pañcamaṃ pupphāsanadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (315)    
[317] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         sataraṃsīva [^391-1] bhāṇumaṃ    
avidūrena gacchantaṃ          siddhatthaṃ aparājitaṃ .

Tassa paccuggamitvāna        pavesetvāna assamaṃ    
pupphāsanaṃ mayā dinnaṃ        vippasannena cetasā .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        vedajāto tadā ahaṃ    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā     kammantaṃ pariṇāmayiṃ .

Yaṃ me atthi kataṃ puññaṃ       sayambhuṃ [^391-2] aparājitaṃ    
sabbena tena kusalena       vimalo homi sāsane .

Catunavute ito kappe       pupphāsanamadaṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphāsanadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphāsanadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ āsanatthavikattherāpadānaṃ (316)    
[318] cetiyaṃ uttamaṃ nāma         sikhino lokabandhuno    
araññe iriṇe vivane [^391-3]    anvāhiṇḍāmahaṃ [^391-4] tadā .

#[^391-1] ma. Pitaraṃsīva . [^391-2] ma. Yu. Sayambhumhaparājite . [^391-3] ma. Vane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 392 -->
<a id="page-392"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 392]*

Pavanā nikkhamantena         diṭṭhaṃ sīhāsanaṃ mayā    
ekaṃsaṃ añjaliṃ katvā        santhaviṃ [^392-1] lokanāyakaṃ .

Divasabhāgaṃ thavitvāna         buddhaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      imaṃ vācamudīrayiṃ .

Namo te purisājañña        namo te purisuttama    
sabbaññūsi mahāvīra          lokajeṭṭha narāsabha .

Abhitthavitvā sikhinaṃ          nimittakaraṇenahaṃ    
āsanaṃ abhivādetvā        pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ thaviṃ vadataṃ varaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         thomanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse ito kappe       atulyā [^392-2] satta āsu [^392-3] te    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āsanatthaviko [^392-4] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Āsanatthavikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ saddasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (317)    
[319] sudassano mahāvīro         deseti amataṃ padaṃ    
parivuto sāvakehi          vasati gharamuttame .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 393 -->
<a id="page-393"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 393]*

Tāya vācāya madhurāya       saṅgaṇhāti mahājanaṃ    
ghoso ca vipulo āsi       āsi [^393-1] so devamānuse .

Nigghosasaddaṃ sutvāna        siddhatthassa mahesino    
sadde cittaṃ pasādetvā     avandiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saddasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ tiraṃsiyattherāpadānaṃ (318)    
[320] kesariṃ abhijātaṃva           aggikkhandhaṃva pabbate    
obhāsentaṃ disāsinnaṃ [^393-2]    siddhatthaṃ pabbatantare .

Suriyassa ca ālokaṃ         candālokaṃ tatheva ca    
buddhālokañca disvāna       pīti [^393-3] me upapajjatha .

Tayo āloke disvāna      disvāna [^393-4] sāvakuttamaṃ    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

#[^393-1] yu. Sadevamānusena so . [^393-2] ma. Yu. Disā sabbā . [^393-3] ma. Yu. Vitti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 394 -->
<a id="page-394"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 394]*

Tayo hi ālokakarā        loke lokatamonudā    
cando ca suriyo cāpi       buddho ca lokanāyako .(    
upamaṃ upadassetvā         kittito me mahāmuni    
buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ kittetvā     kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekasaṭṭhimhito kappe       eko ñāṇavaro [^394-1] ahu    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiraṃsiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiraṃsiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ nālipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (319)    
[321] sindhuyā nadiyā tīre        ahosiṃ kassako tadā    
parakammāyane yutto        parabhattaṃ apassito .

Sindhadha anucarantohaṃ          siddhatthaṃ jinamaddasaṃ    
samādhinā nisinnaṃ ca [^394-2]      sattapattaṃva pupphitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 395 -->
<a id="page-395"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 395]*

sattakaṃ nālipupphāni         vaṇṭe chetvānahantadā    
matthake abhiropesiṃ         buddhassādiccabandhuno .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         anukūle samāhitaṃ    
tidhāppabhinnaṃ mātaṅgaṃ        kuñjaraṃva durāsadaṃ .

Tamahaṃ upagantvāna          nipakaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        avandiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nālipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nālipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ kumudamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (320)    
[322] usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ            mahesiṃ vijitāvinaṃ    
vipassinaṃ mahāvīraṃ           abhijātaṃva kesariṃ .

Rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ           āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
gahetvā kumudaṃ mālaṃ        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ samokiriṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 396 -->
<a id="page-396"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 396]*

ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumudamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kumudamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
ārakkhado bhojanado        gatasaññī padumiko    
pupphāsani santhaviko         saddasaññī tiraṃsiyo    
nāliko kumudī ceva         sattapaññāsa gāthakā .

Ārakkhadāyakavaggo battiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 397 -->
<a id="page-397"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 397]*

Tettiṃso ummāpupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ummāpupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (321)    
[323] samāhitaṃ samāpannaṃ          siddhatthamaparājitaṃ    
samādhinā upaviṭṭhaṃ          addasāsiṃ [^397-1] naruttamaṃ .

Ummāpupphaṃ gahetvāna       buddhassa abhipūjayiṃ [^397-2]    
sabbe pupphā ekasīsā      uddhaṃvaṇṭā adhomukhā .

Sucittā viya tiṭṭhante       ākāse pupphasantharā    
tena cittappasādena        tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pañcapaññāsito kappe       eko āsi mahīpati    
samantachadano nāma          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ummāpupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ummāpupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 398 -->
<a id="page-398"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 398]*

Dutiyaṃ puḷinapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (322)    
[324] kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva            nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā    
osadhīva virocantaṃ          obhāsantaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        avandiṃ satthuno ahaṃ    
satthāraṃ parivaṇṇesiṃ         sakakammena tosayiṃ [^398-1] .

Susuddhaṃ puḷinaṃ gayhaṃ [^398-2]       gatamagge samokiriṃ    
ucchaṅgena gahetvāna       vipassissa mahesino .

Tato upaḍḍhapuḷinaṃ           vippasannena cetasā    
divāvihāre osiñciṃ        dipadindassa tādino .

Ekanavute ito kappe      puḷinaṃ yaṃ asiñcihaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puḷinassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puḷinapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Puḷinapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ hāsajanakattherāpadānaṃ (323)    
[325] dumagge paṃsukūlikaṃ [^398-3]        laggaṃ disvāna satthuno    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        bhiyyo uccāritaṃ mayā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 399 -->
<a id="page-399"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 399]*

Dūrato paṭidisvāna [^399-1]       hāso me upapajjatha    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā hāsajanako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Hāsajanakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ yaññasāmikattherāpadānaṃ (324)    
[326] jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        ahosiṃ mantapāragū    
kulavaṃsaṃ [^399-2] adhāresiṃ        yañño ussāpito [^399-3] mayā .

Cullāsītisahassāni          pasū haññanti me tadā    
sārasmiṃ [^399-4] hi upanītāni     yaññatthāya upaṭṭhitā .

Ukkāmukho pahaṭṭhova [^399-5]     khadiraṅgārasannibho    
udayantova suriyo          puṇṇamāyeva candimā .

Siddhattho sabbasiddhattho      tilokamahitohito    
upagantvāna sambuddho       idaṃ vacanamabravi .

#[^399-1] ma. Dūrato pana disvāna . [^399-2] ma. Kulavattaṃ . [^399-3] ma. Yu. Ussāhito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 400 -->
<a id="page-400"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 400]*

Ahiṃsā sabbapāṇānaṃ [^400-1]      kumāra mama ruccati    
theyyā ca aticārā ca      majjapānā ca ārati .

Rati ca samacariyāya          bāhusaccaṃ kataññutā    
diṭṭhe dhamme parattha ca      dhammā ete pasaṃsiyā .

Ete dhamme bhāvayitvā     sabbasattahite rato    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā     bhāvehi maggamuttamaṃ .

Idaṃ vatvāna sabbaññū        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
mamevaṃ anusāsitvā         vehāsaṃ uggato gato .

Pubbe cittaṃ visodhetvā     pacchā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
tena cittappasādena        tusitaṃ upapajjahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yadā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yaññasāmiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Yaññasāmikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ nimittasaññakattherāpadānaṃ (325)    
[327] candabhāgānadītīre          vasāmi assame ahaṃ    
suvaṇṇamigamaddakkhiṃ           carantaṃ vivane [^400-2] ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 401 -->
<a id="page-401"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 401]*

Mige cittaṃ pasādetvā      lokajeṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ    
tena cittappasādena        aññe buddhe anussariṃ .

Abbhatītā ca ye buddhā      vattamānā anāgatā [^401-1]    
evameva virocanti         migarājāva te [^401-2] tayo .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ saññamalabhiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhasaññāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sattavīse ito kappe       eko āsi mahīpati    
araññasatto nāmena        cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nimittasaññako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nimittasaññakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ annasaṃsāvakattherāpadānaṃ (326)    
[328] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          battiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Siddhatthaṃ sabbasiddhatthaṃ        aneñjaṃ aparājitaṃ    
sambuddhaṃ atināmetvā       bhojayiṃ taṃ mahāmuniṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 402 -->
<a id="page-402"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 402]*

muni kāruṇiko loke        obhāsayi mamaṃ tadā    
buddhe cittaṃ pasādetvā     kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhikkhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā annasaṃsāvako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Annasaṃsāvakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ nigguṇḍipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (327)    
[329] yadā devo devakāyā      cavati āyusaṅkhayā    
tayo saddā niccharanti       devānaṃ anumodataṃ .

Ito bho sugatiṃ gaccha        manussānaṃ sahabyataṃ    
manussabhūto saddhamme        labha saddhaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Sā te saddhā niviṭṭhassa     mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā    
yāvajīvaṃ asaṃhirā           saddhamme supavedite .

Kāyena kusalaṃ katvā        vācāya kusalaṃ bahuṃ    
manasā kusalaṃ katvā         abyāpajjhaṃ nirūpadhiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 403]*

Tato opadhikaṃ puññaṃ         katvā dānena taṃ bahuṃ    
aññe [^403-1] pavare saddhamme   brahmacariye nivesaya .

Imāya anukampāya          devadevaṃ yathā vidū    
cavantaṃ anumodanti          ehi deva punappunaṃ .

Saṃviggohaṃ [^403-2] tadā āsiṃ     devasaṅghe samāgate    
kaṃsu nāma ahaṃ yoniṃ         gamissāmi ito cuto .

Mama saṃvegamaññāya          samaṇo bhāvitindriyo    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgacchi mama santikaṃ .

Sumano nāma nāmena        padumuttarasāvako    
atthadhammānusāsitvā        saṃvejesi mamantadā .

Dvādasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        buddhe cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Taṃ dhīraṃ [^403-3] abhivādetvā     tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Upapajjiṃ [^403-4] sa tattheva      sukkamūlena codito    
vasanto mātukucchimhi        puna dhāretu mātuyā .

Tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      tidase upapajjahaṃ    
etthantare na passāmi      domanassaṃ ahaṃ tadā .

Tāvatiṃsā cavitvāna         mātukucchiṃ samokkamiṃ    
nikkhamitvāna kicchimhā       kiñci [^403-5] dukkhaṃ ajānihaṃ .

#[^403-1] ma. Aññepi macce ... . Yu. Aññe nicceva ... . [^403-2] ma. Saṃvego me tadā āsi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 404 -->
<a id="page-404"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 404]*

Jātiyā sattavassena [^404-1]     ārāmaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ    
gotamassa bhagavato          sakyaputtassa tādino .

Vitthārike pāvacane        bāhujaññamhi sāsane    
addasaṃ sāsanaṃ kāre        bhikkhavo tattha satthuno .

Sāvatthi nāma nagaraṃ         rājā tatthāsi kosalo    
rathena nāgayuttena         upesi bodhimuttamaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ nāgaṃ disvāna       pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        samayaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassova        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
yo so buddhaṃ upaṭṭhāsi      ānando nāma sāvako .

Gatimā dhitimā ceva         satimā ca bahussuto    
rañño cittaṃ pasādento     niyyādesi mahājuti .

Tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ    
tattheva ṭhitako santo       arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Ekaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā         sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       imaṃ vācaṃ udīrayiṃ .

Padumuttarabuddhassa           dipadindassa tādino [^404-2]    
niguṇḍipupphaṃ paggayha         sīhāsane ṭhapesahaṃ .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 405 -->
<a id="page-405"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 405]*

Pañcavīsasahassamhi           kappānaṃ manujādhipā    
abbudanirabbudāni           aṭṭhaṭṭhāsiṃsu khattiyā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nigguṇḍipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nigguṇḍipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sumanāveḷiyattherāpadānaṃ (328)    
[330] vessabhussa bhagavato         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
sabbe janā samāgamma       mahāpūjaṃ karonti te .

Sudhāya piṇḍaṃ katvāna        āveḷaṃ sumanāyahaṃ    
sīhāsanassa purato          abhiropesahaṃ tadā .

Sabbe janā samāgamma       pekkhanti pupphamuttamaṃ    
kenidaṃ pūjitaṃ pupphaṃ          buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino .

Tena cittappasādena        nimmānaṃ upapajjahaṃ    
anubhomi [^405-1] sakaṃ kammaṃ       pubbe sukatamattano .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbesānaṃ piyo homi       pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 406 -->
<a id="page-406"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 406]*

nābhijānāmi kāyena        vācāya uda cetasā    
saṃyatānaṃ tapassīnaṃ           kataṃ upaṭṭhāhitaṃ mayā .

Tena sucaritenāhaṃ          cittassa paṇidhīhi ca    
sabbesaṃ pūjito homi        anakkosassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ito ekādase kappe      sahassārosi khattiyo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanāveḷiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumanāveḷiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ pupphachattiyattherāpadānaṃ (329)    
[331] siddhatthassa bhagavato         lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
saccaṃ pakāsayantassa         nibbāpentassa pāṇino .

Jalajaṃ āharitvāna          sattapattaṃ manoramaṃ    
pupphassa chattaṃ katvāna       buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 407 -->
<a id="page-407"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 407]*

Siddhattho ca lokavidū        āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imaṃ [^407-1] gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Yo me cittaṃ pasādetvā    pupphachattaṃ adhārayi [^407-2]    
tena cittappasādena        duggatiṃ so na gacchati .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       siddhattho lokanāyako    
uyyojetvāna parisaṃ        vehāsaṃ nabhamuggami .

Uṭṭhite naradevamhi         setacchattaṃpi vuṭṭhahi    
purato buddhaseṭṭhassa        gacchati chattamuttamaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ chattaṃ abhiropayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pupphachattassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Catusattatikappamhi           aṭṭha jalasikhā ahu    
sattaratanasampannā          cakkavattī mahabbalā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphachattiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pupphachattiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ saparivārachattadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (330)    
[332] padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
ākāse jalavuṭṭhīva         vassate dhammavuṭṭhiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 408 -->
<a id="page-408"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 408]*

Tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         desentaṃ amataṃ padaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       agamāsiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Chattaṃ alaṅkataṃ gayha         upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ .

Susaṅgahitayānaṃva            dantova sāvakuttamo    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        matthake sampatiṭṭhahi .

Anukampako kāruṇiko        buddho lokagganāyako    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yena chattamidaṃ dinnaṃ         alaṅkataṃ manoramaṃ    
tena cittappasādena        duggatiṃ so na gacchati .

Sattakkhattuṃ ca devesu       devarajjaṃ karissati    
dvattiṃsakkhattuṃ [^408-1] rājā ca   cakkavatti bhavissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena [^408-2]    satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Buddhassa giramaññāya         vācāsabhimudīritaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         bhiyyo hāsaṃ janesahaṃ .

Jahitvā mānusaṃ yoniṃ        dibbayoniṃ [^408-3] samajjhagaṃ    
vimānamuttamaṃ mayhaṃ          abbhuggataṃ manoramaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 409 -->
<a id="page-409"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 409]*

Vimānā nikkhamantassa        setacchattaṃ dharīyati    
tadā saññaṃ paṭilabhāmi        pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna        manussattañca āgamiṃ    
chattiṃsakkhattuṃ cakkavatti       sattakappasatamhito .

Tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      āgacchiṃ tidasaṃ puraṃ    
saṃsaritvānupubbena          mānusaṃ punarāgamiṃ .

Okkantaṃ mātukucchiṃ maṃ       setacchattaṃ adhārayuṃ    
jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Sunando nāma nāmena       brāhmaṇo mantapāragū    
phalikaṃ [^409-1] chattamādāya       sāvakaggassa so adā [^409-2] .

Anumodi mahāvīro          sārīputto mahākathī    
sutvānumodanaṃ tassa         pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
saritvā purimaṃ kammaṃ         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Uṭṭhāya āsanā tamhā      sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       imaṃ vācamudīrayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      buddho loke anuttaro    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Tassa chattaṃ mayā dinnaṃ       vicittaṃ samalaṅkataṃ    
ubhohatthehi paggaṇhi        sayambhū aggapuggalo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 410 -->
<a id="page-410"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 410]*

Aho buddho [^410-1] aho dhammo [^410-2]  aho no satthusampadā    
ekachattassa dānena        duggatiṃ nūpapajjahaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saparivārachattadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saparivārachattadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
ummāpupphañca puḷinaṃ         hāso yañño nimittako    
saṃsāvako nigguṇḍi ca        sumanā pupphachattako    
saparivārachatto ca          gāthā sattasatuttarā .

Ummāpupphiyavaggo tettiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 411 -->
<a id="page-411"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 411]*

catuttiṃso gandhodakavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ gandhadhūpiyattherāpadānaṃ (331)    
[333] siddhatthassa bhagavato         gandhadhūpaṃ adāsahaṃ    
sumanehi paṭicchannaṃ          buddhānucchavikañca taṃ .

Kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          buddhaṃ lokagganāyakaṃ    
indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ            ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Byagghusabhaṃva paramaṃ           abhijātaṃva kesariṃfa    
nisinnaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ          bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Disvā cittaṃ pasādetvā     paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ gandhamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         gandhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhadhūpiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhadhūpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 412 -->
<a id="page-412"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 412]*

dutiyaṃ udakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (332)    
[334] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ anilañjase    
ghatāsanaṃva jalitaṃ            ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Pāṇinā udakaṃ gayha         ākāse ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ    
sampaṭicchi mahāvīro         buddho kāruṇiko isi .

Antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      padumuttaranāmako    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha .

Iminā dakadānena          pītiuppādanena ca่    
kappasatasahassaṃpi            duggatiṃ nūpapajjasi .

Tena kammena dipadinda       lokajeṭṭha narāsabha    
pattomhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ        hitvā jayaparājayaṃ .

Sahassarājanāmena          tayo ca cakkavattino    
pañcasaṭṭhikappasate          cāturanto janādhipā .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 413 -->
<a id="page-413"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 413]*

tatiyaṃ punnāgapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (333)    
[335] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha         vasāmi luddako ahaṃ    
punnāgaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ .

Taṃ pupphaṃ ocinitvāna        sugandhaṃ gandhitaṃ subhaṃ    
thūpaṃ katvāna puḷine         buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ekamhi navute kappe       eko āsi tamonudo    
sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā punnāgapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Punnāgapupphittherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ ekadussadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (334)    
[336] nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ tiṇahārako    
tiṇahārena jīvāmi          tena posemi dārake .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 414 -->
<a id="page-414"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 414]*

Padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
tamandhakāraṃ nāsetvā       uppajji lokanāyako .

Sake ghare nisīditvā        evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā    
buddho loke samuppanno     deyyadhammo ca natthi me .

Idaṃ me sāṭakaṃ ekaṃ        natthi me koci dāyako    
dukkho nirayasamphasso        ropayissāmi dakkhiṇaṃ .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
ekaṃ dussaṃ gahetvāna       buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekaṃ dussaṃ daditvāna        ukkuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayiṃ    
yadi buddho tuvaṃ vīra         tārehi maṃ mahāmuni .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mama dānaṃ pakittento       akā me anumodanaṃ .

Iminā ekadussena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
kappasatasahassāni           vinipātaṃ na gacchati .

Chattiṃsakkhattu devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati    
tettiṃsakkhattu rājā ca      cakkavatti bhavissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
devaloke manusse vā      saṃsaranto tuvaṃ bhave .

Rūpavā guṇasampanno         anavakkantadehavā    
akkhobhaṃ amitaṃ dussaṃ         bhavissati [^414-1] yathicchakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 415 -->
<a id="page-415"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 415]*

idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       jalajuttamanāmako    
nabhaṃ abbhuggami dhīro         haṃsarājāva ambare .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi      ekadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paduddhāre paduddhāre       dussaṃ nibbattate mamaṃ    
heṭṭhā dussamhi tiṭṭhāmi     upari chadanaṃ mama .

Cakkavāḷamupādāya          sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ    
icchamāno cahaṃ ajja        dussehacchādayeyyahaṃ .

Teneva ekadussena        saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇo hutvāna        saṃsarāmi bhavābhave .

Vipākaṃ ekadussassa         najjhagaṃ katthacikkhayaṃ    
ayaṃ me antimā jāti       vipaccati idhāpi me .

Satasahassito kappe         yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ekadussassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekadussadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekadussadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 416 -->
<a id="page-416"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 416]*

Pañcamaṃ phussitakammiyattherāpadānaṃ (335)    
[337] vipassī nāma sambuddho       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
khīṇāsavehi sahito          saṅghārāme vasī tadā .

Ārāmadvārā nikkhamma      vipassī lokanāyako    
saha satasahassehi           aṭṭhakhīṇāsavehi so .

Ajinena nivatthohaṃ          vākacīradharopi ca    
kusumbhodakamādāya [^416-1]       sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       vedajāto katañjalī    
kusumbhodakamādāya [^416-2]       buddhamabbhukiriṃ [^416-3] ahaṃ .

Tena kammena sambuddho      jalajuttamanāmako    
mama kammaṃ pakittetvā       agamā yena patthitaṃ .

Phussitā pañcasahassā        yehi pūjesahaṃ jinaṃ    
aḍḍhateyyasahassehi         devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Aḍḍhateyyasahassehi         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
avasesena kammena         arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Devarājā yadāhosiṃ [^416-4]     manujādhipati tadā [^416-5]    
taṃyeva nāmadheyyaṃ me       phussito nāmahosahaṃ [^416-6] .0    
devabhūtassa santassa         athāpi mānusassa vā    
samantā byāmato mayhaṃ      phussitaṃpi [^416-7] pavassati .

#1-[^416-2] ma. Kusumodakamādāya . [^416-3] ma. Yu. Buddhamabbhukkiriṃ . [^416-4] ma. Yu. Yathā homi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 417 -->
<a id="page-417"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 417]*

Bhavā ugghāṭitā mayhaṃ       kilesā jhāpitā mama    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo          phussitassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Candanasseva me vasso [^417-1]   tathā gandho pavāyati    
sārīrako [^417-2] mama gandho     aḍḍhakose pavāyati .

Dibbagandhaṃ sampavantaṃ         puññakammasamaṅginaṃ    
gandhaṃ ghatvāna jānanti       phussito āgato idha .

Sākhā palāsakaṭṭhāni        tiṇānipica sabbaso    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         gandho sampajjate khaṇe .

Satasahasse ito kappe      candanaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phussitadassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phussitakammiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phussitakammiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ pabhaṅkarattherāpadānaṃ (336)    
[338] padumuttarabhagavato           lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
pavane [^417-3] cetiyaṃ āsi      vāḷamigasamākule .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 418 -->
<a id="page-418"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 418]*

Na koci visahi gantuṃ         cetiyaṃ abhivandituṃ    
tiṇakaṭṭhalatonaddhaṃ           paluggaṃ āsi cetiyaṃ .

Vanakammiko tadā āsiṃ       pitāpetāmahenahaṃ [^418-1]    
addasaṃ pavane thūpaṃ          luggaṃ tiṇalatākulaṃ .

Disvānāhaṃ buddhathūpaṃ         garucittaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ    
buddhaseṭṭhassa thūpoyaṃ        paluggo acchati vane .

Nacchannaṃ nappaṭirūpaṃ          jānantassa guṇāguṇaṃ[^418-5]    
buddhathūpaṃ asodhetvā        aññaṃ kammaṃ payojaye .

Tiṇakaṭṭhañca valliñca         sodhayitvāna cetiye    
vanditvā aṭṭha vārāni      paṭikuṭiko agacchahaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ       sovaṇṇaṃ sappabhassaraṃ    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbiddhaṃ          tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Tisatāni ca vārāni         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
pañcavīsatikkhattuñca          cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Bhavābhave saṃsaranto         mahābhogaṃ labhāmahaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi      sodhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Siviyā [^418-2] hatthikhandhena      pavane gacchato mama [^418-3]    
yaṃ yaṃ disāhaṃ gacchāmi        saraṇaṃ sampajjate [^418-4] vanaṃ .

#[^418-1] ma. Pitumātumatenahaṃ . Yu. Pitupetāmahenahaṃ . [^418-2] ma. Sivikā . [^418-3] yu. Mamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 419 -->
<a id="page-419"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 419]*

Khāṇaṇa vā kaṇṭakaṃ vāpi       nāhaṃ passāmi cakkhunā    
puññakammena saṃyutto        sayamevāpanīyare .

Kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍo kilāso ca      apamāro vitacchikā    
daddu kaṇḍu ca me natthi      sodhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi [^419-1] sodhite    
nābhijānāmi me kāye      jātaṃ piḷakabindukaṃ .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi sodhite    
duve bhave saṃsarāmi         devatte atha mānuse .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi sodhite    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇo sabbattha        sappabhāso bhavāmahaṃ .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi sodhite    
amanāpaṃ vivajjeti          manāpaṃ upatiṭṭhati .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi sodhite    
visuddhaṃ hoti me cittaṃ       ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ .

Aññaṃpi me acchariyaṃ         buddhathūpamhi sodhite    
ekāsane nisīditvā        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sodhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 420 -->
<a id="page-420"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 420]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pabhaṅkaro thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pabhaṅkarattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ tiṇakuṭidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (337)    
[339] nagare bandhumatiyā          ahosiṃ parakammiko    
parakammāyane yutto        parabhattaṃ apassito .

Rahogato nisīditvā         evaṃ cintesahaṃ [^420-1] tadā    
buddho loke samuppanno     adhikāro ca natthi me .

Kālo gatiṃ me sodhetuṃ      khaṇo me paṭipādito    
dukkho nirayasamphasso        apuññānaṃ hi pāṇinaṃ .

Evāhaṃ cintayitvāna        kammasāmiṃ upāgamiṃ    
ekāhaṃ kammaṃ yācitvā      pavanaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ .

Tiṇakaṭṭhañca valliñca         āharitvānahantadā    
tidaṇḍake ṭhapetvāna        akaṃ tiṇakuṭiṃ ahaṃ .

Saṅghassatthāya kuṭikaṃ         niyyādetvāna taṃ ahaṃ    
tadaheyeva āgantvā       kammāsāmiṃ upāgamiṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena       tāvatiṃsaṃ agañchahaṃ    
tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ       tiṇakuṭikāya [^420-2] nimmitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 421 -->
<a id="page-421"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 421]*

Sahassakaṇḍo satageṇḍu       dhajālu haritāmayo    
satasahassaniyyūhā           byamhe pātubhaviṃsu me .

Yaṃ yaṃ yonūpapajjāmi         devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         pāsādo upatiṭṭhati .

Bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā       lomahaṃso na vijjati    
tāsaṃ mama na jānāmi        tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
sīhā byagghā ca dīpi ca      acchakokataracchayo [^421-1]    
sabbe maṃ parivajjanti        tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Siriṃsapā ca bhūtā ca         ahikumbhaṇḍarakkhasā    
tepi maṃ parivajjanti         tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Na pāpasupinassāhaṃ          sarāmi dassanaṃ mama    
upaṭṭhitā sati mayhaṃ         tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tāyeva tiṇakuṭikāya         anubhotvāna sampadā    
gotamassa bhagavato          dhammaṃ sacchikariṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 422 -->
<a id="page-422"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 422]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇakuṭidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇakuṭidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ uttareyyadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (338)    
[340] nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ brāhmaṇo tadā    
ajjhāyiko mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū .

Purakkhato sasissehi         jātimo [^422-1] ca susikkhito    
toyābhisecanatthāya         nagarā nikkhamiṃ tadā .

Padumuttaro [^422-2] lokavidū      sabbadhammāna pāragū    
khīṇāsavasahassehi           nagaraṃ pāvisī jino .

Sucārurūpaṃ disvāna          aneñjaṃ kāritaṃ viya    
parivutaṃ arahantehi          disvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ .

Sirasi añjaliṃ katvā         namassitvāna subbataṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         uttariyamadāsahaṃ .

Ubhohatthehi paggayha        sāṭakaṃ ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ    
yāvatā buddhaparisā         tāva chādesi sāṭakaṃ .

Piṇḍacāraṃ carantassa         mahābhikkhugaṇādino [^422-3]    
chādaṃ karonto aṭṭhāsi      hāsayanto mamaṃ tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 423 -->
<a id="page-423"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 423]*

Gharato nikkhamantassa         sayambhū aggapuggalo    
vīthiyaṃ [^423-1] ṭhitako satthā      akāsi anumodanaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         yo me pādāsi [^423-2] sāṭakaṃ    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
paññāsakkhattuṃ devindo      devarajjaṃ karissati .

Devaloke vasantassa        puññakammasamaṅgino    
samantā yojanasataṃ          dussacchannaṃ bhavissati .

Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Bhave saṃsaramānassa          puññakammasamaṅgino    
manasā patthitaṃ sabbaṃ         nibbattissati tāvade .

Koseyyakambalāni [^423-3] ca     khomakappāsikāni ca    
mahagghāni ca dussāni        paṭilacchatiyaṃ naro .

Manasā patthitaṃ sabbaṃ         paṭilacchatiyaṃ naro    
ekadussassa vipākaṃ         anubhossati sabbadā .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          dhammaṃ sacchikarissati .

Aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        sambuddhassa [^423-4] mahesino    
ekāhaṃ sāṭakaṃ datvā       pattomhi amataṃ padaṃ .

#[^423-1] ma. Yu. Vithiyaṃva ṭhito satthā akāme anumodanaṃ . [^423-2] ma. Adāsi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 424 -->
<a id="page-424"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 424]*

Maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā       vasato suññake ghare    
dhāreti dussachadanaṃ          samantā byāmato mama .

Aviññattaṃ [^424-1] nivāsemi      cīvaraṃ paccayañcahaṃ    
lābhimhi [^424-2] annapānassa     uttareyyassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vatthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttareyyadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Uttareyyadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ dhammassavaniyattherāpadānaṃ (339)    
[341] padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento        santāreti [^424-3] bahujjanaṃ .

Ahaṃ tena samayena          jaṭilo uggatāpano    
dhunanto vākacīrāni         gacchāmi ambare tadā .

Buddhaseṭṭhassa upari         gantuṃ na visahāmahaṃ    
pakkhīva selamāpajja [^424-4]      gamanaṃ na labhintadā [^424-5] .

#[^424-1] yu. Aviññatti nisevāmi . [^424-2] ma. Lābhī . Yu. Lābhīhi . [^424-3] ma. Santāresi bahuṃ janaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 425 -->
<a id="page-425"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 425]*

Na me idaṃ bhūtapubbaṃ         iriyassa vikopanaṃ    
dake yathā ummilitvā       evaṃ gacchāmi ambare .

Uḷārabhūto manujo ca        heṭṭhāsīno bhavissati    
handa metaṃ gavesissaṃ        api atthaṃ labheyyahaṃ .

Orohanto antalikkhā      saddamassosi satthuno    
aniccataṃ kathentassa         tamahaṃ uggahiṃ tadā .

Aniccasaññamuggayha          agamāsiṃ mamassamaṃ    
yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Carime vattamānamhi         taṃ dhammassavanaṃ sariṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
ekapaññāsakkhattuñca        devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Ekasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Pitughare nisīditvā          samaṇo bhāvitindriyo    
gāthāya paridīpento        aniccavatthudāhari [^425-1] .

Anussarāmi taṃ saññaṃ         saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
na koṭiṃ paṭivijjhāmi         nibbānaṃ accutaṃ padaṃ .

Aniccā vata saṅkhārā       uppādavayadhammino    
uppajjitvā nirujjhanti       tesaṃ vūpasamo sukho .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 426 -->
<a id="page-426"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 426]*

Saha gāthaṃ suṇitvāna         pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ    
ekāsane nisīditvā        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Jātiyā sattavassohaṃ        arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ    
upasampādayi buddho         guṇamaññāya cakkhumā .

Dārakova ahaṃ santo        karaṇīyaṃ samāpayiṃ    
kiṃ me karaṇīyaṃ ajja         sakyaputtassa sāsane .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saddhammassavane phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammassavaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhammassavaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ ukkhittapadumiyattherāpadānaṃ (340)    
[342] nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ māliko tadā    
ogāhetvā padumasaraṃ       sattapatte [^426-1] ocināmahaṃ .

Padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
saha satasahassehi           santacittehi tādibhi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 427 -->
<a id="page-427"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 427]*

Khīṇāsavehi suddhehi         chaḷabhiññehi jhāyibhi    
mama vuḍḍhiṃ samanvesaṃ         āgañchi purisuttamo [^427-1] .

Disvānahaṃ devadevaṃ         sayambhuṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
vaṇṭe chetvā sattapattaṃ     ukkhipiṃ ambare tadā .

Yadi buddho tuvaṃ dhīra         lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
sayaṃ gantvā sattapatto      matthake dhārayantu te .

Adhiṭṭhahi mahāvīro          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        matthake dhārayiṃsu te .

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Tattha me sukataṃ byamhaṃ       sattapattanti vuccati    
saṭṭhiyojanamubbiddhaṃ          tiṃsayojanavitthataṃ .

Sahassakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjamakārayiṃ    
pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ          pubbe sukatamattano .

Tenevekapadumena          anubhotvāna sampadā    
gotamassa bhagavato          dhammaṃ sacchikariṃ ahaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 428 -->
<a id="page-428"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 428]*

satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ekapadumassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ukkhittapadumiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ukkhittapadumiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
gandhodakaṃ pūjanī ca          punnāgaṃ ekadussikā    
phussito ca pabhaṅkaro        kuṭido uttarīyako .

Savanaṃ ekapadumi            gāthāyo sabbapiṇḍitā    
ekagāthāsatañceva         catutāḷīsameva ca .

Gandhodakavaggo catuttiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 429 -->
<a id="page-429"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 429]*

Pañcattiṃso ekapadumavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ekapadumiyattherāpadānaṃ (341)    
[343] padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
bhavābhave vibhāvento       tāreti [^429-1] janataṃ bahuṃ .

Haṃsarājā  tadā homi       dijānaṃ pavaro ahaṃ    
jātassaraṃ samogayha         kīḷāmi haṃsakīḷitaṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
jātassarassa upari          āgacchi tāvade jino .

Disvānahaṃ devadevaṃ         sayambhuṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
vaṇṭe chetvāna padumaṃ       sattapattaṃ manoramaṃ .

Mukhatuṇḍena paggayha         vippasannena [^429-2] cetasā    
ukkhipitvāna gagane         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
antalikkhe ṭhito satthā      akāsi [^429-3] anumodanaṃ .

Iminā ekapadumena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ          vinipātaṃ na gacchasi .

Idaṃ vatvāna sambuddho       jalajuttamanāmako    
mama kammaṃ pakittetvā       agamā yena patthitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 430 -->
<a id="page-430"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 430]*

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekapadumiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekapadumiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ tīṇuppalamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (342)    
[344] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ vānaro tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare .

Obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     sālarājaṃva phullitaṃ    
lakkhaṇabyañjanūpetaṃ          disvā attamano ahu .

Udaggacitto sumano         pītiyā haṭṭhamānaso    
tīṇi uppalapupphāni          matthake abhiropayiṃ .

Pupphāni abhiropetvā       vipassissa mahesino    
sagāravo bhavitvāna [^430-1]      pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Gacchanto paṭikuṭiko         vippasannena cetasā    
selantare papatitvā [^430-2]     pāpuṇiṃ jīvitakkhayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 431 -->
<a id="page-431"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 431]*

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇuppalamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇuppalamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ dhajadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (343)    
[345] tisso nāma ahu satthā      lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
tayopadhikkhaye [^431-1] disvā     dhajaṃ āropitaṃ mayā .

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ        tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ  
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuñca        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .(  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 432 -->
<a id="page-432"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 432]*

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
anubhomi sakaṃ kammaṃ          pubbe sukatamattano .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Icchamāno cahaṃ ajja        sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ    
khomadussena chādeyyaṃ       tadā mayhaṃ kate phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhajadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhajadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ tīṇikiṅkaṇipūjakattherāpadānaṃ (344)    
[346] himavantassavidūre [^432-1]        bhūtagaṇo nāma pabbato    
tatthaddasaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ           dumaggamhi vilaggitaṃ .

Tīṇi kiṅkaṇipupphāni          ocinitvānahaṃ tadā    
haṭṭho haṭṭhena cittena      paṃsukūlaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tiṇṇaṃ pupphānidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 433 -->
<a id="page-433"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 433]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇikiṅkaṇipūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇikiṅkaṇipūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ naḷāgārikattherāpadānaṃ (345)    
[347] himavantassavidūre           hāriko nāma pabbato    
sayambhū [^433-1] nāma nāmena     rukkhamūle vasī tadā .

Naḷāgāraṃ karitvāna         tiṇena chādayiṃ ahaṃ    
caṅkamaṃ sodhayitvāna         sayambhussa adāsahaṃ .

Catuddasesu kappesu         devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
catusattatikkhattuñca          devarajjamakārayiṃ .

Sattasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ         indalaṭṭhīva uggataṃ    
sahassathambhaṃ atulaṃ           vimānaṃ sappabhassaraṃ .

Dvesampattiṃ anubhotvāna     sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajiṃ ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 434 -->
<a id="page-434"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 434]*

Padhānaṃ pahitattomhi         upasanto nirūpadhi    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷāgāriko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Naḷāgārikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ campakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (346)    
[348] himavantassavidūre           chāpalo [^434-1] nāma pabbato    
buddho sudassano nāma       vihāsi pabbatantare .

Pupphaṃ hemavaṇṇaṃ [^434-2] gayha     gacchaṃ vehāyasenahaṃ [^434-3]    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Sattacampakapupphāni          sīse katvānahaṃ tadā    
buddhassa abhiropesiṃ         sayambhussa mahesino .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

#[^434-1] ma. Jāpalo nāma . Yu. Cāvalo nāma . [^434-2] ma. Hemavantaṃ . Yu. Hemavataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 435 -->
<a id="page-435"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 435]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā campakapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Campakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .}    
sattamaṃ padumapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (347)    
[349] himavantassavidūre           romaso nāma pabbato    
buddho ca [^435-1] sobhito nāma   abbhokāse vasī tadā .

Bhavanā nikkhamitvāna         padumaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ    
ekāhaṃ dhārayitvāna        punabbhavaṃ upāgamiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Terasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakattherāpadānaṃ (348)    
[350] himavantassavidūre           lambako nāma pabbato    
upatisso nāma sambuddho     abbhokāsamhi caṅkami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 436 -->
<a id="page-436"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 436]*

Migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane ahaṃ    
disvāna taṃ devadevaṃ        sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ .

Vippasannena cittena        tadā tassa mahesino    
nisīdanatthaṃ buddhassa          tiṇamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ .

Datvāna devadevassa        bhiyyo cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       pakkāmi [^436-1] uttarāmukho .

Aciraṃ gatamattaṃ maṃ           migarājā aheṭhayi [^436-2]    
sīhena pātito [^436-3] santo    tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Āsanne me kataṃ kammaṃ      buddhaseṭṭhe anāsave    
sumutto saravegova         devalokaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Yūpo tattha subho āsi       puññakammābhinimmito    
sahassakaṇḍo sattageṇḍu      dhajālu haritāmayo .

Pabhā niddhāvate tassa       sataraṃsīva uggato    
ākiṇṇo devakaññāhi       āmodiṃ kāmakāmihaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito    
āgantvāna manussattaṃ       pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       nisīdanamadāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tiṇamuṭṭhiyidaṃ [^436-4] phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 437 -->
<a id="page-437"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 437]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇamuṭṭhidāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ tindukaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (349)    
[351] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ .

Tindukaṃ saphalaṃ disvā         bhinditvāna sakoṭikaṃ [^437-1]    
pasannacitto sumano         vessabhussa [^437-2] adāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tindukaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tindukaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ ekañjaliyattherāpadānaṃ (350)    
[352] revato [^437-3] nāma sambuddho   nadīkūle vasī tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          pītaraṃsīva bhāṇumaṃ .

#[^437-1] ma. Sakosakaṃ . Po. Yu. Sakoṭakaṃ . [^437-2] ma. Sayambhussa madāsahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 438 -->
<a id="page-438"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 438]*

ukkāmukhaṃ pahaṭṭhaṃva          khadiraṅgārasannibhaṃ    
osadhī viya rocantaṃ         ekañjalimakāsahaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ añjalimakāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         añjaliyā idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekañjaliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekañjaliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
padumī uppalamālī           dhajo kiṅkaṇikaṃ naḷaṃ    
campako padumamuṭṭhi          tindukekañjalī tathā    
cha ca saṭṭhi ca gāthāyo      gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi .

Ekapadumavaggo pañcattiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 439 -->
<a id="page-439"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 439]*

Chattiṃso saddasaññikavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ saddasaññikattherāpadānaṃ (351)    
[353] migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane ahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ        dvesaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Catusaccaṃ pakāsentaṃ         uddharantaṃ mahājanaṃ    
assosiṃ madhuraṃ vācaṃ         karavikarudopamaṃ .

Brahmasarassa munino         sikhino lokabandhuno    
ghose cittaṃ pasādetvā     pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         pasādassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasaññiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti  .

Saddasaññikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ yavakalāpiyattherāpadānaṃ (352)    
[354] nagare aruṇavatiyā          āsiṃ yavasiko tadā    
panthe disvāna sambuddhaṃ      yavakalāpaṃ apatthariṃ [^439-1] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 440 -->
<a id="page-440"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 440]*

anukampako kāruṇiko        sikhī lokagganāyako    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         nisīdi yavasanthare .

Disvā nisinnaṃ vimalaṃ         mahājhāyiṃ vināyakaṃ    
pāmojjaṃ janayitvāna        tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         yavatthare idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yavakalāpiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Yavakalāpiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ kiṃsukapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (353)    
[355] kiṃsukaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā         paggahetvāna añjaliṃ    
buddhaṃ saritvā siddhatthaṃ       ākāse abhipūjayiṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 441 -->
<a id="page-441"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 441]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃsukapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kiṃsukapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ sakoṭakakoraṇḍadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (354)    
[356] akkantañca padaṃ disvā       sikhino lokabandhuno    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         padaṃ [^441-1] seṭṭhaṃ avandahaṃ .

Koraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ    
sakoṭakaṃ [^441-2] gahetvāna      pade cakkaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ    
paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakoṭakakoraṇḍadāyako thero imā    
Gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Sakoṭakakoraṇḍadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ daṇḍadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (355)    
[357] kānanaṃ vanamoggayha         veḷaḷa chetvānahantadā    
ālambanaṃ gahetvāna        saṅghassa adadaṃ ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 442 -->
<a id="page-442"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 442]*

Tena cittappasādena        subbate [^442-1] abhivādiya    
ālambanampi datvāna        pakkāmi uttarāmukho .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ daṇḍamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         daṇḍadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā daṇḍadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Daṇḍadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ ambayāgudāyakattherāpadānaṃ (356)    
[358] sataraṃsī nāma sambuddho       sayambhū aparājito    
vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā       bhikkhāya mamupāgami .

Paccekabuddhaṃ disvāna        ambayāguṃ adāpayiṃ [^442-2]    
vippasannamanantassa          vippasannena cetasā .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         ambayāguyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 443 -->
<a id="page-443"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 443]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambayāgudāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ambayāgudāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ puṭakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (357)    
[359] divāvihārā nikkhanto       vipassī lokanāyako    
bhikkhāya vicaranto so       mama santikamupāgami .

Tato pītito [^443-1] sumano      buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino    
loṇasupuṭakaṃ datvā          kappaṃ saggamhi modahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ puṭakamadāsahaṃ [^443-2]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         puṭakassa [^443-3] idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṭakapūjako [^443-4] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Puṭakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ vacchadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (358)    
[360] vipassino bhagavato          lokajeṭṭhassa tādino    
ekavaccho [^443-5] mayā dinno   pasannena sapāṇinā .

#[^443-1] ma. Yu. Patito . [^443-2] po. Puthukatamadāsahaṃ . Yu. Supuṭakamadāsahaṃ .

#[^443-3] po. Puthukatassa . Yu. Supuṭakassa . [^443-4] ma. Yu. Supuṭakapūjako . [^443-5] ma. Ekaṃ mañcaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 444 -->
<a id="page-444"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 444]*

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ         dibbayānaṃ samajjhagaṃ    
tena vacchakadānena [^444-1]      pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ vacchamadadiṃ [^444-2] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vacchadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vacchadāyako [^444-3] thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Vacchadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ saraṇāgamaniyattherāpadānaṃ (359)    
[361] āruhimhā tadā nāvaṃ       bhikkhu cājīvako cahaṃ    
nāvāya bhijjamānāya        bhikkhu me saraṇaṃ adā .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yañca me saraṇaṃ adā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         saraṇāgamane phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇāgamaniyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Saraṇāgamaniyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^444-1] ma. Mañcakadānena . Yu. Sajjhakadānena . [^444-2] ma. Mañcamadadiṃ . Yu. Sajjhamadadiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 445 -->
<a id="page-445"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 445]*

Dasamaṃ piṇḍapātikattherāpadānaṃ (360)    
[362] tisso nāmāsi sambuddho     vihāsi pavane [^445-1] tadā    
tusitāhi idhāgantvā        piṇḍapātaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       tissaṃ nāma mahāyasaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piṇḍapātiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Piṇḍapātikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
saddasaññī yavasiko          kiṃsukoraṇḍapupphiyo    
ālambanambayāgū ca         puṭakavacchadāyako    
saraṇaṃ piṇḍapāto ca         gāthā tāḷīsameva ca .

Saddasaññikavaggo chattiṃso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 446 -->
<a id="page-446"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 446]*

Sattattiṃso mandāravapupphiyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ mandāraviyattherāpadānaṃ (361)    
[363] tāvatiṃsā idhāgantvā       maṅgalo nāma māṇavo    
mandāravaṃ gahetvāna        vipassissa mahesino .

Samādhinā nisinnassa         matthake dhārayiṃ ahaṃ    
sattāhaṃ dhārayitvāna        devalokaṃ punāgamiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ buddhaṃ [^446-1] abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mandāraviyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mandāraviyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ kakkārupupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (362)    
[364] yāmā devā idhāgantvā    gotamaṃ sirivacchayaṃ [^446-2]    
kakkārupupphaṃ [^446-3] paggayha     buddhassa abhiropayiṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ [^446-4] abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^446-1] ma. ... Pupphamabhipūjayiṃ . [^446-2] ma. Yu. Sirivacchasaṃ . [^446-3] ma. Kakkārumālaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 447 -->
<a id="page-447"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 447]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kakkārupupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kakkārupupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ bhisamuḷāladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (363)    
[365] phusso nāmāsi sambuddho     sabbadhammāna pāragū    
vivekakāmo sappañño [^447-1]    āgacchi mama santike .

Tasmiṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā      mahākāruṇike jine    
bhisamuḷālaṃ paggayha          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ bhisaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisamuḷāladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bhisamuḷāladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 448 -->
<a id="page-448"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 448]*

Catutthaṃ kesarapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (364)    
[366] vijjādharo tadā āsiṃ       himavantamhi pabbate    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          caṅkamantaṃ mahāyasaṃ .

Tīṇi kesarapupphāni          sīse katvānahaṃ tadā    
upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ         vessabhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kesarapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kesarapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Pañcamaṃ aṅkolapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (365)    
[367] padumo nāma sambuddho       cittakūṭe vasī tadā    
disvāna tamahaṃ buddhaṃ         sayambhuṃ upagañchahaṃ [^448-1] .

Aṅkolaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā       ocinitvānahaṃ tadā    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        pūjesiṃ padumaṃ jinaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ [^448-2] tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 449 -->
<a id="page-449"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 449]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aṅkolapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Aṅkolapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ kadambapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (366)    
[368] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇe    
kañcanagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ          dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ .

Nisajja pāsādavare         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
kadambapupphaṃ paggayha         vipassiṃ abhipūjayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ [^449-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadambapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kadambapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 450 -->
<a id="page-450"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 450]*

Sattamaṃ uddālakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (367)    
[369] sujāto [^450-1] nāma sambuddho   gaṅgākūle vasī tadā    
uddālakaṃ gahetvāna        pūjayiṃ aparājitaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ abhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uddālakapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Uddālakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ ekacampakapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (368)    
[370] upasanto ca [^450-2] sambuddho    vasati pabbatantare    
ekacampakamādāya          upagañchiṃ naruttamaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         paccekamunimuttamaṃ    
ubhohatthehi paggayha        pūjayiṃ aparājitaṃ .

Ekattiṃse [^450-3] ito kappe   yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 451 -->
<a id="page-451"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 451]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ekacampakapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ekacampakapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ timirapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (369)    
[371] candabhāgānadītīre          anusotaṃ vajāmahaṃ    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          sālarājaṃva pupphitaṃ [^451-1] .

Pasannacitto sumano         paccekamunimuttamaṃ    
gahetvā timiraṃ pupphaṃ        matthake okiriṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā timirapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Timirapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 452 -->
<a id="page-452"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 452]*

Dasamaṃ salaḷapupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (370)    
[372] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ kinnaro tadā    
athaddasaṃ [^452-1] devadevaṃ       caṅkamantaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Ocinitvāna salaḷaṃ          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ [^452-2]    
upasiṅghi mahāvīro          salaḷaṃ devagandhikaṃ .

Paṭiggahetvā sambuddho      vipassī lokanāyako    
upasiṅghi mahāvīro          pekkhamānassa me tadā [^452-3] .

Pasannacitto sumano         vanditvā dipaduttamaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        puna pabbatamāruhiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salaḷapupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Salaḷapupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ  
mandāravañca kakkāru        bhisakesarapupphiyo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 453 -->
<a id="page-453"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 453]*

Aṅkolako kadambī ca        uddālaṃ ekacampako    
timiraṃ salaḷañceva           gāthā tāḷīsameva ca .

Mandāravapupphiyavaggo sattattiṃso .

aṭṭhattiṃso bodhivandanavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ bodhivandakattherāpadānaṃ (371)    
[373] pāṭaliṃ jalitaṃ [^453-1] disvā      pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ    
ekaṃsaṃ añjaliṃ katvā        avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ .

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        garuṃ katvāna mānasaṃ    
antosuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ         suvimuttaṃ anāsavaṃ .

Vipassiṃ lokamahitaṃ           karuṇāñāṇasāgaraṃ    
sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ        avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ bodhimabhivandihaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bodhivandako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bodhivandakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 454 -->
<a id="page-454"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 454]*

Dutiyaṃ pāṭalipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (372)    
[374] vipassī nāma bhagavā         sayambhū aggapuggalo    
purakkhato sasissehi         bandhumaṃ pāvisī jino .

Tīṇi pāṭalipupphāni          ucchaṅge ṭhapitāni me    
sīsaṃ nhāyitukāmohaṃ [^454-1]      nadītīraṃ [^454-2] agacchahaṃ .

Nikkhamma bandhumatiyā         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ            ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ .

Byagghūsabhaṃva pavaraṃ           abhijātaṃva kesariṃ    
gacchantaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ         bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ .

Tasmiṃ pasanno sugate        kilesamaladhovane    
gahetvā tīṇi pupphāni       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭalipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pāṭalipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 455 -->
<a id="page-455"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 455]*

Tatiyaṃ tīṇuppalamāliyattherāpadānaṃ (373)    
[375] candabhāgānadītīre          ahosiṃ vānaro tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare .

Obhāsentaṃ disā sabbā     sālarājaṃva phullitaṃ    
lakkhaṇabyañjanūpetaṃ          disvā attamano ahaṃ .

Udaggacitto sumano         pītiyā haṭṭhamānaso    
tīṇi uppalapupphāni          matthake abhiropayiṃ .

Pūjayitvāna pupphāni         phussassāpi [^455-1] mahesino    
sagāravo bhavitvāna         pakkāmiṃ uttarāmukho .

Gacchanto paṭikuṭiko         vippasannena cetasā    
selantare patitvāna        pāpuṇiṃ jīvitakkhayaṃ .

Tena kammena sukatena       cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
jahitvā purimaṃ jātiṃ         tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ .

Satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñca         devarajjamakārayiṃ    
satānaṃ pañcakkhattuṃ ca        cakkavatti ahosahaṃ .

Dvenavute ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 456 -->
<a id="page-456"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 456]*
