itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇuppalamāliyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tīṇuppalamāliyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ pattipupphiyattherāpadānaṃ (374)    
[376] yadā nibbāyi sambuddho      mahesī padumuttaro    
samāgamma janā sabbe       sarīraṃ nīharanti te .

Nīharante sarīramhi          vijjamānāsu bherisu    
pasannacitto sumano         pattipupphamabhipūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sarīre pūjite phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pattipupphiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pattipupphiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 457 -->
<a id="page-457"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 457]*

Pañcamaṃ sattapaṇṇiyattherāpadānaṃ (375)    
[377] sumano nāma sambuddho       uppajji lokanāyako    
pasannacitto sumano         sattapaṇṇimapūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      sattapaṇṇimapūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sattapaṇṇipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapaṇṇiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sattapaṇṇiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ gandhamuṭṭhiyattherāpadānaṃ (376)[^457-7]    
[378] citake kayiramāne          nānāgandhe samāgate [^457-1]    
pasannacitto sumano         gandhamuṭṭhiṃ apūjayiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 458 -->
<a id="page-458"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 458]*

Satasahasse ito kappe      citakaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhamuṭṭhiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Gandhamuṭṭhiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ citakapūjakattherāpadānaṃ (377)    
[379] parinibbute bhagavati          jalajuttamanāmake    
āropitamhi citake         sālapupphaṃ apūjayiṃ .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ pupphaṃ apūjayiṃ [^458-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa [^458-2] santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 459 -->
<a id="page-459"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 459]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Citakapūjakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ sumanatālavaṇṭiyattherāpadānaṃ (378)    
[380] siddhatthassa bhagavato         tālavaṇṭaṃ adāsahaṃ    
sumenahi paṭicchannaṃ          dhārayāmi mahāyasaṃ [^459-1] .

Catunavute ito kappe       tālavaṇṭaṃ adāsahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppatā      kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 460 -->
<a id="page-460"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 460]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanatālavaṇṭiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumanatālavaṇṭiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sumanadāmiyattherāpadānaṃ (379)(    
[381] siddhatthassa bhagavato         nhātakassa tapassino    
katvāna sumanadāmaṃ          dhārayiṃ purato ṭhito .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ dāmaṃ abhipūjayiṃ [^460-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sumanadāmassidaṃ [^460-2] phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanadāmiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sumanadāmiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 461 -->
<a id="page-461"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 461]*

Dasamaṃ kāsumāriphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (380)    
[382] kaṇikāraṃva jotantaṃ          nisinnaṃ pabbatantare    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          lokajeṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
kāsumāriphalaṃ gayha          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā [^461-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāsumāriphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kāsumāriphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ  
bodhipāṭaliuppali           patti ca sattapaṇṇiyo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 462 -->
<a id="page-462"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 462]*

Gandhamuṭṭhī ca citako         tālaṃ sumanadāmako    
kāsumāriphalīpica            gāthā ekūnasaṭṭhiyo .

Bodhivandanavaggo aṭṭhattiṃso .

ekūnacattāḷīso ambaṭaphalavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ ambaṭaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (381)    
[383] sataraṃsī nāma bhagavā         sayambhū aparājito    
vivekakāmo sambuddho       gocarāyābhinikkhami .

Phalahattho ahaṃ disvā        upagañchiṃ narāsabhaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         ambaṭaṃ [^462-1] adadaṃ phalaṃ .

Catunavute ito kappe       yaṃ phalaṃ adadaṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 463 -->
<a id="page-463"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 463]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambaṭaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ambaṭaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ labujadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (382)    
[384] nagare bandhumatiyā          āsiṃ ārāmiko tadā    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          gacchantaṃ anilañjase .

Labujassa phalaṃ gayha          buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
ākāse ṭhitako santo      paṭiggaṇhi mahāyaso .

Pītisañjananaṃ [^463-1] mayhaṃ        diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahaṃ    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna        vippasannena cetasā .

Adhigañchiṃ tadā pītiṃ          vipulañca sukhuttamaṃ    
uppajjateva ratanaṃ          nibbattassa yahiṃ [^463-2] tahiṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 464 -->
<a id="page-464"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 464]*

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā labujadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Labujadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ udumbaraphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (383)    
[385] ninnagānadiyā [^464-1] tīre      vihāsi purisuttamo    
addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ          ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ .

Tasmiṃ pasannamānaso         kilesamaladhovane    
udumbaraphalaṃ gayha           buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 465 -->
<a id="page-465"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 465]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udumbaraphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udumbaraphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ milakkhuphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (384)    
[386] vanantare buddhaṃ disvā       atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         milakkhussa [^465-1] phalaṃ adaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā milakkhuphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Milakkhuphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 466 -->
<a id="page-466"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 466]*

pañcamaṃ phārusaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (385)    
[387] suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ    
rathiyaṃ paṭipajjantaṃ           phārusaphalaṃ adāsahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadaṃ ahaṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phārusaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Phārusaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ valliphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (386)    
[388] sabbe janā samāgamma       āgamiṃsu vanaṃ tadā    
phalamanvesamānā te        alabhiṃsu phalaṃ tadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 467 -->
<a id="page-467"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 467]*

Tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ        sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         valliphalamadāsahaṃ .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā valliphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Valliphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ kadaliphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (387)    
[389] kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ            puṇṇamāyeva candimaṃ    
jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva           addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Kadaliyā [^467-1] phalaṃ gayha       adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ    
pasannacitto sumano         vanditvāna apakkamiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 468 -->
<a id="page-468"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 468]*

ekattiṃse ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadaliphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Kadaliphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ panasaphaladāyakattherāpadānaṃ (388)    
[390] ajjuno nāma sambuddho      himavante vasī tadā    
caraṇena ca sampanno        samādhikusalo muni .

Kumbhamattaṃ gahetvāna        panasaṃ jīvajīvakaṃ    
chattapaṇṇe ṭhapetvāna       adāsiṃ satthuno ahaṃ .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 469 -->
<a id="page-469"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 469]*

svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā panasaphaladāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Panasaphaladāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ soṇakoṭivisattherāpadānaṃ (389)    
[391] vipassino pāvacane         ekaṃ leṇaṃ mayā kataṃ    
cātuddisassa saṅghassa        bandhumārājadhāniyā .

Dussehi bhūmiṃ leṇassa        santharitvā pariccajiṃ    
udaggacitto sumano         akāsiṃ paṇidhiṃ tadā .

Ārādheyyaṃ sambuddhaṃ        pabbajjañca labheyyahaṃ    
anuttarañca nibbānaṃ         phuseyyaṃ santimuttamaṃ .

Teneva sukkamūlena         kappe navuti saṃsariṃ    
devabhūto manusso ca        katapuñño virocahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 470 -->
<a id="page-470"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 470]*

Tato kammāvasesena        idha pacchimake bhave    
campāyaṃ aggaseṭṭhissa       jātomhi ekaputtako .

Jātamattassa me sutvā      pituchando ayaṃ ahu    
dadāmahaṃ kumārassa          vīsakoṭī anūnakā .

Caturaṅgulā ca me lomā     jātā pādatale ubho    
sukhumā mudusamphassā         tulāpicusamā subhā .

Atītā navuti kappā         ayaṃ eko ca uttariṃ    
nābhijānāmi nikkhitte       pāde bhumme [^470-1] asanthate .

Ārādhito me sambuddho     pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
arahattañca me pattaṃ        sītibhūtomhi nibbuto .

Aggāraddhaviriyānaṃ          niddiṭṭho sabbadassinā    
khīṇāsavo [^470-2] ca arahā      chaḷabhiñño mahiddhiko .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         leṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 471 -->
<a id="page-471"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 471]*

Thero [^471-1] koṭiviso soṇo   bhikkhusaṅghassa aggato    
pañhaṃ puṭṭho viyākāsi       anotattamahāsareti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā soṇo koṭiviso thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Soṇakoṭivisattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ pubbakammapiloti nāma buddhāpadānaṃ (390)    
[392] anotattasarāsanne         ramaṇīye silātale    
nānāratanapajjote         nānāgandhavanantare .

Mahatā bhikkhusaṅghena         pareto lokanāyako    
āsīno byākari tattha       pubbakammāni attano .

Suṇātha bhikkhave mayhaṃ        yaṃ kammaṃ pakataṃ mayā    
ekaṃ araññikaṃ bhikkhuṃ         disvā dinnaṃ pilotikaṃ .

Patthitaṃ paṭhamaṃ buddhaṃ          buddhattāya mayā tadāh    
pilotiyassa kammassa         buddhattepi vipaccati .

Gopālako pure āsiṃ       gāviṃ pājeti gocaraṃ    
pivantiṃ udakaṃ āvilaṃ         gāviṃ disvā nivārayiṃ .

Tena kammavipākena         idha pacchimake bhave    
pipāsito yathicchakaṃ          na hi pātuṃ labhāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 472 -->
<a id="page-472"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 472]*

Punāli nāmahaṃ dhutto        pubbe aññāsu jātisu    
paccekabuddhaṃ surabhiṃ          abbhācikkhiṃ adūsakaṃ .

Tena kammavipākena         niraye saṃsariṃ ciraṃ    
bahū vassasahassāni          dukkhaṃ vedesi vedanaṃ .

Tena kammāvasesena        idha pacchimake bhave    
abbhakkhānaṃ mayā laddhaṃ       sundarikāya kāraṇā .

Sabbābhibhussa buddhassa        nando nāmāsi sāvako    
taṃ abbhācikkhāya [^472-1] niraye   ciraṃ saṃsaritaṃ mayā .

Dasavassasahassāni           niraye saṃsariṃ ciraṃ    
manussalābhaṃ [^472-2] laddhāhaṃ      abbhakkhānaṃ bahuṃ labhiṃ .

Tena kammāvasesena        ciñcamānavikā mamaṃ    
abbhācikkhi abhūtena         janakāyassa āgatā [^472-3] .

Brāhmaṇo sutavā āsiṃ      ahaṃ sakkatapūjito    
mahāvane pañcasate         mante vācemi māṇave .

Tatthāgato isībhimo [^472-4]      pañcābhiñño mahiddhiko    
tañcāhaṃ āgataṃ disvā       abbhācikkhiṃ adūsakaṃ .

Tatohaṃ avacaṃ sisse         kāmabhogī ayaṃ isi    
mayhaṃpi bhāsamānassa         anumodiṃsu māṇavā .

Tato māṇavakā sabbe       bhikkhamānā kule kule    
mahājanassa āhaṃsu          kāmabhogī ayaṃ isi .

#[^472-1] ma. Yu ... Abbhakkhāya ... . [^472-2] ma. Yu. Manussabhāvaṃ . [^472-3] ma. Yu. Aggato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 473 -->
<a id="page-473"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 473]*

Tena kammavipākena         pañcabhikkhusatā ime    
abbhakkhānaṃ labhuṃ sabbe       sundarikāya kāraṇā .

Vemātu [^473-1] bhātaraṃ pubbe    dhanahetu haniṃ ahaṃ    
pakkhipiṃ gariduggesu          silāya ca apiṃsayiṃ .

Tena kammavipākena         devadatto silaṃ khipi    
aṅguṭṭhaṃ piṃsayi pāde        mama pāsāṇasakkharā .

Purehaṃ dārako hutvā       kīḷamāno mahāpathe    
paccekabuddhaṃ disvāna        magge sakalikaṃ dahiṃ [^473-2] .

Tena kammavipākena         idha pacchimake bhave    
vadhatthaṃ maṃ devadatto        atimāre payojayi .

Hatthāroho pure āsiṃ      paccekamunimuttamaṃ    
piṇḍāya vicarantaṃpi [^473-3]       āsādesiṃ gajenahaṃ .

Tena kammavipākena         bhanto nāḷāgirī gajo    
giribbaje puravare          dāruṇo samupāgami .

Rājāhaṃ pattiko [^473-4] āsiṃ    sattiyā purise [^473-5] haniṃ    
tena kammavipākena         niraye paccisaṃ bhusaṃ .

Kammuno tassa sesena       so [^473-6] dāni sakalaṃ mama    
pāde chaviṃ pakappesi        na hi kammaṃ vinassati .

Ahaṃ kevaṭṭagāmasmiṃ         ahu kevaṭṭadārako    
macchopaghātake [^473-7] disvā    janayiṃ somanassakaṃ .

#[^473-1] yu. Dve mātā bhātaro pubbe . [^473-2] ma. Khapiṃ . [^473-3] ma. Yu. Vicarantaṃ taṃ .

#[^473-4] ma. Yu. Patthivo . [^473-5] ma. Yu. Purisaṃ . [^473-6] ma. Idāni .. . Yu. Cādiṇṇaṃ ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 474 -->
<a id="page-474"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 474]*

Tena kammavipākena         sīsadukkhaṃ ahu mama    
sakkesu haññamānesu        yadā hani viṭaṭubho [^474-1] .

Phussassāhaṃ pāvacane        sāvake paribhāsayiṃ    
yavaṃ khādatha bhuñjatha          mā ca bhuñjatha sāliyo .

Tena kammavipākena         temāsaṃ khāditaṃ yavaṃ    
nimantito brāhmaṇena       verañjāyaṃ vasiṃ tadā .

Nibbuddhe vattamānamhi       mallaputtaṃ nisedhayiṃ [^474-2]    
tena kammavipākena         piṭṭhidukkhaṃ ahu mama .

Tikicchako ahaṃ āsiṃ         seṭṭhiputtaṃ virecayiṃ    
tena kammavipākena         hoti pakkhandikaṃ mama .

Avacāhaṃ jotipālo         sugataṃ kassapaṃ tadā    
kuto nu bodhimaṇḍassa        bodhi paramadullabhā .

Tena kammavipākena         acariṃ dukkaraṃ bahuṃ    
chabbassānuruvelāyaṃ         tato bodhiṃ apāpuṇiṃ .

Nāhaṃ etena maggena       pāpuṇiṃ bodhimuttamaṃ    
kumaggena gavesissaṃ         pubbakammena codito [^474-3] .

Puññapāpaparikkhīṇo          sabbasantāpavajjito    
asoko anupāyāso        nibbāyissaṃ anāsavo .

Evaṃ jino viyākāsi        bhikkhusaṅghassa atthato [^474-4]    
sabbābhiññābalappatto       anotatte mahāsareti .

#[^474-1] po. Ma. Viṭṭubho . Yu. Viḍūḍabho . [^474-2] ma. Viheṭhayiṃ . [^474-3] ma. Vārito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 475 -->
<a id="page-475"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 475]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ bhagavā attano pubbacaritaṃ pubbakammapilotiṃ [^475-1] nāma    
Buddhāpadānaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsitthāti .

Pubbakammapiloti nāma buddhāpadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
ambaṭaṃ abujañceva          udumbaramilakkhu ca    
phāruvallī ca kadali          panaso koṭivisako .

Pubbakammapiloti ca          apadānaṃ mahesino    
gāthāyo ekanavuti         gaṇitāyo vibhāvihi .

Ambaṭaphalavaggo ekūnacattāḷīso .

Cuddasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 476 -->
<a id="page-476"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 476]*

Cattāḷīso pilindavaggol    
paṭhamaṃ pilindavacchattherāpadānaṃ (391)    
[393] nagare haṃsavatiyā           āsiṃ dovāriko ahaṃ    
akkhobbhaṃ [^476-1] amitaṃ bhogaṃ     ghare sannicitaṃ mama .

Rahogato nisīditvā         pahaṃsitvāna mānasaṃ    
nisajja pāsādavare         evaṃ cintesahaṃ tadā .

Bahū meva [^476-2] gatā bhogā    phītaṃ antepuraṃ mama    
rājāpi maṃ nimantesi [^476-3]     ānando paṭhavissaro .

Ayañca buddho uppanno      adhiccuppattiko muni    
saṃvijjanti ca me bhogā      dānaṃ dassāmi satthuno .

Padumena [^476-4] rājaputtena     dinnaṃ dānavaraṃ jine    
hatthināge ca pallaṅke      apassenaṃ canappakaṃ .

Ahaṃpi dānaṃ dassāmi         saṅghe gaṇavaruttame [^476-5]    
adinnapubbaṃ dānavaraṃ [^476-6]      bhavissaṃ ādikammiko .

Cintetvāhaṃ bahuvidhaṃ         yāge yassa sukhaṃ phalaṃ    
parikkhāradānamaddakkhiṃ        mama saṅkappapūraṇaṃ .

Parikkhārāni dassāmi        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame [^476-7]    
adinnapubbamaññesaṃ          bhavissaṃ ādikammiko .

#[^476-1] ma. Yu. Akkhobhaṃ . [^476-2] ma. Yu. Bahūmedhigatā . [^476-3] ma. Yu. Rājāpi sannimantesi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 477 -->
<a id="page-477"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 477]*

Naḷakāre upāgamma         chattaṃ kāresi tāvade    
chattasatasahassāni           ekato sannipātayiṃ .

Dussasatahassāni            ekato sannipātayiṃ    
pattasatasahassāni           ekato sannipātayiṃ .

Vāsiyo satthake cāpi       sūciyo nakhachedane    
heṭṭhā chatte ṭhapāpesiṃ     kāretvā tadanucchave .

Vidhūpane tālapaṇṇe [^477-1]      morahatthe ca cāmare    
parissāvane teladhāre      kārayiṃ tadanucchave .

Sūcighare aṃsavaddhe          athopi kāyabandhane    
ādhārake ca sukate        kārayiṃ tadanucchave .

Paribhogabhājane ca          athopi lohathālake    
bhesajje [^477-2] pūrayitvāna     heṭṭhāchatte ṭhapesahaṃ .

Vacaṃ usīraṃ laṭṭhimadhadha          pipphalimaricāni ca    
harītakiṃ siṅgiveraṃ           sabbe pūresi bhājane .

Upāhanā pādukāyo        atho udakapuñchane    
kattaradaṇḍe sukate         kārayiṃ tadanucchave .

Osathaṃ añjanāpica [^477-3]       salākā dhammakuttarā    
kuñcikā pañcavaṇṇehi        sibbite kuñcikāghare .

Āyoge dhūmanette ca      athopi dīpadhārake    
tumbake ca karaṇḍe ca       kārayiṃ tadanucchave .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 478 -->
<a id="page-478"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 478]*

Saṇḍāse pipphale ceva      athopi malahārino [^478-1]    
bhesajjathavike ceva         kārayiṃ tadanucchave .

Āsandiyo pīṭhake ca        pallaṅke caturomaye    
tadanucchave kāretvā       heṭṭhāchatte ṭhapesahaṃ .

Uṇṇābhisiṃ [^478-2] tulabhisiṃ        athopi pīṭhakābhisiṃ    
bibbohane ca sukate        kārayiṃ tadanucchave .

Kuruvinde madhusitthe         telahatthapatāpakaṃ    
siveṭiphalakaṃ [^478-4] suciṃ         mañcaṃ attharaṇena ca .

Senāsane pādapuñche       sayanāsanaḍaṇḍake    
dantapoṇe ca kathaliṃ [^478-5]      sīsalepanagandhake [^478-6] .

Araṇiṃ [^478-7] phalapīṭhe ca        pattapidhānathālake    
udakassa kaṭacchuñca [^478-8]       cuṇṇakaṃ rajanambaṇaṃ .

Sammuñjanaṃ [^478-9] udavatthaṃ       tathā vassikasāṭakaṃ    
nisīdanaṃ kaṇḍucchādiṃ          atha antaravāsakaṃ .

Uttarāsaṅgasaṅghāṭiṃ         natthukaṃ mukhasodhanaṃ    
bilaṅgaloṇabhūtañca 10       madhadha ca dadhipānakaṃ .

Pupphasitthaṃ pilotiñca         mukhapuñchanasuttakaṃ    
dātabbaṃ nāma yaṃ atthi       yañca kappati satthuno .

Sabbametaṃ samānetvā       ānandaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ    
upasaṅkamma rājānaṃ         janetāraṃ mahāyasaṃ 1[^478-1] .

#[^478-1] ma. Yu. Malahārake . [^478-2] ma. Yu. Ūṇṇābhisī ... Piṭhakābhisī . [^478-3] ma. Yu. Bimbohane .

#[^478-4] ma. Yu. Sipāṭiphalake sucī . [^478-5] ma. Āṭlī . [^478-6] ma. Yu. Sīsālepanagandhake .

#[^478-7] ma. Yu. Araṇī . [^478-8] ma. Kaṭacchū ca . [^478-9] ma. Sammajjanaṃ . 10 ma. Bilaṅgaloṇaṃ pahūtaṃ ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 479 -->
<a id="page-479"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 479]*

Sirasā abhivādetvā        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
ekato jātisaṃvaḍḍhā        ubhinnaṃ ekato yaso [^479-1] .

Sādhāraṇā sukhe dukkhe      ubho ca anuvattakā    
atthi cetasikaṃ dukkhaṃ         tavādheyyaṃ [^479-2] arindama .

Yadi sakkosi taṃ dukkhaṃ        vinodeyyāsi khattiya    
tava dukkhaṃ mama dukkhaṃ         abhinnaṃ ekato manaṃ [^479-3] .

Niṭṭhitanti vijānāsi [^479-4]      taṃ moceyya sace tuvaṃ    
jānāhi kho mahārāja       dukkhaṃ me dubbinodayaṃ .

Bahuṃ samāno gajjassu        etante duccajaṃ dhanaṃ [^479-5]    
yāvatā vijite [^479-6] atthi     yāvatā mama jīvitaṃ .

Etehi yadi te attho      dassāmi avikampito    
gajjitaṃ kho tayā deva       micchā taṃ bahugajjitaṃ .

Jānissāmi tuvaṃ ajja        sabbadhamme patiṭṭhitaṃ    
atibāḷhaṃ nipīḷesi          dadamānassa me sato .

Kinte [^479-7] sallapitenattho    patthitaṃ te kathehi me    
icchāmahaṃ mahārāja         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ .

Bhojayissāmi sambuddhaṃ        vajjaṃ me māhu jīvitaṃ    
aññaṃ tehaṃ varaṃ dammi        ayācito [^479-8] tathāgato .

Adeyyo kassaci buddho      maṇi jotiraso yathā    
nanu te gajjitaṃ deva        yāva [^479-9] jīvitamatthikaṃ .

#[^479-1] ma. Manaṃ . Yu. Yasaṃ . [^479-2] po. Vinodetuṃ . [^479-3] ma. Mano . [^479-4] ma. Yu. Vijānāhi .

#[^479-5] ma. Varaṃ . [^479-6] yu. Vijitaṃ . [^479-7] ma. Kiṃ te me piḷitenattho . [^479-8] ma. Māyācittho  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 480 -->
<a id="page-480"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 480]*

Jīvitaṃ dadamānena           yuttaṃ dātuṃ tathāgataṃ    
ṭhapanīyo mahāvīro          adeyyo kassaci jino .

Na me paṭissuto buddho      varassu amitaṃ dhanaṃ    
vinicchayaṃ pāpuṇāmi [^480-1]       pucchissāmi [^480-2] vinicchaye .

Yathā saṇhaṃ kathessanti       paṭipucchāma taṃ tathā    
rañño hatthe gahetvāna     agamāsiṃ vinicchayaṃ .

Purato akkhadassānaṃ         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
suṇantu me akkhadassā       rājā varaṃ adāsi me .

Na kiñci ṭhapayitvāna         jīvitaṃpi pavārayiṃ [^480-3]    
tassa me varadinnassa        buddhaseṭṭhaṃ variṃ ahaṃ .

Sudinno hoti me buddho     chindatha [^480-4] saṃsayaṃ mama    
sussāma tava vacanaṃ          bhūmipālassa rājino .

Ubhinnaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā         chindissāmettha saṃsayaṃ    
sabbaṃ deva tayā dinnaṃ       imassa sabbagāhitaṃ [^480-5] .

Na kiñci ṭhapayitvāna         jīvitaṃpi pavārayiṃ    
kicchappattova hutvāna       yāvajīvamanuttaraṃ [^480-6] .

Imaṃ sudukkhitaṃ ñatvā         adāsiṃ sabbagāhitaṃ [^480-7]    
parājayo tuvaṃ deva         assa deyyo tathāgato .

Ubhinnaṃ saṃsayo chinno        yathā saṇṭhamhi tiṭṭhatha    
rājā tattheva ṭhatvāna      akkhadassetadabravi .

#[^480-1] ma. Yu. Pāpuṇāma . [^480-2] ma. Yu. Pucchissāma . [^480-3] po. Ma. Yu. Pavārayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 481 -->
<a id="page-481"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 481]*

Sammā mayhaṃpi deyyātha      puna buddhaṃ labhāmahaṃ    
pūretvā tava saṅkappaṃ       bhojayitvā tathāgataṃ .

Puna deyyāsi sambuddhaṃ       ānandassa yasassino    
akkhadassebhivādetvā       ānandamapi [^481-1] khattiyaṃ .

Tuṭṭho pamudito hutvā       sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ    
upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ         oghatiṇṇaṃ anāsavaṃ .

Sirasā abhivādetvā        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
vasīsatasahassehi            adhivāsehi cakkhumā .

Hāsayanto mama cittaṃ        nivesanamupehi me    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .Ai    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         adhivāsesi cakkhumā    
adhivāsanamaññāya           abhivādiya satthuno .

Haṭṭho udaggacittohaṃ        nivesanamupāgamiṃ    
mittāmacce samānetvā     idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Sudullabho mayā laddho       maṇi jotiraso yathā    
kena taṃ pūjayissāmi [^481-2]      appameyyo anūpamo .

Atulo asamo dhīro [^481-3]      jino appaṭipuggalo    
tathāsamasamo ceva          adutiyo narāsabho .

Dukkaraṃ adhikāraṃ hi          buddhānucchavikaṃ mayā [^481-4]    
nānāpupphe samānetvā     karoma pupphamaṇḍapaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 482 -->
<a id="page-482"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 482]*

Buddhānucchavikaṃ etaṃ         sabbapūjā bhavissati    
uppalaṃ padumaṃ vāpi          vassikaṃ adhimuttakaṃ .

Campakaṃ nāgapupphañca         maṇḍapaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ    
satāsanasahassāni           chattachāyāya paññapiṃ .

Pacchimaṃ āsanaṃ mayhaṃ         adhikaṃ satamagghati    
satāsanasahassāni           chattachāyāya paññapiṃ .

Paṭiyādetvā annapānaṃ      kālaṃ ārocayiṃ ahaṃ    
ārocitamhi kālamhi        padumuttaro mahāmuni .

Vasīsatasahassehi            nivesanamupesi me    
dhārentaṃ uparicchattaṃ        suphullapupphamaṇḍape .

Vasīsatasahassehi            nisīdi purisuttamo    
chattasatasahassāni           satasahassamāsanaṃ .

Kappiyaṃ anavajjañca          paṭiggaṇhāhi cakkhumā    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Mamaṃ tāretukāmo so       sampaṭicchi mahāmuni    
bhikkhuno ekamekassa        paccekaṃ pattadāsahaṃ .

Jahiṃsu sambhataṃ [^482-1] pattaṃ       lohapattaṃ adhārayuṃ    
sattarattindivaṃ buddho        nisīdi pupphamaṇḍape .

Bodhayanto bahū satte       dhammacakkaṃ pavattayi    
dhammacakkaṃ pavattento       heṭṭhato pupphamaṇḍape .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 483 -->
<a id="page-483"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 483]*

Cullāsītisahassānaṃ          dhammābhisamayo ahu    
sattame divase patte       padumuttaro mahāmuni .

Chattachāyāya nisinno        imā gāthā abhāsatha    
anūnakaṃ dānavaraṃ            yo me pādāsi māṇavo .

Tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato    
hatthī assā rathā pattī      senā ca caturaṅginī .

Parivāressanti taṃ [^483-1] niccaṃ   sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ         sivikaṃ [^483-2] sandamānikaṃ .

Upaṭṭhissanti taṃ niccaṃ        sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
saṭṭhī rathasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā .

Parivāressanti taṃ niccaṃ      sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         bheriyo samalaṅkatā .

Vajjayissanti taṃ niccaṃ        sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
chaḷāsītisahassāni           nāriyo samalaṅkatā .

Vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā    
āḷāramukhā [^483-3] hasulā      susaññā tanumajjhimā .

Parivāressanti taṃ niccaṃ      sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati .

Sahassakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 484 -->
<a id="page-484"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 484]*

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
devaloke vasantassa        puññakammasamaṅgino .

Devalokānupariyante [^484-1]     ratanachattaṃ dharissati    
icchissati yadā vāyaṃ [^484-2]     chadanaṃ dussapupphajaṃ .

Imassa cittamaññāya         nivaddhaṃ chādayissati    
devalokā cavitvāna        sukkamūlena codito .

Puññakammena saṃyutto        brahmabandhu bhavissati    
kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo .

Gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati    
sabbametaṃ abhiññāya         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo .

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapessati    
pilindavaccho nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Devānaṃ asurānañca         gandhabbānañca sakkato    
bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañca          gihīnañca tatheva so .

Piyo hutvāna sabbesaṃ       viharissatināsavo    
satasahasse kataṃ kammaṃ        phalaṃ dassesi me idha .

Sumutto saravegova         kilese jhāpayiṃ [^484-3] ahaṃ    
aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        puññakkhette anuttare .

Yattha kāraṃ karitvāna        pattosmi acalaṃ padaṃ    
anūnakaṃ dānavaraṃ            adāsi yo hi māṇavo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 485 -->
<a id="page-485"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 485]*

Ādipubbaṅgamo āsi        tassa dānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
chatte [^485-1] sugate datvāna    saṅghe gaṇavaruttame [^485-2] .

Aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
sītaṃ uṇhaṃ na jānāmi        rajojallaṃ na limpati .

Anupaddavo anīti ca         homi apacito sadā    
sukhumacchaviko homi          visadaṃ homi mānasaṃ .

Chattasatasahassāni           bhave saṃsarato mama    
sabbālaṅkārayuttāni        tassa kammassa vāhasā .

Imaṃ [^485-3] jātiṃ ṭhapetvāna     matthake dhārayanti me    
tasmā [^485-4] imāya jātiyā    natthi me chattadhāraṇā .

Mama sabbaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ         vimuttichattapattiyā    
dussāni sugate datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

Aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
suvaṇṇavaṇṇo virajo         sappabhāso patāpavā .

Siniddhaṃ hoti me gattaṃ       bhave saṃsarato mama    
dussasatasahassāni           setā pītā ca lohitā .

Dhārenti matthake mayhaṃ      dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ    
koseyyakambaliyāni         khomakappāsikāni ca .

Sabbattha paṭilabhāmi          tesaṃ nissandato ahaṃ    
patte ca sugate datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

#[^485-1] ma. Chatte ca sukate datvā . [^485-2] po. Ma. Yu. Guṇavaruttame . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 486 -->
<a id="page-486"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 486]*

Dasānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama    
suvaṇṇathāle maṇithāle       rajatepica thālake .

Lohitaṅkamaye thāle        paribhuñjāmi sabbadā    
anupaddavo anīti ca         homi apacito sadā .

Lābhī annassa pānassa       vatthassa sayanassa ca    
na vinassanti me bhogā      ṭhitacitto bhavāmahaṃ .

Dhammakāmo sadā homi       appakleso anāsavo    
devaloke manusse vā      anubandhā ime guṇā .

Chāyā yathāpi rūpassa [^486-1]     sabbattha na jahanti maṃ    
cittabandhanasambaddhā         sukatā vāsiyo bahū .

Datvāna buddhaseṭṭhassa       saṅghassa ca tathevahaṃ    
aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Sūro [^486-2] homi [^486-3] visārī ca  vesārajjesu pāramī    
dhitiviriyavā homi           paggahitamano sadā .

Kilesacchedanaṃ ñāṇaṃ         sukhumaṃ atulaṃ suciṃ    
sabbattha paṭilabhāmi          tassa nissandato mama .

Akakkase apharuse          adhote satthake bahū    
pasannacitto datvāna        buddhe saṅghe tatheva ca .

Pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
kalyāṇamittaṃ viriyaṃ          khantiñca mettisatthakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 487 -->
<a id="page-487"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 487]*

Taṇhāsallassa chinnattā      paññāsatthaṃ anuttaraṃ    
vajirena samaṃ ñāṇaṃ          tesaṃ nissandato labhe .

Sūciyo sugate datvā        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Namassiyo kaṅkhacchedo       abhirūpo ca bhogavā    
tikkhapañño sadā homi       saṃsaranto bhavābhave .

Gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ ṭhānaṃ          atthaṃ ñāṇena passayiṃ    
vajiraggasamaṃ ñāṇaṃ           hoti me tamaghātanaṃ .

Nakhacchedane sugate datvā    saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Dāsadāsī gavasse ca        bhaṭake nāṭake [^487-1] bahū    
nhāpite bhattake sūde      sabbattheva labhāmahaṃ .

Vidhūpane sugate datvā       tālapaṇṇe ca sobhane    
aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Sītaṃ uṇhaṃ na jānāmi        pariḷāho na vijjati    
darathaṃ nābhijānāmi          cittasantāpanaṃ mama .

Rāgaggi dosamohaggi        mānaggi diṭṭhiaggi ca    
sabbaggi nibbuto mayhaṃ       tassa nissandato mama .

Morahatthe cāmariyo        datvā saṅghe gaṇuttame    
upasantakilesohaṃ           viharāmi anaṅgaṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 488 -->
<a id="page-488"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 488]*

Parissāvane sugate datvā    sugate [^488-1] dhammakuttare    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Sabbesaṃ samatikkamma         dibbaṃ āyuṃ labhāmahaṃ    
appasayho sadā homi       corapaccatthikehi vā .

Satthena vā visena vā      vihesaṃpi na kubbate    
antarāmaraṇaṃ natthi          tesaṃ nissandato mama .

Teladhāre sugate datvā     saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Sucārurūpo subhaddo [^488-2]      susamuggatamānaso    
avikkhepamano homi         sabbārakkhehi rakkhito .

Sūcighare sugate datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Cetosukhaṃ kāyasukhaṃ          iriyāpathajaṃ sukhaṃ_    
ime guṇe paṭilabhāmi [^488-3]     tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Aṃsavaddhe jine datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Saddhamme gāḷhaṃ [^488-4] vindāmi  sarāmi dutiyaṃ bhavaṃ    
sabbattha succhavi homi        tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Kāyabandhe jine [^488-5] datvā   saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
chānisaṃse anubhomi          kammānucchavike mama .

#[^488-1] yu. Saṅghe gaṇuttame . Ma. Dhammakaruttame . [^488-2] yu. Sugato . [^488-3] ma. Paṭilabhe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 489 -->
<a id="page-489"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 489]*

Samādhīsu na kampāmi         vasī homi samādhisu    
abhejjapariso homi         ādeyyavacano sadā .

Upaṭṭhitassati homi          tāso mayhaṃ na vijjati    
devaloke manusse vā      anubandhā ime guṇā .

Ādhārake jine datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcavaṇṇe [^489-1] bhayābhāvo    acalo homi kenaci .

Yekeci me sutā dhammā     satiñāṇappabodhanā    
dhatā [^489-2] me na vinassanti    bhavanti suvinicchitā .

Bhājane paribhoge ca        datvā buddhe gaṇuttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Sovaṇṇamaye maṇimaye        athopi phalikāmaye    
lohitaṅkamaye ceva         labhāmi bhājane ahaṃ .

Bhariyā dāsadāsī ca         hatthissarathapattike    
itthī patibbatā ceva        paribhogāni sabbadā .

Vijjā mantapade ceva       vividhe āgame bahū    
sabbasippaṃ nisāmemi         paribhogāni sabbadā .

Thālake sugate datvā       saṅghe guṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Sovaṇṇamaye maṇimaye        athopi phalikāmaye    
lohitaṅkamaye ceva         labhāmi thālake ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 490 -->
<a id="page-490"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 490]*

Assaṭṭhake phalamaye         atho pokkharapattake    
madhupānakasaṅkhe ca          labhāmi thālake ahaṃ .

Vatte guṇe paṭipattiṃ [^490-1]     ācārakiriyāsu ca    
ime guṇe paṭilabhe         tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Bhesajjaṃ sugate datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
dasānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Āyuvā balavā dhīro        vaṇṇavā yasavā sukhī    
anupaddavo anīti ca         homi [^490-2] apacito sadā    
na me piyaviyogatthi         tassa nissandato mama .

Upāhane jine datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ         sivikaṃ sandamānikaṃ    
saṭṭhī rathasahassāni          parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Maṇimayā tambamayā [^490-3]       soṇṇarajatapādukā    
nibbattanti paduddhāre       bhave saṃsarato mama .

Niyāmaṃ paṭidhāvanti [^490-4]       ācāraguṇasodhanaṃ [^490-5]    
ime guṇe paṭilabhe         tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Pāduke sugate datvā       saṅghe guṇavaruttame    
iddhipādukamāruyha          viharāmi yathicchakaṃ .

#[^490-1] ma. Paṭipatti . Yu. Paṭilabhe . [^490-2] yu. Bhomi cāpacito sadā . [^490-3] yu. Maṇḍalakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 491 -->
<a id="page-491"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 491]*

Mukhapuñchanaṃ [^491-1] sugate datvā   saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Suvaṇṇavaṇṇo virajo         sappabhāso patāpavā    
siniddhaṃ hoti me gattaṃ       rajojallaṃ na limpati .

Ime guṇe paṭilabhe         tassa nissandato ahaṃ    
kattaradaṇḍe sugate datvā    saṅghe gaṇavaruttame [^491-2] .

Pañcānisaṃse [^491-3] anubhomi     kammānucchavike mama    
puttā mayhaṃ bahū honti      tāso mayhaṃ na vijjati .

Appasayho sadā homi       sabbārakkhehi rakkhito    
khalitampi [^491-4] na jānāmi      abhantaṃ mānasaṃ mama .

Osathaṃ añjanaṃ datvā        saṅghe [^491-5] gaṇavaruttame    
aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Visālanayano homi          setapito ca lohito    
anāvilapasannakkho          sabbarogavivajjito .

Labhāmi dibbanayanaṃ           paññācakkhuṃ anuttaraṃ    
ime guṇe paṭilabhe         tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Kuñcike sugate datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
dhammadvāravivaraṇaṃ           labhāmi ñāṇakuñcikaṃ .

Kuñcikānaṃ ghare datvā       saṅghe [^491-6] gaṇavaruttame    
dvānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

#[^491-1] ma. Yu. Mukhapuñchanacole datvā buddhe gaṇuttame . [^491-2] ma. Gaṇuttame .

#[^491-3] ma. Yu. Chānisaṃse . [^491-4] yu. Jalitaṃ maṃ na jānāmi . 5-[^491-6] ma. Yu. Buddhe  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 492 -->
<a id="page-492"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 492]*

Appakodho anupāyāso      saṃsaranto bhave ahaṃ    
āyoge sugate datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

Pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
samādhīsu na kampāmi         vasī homi samādhisu .

Abhejjapariso homi         ādeyyavacano sadā    
jāyati [^492-1] bhogasampatti      bhave saṃsarato mama .

Dhūmanette jine datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Sati me ujukā hoti        susambandhā ca nhāruyo [^492-2]    
labhāmi dibbasayanaṃ [^492-3]        tassa nissandato ahaṃ .

Dīpadāne [^492-4] jine datvā    saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
tīṇānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Jātimā aṅgasampanno       paññavā buddhasammato    
ime guṇe paṭilabhe         tassa nissandato mama [^492-5] .

Tumbake ca karaṇḍe ca       datvā buddhe gaṇuttame    
dasānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Tadā [^492-6] gutto [^492-7] sukhasamaṅgī mahāyasavā tathā gati    
vibhattigatto [^492-8] sukhumālo    sabbītiparivajjito .

Vipule ca guṇe lābhī        sammānacalanā [^492-9] mama    
suvivajjitaübbego          tumbake ca karaṇḍake .

#[^492-1] yu. Jāticca . [^492-2] ma. Nahāravo . [^492-3] ma. Yu. Dibbanayanaṃ . [^492-4] ma. Yu. Dīpadhāre .

#[^492-5] ma. Yu. Ahaṃ . [^492-6] yu. Sadā . [^492-7] ma. Sugutto . [^492-8] ma. Vipattivigato . Yu. Bhattikato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 493 -->
<a id="page-493"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 493]*

Labhāmi caturo vaṇṇe        hatthissaratanāni ca    
tāni me na vinassanti       tumbakāre [^493-1] idaṃ phalaṃ .

Hatthalīlaṅgake [^493-2] datvā     buddhe saṅghe gaṇuttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Sabbalakkhaṇasampanno         āyupaññāsamāhito    
sabbāyāsavinimutto         kāyo me hoti sabbadā .

Tanudhāre sunisite          saṅghe datvāna pipphale    
kilesakantanaṃ ñāṇaṃ          labhāmi atulaṃ suciṃ .

Saṇḍāse sugate datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
kilesaluñcanaṃ [^493-3] ñāṇaṃ       labhāmi atulaṃ suciṃ .

Natthuke sugate datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
aṭṭhānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Saddhā sīlaṃ hiriñcāpi        atha ottappiyaṃ guṇaṃ    
sutaṃ cāgañca khantī [^493-4] ca     paññā me aṭṭhamaṃ guṇaṃ .

Pīṭhake sugate datvā        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama .

Ucce kule pajāyāmi       mahābhogo bhavāmahaṃ    
sabbe maṃ apacāyanti        kitti abbhuggatā mama .

Kappasatasahassāni           pallaṅkā caturassakā    
parivārenti maṃ niccaṃ        saṃvibhāgarato ahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 494 -->
<a id="page-494"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 494]*

Bhisiyo sugate datvā        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
chānisaṃse anubhomi          kammānucchavike mama .

Samasugattopacito [^494-1]        muduko cārudassano    
labhāmi ñāṇapavaraṃ [^494-2]        bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tūlikā vikatikāyo          kaṭissā cittakā bahū    
varapotthake kambale ca      labhāmi vividhe ahaṃ .

Pāvārake ca muduke        mudukājinaveṇiyo    
labhāmi vividhaṭṭhāne [^494-3]      bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Yato sarāmi attānaṃ        yato pattosmi viññutaṃ    
atuccho jhānamañcomhi       bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Bibbohane jine datvā      saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
chānisaṃse anubhomi          kammānucchavike mama .

Uṇṇike padumake ca         atho lohitacandane    
bibbohane upātemi [^494-4]     uttamaṅgaṃ sadā mama .

Aṭṭhaṅgike maggavare        sāmaññe caturo phale    
tesu ñāṇaṃ uppādetvā [^494-5]  vihare niccakālikaṃ .

Dāne dame saññame ca      appamaññāsu rūpisu    
tesu ñāṇaṃ uppādetvā     vihare sabbakālikaṃ .

Vatte guṇe ca [^494-6] paṭime    ācārakiriyāsu ca    
tesu ñāṇaṃ uppādetvā     vihare sabbadā ahaṃ .

#[^494-1] yu. Samaṃ sambhattopacito . [^494-2] ma. Yu. Ñāṇaparivāraṃ . [^494-3] ma. Yu. Vividhatthāre .

#[^494-4] ma. Upādhemi . Yu. Upādemi . [^494-5] yu. Sabbattha upānetvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 495 -->
<a id="page-495"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 495]*

Caṅkame vā padhāne vā     viriye bodhipakkhike [^495-1]    
tesu ñāṇaṃ uppādetvā     viharāmi yathicchakaṃ .

Sīlaṃ samādhi paññā ca        vimutti ca anuttarā    
tesu ñāṇaṃ uppādetvā     viharāmi sukhaṃ ahaṃ .

Phalapīṭhe jine datvā        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
dvānisaṃse [^495-2] anubhomi      kammānucchavike mama .

Soṇṇamaye maṇimaye         dantasāramaye bahū    
pallaṅkaseṭṭhe vindāmi      phalapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Pādapīṭhe jine datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
dvānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Labhāmi bahuke yāne        pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ    
dāsī dāsā ca bhariyā       ye caññe anujīvino .

Sammā paricaranteva [^495-3]      pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ    
telaabbhañjane datvā       saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

Pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
abyādhitā rūpavatā         khippaṃ dhammanisandhitā [^495-4] .

Lābhitā annapānassa        āyuṃ pañcamakaṃ mama    
sappitelañca datvāna        saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

Pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
thāmavā rūpasampanno        pahaṭṭhatanujo sadā .

#[^495-1] ma. Bodhipakkhiye . [^495-2] yu. Caturānisaṃse . [^495-3] ma. Yu. Paricarante maṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 496 -->
<a id="page-496"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 496]*

Abyādhi [^496-1] ca sadā homi    sappitelassidaṃ phalaṃ    
mukhadhovanaṃ datvāna          saṅghe gaṇavaruttame .

Pañcānisaṃse anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
visuddhakaṇṭho madhussaro       kāsasāsavivajjito .

Uppalagandho mukhato         upavāyati  me sadā    
dadhiṃ datvāna sampannaṃ        buddhe saṅghe gaṇuttame .

Bhuñjāmi amataṃ bhattaṃ [^496-2]      varaṃ kāyagatāsatiṃ    
vaṇṇagandharasopetaṃ          madhadha datvā jine gaṇe .

Anūpamaṃ anaññampi [^496-3]        labhāmi vimuttirasaṃ    
yathābhūtaṃ rasaṃ datvā         buddhe saṅghe gaṇuttame .

Caturo phale anubhomi        kammānucchavike mama    
annapānañca datvāna        buddhe saṅghe gaṇuttame .

Dasānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama    
āyuvā balavā dhīro        vaṇṇavā yasavā sukhī .

Lābhī annassa pānassa       sūro paññāṇavā tathā [^496-4]    
ime guṇe paṭilabhe         saṃsaranto bhave ahaṃ .

Dhūpaṃ datvāna sugate         saṅghe gaṇavaruttame    
dasānisaṃse anubhomi         kammānucchavike mama .

Sugandhadeho yasavā         sīghapañño ca kittimā    
tikkhapañño bhūripañño        hāsagambhīrapaññavā .

#[^496-1] ma. Abyādhi visado homi . Yu. ... Visajī ... . [^496-2] yu. Cittaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 497 -->
<a id="page-497"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 497]*

Vepullajavanasappañño [^497-1]     saṃsaranto bhavābhave    
tasseva vāhasā dāni       patto santisukhaṃ sivaṃ .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilindavaccho thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Pilindavacchattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ selattherāpadānaṃ (392)    
[394] nagare haṃsavatiyā           vīthisāmi ahosahaṃ    
mama ñātī samānetvā       idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Buddho loke samuppanno     puññakkhetto anuttaro    
āsi [^497-2] so sabbalokassa    āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Khattiyā negamā ceva       mahāsālā ca brāhmaṇā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 498 -->
<a id="page-498"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 498]*

Hatthārohā anīkatthā       rathikā pattikārakā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Uggā ca rājaputtā ca      vesiyānā ca brāhmaṇā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Āḷārikā ca sūdā [^498-1] ca    nhāpakā mālakārakā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Rajakā pesakārā ca        tunnavāyā ca nhāpikā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Usukārā bhamakārā         cammakārā ca tacchakā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Kammārā soṇṇakārā ca     tipulohakarā tathā    
pasannacittā sumanā         pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Bhatakā celakā [^498-2] ceva     dāsakammakarā bahū    
yathā sakena thāmena        pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Udahārā kaṭṭhahārā        kasikā [^498-3] tiṇahārakā    
yathā sakena thāmena        pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Pupphikā mālikā ceva       paṇṇikā phalahārakā    
yathā sakena thāmena        pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .

Gaṇikā kumbhadāsī ca         pūvikā macchikāpica [^498-4]    
yathā sakena thāmena        pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 499 -->
<a id="page-499"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 499]*

Ete [^499-1] sabbe samāgantvā gaṇabandhāva [^499-2] ekato    
adhikāraṃ karissāma          puññakkhette anuttare .

Te me sutvāna vacanaṃ       gaṇaṃ bandhiṃsu tāvade    
upaṭṭhānasālaṃ sukataṃ         bhikkhusaṅghassa kārayuṃ .

Niṭṭhāpetvāna taṃ sālaṃ      udaggo tuṭṭhamānaso    
pareto tehi sabbehi       sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ         lokanāthaṃ narāsabhaṃ    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Ime tīṇisatā vīra          purisā ekato gaṇā    
upaṭṭhānasālaṃ sukataṃ         niyyādenti tava [^499-3] muni .

Bhikkhusaṅghassa purato         sampaṭicchatu cakkhumā    
tiṇṇaṃ satānaṃ purato         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Tisatāpi ca jeṭṭho ca       anuvattiṃsu ekato    
sampattī [^499-4] hi karitvāna     sabbe anubhavissatha .

Pacchime bhavasampatte [^499-5]     sītibhāvamanuttaraṃ    
ajaraṃ amaraṃ [^499-6] khemaṃ        nibbānaṃ passayissatha [^499-7] .

Evaṃ buddho viyākāsi       sabbaññūtamanuttaro [^499-8]    
buddhassa vacanaṃ sutvā        somanassampavedayiṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
devādhipo pañcasataṃ         devarajjamakārayiṃ .

#[^499-1] ma. Etha . [^499-2] ma. Yu. Gaṇaṃ bandhāma . [^499-3] ma. Yu. Tavaṃ . [^499-4] ma. Yu. Sampattiṃ .

#[^499-5] ma. Yu. Bhave sampatte . [^499-6] ma. Amataṃ santaṃ . Yu. Amaraṃ santiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 500 -->
<a id="page-500"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 500]*

Sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti ahosahaṃ    
padesarajjaṃ [^500-1] vipulaṃ        gaṇanāto [^500-2] asaṅkhayaṃ .

Idha mānusake rajje [^500-3]     parisā honti bandhavā    
pacchime bhavasampatte        vāseṭṭho nāma brāhmaṇo .

Asītikoṭiniccayo           tassa putto ahosahaṃ    
selo iti mama nāmaṃ        chaḷaṅge pāramiṃ gato .

Jaṅghāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ          sasissehi purakkhato    
jaṭābhārabharitaṃpi [^500-4]         keniyaṃ nāma tāpasaṃ .

Paṭiyattāhutiṃ disvā         idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ    
āvāho vā vivāho vā    rājā vā te nimantito .

Āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmohaṃ         brāhmaṇe devasammate    
na nimantemi rājānaṃ        āhuti me na vijjati .

Na catthi mayhamāvāho       vivāho me na vijjati    
sakyānaṃ nandijanano         seṭṭho loke sadevake .

Sabbalokahitatthāsi [^500-5]       sabbasattasukhāvaho    
so me nimantito ajja      tassetaṃ paṭiyādanaṃ .

Tambarusakavaṇṇābho          appameyyo anūpamo    
rūpenāsadiso buddho        svātanāya nimantito .

Ukkāmukhapahaṭṭho [^500-6] ca [^500-7]   khadiraṅgārasannibho    
vijjūpamo mahāvīro         so me nātho [^500-8] nimantito .

#[^500-1] ma. Yu. Devarajjaṃ karontassa . [^500-2] ma. Yu. Mahādevā avandisuṃ . [^500-3] ma. Rajjaṃ .

#[^500-4] ma. Jaṭābhārikabharitaṃ . Yu. Jaṭābhārabharitaṃ . [^500-5] ma. Yu. Sabbalokahitatthāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 501 -->
<a id="page-501"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 501]*

Pabbatagge yathā aggi       puṇṇamāyeva candimā    
naḷaggivaṇṇasaṅkāso         so me buddho nimantito .

Asambhīto bhayātīto         bhavantakaraṇo muni    
sīhūpamo mahāvīro          so me buddho nimantito .

Kusalo buddhadhammesu         appasayho parehi so    
nāgūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Saddhammapārakusalo          buddhanāgo asādiso    
usabhūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Anantavaṇṇo amitayaso       vicittasabbalakkhaṇo    
sakkūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Vasī gaṇī patāpī ca          tejasī ca durāsado    
brahmūpamo mahāvīro        so me buddho nimantito .

Mahaggadhammo [^501-1] dasabalo     balātibalapāragū [^501-2]    
dharaṇūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Sīladhītisamākiṇṇo           dhammaviññāṇakhobhito    
udadhūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Durāsado duppasaho         acalo uggato brahmā [^501-3]    
nerūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Anantañāṇo asamo [^501-4]      atulo aggataṃ gato    
gaganūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Pannarasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

#[^501-1] ma. Pattadhammo . Yu. Mahantadhammo . [^501-2] ma. Yu. Balātibalapārago .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 502 -->
<a id="page-502"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 502]*

Patiṭṭhā bhayabhītānaṃ          tāṇo saraṇagāminaṃ    
assāsako mahāvīro        so me buddho nimantito .

Āsayo buddhimantānaṃ        puññakkhettaṃ sukhesinaṃ    
ratanākaro mahāvīro        so me buddho nimantito .

Assāsako devakaro        sāmaññaphaladāyako    
meghūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Loke [^502-1] samussito vīro    sabbatamavinodano    
suriyūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Ārammaṇavimuttīsu           sabhāvadassako muni    
candūpamo mahāvīro         so me buddho nimantito .

Buddho samussito loke      lakkhaṇehi alaṅkato    
appameyyo mahāvīro       so me buddho nimantito .

Yassa ñāṇaṃ appameyyaṃ       sīlaṃ yassa anūpamaṃ    
vimutti asadisā yassa        so me buddho nimantito .

Yassa dhīti asadisā          thāmo yassa acintiyo    
yassa parakkamo seṭṭho      so me buddho nimantito .

Rāgo doso ca moho ca    visā sabbe samūhatā    
agadūpamo [^502-2] mahāvīro      so me buddho nimantito .

Kilesabyādhi bahudukkhā       osathīva [^502-3] vinodako    
vijjūpamo [^502-4] mahāvīro      so me buddho nimantito .

#[^502-1] ma. Lokacakkhu mahātejo . [^502-2] yu. Agarūpamo . [^502-3] ma. Sabbatamavinodano .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 503 -->
<a id="page-503"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 503]*

Buddhoti ghosaṃ [^503-1] vadesi     ghoso me so sudullabho    
buddhoti [^503-2] ghosanaṃ sutvā    pīti me upapajjatha .

Abbhantaraṃ agaṇhantaṃ         pīti me bahi nicchare    
sohaṃ pītimano santo        idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Kahaṃ nu kho so bhagavā       lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
tattha gantvā namassissaṃ      sāmaññaphaladāyakaṃ .

Paggayha dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ        vedajāto katañjalī    
ācikkha [^503-3] me dhammarājaṃ    sokasallavinodanaṃ .

Udentaṃva mahāmeghaṃ         nīlaṃ añjanasannibhaṃ    
sāgaraṃ viya dissantaṃ         passasetaṃ mahāvanaṃ .

Ettha so vasate buddho     adantadamako muni    
vinayanto ca veneyye      bodhento bodhipakkhiye .

Pipāsitova udakaṃ           bhojanaṃva jigacchato [^503-4]    
gāvī yathā vacchagiddhā       evāhaṃ viciniṃ jinaṃ .

Ācāraüpacāraññū          dhammānucchavisaṃvaraṃ    
sikkhāpesiṃ sake sisse      gacchante [^503-5] jinasantikaṃ .

Durāsadā bhagavanto         sīhāva ekacārino    
pāde pādaṃ nikkhipantā      āgaccheyyātha māṇavā .

Āsiviso yathā ghoro       migarājāva kesarī    
mattova kuñjaro danto [^503-6]   evaṃ buddhā durāsadā .

#[^503-1] ma. Yu. Bho yaṃ . [^503-2] ma. Yu. Buddho buddhoti sutvāna . [^503-3] ma. Ācikkhi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 504 -->
<a id="page-504"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 504]*

Ukkāsitañca khipitaṃ          ajjhupekkhāya māṇavā    
pāde pādaṃ nikkhipantā      upetha buddhasantikaṃ .

Paṭisallānagarukā           appasaddā durāsadā    
durūpasaṅkamā buddhā         garū honti sadevake .

Yamahaṃ [^504-1] pañhaṃ pucchāmi      paṭisammodayāmi [^504-2] vā    
appasaddā tadā hotha       munībhūtāva tiṭṭhatha .

Yaṃ so desesi saddhammaṃ [^504-3]   khemaṃ nibbānapattiyā    
tamevatthaṃ nisāmetha         saddhammassavanaṃ subhaṃ .

Upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ         sammodiṃ muninā ahaṃ    
taṃ kathaṃ vītisāretvā        lakkhaṇe upadhārayiṃ .

Lakkhaṇe dve na dakkhāmi [^504-4]  passāmi tiṃsalakkhaṇe    
kosohitavatthuṃ [^504-5] guyhaṃ      iddhiyā dassayi muni .

Jivhaṃ ninnāmayitvā ca [^504-6]    kaṇṇasote ca nāsike    
paṭimassa [^504-7] nalātantaṃ       kevalaṃ chādayi jino .

Tassāhaṃ lakkhaṇe disvā      paripuṇṇe sabyañjane    
buddhoti niṭṭhaṃ gantvāna      saha sissehi pabbajiṃ .

Satehi tīhi sahito          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
aḍḍhamāse asampatte       sabbe pattamha nibbutiṃ .

Ekato kammaṃ katvāna       puññakkhette anuttare    
ekato saṃsaritvāna         ekato vinivaṭṭayuṃ .

#[^504-1] ma. Yadāhaṃ . Yu. Yāvāhaṃ . [^504-2] yu. Paṭisammodiyāmi vā . [^504-3] ma. Sambuddho .

#[^504-4] ma. Ca kaṅkhāmi . Yu. Va kaṅkhāmi . [^504-5] ma. Yu. Kosohitavatthaguyhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 505 -->
<a id="page-505"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 505]*

Gopānasiyo datvāna        pūgadhamme vasiṃ ahaṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       aṭṭha hetū labhāmahaṃ .

Disāsu pūjito homi         bhogā ca amitā mama    
patiṭṭhā homi sabbesaṃ       tāso mama na vijjati .

Byādhayo [^505-1] me na vijjanti  dīghāyuṃ pālayāmi ca    
sukhumacchaviko homi          āvāse pattite vasse [^505-2] .

Aṭṭha gopānasī datvā       pūgadhamme vasiṃ ahaṃ    
paṭisambhidā arahattañca       etaṃ me aparaṭṭhamaṃ .

Sabbavositavosāno         katakicco anāsavo    
aṭṭhagopānasī nāma         tava putto mahāmuni    
pañca thambhāni datvāna       pūgadhamme vasiṃ ahaṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       pañca hetū labhāmahaṃ .

Acalo homi mettāya       anūnabhogavāmahaṃ [^505-3]    
ādeyyavacano homi        na dhaṃsemi yathā ahaṃ .

Abhantaṃ hoti me cittaṃ       akhilo [^505-4] homi kassaci    
tena kammena sukatena       vimalo homi sāsane .

Sagāravo sappatisso        katakicco anāsavo    
sāvako te mahāvīra        bhikkhu taṃ vandate muni .

Katvā sukatapallaṅkaṃ         sālāyaṃ paññapesahaṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       pañca hetū labhāmahaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 506 -->
<a id="page-506"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 506]*

Ucce kule pajāyāmi [^506-1]    mahābhogo bhavāmahaṃ    
sabbasampattiko homi        maccheraṃ me na vijjati .

Gamane patthite mayhaṃ        pallaṅko upatiṭṭhati    
saha pallaṅkaseṭṭhena        gacchāmi mama patthitaṃ .

Tena pallaṅkadānena        tamaṃ sabbaṃ vinodayiṃ    
sabbābhiññābalappatto       thero vandati taṃ mune [^506-2] .

Parakiccattakiccāni          sabbakiccāni sādhayiṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       pāvisiṃ abhayaṃ puraṃ .

Pariniṭṭhitasālasmiṃ           paribhogamadāsahaṃ    
tena kammena sukatena       seṭṭhattaṃ ajjhupāgato .

Yekeci damakā loke       hatthiasse damenti te [^506-3]    
kāretvā [^506-4] kāraṇā nānā dāruṇena damenti te .

Naheva [^506-5] tvaṃ mahāvīra      damesi naranāriyo    
adaṇḍena asatthena         damesi uttame dame .

Dānassa vaṇṇe kittento    desanākusalo muni    
ekapañhaṃ kathento ca [^506-6]    bodhesi tisate muni .

Dantā mayaṃ sārathinā        suvimuttā anāsavā    
sabbābhiññābalappattā       nibbutā upadhikkhaye .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ dānamadadintadā    
atikkantā bhayā sabbe      sāladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

#[^506-1] ma. Pajāyitvā . [^506-2] ma. Yu. Muni . [^506-3] ma. Ye . Yu. Me .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 507 -->
<a id="page-507"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 507]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .8    
paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā selo sapariso bhagavato santike imā    
Gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

Selattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Tatiyaṃ sabbakittikattherāpadānaṃ (393)    
[395] kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ            dīparukkhaṃva jotitaṃ [^507-1]    
osadhiṃva virocantaṃ          vijjūva gagane yathā .

Asambhītaṃ anutrāsiṃ          migarājaṃva kesariṃ    
ñāṇālokaṃ pakāsentaṃ       maddantaṃ titthiye gaṇe .

Uddharantaṃ imaṃ lokaṃ         chindantaṃ sabbasaṃsayaṃ    
asambhītaṃ [^507-2] migarājaṃva       addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Jaṭājinadharo āsiṃ          brahmā [^507-3] uju patāpavā    
vākacīraṃ gahetvāna         pādamūle apatthariṃ .

Kāḷānusāriyaṃ gayha         anulimpiṃ tathāgataṃ    
sambuddhaṃ upalimpitvā        santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

#[^507-1] ma. Ujjalaṃ . Ma. Vijjutaṃ .. . Yu. Vijjuṃ abbhaghane . [^507-2] ma. Gajjantaṃ .. .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 508 -->
<a id="page-508"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 508]*

Samuddharasimaṃ lokaṃ           oghatiṇṇo mahāmuni    
ñāṇālokena jotesi       pavaraṃ [^508-1] ñāṇamuttamaṃ .

Dhammacakkaṃ pavattesi         maddase paratitthiye    
usabho jitasaṅgāmo [^508-2]      sampakampesi medaniṃ .

Mahāsamudde ummīva [^508-3]      velantamhi pabhijjati [^508-4]    
tatheva tava ñāṇasmiṃ         sabbadiṭṭhi pabhijjati [^508-5] .Ya    
sukhumacchikajālāni [^508-6]        saramhi sampatāni te    
antojāligatā pāṇā       pīḷitā honti tāvade .

Tatheva titthiyā loke       muḷhā [^508-7] saccavinissitā    
antoñāṇavare tuyhaṃ        parivattanti mārisa .

Patiṭṭhā va vuyhataṃ oghe     tvañhi nātho abandhunaṃ    
bhayaṭṭhitānaṃ saraṇo [^508-8]       muttatthīnaṃ parāyano [^508-9] .

Ekaccaro 10 asadiso     mettākaruṇasaññuto    
paññavā 1[^508-1] yuttacāgo ca   vasī tādī guṇālayo .

Dhīro vigatasammoho         aneñjo akathaṃkathī    
tussito 1[^508-2] vantadososi    nimmalo saṃyato suci .

Saṅgātīto 1[^508-3] gatamado     tevijjosi 1[^508-4] bhavantago    
sīmātigo dhammagaru          tatattho hitavappako 1[^508-5] .

#[^508-1] yu. Vajira .. . [^508-2] yu. Jitasaṅgāme . [^508-3] ma. Ūmiyo . 4-[^508-5] ma. Yu. Pabhijjare .

#[^508-6] ma. .. Jālena . Yu. Jāleva . [^508-7] ma. Puthupasaṇḍanissitā . Yu. Phuṭā sacca .. .

#[^508-8] ma. Saraṇaṃ . [^508-9] ma. Parāyanaṃ . 10 ma. Yu. Ekavīro . 1[^508-1] ma. .. Sañcayo . Yu.

#mettākāruṇādisañcayo . 1[^508-2] ma. Asamo susamo santo vasī tādī jitañjayo . Yu.

#susīlo asamo santo vasitavijitañjayo . 1[^508-3] yu. Vusito . 1[^508-4] yu. Saṅgātigo hatamado .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 509 -->
<a id="page-509"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 509]*

Tārako tvaṃ yathā nāvā     nidhivassāsakārako    
asambhīto yathā sīho        gajarājāva dammito .

Thometvā dasagāthāhi       padumuttaraṃ mahāmuniṃ [^509-1]    
vanditvā satthuno pāde     tuṇhī aṭṭhāsahaṃ tadā .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusaṅghe ṭhito satthā      imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me sīlañca paññañca [^509-2]   dhammañcāpi [^509-3] pakittayi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Saṭṭhī kappasahassāni         devaloke ramissati    
aññe deve abhibhavitvā     issaraṃ kārayissati .

So pacchā pabbajitvāna      sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsane pabbajissati .

Pabbajitvāna kāyena        pāpakammaṃ vivajjiya    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Yathā ca [^509-4] megho thanayaṃ     tappeti medaniṃ imaṃ    
tatheva tvaṃ mahāvīra         dhammena tappayi mamaṃ .

Sīlaṃ paññañca dhammañca        thavitvā lokanāyakaṃ    
pattomhi paramaṃ santaṃ        nibbānaṃ padamaccutaṃ .

Aho nūna sa bhagavā         ciraṃ tiṭṭheyya cakkhumā    
aññātañca vijāneyyaṃ       passeyyaṃ amataṃ padaṃ .

#[^509-1] ma. Yu. Mahāyasaṃ . [^509-2] ma. Yu. Ñāṇañca . [^509-3] ma. Saddhammañcāpi vaṇṇayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 510 -->
<a id="page-510"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 510]*

Ayaṃ me pacchimā jāti       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ buddhamabhithomayiṃ [^510-1]    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbakittiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sabbakittikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Catutthaṃ madhudāyakattherāpadānaṃ (394)    
[396] sindhuyā nadiyā tīre        sukato assamo mama    
tattha vācemahaṃ sisse       itihāsaṃ salakkhaṇaṃ .

Dhammakāmā vinītā te       sotukāmā susāsanaṃ    
chaḷaṅge pāramippattā       sindhukūle vasanti te .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 511 -->
<a id="page-511"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 511]*

Uppādāgamane ceva        lakkhaṇesu ca kovidā    
uttamatthaṃ gavesantā        vasanti pavane tadā .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho      loke uppajji tāvade    
amhākaṃ anukampanto        upagañchi vināyako .

Upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ           sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
tiṇasanthārakaṃ katvā         lokajeṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Pavanāto madhadha gayha         buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ    
sambuddho paribhuñjitvā       idaṃ vacanamabravi .

Yo [^511-1] taṃ adāsi madhadha me    pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Iminā madhudānena          tiṇasanthārakena ca    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Devalokā idhāgantvā      mātukucchimhupāgato    
madhuvasso pavassittha         chādayaṃ madhunā mahiṃ .

Mama nikkhantamattamhi         kumbhiyā ca suduttarā    
tatrāpi madhuvasso [^511-2] me    vassate niccakālikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 512 -->
<a id="page-512"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 512]*

Agārā abhinikkhamma         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
lābhī annassa pānassa       madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Sabbakāmasamiddhohaṃ          bhavitvā devamānuse    
teneva madhudānena         pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Vuṭṭhamhi deve caturaṅgule tiṇe    
supupphite dharaṇīruhe vappadese [^512-1]    
suññe ghare maṇḍaparukkhamūlake    
vasāmi niccaṃ sukhito anāsavo .

Majjhe [^512-2] mayhaṃ bhavā assu   ye bhave samatikkamiṃ    
ajja me āsavā khīṇā      natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā madhudāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Madhudāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 513 -->
<a id="page-513"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 513]*

Pañcamaṃ padumakūṭāgārikattherāpadānaṃ (395)    
[397] piyadassī nāma bhagavā        sayambhū lokanāyako    
vivekakāmo sambuddho       samādhikusalo muni .

Vanasaṇḍaṃ samoggayha         piyadassī mahāmuni    
paṃsukūlaṃ pattharitvā          nisīdi purisuttamo .

Migaluddho pure āsiṃ        irine [^513-1] kānane ahaṃ    
pasadaṃ migamesanto          āhiṇḍāmi ahaṃ tadā .

Tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ        oghatiṇṇaṃ anāsavaṃ    
pupphitaṃ sālarājaṃva          sataraṃsīva uggataṃ .

Disvānāhaṃ devadevaṃ        piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
jātassaraṃ samoggayha        padumaṃ āhariṃ tadā .

Āharitvāna padumaṃ          satapattaṃ manoramaṃ    
kūṭāgāraṃ karitvāna         chādayiṃ padumenahaṃ .

Anukampako kāruṇiko        piyadassī mahāmuni    
satta rattindive buddho      kūṭāgāre vasī jino .

Purāṇaṃ chaḍḍayitvāna         navena chādayiṃ ahaṃ    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        aṭṭhāsiṃ tāvade ahaṃ .

Vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā       piyadassī mahāmuni    
disā [^513-2] anuvilokento     nisīdi lokanāyako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 514 -->
<a id="page-514"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 514]*

Tadā sudassano nāma        upaṭṭhāko mahiddhiko    
cittamaññāya buddhassa        piyadassissa satthuno .

Asītiyā sahassehi          bhikkhūhi parivārito    
vanante sukhamāsīnaṃ          upesi lokanāyakaṃ .

Yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi         adhivatthā ca devatā    
buddhassa cittamaññāya        sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Samāgatesu yakkhesu         kumbhaṇḍe saharakkhase    
bhikkhusaṅghe ca sampatte      gāthā sabyāharī [^514-1] jino .

Yo maṃ sattāhaṃ pūjesi       āvāsañca akāsi me    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Sududdasaṃ sunipuṇaṃ            gambhīraṃ suppakāsitaṃ    
ñāṇena kittayissāmi        suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Catuddasāni kappāni         devarajjaṃ karissati    
kūṭāgāraṃ brahantassa [^514-2]     padumapupphehi [^514-3] chāditaṃ .

Ākāse dhārayissanti       pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ    
catuddase [^514-4] kappasate      vokiṇṇaṃ saṃsarissati .

Tattha pupphamayaṃ byamhaṃ        ākāse dhārayissati    
yathā padumapattamhi          toyaṃ na upalimpati .

Tathevimassa ñāṇamhi         kilesā nopalimpare    
manasā vinivaṭṭetvā        pañcanīvaraṇe ayaṃ .

#[^514-1] ma. Pabyāharī . Yu. Mabyāharī . [^514-2] ma. Mahantassa . [^514-3] ma. Padmapupphehi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 515 -->
<a id="page-515"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 515]*

Cittaṃ janetvā nikkhamme     agārā pabbajissati    
tato pupphamayaṃ [^515-1] byamhaṃ     dhārentaṃ nikkhamissati .

Rukkhamūle vasantassa         nipakassa satīmato    
tattha pupphamayaṃ byamhaṃ        matthake dhārayissati .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca          paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
daditvā bhikkhusaṅghassa        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Kūṭāgārena carite [^515-2]      pabbajjaṃ abhinikkhami [^515-3]    
rukkhamūle vasantaṃpi          kūṭāgāraṃ dharīyati .

Cīvare piṇḍapāte ca        cetanā me na vijjati    
puññakammena saṃyutto        labhāmi pariniṭṭhitaṃ .

Gaṇanāto asaṅjeyyā       kappakoṭī bahū mama    
rittakā te atikkantā      sumuttā [^515-4] lokanāyakā .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        piyadassī vināyako    
tamahaṃ payirupāsitvā         imaṃ yoniṃ [^515-5] upāgato .

Tamaddasāmi [^515-6] sambuddhaṃ      anomaṃ nāma cakkhumaṃ    
tamahaṃ upagantvāna          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Dukkhassantaṃ karo buddho      saddhamme [^515-7] desayī jino    
tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna        pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Tosayitvāna sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^515-1] ma. Tato pupphamaye byamhe dhārente nikkhamissati . [^515-2] ma. Caratā . Yu. Caraṇā .

#[^515-3] ma. Yu. Abhinikkhamiṃ . [^515-4] ma. Pamuttā . Yu. Pavuttā lokanāyinā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 516 -->
<a id="page-516"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 516]*

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthidāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā [^516-1]  kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumakūṭāgāriko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Padumakūṭāgārikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ bakkulattherāpadānaṃ (396)    
[398] himavantassavidūre           sobhito nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ        sakasissehi māpito .

Maṇḍapā ca bahū tattha        pupphitā sinduvāritā [^516-2]    
kapiṭṭhā [^516-3] ca bahū tattha     campakā [^516-4] nāgaketakā .

Niguṇḍiyo bahū tattha         badarāmalakāpi [^516-5] ca    
phārusakā alābū ca         puṇḍarīkā ca pupphitā .

#[^516-1] po. Sacchikatā . [^516-2] ma. Yu. Sinduvārakā . [^516-3] yu. Kapiṭṭhaṃ ca bahuṃ tattha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 517 -->
<a id="page-517"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 517]*

Aḷakkā beluvā tattha       kadalī mātuluṅgakā    
mahānāmā bahū tattha        ajjunā ca piyaṅgukā .

Kosumbhā [^517-1] salaḷā nīpā    nigrodhā ca kapiṭṭhanā    
ediso assamo mayhaṃ       sasissohaṃ tahiṃ [^517-2] vasiṃ .

Anomadassī bhagavā          sayambhū lokanāyako    
gavesaṃ paṭisallānaṃ          mamassamaṃ upāgami .

Upetañca [^517-3] mahāvīraṃ       anomadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ    
khaṇena lokanāthassa         vātābādho samuṭṭhahi .

Vicaranto araññamhi         addasaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        cakkhumantaṃ mahāyasaṃ .

Iriyāpathañca [^517-4] disvāna     upalakkhesahaṃ tadā    
nissaṃsayaṃ [^517-5] hi buddhassa      byādhino upapajjatha .

Khippaṃ assamamāgañchiṃ         mama sissāna santike    
bhesajjaṃ kattukāmohaṃ        sissaṃ [^517-6] āmantayiṃ tadā .

Paṭisuṇitvāna me vākyaṃ      sissā sabbe sagāravā    
ekajjhaṃ sannipatiṃsu          satthu gāravatā mama .

Khippaṃ pabbatamāruyha         sabbosathamakāsahaṃ [^517-7]    
pānīyayogaṃ katvāna         buddhaseṭṭhassadāsahaṃ .

Paribhutte [^517-8] mahāvīre      sabbaññulokanāyake    
khippaṃ vāto vūpasami         sugatassa mahesino .

#[^517-1] ma. Kosambā salaḷā nimbā . [^517-2] tadā . [^517-3] ma. Upetamhi mahāvīre anomadassimahāyase .

#[^517-4] ma. Yu. Iriyañcāpi disvāna . [^517-5] ma. Yu. Asaṃsayaṃ . [^517-6] ma. Yu. Sisse .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 518 -->
<a id="page-518"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 518]*

Passaddhaṃ darathaṃ disvā        anomadassī mahāyaso    
sakāsane nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me adāsi [^518-1] bhesajjaṃ   byādhiñca samayī [^518-2] mama    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Kappasatasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
vādite tūriye tattha        modissati sadā ayaṃ .

Manussalokamāgantvā        sukkamūlena codito    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājāpi        cakkavatti bhavissati .

Pañcapaññāsakappamhi         anomi [^518-3] nāma khattiyo    
cāturanto vijitāvī         jambūdīpassa [^518-4] issaro .

Sattaratanasampanno          cakkavatti mahabbalo    
tāvatiṃsehi [^518-5] khobhetvā    issaraṃ kārayissati .

Devabhūto manusso vā       appābādho bhavissati    
paridāhaṃ [^518-6] vivajjetvā     byādhiṃ loke tarissati .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena [^518-7]    satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Kilese jhāpayitvāna        taṇhāsotaṃ tarissati    
bakkulo [^518-8] nāma nāmena    hessati satthusāvako .

#[^518-1] ma. Yu. Pādāsi . [^518-2] yu. Samayī mayī . [^518-3] ma. Anomo ... . Yu. Anoma ... .

#[^518-4] ma. Jambumaṇḍassa . Yu. Jambusaṇḍassa . [^518-5] ma. Tāvatiṃsepi . Yu. Tāvatiṃseto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 519 -->
<a id="page-519"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 519]*

Idaṃ sabbaṃ abhiññāya         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapessati .

Anomadassī bhagavā          sayambhū lokanāyako    
vivekamanulokento         mamassamaṃ upāgami .

Upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ           sabbaññaña lokanāyakaṃ    
sabbosathena tappesiṃ        pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Tassa me sukataṃ kammaṃ        sukhette bījasampadā    
khepetuṃ neva sakkomi       tadā hi sukataṃ mama .

Lābhā mama suladdhaṃ me       yohaṃ addakkhi nāyakaṃ    
tena kammāvasesena        pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Sabbametaṃ abhiññāya         gotamo sakyapuṅgavo    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        etadagge ṭhapesi maṃ .

Aparimeyye ito kappe     yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         bhesajjassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo [^519-1]       natthidāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 520 -->
<a id="page-520"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 520]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bakkulo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Bakkulattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Sattamaṃ girimānandattherāpadānaṃ (397)    
[399] bhariyā me kālaṃ katā       putto sīvathikaṃ gato    
mātā [^520-1] pitā ca bhātā ca  ekacitamhi [^520-2] ḍayhare .

Tena sokena santatto      kiso paṇḍu ahosahaṃ    
cittukkhepañca [^520-3] me āsi   tena sokena aḍḍito .

Sokasallaparetopi [^520-4]       vanantaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ    
pavattaphalaṃ bhuñjitvā         rukkhamūle vasāmahaṃ .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho      dukkhassantaṃ karo jino    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi mama santike [^520-5] .

Padasaddaṃ suṇitvāna          sumedhassa mahesino    
paggahetvānahaṃ sīsaṃ         olokesiṃ mahāmuniṃ .

Upagañchi [^520-6] mahāvīro       pīti me upapajjatha    
tadāsi mekaggamano         disvā taṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Satiṃ paṭilabhitvāna           paṇṇamuṭṭhiṃ adāsahaṃ    
nisīdi bhagavā tattha          anukampāya cakkhumā .

#[^520-1] ma. Mātā pitā matā bhātā . [^520-2] yu. Ekacitakamhi ... . [^520-3] ma. Cittakkhepo ca ... .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 521 -->
<a id="page-521"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 521]*

Nisajja tattha bhagavā         sumedho lokanāyako    
dhammaṃ me kathayi buddho       sokasallavinodanaṃ .

Anavhatā [^521-1] tato āgā [^521-2] nānuññātā ito gatā    
yathā gatā tathā gatā       tattha kā paridevanā .

Yathāpi pattikā [^521-3] sattā    vassamānāya vuṭṭhiyā    
sabhatte [^521-4] upagacchanti      vassāya [^521-5] patanāya te .

Vasseva [^521-6] te oramite    sampayanti yadicchakaṃ    
evaṃ mātā pitā tuyhaṃ      tattha kā paridevanā .

Āgantukā pāhunakā        caliteditakampikā [^521-7]    
evaṃ mātā pitā tuyhaṃ      tattha kā paridevanā .

Yathāpi urago jiṇṇaṃ         hitvā gacchati santanana [^521-8]    
evaṃ mātā pitā tuyhaṃ      santanana idha hiyyare .

Buddhassa giramaññāya         sokasallaṃ vivajjayiṃ    
pāmujjaṃ janayitvāna         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ avandahaṃ .

Vanditvāna mahānāgaṃ        girimañjari [^521-9] pūjayī    
dibbagandhena sampannaṃ        sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Pūjayitvāna sambuddhaṃ         sire katvāna añjaliṃ    
anussaraṃ guṇaggāni          santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Vitiṇṇosi 10 mahāvīra      sabbaññū lokanāyako    
sabbe satte uddharesi      ñāṇena tvaṃ mahāmune .

#[^521-1] ma. Anavhitā . [^521-2] ma. Yu. Āguṃ . [^521-3] ma. Yu. Pathikā . [^521-4] ma. Yu. Sabhaṇḍā .

#[^521-5] ma. Yu. Vassassāpatanāya ... . [^521-6] yu. Vasse ete . [^521-7] ma. Yu. Caliteritakampitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 522 -->
<a id="page-522"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 522]*

Vimatiṃ dveḷhakaṃ vāpi        sañchindasi mahāmune    
paṭipādesi me maggaṃ        tava ñāṇena cakkhuma .

Arahā siddhipattā [^522-1] ca     chaḷabhiññā mahiddhikā    
antalikkhacarā dhīrā         parivārenti tāvade .

Paṭipannā ca sekhā ca       phalaṭṭhā santi sāvakā .

Sūrodayeva padumā          pupphanti tava sāvakā .

Mahāsamuddo vakkhobbho      atulopi duruttaro    
evaṃ ñāṇena sampanno      appameyyosi cakkhuma .

Vanditvāhaṃ lokajinaṃ         cakkhumantaṃ mahāyasaṃ    
catuddisā [^522-2] namassanto     paṭikuṭiko agañchahaṃ .

Devalokā cavitvāna        sampajāno paṭissato    
okkamiṃ mātuyā kucchiṃ       sandhāvanto bhavābhave .

Agārā abhinikkhamma         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
ātāpī nipako cāpi        paṭisallānagocaro .

Padhānaṃ padahitvāna          tosayitvā mahāmuniṃ    
candovabbhaghanā mutto       vicarāmi ahaṃ sadā [^522-3] .

Vivekamanuyuttomhi          upasanto nirūpadhi    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 523 -->
<a id="page-523"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 523]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā        natthidāni punabbhavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā girimānando thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Girimānandattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ salaḷamaṇḍapiyattherāpadānaṃ (398)    
[400] nibbute kakusandhamhi         brāhmaṇamhi vusīmate    
gahetvā salaḷaṃ mālaṃ        maṇḍapaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ .

Tāvatiṃsaṃ gato santo        labhāmi byamhamuttamaṃ
