aññe deve abhibhomi [^523-1]    puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Divā vā yadivā rattiṃ       caṅkamanto ṭhito cahaṃ    
channo salaḷapupphehi         puññakammassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Imasmiṃyeva kappamhi         yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 524 -->
<a id="page-524"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 524]*

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salaḷamaṇḍapiyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Salaḷamaṇḍapiyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Navamaṃ sabbadāyakattherāpadānaṃ (399)    
[401] mahāsamuddamoggayha         bhavanaṃ me sunimmitaṃ    
sunimmitā pokkharaṇī         cakkavākupakujjitā [^524-1] .

Mandālakehi sañchannā       padumuppalakehi ca    
nadī ca sandate tattha        supatitthā manoramā .

Macchakacchapasañchannā         nānāmigasamotthaṭā [^524-2]    
mayūrakoñcābhirudā          kokilādīhi vagguhi .

Pārevatā ravihaṃsā         cakkavākā nadīcarā    
dindibhā sālikā cettha      cappakā [^524-3] jīvajīvakā .

#[^524-1] ma. Yu. Cakkavākapakujjitā . [^524-2] ma. Nānādijasamotthaṭā . [^524-3] ma. Pammakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 525 -->
<a id="page-525"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 525]*

Haṃsā koñcābhinadikā [^525-1]     kosiyā [^525-2] siṅgalā bahū    
sattaratanasampannā          maṇimuttikavālukā .

Sabbasoṇṇamayā [^525-3] rukkhā    nānāgandhasameritā    
ujjotenti divā rattiṃ      bhavanaṃ sabbakālikaṃ .

Saṭṭhī turiyasahassāni         sāyaṃ pāto pavajjare    
soḷasitthīsahassāni          parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Abhinikkhamma bhavanā          sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ(    
pasannacitto sumano         vandiya [^525-4] taṃ mahāyasaṃ .

Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       sasissantaṃ nimantayiṃ    
adhivāsesi so dhīro        sumedho lokanāyako .

Mama dhammakathaṃ katvā         uyyojesi mahāmuni!    
Sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       bhavanaṃ me upāgamiṃ .

Āmantayiṃ parijanaṃ           sabbe sannipatātha vo    
pubbaṇhasamayaṃ buddho         bhavanaṃ āgamissati .

Lābhā amhaṃ suladdhā no     ye vasāma tavantike    
mayaṃpi buddhaseṭṭhassa         pūjaṃ kassāma satthuno .

Annapānaṃ paṭṭhapetvā       kālaṃ ārocayiṃ ahaṃ    
vasīsatasahassehi            upesi lokanāyako .

Saṅgītehi [^525-5] turiyehi       paccuggamanakāsahaṃ    
sabbasoṇṇamaye pīṭhe        nisīdi purisuttamo .

#[^525-1] ma. ... Koñcāpinaditā . Yu. Koñcābhināditā . [^525-2] ma. Kosiyā piṅgalā bahū .

#yu. Kosikā ... . [^525-3] po. Yu. Sabbe sovaṇṇayā rukkhā . [^525-4] ma. Vandayiṃ . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 526 -->
<a id="page-526"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 526]*

Uparicchadanaṃ āsi [^526-1]        sabbasoṇṇamayaṃ tadā    
vījaniyo pavāyanti          bhikkhusaṅghassa antare .

Pahūtenannapānena          bhikkhusaṅghaṃ atappayiṃ    
paccekadussayugale          bhikkhusaṅghassadāsahaṃ .

Yaṃ vadanti sumedhoti         lokāhutipaṭiggaho [^526-2]    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me annañca [^526-3] pānañca  sabbametena tappayi [^526-4]    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Upapajjati yaṃ yoniṃ          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbadā sabbasovaṇṇaṃ        chadanaṃ dhārayissati .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        sīhanādaṃ nadissati    
citake chattaṃ dhārenti       heṭṭhāchattamhi ḍayhatha .

Sāmaññaṃ me anuppattaṃ       kilesā jhāpitā mayā    
maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā       santāpo me na vijjati .

#[^526-1] yu. Kāsiṃ . [^526-2] ma. Yu. Lokāhutapaṭiggahaṃ . [^526-3] ma. Annena pānena .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 527 -->
<a id="page-527"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 527]*

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           yaṃ dānamadadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbadāyako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Sabbadāyakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ ajitattherāpadānaṃ (400)    
[402] padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
ajjhogahetvā himavantaṃ      nisīdi lokanāyako .

Nāhaṃ addakkhi [^527-1] sambuddhaṃ    napi saddaṃ suṇomahaṃ    
mama bhikkhaṃ [^527-2] gavesanto     āhiṇḍāmi vane ahaṃ .

Tatthaddassāmi sambuddhaṃ       dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ    
disvāna cittamāpajjiṃ [^527-3]     satto ko nāmayaṃ bhave .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 528 -->
<a id="page-528"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 528]*

Lakkhaṇāni viloketvā       mama vijjaṃ anussariṃ    
sutaṃ hi metaṃ buddhānaṃ        paṇḍitānaṃ subhāsitaṃ .

Tesaṃ yathā [^528-1] taṃ vacanaṃ      ayaṃ buddho bhavissati    
yannūna [^528-2] taṃ sakkareyyaṃ     gatiṃ me sodhayissati .

Khippaṃ assamamāgantvā       madhutelaṃ [^528-3] gahiṃ ahaṃ    
kolambakaṃ gahetvāna        upagañchiṃ vināyakaṃ .

Tidaṇḍake gahetvāna        abbhokāse ṭhapesahaṃ    
padīpaṃ pajjalitvāna          aṭṭhakkhattuṃ avandahaṃ .

Sattarattindivaṃ buddho        nisīdi purisuttamo    
tato ratyā vivasane        vuṭṭhāsi lokanāyako .

Pasannacitto sumano         sabbarattindivaṃ ahaṃ    
dīpaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ        pasanno sehi pāṇibhi .

Sabbe gandhā [^528-4] vanamayā    pabbate gandhamādane    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        āgañchacha buddhasantike .

Yekeci pupphagandhā se      pupphitā dharaṇīruhā    
buddhassa ānubhāvena        sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Yāvatā himavantamhi         nāgā ca garuḷā ubho    
dhammañca sotukāmā te      āgañchacha buddhasantike .

Devalo nāma samaṇo        buddhassa aggasāvako    
vasīsatasahassehi            buddhasantikupāgami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 529 -->
<a id="page-529"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 529]*

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me dīpaṃ padīpesi        pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Saṭṭhī kappasahassāni         devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Soḷasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Chattiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati    
paṭhaviyaṃ sattasataṃ            vipulaṃ rajjaṃ karissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
iminā dīpadānena          dibbacakkhu bhavissati .

Samantato aḍḍhakosaṃ         passissati ayaṃ sadā    
devalokā cavantassa        nibbattantassa jantuno .

Divā vā yadivā rattiṃ       padīpaṃ dhārayissati    
jāyamānassa santassa        puññakammasamaṅgino .

Yāvatā nagaraṃ āsi         tāvatā jotayissati    
upapajjati yaṃ yoniṃ          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ .

Asseva dīpadānassa         aṭṭhadīpaphalena ca [^529-1]    
upaṭṭhissantimaṃ [^529-2] jantuṃ      dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 530 -->
<a id="page-530"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 530]*

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Tosayitvāna sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
ajito nāma nāmena        hessati satthusāvako .

Saṭṭhikappasahassāni          devaloke ramiṃ ahaṃ    
tatrāpi me dīpasataṃ         jotate [^530-1] niccakālikaṃ .

Devaloke manusse vā      niddhāvanti pabhā mama    
buddhaseṭṭhaṃ saritvāna        bhiyyo hāsaṃ janesahaṃ .

Tusitāhaṃ cavitvāna          okkamiṃ mātukucchiyā    
jāyamānassa santassa        āloko vipulo ahu .

Agārā abhinikkhamma         pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
bāvariṃ upasaṅkamma          sissattaṃ ajjhupāgamiṃ .

Himavante vasantohaṃ         assosiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
uttamatthaṃ gavesanto        upagañchiṃ vināyakaṃ .

Danto buddho dametāpi [^530-2]   oghatiṇṇo nirūpadhi    
nibbānaṃ kathayi buddho        sabbadukkhā pamocanaṃ .

Tamme āgamanaṃ siddhaṃ        tositohaṃ mahāmuniṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 531 -->
<a id="page-531"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 531]*

satasahasse ito kappe      yaṃ dīpaṃ adadintadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ajito thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Ajitattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
pilindavaccho selo ca       sabbakittamadhūdado    
kūṭāgārī bakulo ca         girisalaḷasavhayo .

Sabbado ajito ceva        gāthāyo gaṇitā visā    
satāni pañca gāthānaṃ        vīsati ca taduttari .

Pilindavaggo cattāḷīso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 532 -->
<a id="page-532"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 532]*

atha vagguddānaṃ    
padumārakkhado ceva         ummā gandhodakena ca    
ekappadasaddasaññī          mandāraṃ bodhivandako .

Ambaṭañca pilindī ca         gāthāyo gaṇitāpica    
catusattati gāthāyo         ekādasasatāni ca .

Padumavaggadasakaṃ .

Catutthaṃ satakaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 533 -->
<a id="page-533"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 533]*

Ekacattāḷīsamo metteyyavaggo    
paṭhamaṃ tissametteyyattherāpadānaṃ (401)    
[403] pabbhārakūṭaṃ nissāya         sobhito nāma tāpaso    
pavattaphalaṃ bhuñjitvā         vasati pabbatantare .

Aggidāruṃ āharitvā        ujjalesiṃ ahaṃ tadā    
uttamatthaṃ gavesanto        brahmalokūpapattiyā .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamuddharitukāmo so         āgañchi mama santike .

Kiṃ karosi mahāpuñña         dehi me aggidārukaṃ    
ahaṃ aggiṃ paricare          tato me suddhi hohiti [^533-1] .

Subhaddako tvaṃ manuja         devate tvaṃ pajānasi    
tuvaṃ aggiṃ paricara           handa te aggidārukaṃ .

Tato kaṭṭhaṃ gahetvāna       aggiṃ ujjālayī jino    
na tattha kaṭṭhaṃ pajjhāyi       pāṭiheraṃ mahesino .

Na te aggi pajjalati        āhuti te na vijjati    
niratthakaṃ ca [^533-2] taṃ tuyhaṃ      aggiṃ paricarassu me .

Kīdiso te [^533-3] mahāvīra      aggi tava pavuccati    
mayhaṃpi kathayassetaṃ          ubho paricarāmase .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 534 -->
<a id="page-534"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 534]*

Hetudhammanirodhāya          kilesajhāpanāya ca    
issāmacchariyaṃ hitvā        tayo ete mamāhutī .

Kīdiso tvaṃ mahāvīra         kathaṃgottosi mārisa    
ācārapaṭipatti te         bāḷhaṃ kho mama ruccati .

Khattiyamhi kule jāto       abhiññāpāramiṃ gato    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo          natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Yadi buddhosi sabbaññū        pabhaṅkara tamonuda    
namassissāmi taṃ deva        dukkhassantaṃ karo tuvaṃ .

Pattharitvā jinacammaṃ         nisīdanamadāsahaṃ    
nisīda tattha [^534-1] sabbaññū      upaṭṭhissāmahaṃ tuvaṃ .

Nisīdi bhagavā tattha          ajinamhi suvitthate    
nimantayitvā sambuddhaṃ        pabbataṃ agamāsahaṃ .

Khāribhārañca pūretvā       tindukaphalamāhariṃ    
madhunā yojayitvāna         phalaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ .

Mama nijjhāyamānassa         paribhuñji tadā jino    
tattha cittaṃ pasādesi [^534-2]     pekkhanto lokanāyakaṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
mamassame nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me [^534-3] phalena tappesi   pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 535 -->
<a id="page-535"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 535]*

Pañcavīsatikkhattuṃ so         devarajjaṃ karissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Tassa saṅkappamaññāya        pubbakammasamaṅgino    
annapānañca vatthañca        sayanañca mahārahaṃ .

Puññakammena saṃyuttā        nibbattissanti tāvade    
sadā sammudito [^535-1] cāyaṃ     bhavissati anāmayo .

Upapajjati yaṃ yoniṃ          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
sabbattha sukhito hutvā       manussattaṃ bhavissati .

Ajjhāyako mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
sambuddhaṃ upagantvāna        arahā so bhavissati .

Yato sarāmi attānaṃ        yato pattosmi viññutaṃ    
bhoge me ūnatā natthi      phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Varadhammamanuppatto          rāgadose samūhaniṃ    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo          natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 536 -->
<a id="page-536"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 536]*

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tissametteyyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Tissametteyyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dutiyaṃ puṇṇakattherāpadānaṃ (402)    
[404] pabbhārakūṭaṃ nissāya         sayambhū aparājito    
ābādhiko ca [^536-1] so buddho  vasati pabbatantare .

Mama assamasāmantā         panādo āsi tāvade    
buddhe nibbāyamānamhi       āloko [^536-2] āsi tāvade .

Yāvatā vanasaṇḍasmiṃ         acchakokataracchayo    
bālā [^536-3] ca kesarī sabbe   abhikujjiṃsu [^536-4] tāvade .

Uppādaṃ [^536-5] tamahaṃ disvā     pabbhāraṃ agamāsahaṃ    
tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ        nibbutaṃ aparājitaṃ .

Suphullaṃ sālarājaṃva          sataraṃsīva uggataṃ    
vītaccitaṃva aṅgāraṃ          nibbutaṃ aparājitaṃ .

Tiṇaṃ kaṭṭhañca pūretvā       citakaṃ tatthakāsahaṃ    
citakaṃ sukataṃ katvā          sarīraṃ jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ .

Sarīraṃ jhāpayitvāna          gandhatoyaṃ samokiriṃ    
antalikkhe ṭhito yakkho      nāmaṃ aggahi tāvade .

#[^536-1] yu. ... Va yo ... . [^536-2] ma. Āloko udapajjatha . [^536-3] ma. Yu. Vāḷā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 537 -->
<a id="page-537"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 537]*

Taṃ [^537-1] pūritaṃ tayā kiccaṃ      sayambhussa mahesino    
puṇṇako nāma nāmena       yadā [^537-2] hosi tuvaṃ mune .

Tamhā kāyā cavitvāna      devalokaṃ agañchahaṃ    
tattha dibbamayo gandho       antalikkhā [^537-3] pavassati .

Tatrāpi nāmadheyyaṃ me      puṇṇakoti ahu tadā    
devabhūto manusso vā       saṅkappaṃ pūrayāmahaṃ .

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ          carimo vattatī [^537-4] bhavo    
idhāpi puṇṇako nāma        nāmadheyyaṃ pakāsati [^537-5] .

Tosayitvāna sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Ekanavute ito kappe      yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā    
duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi         tanukiccassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Puṇṇakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

#[^537-1] ma. Yaṃ. . [^537-2] ma. Yu. Sadā hohi . [^537-3] yu. Antalikkhe . [^537-4] ma. Yu. Vattate .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 538 -->
<a id="page-538"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 538]*

Tatiyaṃ mettaguttherāpadānaṃ (403)    
[405] himavantassa avidūre         asoko nāma pabbato    
tatthāpi assamo mayhaṃ       visukammena [^538-1] māpito .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho      aggo kāruṇiko muni    
nivāsayitvā pubbaṇhe       piṇḍāya me upāgami .

Upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ           sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
paggayha sugatapattaṃ [^538-2]       sappitelaṃ apūrayiṃ .

Datvānāhaṃ buddhaseṭṭhe      sumedhe lokanāyake    
añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        bhiyyo hāsaṃ janesahaṃ .

Iminā sappidānena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
devabhūto manusso vā       labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

Vinipātaṃ vivajjetvā        saṃsarāmi bhavābhave    
tattha cittaṃ paṇidhitvā        labhāmi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Lābhā tuyhaṃ suladdhante      yaṃ maṃ addakkhi brāhmaṇa [^538-3]    
mama dassanamāgamma          arahattaṃ gamissati [^538-4] .

Vissaṭṭho hohi mā bhāyi     adhigantvā mahāyasaṃ    
mamaṃ hi sappiṃ datvāna        parimokkhāsi jātiyā .

Iminā sappidānena         cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca    
devabhūto manusso vā       labhāmi vipulaṃ sukhaṃ .

#[^538-1] ma. Visakammena . [^538-2] yu. Subhakaṃ pattaṃ . [^538-3] yu. Brāhmaṇo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 539 -->
<a id="page-539"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 539]*

Iminā adhikārena [^539-1]       mettacittavatāya ca    
aṭṭhārase kappasate        devaloke ramissati [^539-2] .

Aṭṭhārasa ca tikkhattuṃ        devarājā bhavissati 3-2    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Ekapaññāsakkhattuñca        cakkavatti bhavissati    
cāturanto vijitāvī         jambūmaṇḍassa issaro .

Mahāsamuddo vakkhobbho      buddharo paṭhavī yathā    
evameva ca me bhogā      appameyyā bhavissare้    
saṭṭhikoṭiṃ hiraññassa         datvāna [^539-4] pabbajiṃ ahaṃ .

Kiṃ kusalaṃ gavesanto         bāvariṃ upasaṅkamiṃ .

Tattha mante adhiyāmi        chaḷaṅgaṃ nāma lakkhaṇaṃ    
tamandhakāraṃ vidhamaṃ           uppajji tvaṃ mahāmuni .

Tava dassanakāmohaṃ          āgatomhi mahāmuni    
tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         pattomhi acalaṃ padaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassamhi           sappiṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ    
etthantare nābhijāne      sappi viññāpitaṃ mayā .

Mama saṅkappamaññāya         uppajjati yathicchakaṃ    
cittamaññāya nibbattaṃ        sabbe santappayāmahaṃ .

Aho buddhā aho dhammā     aho no satthusampadā    
thokañhi sappiṃ datvāna       appameyyaṃ labhāmahaṃ .

#[^539-1] ma. Sappidānena . [^539-2] ma. Yu. Ramissasi . [^539-3] ma. Yu. Bhavissasi . Ito paraṃ īdisameva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 540 -->
<a id="page-540"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 540]*

Mahāsamudde udakaṃ          yāvatā nerupassato    
mama sappiṃ upādāya         kalabhāgaṃ na hissati .

Yāvatā cakkavāḷassa        kārayantassa rāsito    
mama nibbattavatthānaṃ         okāso so na sammati .

Pabbatarājā himavā         pavaropi siluccayo    
mamānulittagandhassa          upanidhaṃ [^540-1] na hissati .

Vatthaṃ gandhañca sappiñca       aññaṃ vā diṭṭhadhammikaṃ    
asaṅkhatañca nibbānaṃ         sappidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Satipaṭṭhānasayano           samādhijhānagocaro    
bojjhaṅgajanano [^540-2] ajja     sappidānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mettagū thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Mettaguttherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 541 -->
<a id="page-541"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 541]*

Catutthaṃ dhotakattherāpadānaṃ (404)    
[406] gaṅgā bhāgīrasī [^541-1] nāma     himavantā pabhāvitā    
haṃsavatiyā dvārena         anusandati tāvade .

Sobhito nāma ārāmo      gaṅgākūle sumāpito    
tattha padumuttaro buddho      vasate lokanāyako .

Tidasehi yathā indo        manujehi purakkhato    
nisīdi tattha bhagavā          asambhītova kesarī .

Nagare haṃsavatiyā           ahosiṃ [^541-2] brāhmaṇo ahaṃ    
chaḷaṅgo nāma nāmena       evaṃ nāmo mahāmuni .

Aṭṭhārasa sissasatā         parivārenti maṃ tadā    
tehi sissehi samito        gaṅgātīraṃ upāgamiṃ .

Tatthaddasāsiṃ samaṇe         nikkuhe dhotapāpake    
bhāgīrasintarantohaṃ [^541-3]       evaṃ cintesi tāvade .

Sāyaṃ pātaṃ tarantāme       buddhaputtā mahāyasā    
vihesayanti attānaṃ         tesaṃ attā vihaññati .

Sadevakassa lokassa         buddho aggo pavuccati    
natthi me dakkhiṇe kāraṃ      gatimaggavisodhanaṃ .

Yannūna buddhaseṭṭhassa        setuṃ gaṅgāya kāraye    
kārāpetvā imaṃ setuṃ [^541-4]   santarāmi imaṃ bhavaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 542 -->
<a id="page-542"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 542]*

Sataṃ sahassaṃ datvāna         setuṃ kārāpayiṃ ahaṃ    
saddahanto kataṃ kāraṃ        vipulaṃ me bhavissati .

Kārāpetvāna taṃ [^542-1] setuṃ   upesiṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
sirasi añjaliṃ katvā         imaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Sataṃ sahassaṃ savayaṃ [^542-2]        datvā [^542-3] kārāpito mayā    
tavatthāya mahāsetuṃ         paṭiggaṇha mahāmune .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me setuṃ akāresi      pasanno sehi pāṇibhi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Darito pabbatato vā        rukkhato patitopiyaṃ    
cutopi lacchatiṭṭhānaṃ         setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Virūḷhamūlasantānaṃ           nigrodhamiva māluto    
amittā nappasahanti [^542-4]      setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Nāssa corā pasahanti [^542-5]    nātimaññanti khattiyā    
sabbe tarissatāmitte [^542-6]    setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Abbhokāsagataṃ santaṃ         kaṭhinātapatāpitaṃ    
puññakammena saṃyuttaṃ         na bhavissati vedanā .

Devaloke manusse vā      hatthiyānaṃ sunimmitaṃ    
tassa saṅkappamaññāya        nibbattissati tāvade .

#[^542-1] yu. Haṃ . [^542-2] ma. Yu. Satasahassassa vayaṃ . [^542-3] yu. Katvā . [^542-4] yu. Na sahissanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 543 -->
<a id="page-543"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 543]*

Sahassassā vātajavā        sindhavā sīghabāhanā    
sayaṃ pātaṃ upessanti        setudānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Āgantvāna manussattaṃ       sukhitoyaṃ bhavissati    
ihāpi [^543-1] manujasseva       hatthiyānaṃ bhavissati .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        jalajuttamanāmake    
tattha kāraṃ karitvāna        pattohaṃ āsavakkhayaṃ .

Padhānaṃ pahitattomhi         upasanto nirūpadhi    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhotako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Dhotakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ  .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 544 -->
<a id="page-544"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 544]*

Pañcamaṃ upasīvattherāpadānaṃ (405)    
[407] himavantassa avidūre         anomo nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ        paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Nadī ca sandati tattha         supatitthā manoramā    
anūpatitthe jāyanti         padumuppalakā bahū .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      balajā [^544-1] muñjarohitā    
macchakacchapasañchannā         nadikā sandate tadā .

Timirā pupphitā tattha        asokā khuddamālakā    
puṇṇā vā giripuṇṇā vā     sampavanti mamassamaṃ .

Kuṭajā pupphitā tattha        tiṇasūlavanāni ca    
sālā ca salaḷā tattha       campakā pupphitā bahū .

Ajjunā adhimuttā [^544-2] ca     mahānāmā ca pupphitā    
asanā madhugandhī ca          pupphitā te mamassame .

Uddālakā pāṭalikā        yūthikā ca piyaṅgukā    
bimbijālakasañchannā         samantā aḍḍhayojanaṃ .

Mātakarā [^544-3] sattaliyo      pāṭalī sindhuvāritā    
aṅkolakā bahū tattha        tālakuṭā [^544-4] ca pupphitā .

Seleyyakā bahū tattha       pupphitā mama assame    
etesu pupphajātesu        sobhanti pādapā bahū .

#[^544-1] yu. Jalājā . [^544-2] ma. Yu. Atimuttā . [^544-3] ma. Mātaggārā  . Yu. Mātaṅgā vā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 545 -->
<a id="page-545"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 545]*

Samantā tena gandhena       vāyate mama assame    
harītakā āmalakā          ambajambū vibhedakā [^545-1] .

Kolā bhallātakā bellā    phārusakaphalāni ca    
tindukā ca piyālā ca       madhukā kāsamāriyo [^545-2] .

Labujā panasā tattha         kadalī candarīphalā 3-tha    
ambāṭakā bahū tattha        vallikāraphalāni ca .

Cirasaṃrasapākā ca [^545-4]        phalitā mama assame    
āḷakā isimuggā ca        tato soraphalā [^545-5] bahū .

Avaṭā pakkabharitā          milakkhudumbarāni ca    
pipphalī maricā tattha         nigrodhā ca kapiṭṭhanā .

Udumbarakā bahavo          kaṇḍapakkā ca pāriyo [^545-6]    
ete caññe ca bahavo      phalitā assame mama .

Puppharukkhāpi bahavo         pupphitā mama assame    
āluvā ca kadambā ca       biḷālitakkalāni ca .

Ālakā tālakā ceva       vijjanti mama assame    
assamassāvidūre me        mahājātassaro ahu .

Acchodako sītajalo         supatittho manoramo    
padumuppalā bahū tattha        puṇḍarikasamāyutā .

Mandālakehi sañchannā       nānāgandhasamīritā    
gabbhaṃ gaṇhanti padumā        aññe pupphanti kesarī .

#[^545-1] ma. Yu. Vibhītakā . [^545-2] ma. Kāsumārayo . [^545-3] ma. Badarīphalā . [^545-4] ma. Bījapūrasapāriyo .

#yu. Viṭapā ca sapākā ca . [^545-5] ma. Modaphalā . Yu. Coraphalā . [^545-6] ma. Kaṇḍupaṇṇā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 546 -->
<a id="page-546"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 546]*

Opupphapattā tiṭṭhanti       padumā kaṇṇikā bahū    
madhu bhisamhā savati          khīrasappi muḷālibhi .

Samantā tena gandhena       nānāgandhasamohitā    
kumudā ambagandhī ca         nayitā dissare bahū .

Jātassarassānukūlaṃ          ketakā pupphitā bahū    
suphullā bandhujīvā ca        setavārī sugandhikā .

Kumbhīlā sasasumārā [^546-1] ca     gahakā tattha jāyare    
uggāhakā ajagarā         tattha jātassare bahū .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      balajā muñjarohitā    
macchakacchapasañchannā         atho sapaṭakehi [^546-2] ca .

Pārevatā ravihaṃsā         kukkutthakā [^546-3] nadīcarā    
dindibhā [^546-4] cakkavākā ca    cappakā jīvajīvakā .

Kalandakā ukkusā ca        senakā uddharā bahū    
kotthakā sukapotā ca       kuḷīrā [^546-5] camarā bahū .

Kāseniyā [^546-6] ca tilakā     upajīvanti taṃ saraṃ    
sīhā byagghā ca dīpī ca      acchakokataracchayo .

Vānarā kinnarā ceva       dissanti mama assame    
tāni gandhāni ghāyanto      bhakkhayanto phalānahaṃ .

Gandhodakaṃ pivanto ca        vasāmi mama assame    
eṇimigā varāhā ca        pasadā khuddarūpakā .

#[^546-1] ma. Susumārā . [^546-2] ma. Yu. Papaṭakāhi . [^546-3] ma. Kukkutthā ca . [^546-4] yu. Tiṭībhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 547 -->
<a id="page-547"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 547]*

Aggikā jotikā ceva       vasanti mama assame    
haṃsā koñcā mayūrā ca      sahitāpica [^547-1] kokilā .

Mañjarikā [^547-2] bahū tattha      kosikā potthasīsakā [^547-3]    
pisācā dānavā ceva       kumbhaṇḍā rakkhasā bahū .

Garuḷā pannagā ceva        vasanti mama assame    
mahānubhāvā isayo         santacittā samāhitā .

Kamaṇḍaludharā sabbe         ajinuttaravāsino    
jaṭābhārabharitā ca [^547-4]       vasanti mama assame .

Yugamattañca pekkhantā       nipakā santavuttino    
lābhālābhena santuṭṭhā      vasanti mama assame .

Vākacīraṃ dhunantā te        poṭhentā [^547-5] jinacammakaṃ    
sabalehi upatthaddhā         gacchanti ambare tadā .

Navodakaṃ āharanti          kaṭṭhaṃ vā aggidārukaṃ    
sayañca upasampannā         sappāṭihirassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Lohadoṇiṃ gahetvāna        vanamajjhe vasanti te    
kuñjarāva mahānāgā        achambhitāva [^547-6] kesarī .

Aññe gacchanti goyānaṃ      aññe pubbavidehanaṃ [^547-7]    
aññe ca uttarakuruṃ         sakambalamapassitā [^547-8] .

Tato piṇḍaṃ āharitvā       paribhuñjanti ekato    
sabbesaṃ pakkamantānaṃ        uggatejāna tādinaṃ .

#[^547-1] ma. Sālikāpica . [^547-2] ma. Yu. Majjārikā . [^547-3] yu. Potthasīsikā . [^547-4] ma. Va .

#yu. Te jaṭābhārabharitā . [^547-5] ma. Phoṭentā . [^547-6] ma. Yu. Asambhītāva . [^547-7] ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 548 -->
<a id="page-548"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 548]*

Ajinacammasaddena           vanaṃ saddāyate tadā .

Edisā te mahāvīra        sissā uggatapā mama    
parivuto ahaṃ tehi          vasāmi mama assame .

Tositā sakakammena         vinītāpi samāgatā    
ārādhayiṃsu maṃ ete        sakakammābhilāsino [^548-1] .

Sīlavanto ca nipakā         appamaññāsu kovidā    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

Samayaṃ saṃviditvāna           upagañchi vināyako    
upagantvāna sambuddho       ātāpī nipako muni .

Pattaṃ paggayha sambuddho      bhikkhāya samupāgami [^548-2]    
upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ           jalajuttamanāmakaṃ [^548-3] .

Tiṇattharaṃ [^548-4] paññāpetvā    sālapupphehi okiriṃ    
nisīditvāna [^548-5] sambuddhaṃ      haṭṭho saṃviggamānaso .

Khippaṃ pabbatamāruyha         agaḷaḷa aggahiṃ ahaṃ    
kumbhamattaṃ gahetvāna        panasaṃ devagandhikaṃ .

Khandhe āropayitvāna       upagañchiṃ vināyakaṃ    
phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna        agaḷaḷa anulimpahaṃ .

Pasannacitto sumano         buddhaseṭṭhaṃ avandihaṃ    
padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho .

#[^548-1] yu. Sakakammābhilābhino . [^548-2] ma. Yu. Mamupāgami . [^548-3] ma. Yu. Jalajuttamanāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 549 -->
<a id="page-549"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 549]*

Isimajjhe nisīditvā         imā gāthā abhāsatha    
yo ca me phalamagaḷaḷa [^549-1]      āsanañca adāsi me .

Tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato    
gāme vā yadivāraññe      pabbhāresu guhāsu vā .

Imassa cittamaññāya         nibbattissati bhojanaṃ    
devaloke manusse vā      upapanno ayaṃ naro .

Bhojanehi ca vatthehi        parisantappayissati    
upapajjati yaṃ yoniṃ          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ .

Akkhobbhabhogo hutvāna      saṃsarissatiyaṃ naro    
tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati .

Sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati    
ekasattatikkhattuñca         devarajjaṃ karissati .

Padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ    
kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo .

Gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati    
tassa dhammesu dāyādo      oraso dhammanimmito .

Upasīvo nāma nāmena       hessati satthusāvako    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharissatināsavo .

Suladdhalābho laddho me      yohaṃ addakkhi nāyakaṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 550 -->
<a id="page-550"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 550]*

Gāme vā yadivāraññe      pabbhāresu guhāsu vā    
mama saṅkappamaññāya         bhojanaṃ hoti me sadā .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upasīvo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Upasīvattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Chaṭṭhaṃ nandakattherāpadānaṃ (406)    
[408] migaluddho pure āsiṃ        araññe kānane ahaṃ    
pasadaṃ migamesanto          sayambhuṃ addasaṃ ahaṃ .

Anuruddho nāma sambuddho     sayambhū aparājito    
vivekakāmo so dhīro       vanaṃ ajjhogahi tadā .

Catudaṇḍe gahetvāna        catuṭṭhāne ṭhapesahaṃ    
maṇḍapaṃ sukataṃ katvā         padumapupphehi chādayi [^550-1] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 551 -->
<a id="page-551"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 551]*

Maṇḍapaṃ chādayitvāna         sayambhuṃ abhivādayiṃ    
dhanana tattheva nikkhippa        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Na ciraṃ pabbajitassa          byādhi me upapajjatha    
pubbakammaṃ saritvāna         tattha kālaṃ kato ahaṃ .

Pubbakammena saṃyutto        tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ    
tattha soṇṇamayaṃ byamhaṃ       nibbattati yathicchakaṃ .

Sahassayuttaṃ hayavāhiṃ         dibbayānamadhiṭṭhito    
ārohitvāna taṃ yānaṃ       gacchāmahaṃ yathicchakaṃ .

Tato me nīyamānassa        devabhūtassa me sato    
samantā yojanasataṃ          maṇḍapo me dharīyati .

Sayane so [^551-1] tuvaṭṭāmi     acchanne pupphasanthate    
antalikkhā ca padumā        vassante niccakālikaṃ .

Marīcike phandamāne         tappamāne ca ātape    
na maṃ tapati [^551-2] ātappo     maṇḍapassa idaṃ phalaṃ .

Duggatiṃ samatikkanto         apāyā pihitā mama    
maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā       santāpo me na vijjati .

Mahisaññaṃ adhiṭṭhāya          loṇatoyaṃ tarāmahaṃ    
tassa me sukataṃ kammaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Abbhamhi [^551-3] pathaṃ katvāna     gacchāmi anilañjase    
aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 552 -->
<a id="page-552"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 552]*

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi        dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
āsavā me parikkhīṇā       buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Jahitā purimā jāti         buddhassa oraso ahaṃ    
dāyādomhi ca saddhamme     buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Ārādhitomhi sugataṃ         gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
dhammadharo [^552-1] dhammadāyādo   buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ .

Upaṭṭhitvāna sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
pāraṃ gamaniyaṃ maggaṃ          apucchiṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Ajjhiṭṭho kathayi buddho       gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ padaṃ    
tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       pattomhi āsavakkhayaṃ .

Aho me sukataṃ kammaṃ        parimuttomhi jātiyā    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo          natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nandako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Nandakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 553 -->
<a id="page-553"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 553]*

Sattamaṃ hemakattherāpadānaṃ (407)    
[409] pabbhārakūṭaṃ nissāya         anomo nāma tāpaso    
assamaṃ sukataṃ katvā         paṇṇasāle vasiṃ tadā .

Siddhaṃ tassa tapokammaṃ        siddhipatto sake bale    
sakaṃsāmaññavikkanto         ātāpī nipako muni .

Visārado sasamaye          paravāde ca kovido    
paṭṭho bhūmantalikkhasmiṃ        uppādamhi ca kovido .

Vītasoko nirārambho        appāhāro alolupo    
lābhālābhena santuṭṭho      jhāyī jhānarato muni .

Piyadassī nāma sambuddho      aggo kāruṇiko muni    
satte tāretukāmo so     karuṇāya phari tadā .

Bodhaneyyaṃ janaṃ disvā       piyadassī mahāmuni    
cakkavāḷasahassamhi          gantvā ovādako [^553-1] muni .

Mamuddharitukāmo so         mamassamamupāgami    
na diṭṭho me jino pubbe    na sutopi ca kassaci .

Uppādā [^553-2] supinā mayhaṃ    lakkhaṇā suppakāsitā    
paṭṭho bhūmantalikkhasmiṃ        nakkhattapadakovido .

Sohaṃ buddhassa sutvāna       tattha cittaṃ pasādayiṃ    
tiṭṭhanto [^553-3] vā nisinno vā sarāmi niccakālikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 554 -->
<a id="page-554"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 554]*

Mama evaṃ sarantassa [^554-1]      bhagavāpi anussari    
buddhaṃ anussarantassa         pīti me hoti tāvade .

Kālañca punarāgamma         maṃ upesi mahāmuni    
sampattepi [^554-2] na jānāmi    ayaṃ buddho mahāmuni .

Anukampako kāruṇiko        piyadassī mahāmuni    
sañjānāpesi attānaṃ       ahaṃ buddho sadevake .

Sañjānitvāna sambuddhaṃ       piyadassiṃ mahāmuniṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Sabbe [^554-3] pīṭhe ca pallaṅke  āsandīsu nisīdare    
tuvaṃpi sabbadayāvī [^554-4]        nisīda rucirāsane [^554-5] .

Sabbaratanamayaṃ pīṭhaṃ           nimminitvāna tāvade    
piyadassissa munino          āsanaṃ iddhinimmitaṃ .

Rataneva [^554-6] nisinnassa       pīṭhake iddhinimmite    
kumbhamattaṃ jambuphalaṃ          adāsiṃ tāvade ahaṃ .

Mama hāsaṃ janetvāna        paribhuñji mahāmuni    
tadā cittaṃ pasādetvā      satthāraṃ abhivādayiṃ .

Piyadassī tu bhagavā          lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
ratanāsanamāsinno          imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me ratanamayaṃ pīṭhaṃ        amatañca phalaṃ adā    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

#[^554-1] ma. Yu. Sarantamhi . [^554-2] yu. Sampattopi . [^554-3] ma. Yu. Aññe . [^554-4] ma. Tuvampi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 555 -->
<a id="page-555"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 555]*

Sattasattati kappāni         devaloke ramissati    
pañcasattatikkhattuñca         cakkavatti bhavissati .

Dvattiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo      devarajjaṃ karissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Sovaṇṇamayaṃ rucimayaṃ [^555-1]       pallaṅkaṃ sukataṃ bahuṃ    
lohitaṅkamayañceva          lacchati ratanāmayaṃ .

Caṅkamantaṃpi manuññaṃ [^555-2]       puññakammasamaṅginaṃ    
pallaṅkāni anekāni        parivārissare sadā .

Kūṭāgārā ca pāsādā      sayanañca mahārahaṃ    
imassa cittamaññāya         nibbattissanti tāvade .

Saṭṭhī nāgasahassāni         sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
suvaṇṇakacchā mātaṅgā       hemakappanivāsasā [^555-3]    
āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi        tomaraṃkusapāṇibhi    
tepimaṃ paricarissanti         ratanapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī assasahassāni         sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
ājāniyā ca jātiyā       sindhavā sīghabāhanā .

Āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi        cāpahatthehi cammibhi [^555-4]    
tepimaṃ paricarissanti         ratanapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī rathasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
dīpā athopi veyyagghā      sannaddhā ussitaddhajā .

#[^555-1] ma. Yu. Rūpimayaṃ . [^555-2] ma. Yu. Manujaṃ . [^555-3] ma. Yu. Hemakappanavāsasā . [^555-4] ma. Illiyā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 556 -->
<a id="page-556"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 556]*

Āruḷhā gāmaṇīyebhi        cāpahatthehi cammibhi [^556-1]    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ       ratanapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Saṭṭhī dhenusahassāni         gāvī supuṅgavūsabhā [^556-2]    
vacchake janayissanti         ratanapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Soḷasitthīsahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .

Āḷāramukhā [^556-3] hasulā      susaññā tanumajjhimā    
parivāressantimaṃ niccaṃ       ratanapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Aṭṭhārase kappasate        gotamo nāma cakkhumā    
tamandhakāraṃ vidhaṃsetvā [^556-4]    buddho loke bhavissati .

Tassa dassanamāgamma         pabbajissatikiñcano    
tosayitvāna satthāraṃ        sāsanebhiramissati .

Tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna        kilese jhāpayissati [^556-5]    
sabbāsave pariññāya        nibbāyissatināsavo .

Viriyaṃ me dhuradhorayhaṃ        yogakkhemādhivāhanaṃ    
uttamatthaṃ paṭṭhayāno        sāsane viharāmahaṃ .

Idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ          carimo vattate bhavo    
sabbāsavaparikkhīṇo [^556-6]       natthi dāni punabbhavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

#[^556-1] ma. Yu. Vammibhi . [^556-2] ma. Dohaññā puṅgavūsabhe . Yu. Rohaññā puṅgavūsabhā .

#[^556-3] ma. Yu. Āḷārapamhā . [^556-4] ma. Yu. Vidhametvā . [^556-5] ma. Ghātayissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 557 -->
<a id="page-557"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 557]*

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā hemako thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Hemakattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Aṭṭhārasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ .

Aṭṭhamaṃ todeyyattherāpadānaṃ  (408)    
[410] rājā vijitajayo [^557-1] nāma    ketumatipuruttame    
sūro vikkamasampanno        puramajjhāvasī tadā .

Tassa rañño pamattassa       raṭṭhavisā [^557-2] samuṭṭhahuṃ    
uttarā [^557-3] tuṇḍikā ceva    raṭṭhaṃ viddhaṃsayuṃ tadā .

Paccante kuppite khippaṃ      sannipātesirindamo    
bhaṭe ceva balatthe ca       ariṃ niggāhayi tadā .

Hatthārohā anīkatthā       sūrā ca vammayodhino [^557-4]    
dhanuggahā ca rathikā [^557-5]      sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Āḷārakā kappakā ca       nhāpakā mālakārakā    
sūrā vijitasaṅgāmā         sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

#[^557-1] ma. Ajitañjayo . Yu. Jitañjayo . [^557-2] ma. Yu. Aṭaviyo . [^557-3] ma. Otārā . Yu. Ocarā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 558 -->
<a id="page-558"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 558]*

Khaggahatthā ca purisā        cāpahatthā ca cammino [^558-1]    
luddhā vijitasaṅgāmā        sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Tidhappabhinnā mātaṅgā       kuñjarā saṭṭhihāyanā    
suvaṇṇakacchālaṅkārā        sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Khamā sītassa uṇhassa        ukkāruharaṇassa ca    
yodhājīvā katakammā        sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Saṅkhasaddaṃ bherisaddaṃ         atho uddaṭasaddakaṃ [^558-2]    
etehi te hāsayantā      sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Tisūlakontimantehi          dhanūhi tomarehi ca [^558-3]    
koṭṭayantā [^558-4] nivattentā  sabbe sannipatuṃ tadā .

Kavacāni nivāsetvā [^558-5]     sarājā ajitañjino    
saṭṭhipāṇasahassāni          sūle uttāsayiṃ tadā .

Saddaṃ mānusakākaṃsu          aho rājā adhammiko    
niraye paccamānassa         kadā anto bhavissati .

Sayanehaṃ tuvaṭṭento        passāmi [^558-6] niraye tadā    
na supāmi divārattiṃ         sūlena tajjayanti maṃ .

Kiṃ pamādena rajjena        bāhanena balena ca    
na te pahonti dhāretuṃ      tāsayanti [^558-7] mamaṃ sadā .

Kiṃ me puttehi dārehi      rajjena sakalena ca    
yannūna pabbajeyyāhaṃ        gatimaggaṃ visodhaye .

#[^558-1] ma. Yu. Vammino . [^558-2] ma. Utujasaddakaṃ . Yu. Uddhavasaddakaṃ . [^558-3] ma. Yu.

#kavacatomarehi ca . [^558-4] ma. Yu. Koṭṭantānaṃ nipātentā . Yu .. Nivattantā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 559 -->
<a id="page-559"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 559]*

Saṭṭhināgasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsite    
suvaṇṇakacche mātaṅge       hemakappanivāsase .

Āruḷhe gāmaṇīyebhi        tomaraṅkusapāṇibhi    
saṅgāmāvacare ṭhāne [^559-1]    anapekkho vihāyahaṃ .

Sakakammena santatto        nikkhamiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
saṭṭhiassasahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsite .

Ājāniye ca [^559-2] jātiyā    sindhavā [^559-3] sīghabāhane    
āruḷhe gāmaṇīyebhi        cāpahatthehi cammibhi .

Chaḍḍayitvāna sabbete [^559-4]    nikkhamiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
saṭṭhirathasahassāni           sabbālaṅkārabhūsite .

Dīpe athopi veyyagghe      sannaddhe ussitaddhaje    
sabbete parivajjetvā [^559-5]   pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Saṭṭhidhenusahassāni          sabbā kaṃsupadhāraṇā    
gāviyo [^559-6] chaḍḍayitvāna     pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Saṭṭhiitthīsahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā .Bha    
āḷāramukhā hasulā         susaññā tanumajjhimā    
tā hitvā kandamānāyo     pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

Saṭṭhigāmasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
chaḍḍayitvāna taṃ rajjaṃ        pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ .

#[^559-1] yu. Saṅgāmāvacareheva . [^559-2] ma. Yu. Va . [^559-3] ma. Yu. Sindhave sīghavāhane .

#[^559-4] ma. Pahāretvāna te sabbe . Yu. Pahāyitvāna ... . [^559-5] ma. Parihāretvā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 560 -->
<a id="page-560"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 560]*

Nagarā nikkhamitvāna         himavantaṃ upāgamiṃ    
bhāgīrasīnadītīre            assamaṃ māpayiṃ ahaṃ .

Paṇṇasālaṃ karitvāna         agyāgāraṃ akāsahaṃ .

Āraddhaviriyo pahitatto      vasāmi assame ahaṃ    
maṇḍape rukkhamūle vā       suññāgāre va jhāyato    
na tu [^560-1] vijjati tāso me   na passe bhayabheravaṃ .

Sumedho nāma sambuddho      aggo kāruṇiko muni    
ñāṇālokena jotento     loke uppajji tāvade .

Mama assamasāmantā         yakkho āsi mahiddhiko    
buddhaseṭṭhamhi uppanne      ārocesi mamaṃ tadā .

Buddho loke samuppanno     sumedho nāma cakkhumā    
tāresi janataṃ sabbaṃ         tampi so tārayissati .

Yakkhassa vacanaṃ sutvā        saṃviggo āsi tāvade    
buddho buddhoti cintento    assamaṃ paṭisāmayiṃ .

Aggidāruñca chaḍḍetvā      saṃsāmetvāna santhataṃ    
assamaṃ abhivanditvā         nikkhamiṃ pavanā [^560-2] ahaṃ .

Tato candanamādāya         gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ    
devadevaṃ gavesanto        upagañchiṃ vināyakaṃ .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye         sumedho lokanāyako    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento        bodheti janataṃ bahuṃ [^560-3] .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 561 -->
<a id="page-561"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 561]*

Añjaliṃ paggahetvāna        sīse katvāna vandanaṃ [^561-1]    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādetvā       imā gāthā abhāsatha [^561-2] .

Vassike pupphamānasmiṃ        santike upavāyati    
tvaṃ vīra guṇagandhena         disā sabbā pavāyasi .

Campake nāgamalike [^561-3]      adhimuttakaketake    
sālesu pupphamānesu        anuvātaṃ pavāyati .

Tava gandhaṃ ghāyitvāna [^561-4]     himavantā idhāgamiṃ    
pūjemi taṃ mahāvīra          lokajeṭṭha mahāyasa .

Varacandanenānulimpiṃ         sumedhaṃ lokanāyakaṃ    
sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādetvā       tuṇhī aṭṭhāsi tāvade .

Sumedho nāma bhagavā        lokajeṭṭho narāsabho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me guṇaṃ [^561-5] pakittesi    candanañca apūjayi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Ādeyyavākyavacano        brahmā uju patāpavā    
pañcavīsatikappāni           sappabhāso bhavissati .

Chabbīsatikappasate           devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Tettiṃsakkhattuṃ devindo      devarajjaṃ karissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

#[^561-1] ma. Yu. Candanaṃ . [^561-2] ma. Abhāsahaṃ . [^561-3] ma. Yu. Nāgavanike .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 562 -->
<a id="page-562"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 562]*

Tato cutoyaṃ manujo         manussattaṃ gamissati    
puññakammena saṃyutto        brahmabandhu bhavissati .

Ajjhāyako mantadharo        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
tīṇilakkhaṇasampanno          bāvarī nāma brāhmaṇo .

Tassa sisso bhavitvāna       hessati mantapāragū    
upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ        gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ .

Pucchitvā nipuṇe pañhe      hāsayitvāna [^562-1] mānasaṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi [^562-2] anāsavo .

Tividhaggi nibbutā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā todeyyo thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Todeyyattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 563 -->
<a id="page-563"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 563]*

Navamaṃ jatukaṇṇikattherāpadānaṃ (409)    
[411] nagare haṃsavatiyā           seṭṭhiputto ahosahaṃ    
samappito kāmaguṇehi [^563-1]     parivāremahaṃ tadā .

Tato pāsādamāruyha        mahābhoge valañjako    
tattha naccehi gītehi        parivāremahaṃ tadā .

Turiyā āhatā mayhaṃ        sammatālasamāhitā    
rañjantī [^563-2] itthiyo sabbā   harantiyeva me mano .

Celāvakā vāmanikā [^563-3]     kuñjavā sīhimajjhitā [^563-4]    
laṅghikā sokajjhāyī ca       parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Vetāḷino kumbhathūni         naṭā ca naccakā bahū    
naṭakā nāṭakā ceva        parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Kappakā nhāpakā sūdā      mālākārā supāsakā [^563-5]    
jallā mallā ca te sabbe   parivārenti maṃ sadā .

Etesu kīḷamānesu         sikkhite katupāsane    
rattindivaṃ na jānāmi        indova tidase pure [^563-6] .

Addhikā kapaṇā [^563-7] sabbe    yācakā carakā bahū    
upagacchanti te niccaṃ        bhikkhayantā mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Samaṇā brāhmaṇā ceva      puññakkhettā anuttarā    
vaḍḍhayantā mamaṃ puññaṃ        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Paṭakā [^563-8] laṭukā sabbe     niganthā pupphasāṭakā    
#[^563-1] ma. Yu. Kāmaguṇe . [^563-2] ma. Yu. Naccantā . [^563-3] ma. Celāpikā lāmaṇikā . Yu.

#velāmikā vāmanikā . [^563-4] ma. Kuñjavāsī timajjhikā . [^563-5] yu. Sumāsakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 564 -->
<a id="page-564"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 564]*

Tedaṇḍikā ekasikhā        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Ājīvikā viluttāvī         gotamā [^564-1] devadhammikā    
rajojalladharā ete        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Parivattakā siddhipattā [^564-2]    kodhapugganikā [^564-3] bahū    
tapasī vanacārī ca           āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Andhakā [^564-4] damilā ceva     sākuḷā malayāḷakā [^564-5]    
savarā yonakā ceva        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Gandhakā [^564-6] muṇḍakā sabbe   kuṭṭhalā sānuvindakā [^564-7]    
ārāva cīnaraṭṭhā ca        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Alasandakā pallavakā        pabbatānaggamāruhā [^564-8]    
bāhikā [^564-9] cetaputtā ca    āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Madhurakā kosalakā          kāsikā 10 hatthiporikā    
isindā makkalā 1[^564-1] ceva   āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Celāvakā ārambā ca 1[^564-2]  obhāsā meghalā bahū    
khuddakā suddakā ceva       āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Rohakā 1[^564-3] sindhavā ceva   cittakā 1[^564-4] ekakaṇṇikā    
suraṭṭhā aparantā ca        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Suppārakā kumārā 1[^564-5] ca   malayā 1[^564-6] soṇṇabhūmikā    
vajjihārā 1[^564-7] ca te sabbe āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

#[^564-1] ma. Godhammā . [^564-2] ma. Parittakā santipattā . [^564-3] yu. Koṇḍapuggalikā bahū .

#[^564-4] ma. Yu. Oḍḍakā . [^564-5] ma. Malavāḷakā . [^564-6] ma. Yu. Andhakā . [^564-7] ma. Koṭalā    
#hanuvindakā . Yu. Kolakā ... . [^564-8] ma. Dhammarāniggamānusā . Yu. Pabbarābhaggakārusā .

#[^564-9] ma. Gehitā . 10 ma. Kaliṅgā . 1[^564-1] yu. Matthalā . 1[^564-2] ma. Ārabbhā ca oghuṭaṭhā . Yu.

#velāvakā arammā okkalā mekalā . 1[^564-3] ma. Yu. Rohanā . 1[^564-4] yu. Cittāva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 565 -->
<a id="page-565"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 565]*

Naḷakārā pesakārā        cammakārā ca tacchakā    
kammakārā [^565-1] kumbhakārā    āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Maṇikārā lohakārā        soṇṇakārā ca dussikā    
tipukārā ca te sabbe      āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Usukārā [^565-2] bhamakārā      pesakārā ca gandhikā    
rajakā tunnavāyā ca        āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Telikā kaṭṭhahārā ca       udahārā ca pesikā    
supikā saradakkhā ca [^565-3]      āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Dovārikā anīkatthā        ganthikā [^565-4] pupphachaḍḍakā    
hatthārohā hatthipālā      āgacchanti mamaṃ gharaṃ .

Ānandassa nāma rañño      samaggassa [^565-5] adāsahaṃ    
sattavaṇṇena ratanena        ūnattaṃ pūrayāmahaṃ .

Ye mayā kittitā sabbe     nānāvaṇṇā bahū janā    
tesāhaṃ cittamaññāya        tappayiṃ ratanenapi [^565-6] .

Vaggūsu bhāsamānesu         vajjamānāsu bherisu    
saṅkhesu dhamayantesu         sakagehe ramāmahaṃ .

Bhagavā tamhi samaye         padumuttaranāmako [^565-7]    
saha [^565-8] satasahassehi        parikkhīṇāsavehi so .

Bhikkhūhi sahito vīthiṃ          paṭipajjittha cakkhumā    
obhāsento disā sabbā    dīparukkhova jotati .

#[^565-1] ma. Yu. Kammārā . [^565-2] yu. Cāpakārā . [^565-3] ma. Sūpikā sūparakkhā ca . Yu. ...

#rūdasakkā ca . [^565-4] ma. Bandhikā . Yu. Santhikā . [^565-5] ma. Mamattassa ... . Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 566 -->
<a id="page-566"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 566]*

Vajjanti bheriyo sabbā      gacchante lokanāyake    
pabhā niddhāvate tassa       sataraṃsīva uggato .

Kavāṭantarikāyāpi          paviṭṭhena ca rasminā    
antogharesu vipulo         āloko āsi tāvade .

Pabhaṃ disvāna buddhassa        pārisajje avocahaṃ    
nissaṃsayaṃ buddhaseṭṭho        imaṃ vīthimupāgato .

Khippaṃ oruyha pāsādā      agamiṃ antarāpathaṃ [^566-1]    
sambuddhaṃ abhivādento [^566-2]    idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ .

Anukampatu me buddho        jalajuttamanāmako [^566-3]    
vasīsatasahassehi            adhivāsesi so muni .

Nimantayitvāna sambuddhaṃ       abhinesiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ    
tattha annena pānena       santappesiṃ mahāmuniṃ .

Bhuttāvīkālamaññāya         buddhaseṭṭhassa tādino    
saṅgītena [^566-4] turiyena       buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ .

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
antoghare nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo maṃ turiyenupaṭṭhāsi       annapānañcadāsi me    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Pahūtabhakkho hutvāna         sahirañño sabhojano    
catuddīpe ekarajjaṃ         kārayissatiyaṃ naro .

#[^566-1] ma. Yu. Antarāpaṇaṃ . [^566-2] ma. Abhivādetvā . [^566-3] ma. Yu. ... Nāyako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 567 -->
<a id="page-567"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 567]*

Pañcasīle samādāya         dasakammapathe rato [^567-1]    
samādāya pavattanto        parisaṃ sikkhapessati .

Turiyasatasahassāni           nāriyo [^567-2] samalaṅkatā    
vajjayissantimaṃ niccaṃ         upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Tiṃsakappasahassāni           devaloke ramissati    
catusaṭṭhikhattuṃ devindo       devarajjaṃ karissati .

Catusattatikkhattuṃ [^567-3] ca       cakkavatti bhavissati    
padesarajjaṃ vipulaṃ           gaṇanāto asaṅkhayaṃ .

Kappasatasahassamhi           okkākakulasambhavo    
gotamo nāma nāmena       satthā loke bhavissati .

Upapajjati yaṃ yoniṃ          devattaṃ atha mānusaṃ    
anūnabhogo hutvāna         manussattaṃ gamissati .

Ajjhāyako bhavitvāna        tiṇṇaṃ vedāna pāragū    
uttamatthaṃ gavesanto        carissati mahiṃ tadā [^567-4] .

So ca pacchā pabbajitvā     sukkamūlena codito    
gotamassa bhagavato          sāsanebhiramissati .

Ārādhayitvāna sambuddhaṃ      gotamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ    
kilese jhāpayitvāna        arahāyaṃ bhavissati .

Pavane byaggharājāva        migarājāva kesarī    
abhīto viharāmajja          sakyaputtassa sāsane .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 568 -->
<a id="page-568"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 568]*

Devaloke manusse vā      dalidde duggatimhi vā    
nibbattiṃ me na passāmi      upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ .

Vivekamanuyuttomhi          upasanto nirūpadhi    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jatukaṇṇiko thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Jatukaṇṇikattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Dasamaṃ udenattherāpadānaṃ (410)    
[412] himavantassa avidūre         padumo nāma pabbato    
assamo sukato mayhaṃ        paṇṇasālā sumāpitā .

Nadiyo sandare [^568-1] tattha     supatitthā manoramā    
acchodakā sītajalā         sandare [^568-2]  nadiyo sadā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 569 -->
<a id="page-569"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 569]*

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      valajā [^569-1] muñjarohitā    
sobhanti nadiyo ete       vasanti nadiyā tadā .

Ambajambūhi sañchannā        kareritilakā tathā    
uddālakā pāṭaliyo        sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Aṅkolakā [^569-2] bimbijālā    māyāharā [^569-3] ca pupphitā    
gandhena upavāyantā [^569-4]     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Adhimuttā sattalikā         nāgā sālā ca pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā [^569-5] sampavantā  sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Kosumbhā salaḷā nīpā       hatthapātā [^569-6] ca pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Harītakā āmalakā          ambā jambū vibhedakā    
kolā bhallātakā bellā    phalāni bahu assame .

Kalambā kadalī tattha         pupphanti mama assame    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Asokapiṇḍī ca varī [^569-7]       nimbarukkhā [^569-8] ca pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Puṇṇāvā giripuṇṇāvā [^569-9]    timirā tattha pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Nigguṇḍī sirinigguṇḍī         camparukkhettha pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

#[^569-1] yu. Jalajā . [^569-2] yu. Ākolakā . [^569-3] ma. Māyākārī . Yu. Māyācārā .

#4-[^569-5] yu. Dibbagandhasamāpannā . Ito paraṃ īdisameva . [^569-6] ma. Aṭṭhaṅgāpi .

#yu. Kaṭṭhaṅgā ca supupphitā . [^569-7] ma. Asokāpiṇḍivārī ca . [^569-8] yu. Nīparukkhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 570 -->
<a id="page-570"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 570]*

Avidūre pokkharaṇī          cakkavākupakujjitā    
mandālakehi sañchannā       padumuppalakehi ca .

Acchodakā sītajalā         supatitthā manoramā    
acchā phalikasamānā         sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Padumā pupphare tattha        puṇḍarīkā ca uppalā    
mandālakehi sañchannā       sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Pāṭhīnā pāvusā macchā      balajā muñjarohitā    
vicarantāva te tattha        sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Kumbhīlā sasasumārā ca        kacchapā ca gahā bahū    
ogahā [^570-1] ajagarā ca      sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Pārevatā ravihaṃsā         cakkavākā nadīcarā    
dindibhā [^570-2] sālikā ceva    sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Nayitā ambagandhī ca         ketakā tattha pupphitā    
dibbā gandhā sampavantā     sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Sīhā byagghā ca dīpī ca      acchakokataracchayo    
anusañcarantā pavane        sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Jaṭābhārena bharitā         ajinuttaravāsino    
anusañcarantā pavane        sobhanti mama assamaṃ .

Ajināni dharā eke [^570-3]     nipakā santavuttino    
appāhārā ca te sabbe    sobhanti mama assamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 571 -->
<a id="page-571"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 571]*

Khārikājaṃ [^571-1] gahetvāna     ajjhogayha vanaṃ tadā    
mūlaphalāni bhuñjantā         vasanti assame tadā .

Na te dāruṃ āharanti       udakaṃ pādadhovanaṃ    
sabbesaṃ ānubhāvena        sayamevāharīyati .

Cullāsītisahassāni          isayo tattha samāgatā    
sabbeva jhāyino ete      uttamatthaṃ gavesino .

Tapassino brahmacārī        codento appanāva te    
ambarāvacarā sabbe        vasanti assame tadā .

Pañcāhaṃ sannipatanti         ekaggā santavuttino    
aññamaññaṃ [^571-2] abhivādetvā   pakkamanti disāmukhā .

Padumuttaro nāma jino       sabbadhammāna pāragū    
tamandhakāraṃ vidhamaṃ           uppajji tāvade jino .

Mama assamasāmantā         yakkho āsi  mahiddhiko    
so me saṃsittha sambuddhaṃ      jalajuttamanāmakaṃ [^571-3] .

Esa buddho samuppanno      padumuttaro mahāmuni    
khippaṃ gantvāna sambuddhaṃ      payirupāsassu [^571-4] mārisa [^571-5] .

Yakkhassa vacanaṃ sutvā        vippasannena cetasā    
assamaṃ saṃsāmetvāna        nikkhamiṃ pavanā tadā .

Celeva ḍayhamānamhi        nikkhamitvāna assamā    
ekarattiṃ nivāsitvā        upagañchiṃ vināyakaṃ .

#[^571-1] ma. Yu. Khāribhāraṃ . [^571-2] ma. Yu. Aññoññaṃ . [^571-3] ma. Yu. ... Nāyakaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 572 -->
<a id="page-572"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 572]*

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
catusaccaṃ pakāsento        desesi amataṃ padaṃ .

Suphullaṃ padumaṃ gayha          upagantvā mahesino    
pasannacitto sumano         buddhassa abhivādayiṃ [^572-1] .

Pūjayitvāna sambuddhaṃ         jalajuttamanāyakaṃ    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         santhaviṃ lokanāyakaṃ .

Yena ñāṇena sambuddho      vasatīha anāsavo    
taṃ ñāṇaṃ kittayissāmi        suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Saṃsārasotaṃ chinditvā        tāresi sabbapāṇinaṃ    
tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna         taṇhāsotaṃ taranti te .

Tuvaṃ satthā ca ketuva [^572-2]     dhajo yūpova [^572-3] pāṇinaṃ    
parāyano patiṭṭhāsi         dīpo ca dipaduttamo [^572-4] .

Yāvatā gaṇino loke       satthavāhā pavuccare    
tuvaṃ aggosi sappañño       tava antogadhāva te .

Tava ñāṇena sappañño [^572-5]    tāresi janataṃ bahuṃ    
tava dassanamāgamma          dukkhassantaṃ karissare .

Yekecime gandhajātā       loke vāyanti cakkhuma    
tava gandhasamo natthi         puññakkhette mahāmuni .

Tiracchānayoniṃ nirayaṃ         parimocehi [^572-6] cakkhumā    
asaṅkhataṃ padaṃ santaṃ          desesi tvaṃ mahāmuni .

#[^572-1] ma. Yu. Abhiropayiṃ . 2-[^572-3]  ma. Yu. Ca . [^572-4] ma. Yu. Dipaduttama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 573 -->
<a id="page-573"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 573]*

Padumuttaro lokavidū         āhutīnaṃ paṭiggaho    
bhikkhusaṅghe nisīditvā        imā gāthā abhāsatha .

Yo me ñāṇaṃ apūjesi       pasanno sehi pāṇihi    
tamahaṃ kittayissāmi          suṇātha mama bhāsato .

Tiṃsa kappasahassāni          devaloke ramissati    
sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca       cakkavatti bhavissati .

Suladdhalābho [^573-1] laddhomhi    tosayitvāna subbataṃ    
sabbāsave pariññāya        viharāmi anāsavo .

Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
nāgova bandhanaṃ chetvā      viharāmi anāsavo .

Svāgataṃ vata me āsi       mama buddhassa santike    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Paṭisambhidā catasso         vimokkhāpica aṭṭhime    
chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā         kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udeno thero imā gāthāyo    
Abhāsitthāti .

Udenattherassa apadānaṃ samattaṃ .

Uddānaṃ  
metteyyo puṇṇako thero   mettagū dhotakopica  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 574 -->
<a id="page-574"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 574]*

upasīvo ca nando ca        hemako sattamo tahiṃ .

Todeyyo jatukaṇṇī ca       udeno ca mahāyaso    
tīṇi gāthāsatānettha        asīti tīṇi cuttariṃ .

Metteyyavaggo ekacattāḷīsamo .
